Precision Treatment for Severe KCNT1 Epileptic Encephalopathy: Not All Antisense Oligonucleotides are Born Alike.推荐
PMID 43065594 · Epilepsy currents · 2026 11 18
作者：Eyal S
链接：PubMed · DOI

From Gene Sets to Brain Function: A Physiology-Informed Framework for Multi-Omics Integration in Precision Neurophysiology.浏览
PMID 43065518 · Chronic diseases and translational medicine · 2026 11 19
作者：Ölçüoğlu R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Public omics repositories and multi-omics technologies have substantially expanded the molecular characterization of chronic human diseases by enabling large-scale analyses across diverse biological and clinical contexts. However, in neurological disorders, translating omics-derived associations into mechanistic insight and clinical relevance remains challenging due to the dynamic and multiscale nature of brain function. Gene set-based analytical approaches effectively identify enriched molecular patterns, but are limited in capturing temporal dynamics, network-level interactions, and physiological state dependence that define neural systems. This conceptual methodological review synthesizes foundational infrastructure papers, methodological evaluations, and translational neuroscience literature to critically examine the application of public omics resources in human neurophysiology, focusing on the interpretive gap between molecular-level associations and functional brain processes. We examine major public omics infrastructures, widely used gene set analysis frameworks, the principal strategies for multi-omics integration, and the challenge of batch effects in public data reuse, highlighting both their analytical strengths and structural limitations. Core neurophysiological principles, including synaptic transmission, network dynamics, plasticity, and homeostatic regulation, are discussed to clarify why direct inference from static molecular profiles to brain function is inherently constrained. Clinical illustrations from epilepsy, attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder, and migraine demonstrate how chronic neurological disorders emerge from context-dependent neural dynamics that are not readily reducible to gene set-level summaries. Finally, we outline future directions for integrating artificial intelligence with public omics resources, emphasizing physiology-informed modeling, explainable AI, and ethical considerations to support more interpretable and translational precision health strategies in chronic neurological disease.
When regulation disrupts treatment: controlled-substance policies, continuity of chronic disease care, and health equity.推荐
PMID 43065487 · Frontiers in public health · 2026 11 06
作者：Sepulveda R, Shegog R, Saljuqi AT, Czerniak K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Controlled-substance regulation is intended to reduce misuse and diversion, but its implementation can also create barriers to clinically appropriate treatment. This study examines that tension using patient-reported experiences from MINDSET 2.0, an epilepsy self-management trial, and contextualizes these findings within emerging epilepsy evidence and a targeted narrative review of analogous barriers affecting other chronic conditions. METHODS: A qualitative descriptive study was embedded within the larger MINDSET 2.0 randomized controlled trial. Fifty-three adults with epilepsy recruited from neurology clinics in Arizona and Texas completed semi-structured exit interviews at the conclusion of the trial. Interview transcripts were analyzed using thematic content analysis and NVivo 12, with independent coding, collaborative review, consensus resolution of discrepancies, constant comparison, and assessment of thematic saturation. For the present policy-focused analysis, coded data were examined for recurring experiences related to controlled anti-seizure medication (ASM) access and treatment continuity. A targeted narrative review contextualized these patient experiences within U.S. controlled-substance policy and evidence from epilepsy, chronic pain, attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder (ADHD), and medication-access research. RESULTS: Participants described interacting barriers involving refill timing restrictions, pharmacy processing and verification requirements, medication availability and partial fills, insurance refill limitations, prescriber-pharmacy coordination, travel, and transportation. Patient accounts showed how these barriers could disrupt work and travel, require repeated pharmacy visits, create substantial stress and uncertainty, and interfere with established self-management routines. Some participants reported treatment interruptions; one described breakthrough seizures after running out of medication during a refill and pharmacy-stock delay, while another described considering medication discontinuation after repeated refill difficulties. Participants' experiences also demonstrated that regulatory, payer, pharmacy, and supply-related barriers were not always perceived as distinct but could operate as a cumulative medication-access burden. Recent national epilepsy evidence converges with these findings: a 2026 survey of 236 U.S. neurology clinicians found that 99% had encountered refill difficulty or missed medication days related to controlled-substance restrictions and 96% had encountered breakthrough seizures after missed scheduled ASM doses. Evidence from chronic pain and ADHD identified analogous challenges to treatment continuity. CONCLUSION: Controlled-substance policy should be evaluated using a dual public health standard: prevention of misuse and diversion and preservation of timely access to medically necessary treatment. The patient experiences identified in MINDSET 2.0 suggest that regulatory requirements can interact with insurance rules, pharmacy practices, medication availability, transportation, employment, and other structural factors to create cumulative barriers to treatment continuity. Medication- and condition-specific proportionality, safe refill flexibility, streamlined prescriber-pharmacy workflows, and incorporation of patient-reported access outcomes may help reduce unintended harms while maintaining appropriate safeguards.
Development of a machine learning-based treatment-response prediction model for levetiracetam in pediatric epilepsy: integrating ABCB1 polymorphism and therapeutic drug monitoring.推荐
PMID 43065425 · Frontiers in medicine · 2026 11 06
作者：Liao X, Zhang L, Cheng J, Song F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Empirical dosing of levetiracetam (LEV) in pediatric epilepsy often yields variable outcomes. This study aimed to develop a machine learning-based treatment-response prediction model integrating therapeutic drug monitoring (TDM) and ABCB1 C3435 T polymorphism. METHODS: Clinical, TDM, and genetic data from 346 pediatric patients were retrospectively analyzed. Four machine learning algorithms were evaluated. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) was utilized to interpret feature associations. RESULTS: The XGBoost model demonstrated the highest predictive performance (AUC=0.893). SHAP analysis indicated that LEV trough concentration (Cmin) and ABCB1 genotype were the strongest associative factors for clinical outcomes. The homozygous TT mutation was associated with treatment resistance despite adequate peripheral Cmin. SHAP interaction patterns generated an exploratory hypothesis that ABCB1 genotype may modify the associative relationship between systemic exposure and treatment response. CONCLUSION: Integrating ABCB1 genotyping and TDM via the XGBoost model may provides an interpretable approach for predicting LEV treatment response in pediatric epilepsy. Rather than directly generating individualized dosing regimens, these associative findings offer a hypothesis-generating reference for identifying patients at risk of therapeutic failure, warranting further prospective exposure-response validation.
Herpes Simplex Virus Type 1 Encephalitis With Gram-Negative Bacteremia in a Kidney Transplant Recipient With a Failing Allograft.浏览
PMID 43065255 · Experimental and clinical transplantation : official journal of the Middle East Society for Organ Transplantation · 2026 Oct
作者：Eleftheriadis T, Divani M, Lykotsetas E, Balatsouka A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Herpes simplex virus type 1 encephalitis is extremely rare after kidney transplantation. We report a 37-year-old man who had received a deceased donor kidney transplant 16 years previously and had chronic allograft dysfunction. Before his first infectious episode, return to dialysis was anticipated, and arteriovenous fistula formation had been planned. Baseline serum creatinine was 3.55 mg/dL, which corresponded to an estimated glomerular filtration rate of 22 mL/min/1.73 m2. The most recent outpatient assessment before infection showed a serum creatinine of 4.3 mg/dL, a spot urine protein-to-creatinine ratio of 2.19 g/g, and a cyclosporine trough level of 58 ng/mL. In January 2026, the patient was hospitalized with a lower respiratory tract infection, cytopenias, bleeding manifestations, and worsening graft function that required transient hemodialysis. In April 2026, he was readmitted with fever and later developed a seizure. Cerebrospinal fluid showed pleocytosis, and herpes simplex virus type 1 DNA was detected by FilmArray. Blood cultures were positive for Klebsiella pneumoniae and Pseudomonas aeruginosa, and urine culture grew Klebsiella pneumoniae. The patient improved after renal-adjusted acyclovir and broad-spectrum antibacterial therapy. A few weeks later, he was readmitted with graft pyelonephritis and progressive graft failure that led to chronic hemodialysis and further reduction of immunosuppression. Although this work is based on a single observation, this case illustrated how immune dysfunction associated with advanced allograft failure may compound ongoing immunosuppression and emphasized that bacterial infection does not exclude concurrent herpes simplex virus encephalitis when neurological manifestations develop.
Systemic hypersensitivity to acacia gum as a pharmaceutical excipient: a case report.浏览
PMID 43065093 · Allergy, asthma, and clinical immunology : official journal of the Canadian Society of Allergy and Clinical Immunology · 2026 08 19
作者：Bou Rjeily M, Abow-Mohamed I, Denis-D'Ortun S, Kwok M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Acacia gum (E414), derived from Acacia senegal, is a water-soluble emulsifier used in food and pharmaceuticals for its stabilizing properties [1-3]. Hypersensitivity reactions are mainly reported in occupational settings and are thought to be IgE-mediated, though the specific epitope is unknown [1-5]. No cases of allergic reactions due to ingestion have been documented in the literature. CASE PRESENTATION: A 37-year-old man with hypothyroidism and migraines presented with a 10-year history of episodic headaches, weakness, nasal congestion, and presyncope. Extensive investigations, including sinus surgery, immunotherapy, migraine and seizure treatments, and dietary changes, were unsuccessful. His symptoms resolved after discontinuing Synthroid, raising suspicion of an excipient-related reaction. Acacia gum, an inactive ingredient in Synthroid, was identified as a potential trigger. Switching to compounded levothyroxine without acacia gum led to sustained symptom resolution. A self-administered challenge to acacia gum induced throat tightness, dyspnea, tongue swelling, and near-syncope within 15 min. Skin prick testing was negative to Synthroid and pure levothyroxine. Intradermal testing was positive to Synthroid (6 mm wheal, 12 mm flare) and acacia gum (50 mm wheal), but negative to pure levothyroxine. A placebo-controlled oral challenge to Synthroid induced symptoms 10 min after ingestion of 7.5 mcg (10% of usual dose). Oral challenge to 0.5 g acacia gum reproduced similar symptoms. CONCLUSIONS: This case highlights the importance of evaluating excipients as potential allergens in patients with unexplained or multisystem symptoms. Ingestion of acacia gum may cause systemic allergic reactions, even without prior reported cases. Clinicians should be aware of formulation differences between commercial and compounded medications when assessing drug-related reactions.
Health and social awareness and attitudes toward epilepsy among preclinical and clinical medical students in the United Arab Emirates: a cross-sectional study.推荐
PMID 43065049 · BMC medical education · 2026 08 28
作者：Mohamed MA, Houni AE, Shalaby RH, Al Ahbabi M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy is a common neurological condition affecting over 50 million people worldwide. Despite advances in care, stigma and misconceptions persist and can influence help-seeking and clinical management. In the United Arab Emirates (UAE), limited data are available on how medical students' epilepsy-related knowledge, attitudes, and diagnostic confidence vary across training stages. This study assessed these outcomes among preclinical and clinical medical students and examined whether neurology-rotation exposure was associated with diagnostic confidence. METHODS: We conducted a cross-sectional online survey of undergraduate medical students across seven UAE medical colleges (December 2023-December 2024) using a questionnaire assessing demographics, epilepsy-related knowledge, attitudes, and diagnostic confidence. Students were categorized as preclinical or clinical, and completion of a neurology rotation was recorded. Associations were assessed using chi-square tests and logistic regression; discriminative performance was evaluated using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) analysis. Ethics approval was obtained from the Institutional Review Board of Dubai Medical University (IRB/DMCG/AY23-24/F-16). RESULTS: A total of 201 students participated (83% female; 57% preclinical stage, 43% clinical stage). Diagnostic confidence was low overall (24% confident) and differed by training stage; among respondents with complete data for training stage and confidence (n = 199), 45.2% of clinical-stage students versus 7.8% of preclinical-stage students reported confidence (OR = 9.7, 95% CI: 4.4-21.8; p < 0.001). In multivariable logistic regression, completion of a neurology rotation was associated with higher diagnostic confidence (OR = 4.1, 95% CI: 2.1-7.9; p < 0.001), with modest discrimination (AUC = 0.65). Misconceptions persisted regarding epilepsy treatability (47% correctly identifying it as treatable) and independent living (26% believing people with epilepsy can live alone). CONCLUSIONS: Clinical exposure, particularly through neurology rotations, was associated with higher self-reported diagnostic confidence in epilepsy. Moderate associations between training phase and knowledge outcomes suggest persistent misconceptions, notably regarding epilepsy treatability and independent living. Given the cross-sectional design and self-reported measures, these findings should be interpreted as indicators of perceived preparedness rather than competence, and they support earlier and more structured neurology/epilepsy teaching alongside objective assessment. The modest sample size and uneven institutional representation may limit generalizability.
Evaluation of Antiseizure Potential of Rufinamide and Celecoxib Combination in a PTZ-Kindled Mouse Model Through Electrophysiological, Neurobehavioral, and Molecular Analyses.推荐
PMID 43064901 · Molecular neurobiology · 2026 11 20
作者：Parveen A, Ashraf W, Anjum SMM, Mushtaq RMZ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Pentylenetetrazole (PTZ)-induced kindling is a well-established experimental model for investigating epileptogenesis and evaluating the therapeutic potential of antiseizure drugs and their novel combinations. The current study investigated whether rufinamide (RUF) and celecoxib (CEL) administered individually and in combination during PTZ-kindling acquisition, attenuate seizure progression and associated neurobehavioral, biochemical, histopathological, and molecular alterations in PTZ-kindled mice. Male mice received 11 injections of PTZ at the dose of 40 mg/kg on alternate days along with daily treatment of RUF, CEL, or RUF + CEL throughout the 21-day kindling protocol. Seizure severity was monitored using behavioral seizure scoring and validated by electroencephalographic (EEG) recordings of cortical epileptiform activity. The results demonstrated that RUF alone and RUF + CEL combination versus PTZ-kindled mice significantly attenuated seizure progression and reduced epileptiform EEG discharges (p < 0.01). Behavioral assessments revealed that treated animals exhibited reduced anxiety-like behavior along with improved cognitive performance (p < 0.05-0.001). In addition, depression-like behavior evaluated by the sucrose preference test was significantly improved (p < 0.01). Biochemical analyses indicated reduced lipid peroxidation (MDA) and enhanced antioxidant defenses including SOD, GPx, and CAT, with normalization of AChE activity (p < 0.05). Furthermore, RUF and CEL treatment significantly downregulated the expression of TNF-α, IL-1β, and GFAP, indicating attenuation of neuroinflammatory responses. Histopathological outcomes further supported these findings, demonstrating preservation of neuronal architecture and increased neuronal persistence in the hippocampal subregions. Collectively, these findings suggest that the RUF + CEL combination provides enhanced protection against PTZ-induced epileptogenesis and its associated neurobehavioral and neuropathological alterations, potentially through complementary modulation of neuronal excitability, oxidative stress, and neuroinflammatory pathways.
Potential and pitfalls of artificial intelligence to address disparities in epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43064818 · Nature reviews. Neurology · 2026 11 20
作者：Otte WM, Valente KD, van der Maas FI, Braun KPJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The convergence of artificial intelligence (AI) and health care offers unprecedented opportunities to address global disparities in access to specialized epilepsy diagnosis and treatment, particularly in resource-limited settings. Advances in AI models, combined with increasing internet connectivity and smartphone usage in low-income and middle-income countries, offer promising solutions for epilepsy diagnosis and monitoring through AI-based analysis of electroencephalography recordings captured with affordable open-source systems, smartphone data and electronic health records. However, this technological revolution raises important questions about the preservation and expansion of clinical expertise, AI algorithm transparency and health-care equity. In this Review, we examine how AI can help to address disparities and improve global epilepsy care while maintaining high clinical standards and addressing systemic challenges.
Concerns regarding intranasal midazolam versus mouth-dissolving clobazam in terminating seizures: A randomized controlled trial.推荐

Dopamine dynamics in a mouse model of temporal lobe seizures with impaired consciousness.推荐
PMID 43064665 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 20
作者：Zheng Y, Liu J, Li J, Qu J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Focal temporal lobe seizures often lead to impaired consciousness. Recent work has shown depressed cortical arousal, with slow waves and decreased cortical acetylcholine as a possible mechanism for impaired consciousness in focal seizures. However, the role of other key subcortical arousal systems such as dopamine is largely unknown. We aimed to address this gap by investigating dopamine dynamics in a mouse model of focal limbic seizures with impaired behavioral responsiveness. METHODS: We induced focal limbic seizures by brief electrical hippocampal stimulation in awake head-fixed mice with hippocampal and cortical local field potential recordings. Behavior was assessed by a conditioned auditory response task. We measured cortical dopamine with a genetically encoded fluorescent sensor via fiber photometry. Activity of dopaminergic neurons in the ventral tegmental area (VTA) was recorded by calcium measurements. RESULTS: Focal hippocampal seizures were associated with altered cortical electrophysiological activity and impaired behavioral responsiveness. During seizures, we found sustained increases in dopamine in both medial prefrontal and orbital frontal cortex. We also found increased dopaminergic neuronal activity in the VTA during seizures. Despite these sustained increases, transient task-evoked dopaminergic increases in both cortex and VTA were reduced during seizures. Behavioral responses during seizures were reduced, and auditory stimuli without behavioral responses elicited smaller transient VTA dopaminergic signals compared to stimuli with preserved responses. SIGNIFICANCE: Together, those results reveal distinct dysregulation of dopamine dynamics across sustained and fast time scales during focal limbic seizures. The sustained increases in dopamine suggest that the altered cortical function and impaired consciousness in focal limbic seizures may more closely resemble encephalopathy or delirium than deep sleep. Depressed transient dopamine release with sensory stimuli could also contribute to impaired behavioral responsiveness during seizures. Further work on the role of dopamine could have important therapeutic implications for altered cognition and behavior in people with epilepsy.
An intermediate childhood-onset myoclonic-seizure phenotype within the genetic generalized epilepsy-juvenile myoclonic epilepsy spectrum.推荐

Video-Based Automated Seizure Classification Using Multimodal Large Language Models.推荐
PMID 43064535 · Annals of clinical and translational neurology · 2026 11 20
作者：Zhang L, Jiang R, Monsoor T, Pasqua JN et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Accurately distinguishing between epileptic seizures (ES) and nonepileptic seizures (NES) remains a significant clinical challenge, typically requiring resource-intensive inpatient video-EEG monitoring. We developed a multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based framework for automated extraction of semiological features from seizure videos, followed by classification of events as ES or NES. METHODS: We analyzed 90 seizure videos (45 ES, 45 NES) from 29 patients in an epilepsy monitoring unit. Each event was labeled using expert-reviewed video-EEG and annotated for 24 clinically relevant semiological features. An MLLM framework integrating open-source vision-language models (VLMs) and audio-language models (ALMs) was used to extract these features automatically. The extracted features were then used to train classifiers (K-Nearest Neighbors, XGBoost, and Deep Factorization Machine) to distinguish ES from NES. Model performance was evaluated using leave-one-patient-out (LOPO) cross-validation. RESULTS: Expert-annotated features yielded near-perfect classification using KNN (precision = 0.92, recall = 0.92, F1-score = 0.92, AUC = 0.99). Compared to expert annotations, the MLLM pipeline achieved a mean recall of 0.71, accuracy of 0.58, and F1-score of 0.51 for feature extraction. Using MLLM-extracted features, the KNN classifier (k = 7) achieved a precision of 0.77, recall of 0.76, F1-score of 0.76, and AUC of 0.76, correctly identifying 68 out of 90 events. CONCLUSION: Multimodal large language models can extract clinically relevant semiological features from seizure videos and support interpretable classification of ES versus NES. This approach offers a scalable, explainable tool to assist in video-based epilepsy diagnosis.
Prevalence and determinants of antiseizure medication dispensing among pregnant persons in the United States: A retrospective claims-based cohort study, 2004-2021.推荐
PMID 43064399 · British journal of clinical pharmacology · 2026 11 20
作者：Leal LF, Djidjou PK, Pana R, Bérard A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：AIM: To describe trends and identify factors associated with antiseizure medications (ASMs) dispensing among pregnant persons in the United States. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective claims-based cohort study within AMerican PREGNANcy Mother-Child CohorT (AM-PREGNANT). We identified pregnant persons aged 15-45 years continuously enrolled from 12 months before the last menstrual period (LMP) through pregnancy end, with ≥1 ASM dispensing from 2004 to 2021. Annual prevalence of ASM dispensing was estimated by pregnancy start year. Within a first-ever ASM dispensing cohort, we examined dispensing patterns across pregnancy time windows, ASM-related indications and changes in dispensing during pregnancy. We also assessed factors associated with ASM dispensing at LMP among those initiating ASM therapy before pregnancy. RESULTS: The cohort included 337 449 pregnant persons. Annual ASM dispensing prevalence remained stable (10%-13%). Among those with first-ever ASM dispensing (n = 115 896), 56% had dispensing before pregnancy, of whom 65% had no dispensing during pregnancy. The most common starting ASMs were clonazepam (28%), topiramate (25%) and gabapentin (20%). Dispensing patterns varied by indication: epilepsy was characterized by lamotrigine, levetiracetam and topiramate, generally dispensed for >60 days, whereas psychiatric and pain indications showed more variable patterns, typically with <30 days of dispensing. Factors associated with ASM dispensing at LMP included pre-specified indications, comorbidities and pre-conception medication dispensing; among these, epilepsy/seizure had the strongest association (aOR 2.14; 95% CI 1.78-2.59). CONCLUSIONS: ASM dispensing varied by indication, with most pre-pregnancy dispensing not continuing during pregnancy. Epilepsy/seizure was most strongly associated with dispensing at LMP, with additional associations observed for comorbidities and pre-conception medication dispensing.
Mitochondrial-lipid crosstalk and pharmacological intervention in HPDL deficiency.浏览
PMID 43064259 · Free radical biology & medicine · 2026 11 20
作者：Mero S, Bernardi S, Meschini MC, Naef V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The 4-hydroxyphenylpyruvate dioxygenase-like protein (HPDL) converts 4-hydroxyphenylpyruvate into 4-hydroxymandelate (4-HMA), an intermediate in an alternative pathway for the generation of 4-hydroxybenzoate (4-HB), a precursor of the Coenzyme Q10 (CoQ10) headgroup. However, the pathogenic mechanism of HPDL-related encephalopathy and related phenotypes such as spastic paraplegia (SPG83) remains unclear. In this study, we generated an hpdl-/- zebrafish model using the CRISPR/Cas9 methodology. Larvae exhibited microphthalmia, impaired yolk absorption, and a transition from hypokinetic-like to epilepsy-like activity. For the first time in HPDL-related research, we performed extensive lipidomic profiling, revealing a depletion of di- and triglycerides and cholesterol esters alongside mitochondrial membrane instability, marked by decreased acylcarnitines and increased dilysocardiolipins and hydroxy-acylcarnitines, a signature suggestive of impaired β-oxidation and oxidative damage. Bypass and antioxidant therapies using 4-HMA, 4-HB, and the mitochondrial-targeted scavenger MitoTempo were evaluated for their rescue potential. While almost all of the tested drugs successfully rescued the reactive oxygen species production, brain cell death and the altered neuronal activity, we observed that the metabolic recovery occurred independently of the levels of CoQ10 and CoQ9, which remained depleted. Our findings offer support to the hypothesis that Hpdl deficiency causes disease primarily through mitochondrial redox imbalance and lipid metabolic failure, rather than a simple CoQ10 depletion. In spite of unchanged CoQ10 levels, rescue of oxidative stress, brain cell death, and neuronal hyperexcitability in mutant larvae by 4-HMA, 4-HB, and MitoTempo supports a cytoprotective role for these aromatic metabolites, identifies HPDL as a key regulator of mitochondrial homeostasis, and a promising target for aromatic antioxidant therapy.
Neurological adverse events associated with VEGF/VEGFR pathway inhibitors in patients with cancer: a dual-database disproportionality analysis of individual case safety reports from FAERS and VigiBase.浏览
PMID 43064103 · Progress in neuro-psychopharmacology & biological psychiatry · 2026 11 20
作者：Shen J, Wu S, Zhang J, Lin A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: VEGF/VEGFR pathway inhibitors are widely used in cancer therapy, yet their neurological safety profile remains insufficiently defined. METHODS: We conducted a dual-database disproportionality analysis of individual case safety reports with malignant-neoplasm indications from publicly accessible FAERS (United States; January 2013-March 2025) and licensed VigiBase (global; January 2013-January 2025). Neurological adverse events were mapped to MedDRA Nervous system disorders preferred terms. Reporting odds ratios (RORs) with 95% CIs were calculated, with positive signals defined as a lower 95% CI >1 and at least 3 cases. Subgroup, time-to-onset, serious-outcome, paired laboratory, and exploratory TCGA pathway analyses were performed. RESULTS: Among 1,715,588 FAERS and 1,898,046 VigiBase malignant-neoplasm reports, 171,741 and 166,129 involved VEGF/VEGFR inhibitors, respectively. Neurological events were identified in 19,116 FAERS and 17,259 VigiBase reports. We detected 95 positive neurological signals, including 55 shared by both databases. Common signals included headache, taste disorder, somnolence, cerebrovascular accident, cerebral haemorrhage, cerebral infarction, posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome, seizure, and encephalopathy-related events. Serious outcomes were reported in 68.1% and 66.8% of neurological reports in FAERS and VigiBase, respectively. Among date-evaluable FAERS reports, median reported time to onset was shorter with VEGF/VEGFR inhibitors than with comparators (35 vs. 66 days; P < 0.001). CONCLUSION: VEGF/VEGFR inhibitors showed broad, reproducible neurological adverse-event reporting signals in patients with cancer. The shorter reported time to onset among date-evaluable FAERS reports supports early clinical vigilance but requires prospective validation.
Nonverbal reasoning in children and adolescents with cerebral palsy: associations with gross motor function, MRI, and EEG findings.推荐
PMID 43063983 · Child neuropsychology : a journal on normal and abnormal development in childhood and adolescence · 2026 11 20
作者：Karimova DY, Ibodullayev ZR, Safarova MAQ, Boltayeva ZO
链接：PubMed · DOI

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：To examine the association between gross motor function level and Raven's Colored Progressive Matrices (CPM) performance in children and adolescents with cerebral palsy (CP), compare CPM performance with typically developing participants, and explore associations with MRI and EEG findings. This multicenter cross-sectional study included 40 participants with CP and 20 comparison participants aged 10-17 years. CPM totals were converted to center-derived age-referenced scores; the upper available normative age band was applied above age 11.5 years. Gross motor function was classified with the GMFCS. Clinically indicated MRI and EEG findings were categorized using prespecified systems. Median CPM score was 72 (IQR 64-81) in the CP group and 98 (IQR 92-104) in the comparison group (adjusted difference -26.4, 95% CI -31.2 to -21.6). GMFCS level was inversely associated with CPM score (Spearman rho = -.78, 95% CI -.85 to -.68). Predominant white matter injury was associated with lower CPM scores. The adjusted association with epileptiform EEG abnormalities was inconclusive. CPM performance was associated with GMFCS level and MRI pattern, but the cross-sectional findings do not establish causality. Gross motor classification must not substitute for accessible neuropsychological assessment.
Clinical predictors of monogenic aetiology and pharmacotherapy burden in drug-resistant focal epilepsy: Who benefits most from whole exome sequencing?推荐
PMID 43063608 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 20
作者：Chatterjee T, Panda S, Tiwari S, Kumar R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜BEHAVIORAL SCIENCES：Q2；CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3；PSYCHIATRY：Q3
摘要：BACKGROUND: Whole exome sequencing (WES) is of diagnostic value in focal drug-resistant epilepsy (FDRE) but is costly and not universally available. Identifying the clinical, imaging and electrophysiological features that predict a monogenic aetiology - and the determinants of pharmacotherapy burden - would allow rational selection of candidates for testing. We addressed both questions in a well-phenotyped Indian FDRE cohort. METHODS: One hundred and twenty-five patients with ILAE-defined FDRE underwent phenotyping and proband WES. Variants of uncertain significance (VUS) were re-evaluated against ACMG/AMP combining rules. Patients with a pathogenic or likely pathogenic (P/LP) variant (n = 12) were compared with WES-negative patients (n = 84); patients with only VUS (n = 29) were considered undiagnosed and described separately. Groups were compared using the Mann-Whitney U test (epilepsy-related variables) and Fisher's exact test (categorical features). Multiple linear regression identified independent predictors of the number of concurrent anti seizure medications (ASMs), and multivariable Firth-penalised logistic regression examined predictors of a P/LP diagnosis; effect sizes accompanied univariable associations. RESULTS: After reclassification, WES identified a P/LP variant in 12 patients (9.6 %); a further 29 (23.2 %) carried only VUS. No epilepsy-related variable (age of onset, epilepsy duration, seizure frequency, seizure-free interval, or ASM count) differed between P/LP and WES-negative patients. No categorical feature differed significantly: abnormal developmental history (42 % vs 21 %; OR 2.62, p = 0.152), positive family history (33 % vs 21 %; OR 1.83, p = 0.462) and intellectual deficits (25 % vs 15 %; OR 1.82, p = 0.415) were numerically more frequent in P/LP patients. Diagnostic yield was 14.7 % with abnormal development and 12.5 % with a positive family history, versus 7.7 % and 8.6 % in their absence. The number of concurrent ASMs was independently predicted by intellectual deficit (β = 0.813, p = 0.002) and log seizure frequency (β = 0.192, p = 0.030), but not by a P/LP variant (β = - 0.377, p = 0.261) or a VUS (β = - 0.316, p = 0.192). ASM adverse effects were infrequent (15/125) and unrelated to genetic status. CONCLUSIONS: Clinical presentation does not reliably distinguish patients with a P/LP diagnosis from WES-negative patients with FDRE. Abnormal development, family history and intellectual disability showed only non-significant enrichment of diagnostic yield in this P/LP group and require validation in larger cohorts. Pharmacotherapy burden was associated with intellectual disability and seizure frequency but was not explained by WES results.
Validation and reliability of the Epilepsy Self-Stigma Scale (ESSS) in Italian.推荐
PMID 43063607 · Epilepsy & behavior : E&B · 2026 11 20
作者：Spagnoli C, Nucera B, Cerulli Irelli E, Dono F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜BEHAVIORAL SCIENCES：Q2；CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3；PSYCHIATRY：Q3
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Self-stigma is the interiorization and acceptance of prejudicial beliefs of one person's culture of origin. Higher levels of self-stigma in epilepsy negatively affect quality of life and outcome. METHODS: We investigated self-stigma in people with epilepsy (PwE) in Italy, by translating, culturally adapting and validating the Italian version of the Epilepsy Self-Stigma Scale (ESSS). For psychometric validation, the scale (together with GAD7, NDDI-E, QOLIE31 and RSES) was filled-in by 87 adult PwE anonymously and voluntarily participating to an online survey broadcasted on "Fondazione Epilessia LICE ETS" social media. Clinical and demographic data were collected. RESULTS: Internal consistency was good (Cronbach's α = 0.74). Reliability was high (Omega Total = 0.87). Higher stigma scores were moderately associated with greater anxiety (GAD-7; ρ = 0.43, p < 0.001) and depressive symptoms (NDDI-E; ρ = 0.39, p < 0.001). Negative correlations were observed with self-esteem (RSES; r = -0.45, p < 0.001) and quality of life (QOLIE-31; ρ = -0.42, p < 0.001). Individuals with lower levels of independence showed higher stigma scores. SIGNIFICANCE: The 8-item Italian version of ESSS is valid and reliable. The findings suggest self-stigma is closely associated with quality of life through psychological factors, particularly self-esteem, although relationships should be interpreted cautiously given the cross-sectional design.
Safety and effectiveness of surgery for medically refractory insular epilepsy in children: a systematic review and individual participant data meta-analysis.推荐
PMID 43063469 · Journal of neurosurgery. Pediatrics · 2026 11 20
作者：Goel A, Seri S, Lo WB, Pepper J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3；PEDIATRICS：Q2；SURGERY：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Insular epilepsy is an increasingly diagnosed cause of pediatric drug-refractory focal epilepsy. However, adoption of surgical treatments-resective or ablative-has been limited due to concerns about neurological risks. This systematic review and individual participant data meta-analysis aimed to identify predictors of seizure freedom and neurological deficits following surgery in children with insular epilepsy. METHODS: A systematic search of 4 databases up to August 2024 was undertaken to identify studies reporting postsurgical outcomes in patients aged under 18 years with insular epilepsy. Studies with follow-up under 12 months were excluded. Quality assessment was performed using the Grading of Recommendations Assessment, Development, and Education (GRADE) framework, and risk of bias was evaluated using Risk of Bias in Non-randomized Studies of Interventions (ROBINS-I). Published institutional results from 11 patients were included within the analysis. Multivariable logistic regression was performed to identify predictors of seizure freedom and neurological complications. RESULTS: Of 154 identified studies, 12 retrospective studies reporting on 97 patients met the inclusion criteria. The median follow-up duration was 37 months. At last follow up, 57% of patients were seizure free, defined as Engel class I or International League Against Epilepsy class 1 and 2. Patients who underwent preoperative stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG) had significantly lower odds of seizure recurrence (OR 0.25, CI 0.06-0.95, p = 0.047). Procedure type (resection vs ablation; OR 0.66, 95% CI 0.13-3.27, p = 0.606), age at surgery (OR 1.05, 95% CI 0.94-1.18, p = 0.392), and surgical extent (insular only versus insular "plus" surgery; OR 0.81, 95% CI 0.17-3.59, p = 0.777) were not significant predictors of seizure freedom. Postoperative permanent neurological deficits occurred in 11% of children, with motor deficits being the most common. Younger age at surgery was associated with greater odds of motor deficit (OR 0.88, 95% CI 0.78-0.99, p = 0.04). CONCLUSIONS: This is the first and largest systematic review and individual participant data meta-analysis to report the outcomes of insular epilepsy surgery in a purely pediatric cohort. Both ablative and resective approaches are feasible, with over half of patients achieving seizure freedom and over two-thirds of neurological deficits being transient.
Radiation-induced versus sporadic cerebral cavernous malformations: comparative analysis of incidence and hemorrhage risk.推荐
PMID 43063467 · Journal of neurosurgery · 2026 11 20
作者：Bishay AE, Koutsouras GW, Lyons AT, Narayanan SN et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜2区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；SURGERY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Radiation exposure has emerged as a recognized risk factor for cerebral cavernous malformation (CCM). Radiation-induced CCM (XRT-CCM) may arise months to decades after treatment and may carry higher hemorrhage risk than non-XRT-CCM, though studies remain limited. This study aimed to compare clinical characteristics, hemorrhage rates, and surgical management between XRT-CCM and non-XRT-CCM. METHODS: A single-institution retrospective cohort study examined patients with MRI-confirmed CCM (1987-2024). All XRT-CCM and non-XRT-CCM patients, regardless of presentation type, were included. The primary outcome was symptomatic hemorrhage following diagnosis, and the secondary outcome was surgical intervention. Analysis included descriptive statistics, group comparisons, hemorrhage incidence, multivariable logistic regression for hemorrhage risk, and Cox regression for hemorrhage-free survival. RESULTS: The cohort comprised 612 patients: 16 XRT-CCM and 596 non-XRT-CCM patients. The XRT-CCM group included 11 incidental (68.8%) and 5 non-seizure-associated hemorrhage (31.3%) presenters, while the non-XRT-CCM group included 336 incidental (56.4%), 123 non-seizure-associated hemorrhage (20.6%), and 137 seizure (23.0%) presenters. Demographic characteristics were similar between groups. XRT-CCM patients received radiation at a median age of 11.9 years, with diagnosis a median of 13.5 years later. The mean ± SD radiation dosage was 47.8 ± 15.1 Gy, most commonly for arteriovenous malformations (31%). Multivariable analysis showed that radiation was significantly associated with hemorrhage (OR 5.23, 95% CI 1.35-20.36), while presentation type was not. Hemorrhage incidence was 7.77 per 100 person-years for XRT-CCM patients versus 4.36 for non-XRT-CCM patients (incidence rate ratio 1.78). Surgical intervention rates did not differ significantly between groups (25.0% vs 21.1%), nor did primary surgical indications, with hemorrhage being the most common indication in both XRT-CCM (75.0%) and non-XRT-CCM (43.7%) patients. CONCLUSIONS: XRT-CCM demonstrated a higher hemorrhage incidence compared to non-XRT-CCM, with radiation exposure significantly associated with symptomatic hemorrhage. However, radiation exposure was not significantly associated with time to hemorrhage on survival analysis. The extended latency from radiation to CCM diagnosis underscores the need for long-term surveillance in patients exposed to cranial irradiation.
Cyclin-dependent kinase-like 5 (CDKL5) binds talin and is anchored at the postsynaptic density by direct PDZ interactions.浏览
PMID 43063264 · Science advances · 2026 11 20
作者：Otani Y, Goodall E, Srinivasan V, Ball NJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜综合性期刊｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜MULTIDISCIPLINARY SCIENCES：Q1
摘要：Cyclin-dependent kinase-like 5 (CDKL5) is a serine/threonine kinase essential for brain development and function. Mutations in CDKL5 cause CDKL5 deficiency disorder (CDD), a severe early-onset epileptic encephalopathy associated with impaired synapse formation and function. However, how CDKL5 mutations disrupt synaptic function remains unclear. Here, we show that the major neuronal isoform of CDKL5 contains a C-terminal PDZ domain-binding motif that mediates direct binding to PSD-95 and SHANK PDZ domains and recruits CDKL5 to the postsynaptic density. Disrupting this motif mislocalized CDKL5, reduced dendritic spine density, and impaired spine maturation. We also show that the CDKL5 kinase domain binds the R8 rod domain of the mechanosensitive scaffold talin. Mutation of the talin-binding interface or reduced cellular contractility altered CDKL5 positioning relative to focal adhesions. These findings define how CDKL5 is targeted to synapses, suggest that talin mechanoswitching may position CDKL5 kinase activity, and highlight the importance of C-terminal anchoring for interpreting pathogenic truncations.
A de novo HDAC3 catalytic-histidine variant reveals a catalysis-predominant mechanism in infantile epileptic spasms syndrome.推荐
PMID 43062975 · Human molecular genetics · 2026 Nov 19
作者：Chen Y, Li Z, Hu S, Pan H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：De novo missense variants in HDAC3 cause a variable neurodevelopmental disorder. However, previously reported variants produced overlapping abnormalities in catalytic activity, corepressor interactions, and nuclear localization, obscuring the contribution of individual HDAC3 functions. We identified a de novo HDAC3 variant, NM_003883.4:c.403C > A, p.(His135Asn), in a girl with infantile epileptic spasms syndrome and profound developmental impairment. The HDAC3 H135N substitution replaces the second histidine of the conserved H134-H135 catalytic motif within the HDAC3 active center and is spatially separated from the principal NCoR/SMRT deacetylase-activating domain (DAD) interface. In HEK293T cells, variant HDAC3 showed steady-state abundance and nuclear enrichment comparable to wild type, and NCoR1, NCoR2/SMRT, and CoREST/RCOR1 were detectable in immunoprecipitates from both wild-type and H135N samples under the tested conditions, with no overt difference. By contrast, HDAC3-associated deacetylase activity was reduced, with increased H3K9ac and H3K27ac signals but no significant change in pan-H3ac. These findings are consistent with a catalysis-predominant defect under the conditions examined, without marked changes in HDAC3 abundance, nuclear enrichment, or detectable association with the tested corepressors. Together with previously reported variants, this case suggests that severe HDAC3-related neurodevelopmental impairment may occur in the absence of primary microcephaly.
The Benefits of Physical Exercise on Epilepsy Treatment: Possible Mechanisms via Neurotransmitters Modulation.推荐
PMID 43062894 · The International journal of neuroscience · 2026 11 20
作者：Taherkhani S, Bagheri Tadi F, Amani P, Mohajerian R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：This narrative review aims to critically explored current preclinical and clinical evidence on the therapeutic benefits of physical exercise in epilepsy management, with particular emphasis on the underlying mechanisms mediated by modulation of key neurotransmitter systems, including GABAergic, glutamatergic, monoaminergic, and neurotrophic pathways. It seeks to reframe exercise from a historically perceived risk factor to a promising non-pharmacological adjunct capable of reducing seizure susceptibility and improving comorbid outcomes. A comprehensive narrative synthesis of the literature was conducted by searching major databases (PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Web of Science) for peer-reviewed English-language studies examining physical exercise or activity in experimental epilepsy models and people with epilepsy. Inclusion prioritized investigations of neurotransmitter alterations (GABA, glutamate, dopamine, serotonin, noradrenaline), BDNF signaling, neuroinflammation, oxidative stress, and related clinical endpoints such as seizure frequency, cognition, and quality of life. Accumulating evidence demonstrates that regular moderate-to-vigorous aerobic and resistance exercise reduces seizure frequency and severity in animal models and selected clinical cohorts, while concurrently ameliorating depression, anxiety, cognitive deficits, and overall quality of life. Mechanistically, exercise elevates brain GABA levels and GABAergic neurosteroids, restores excitatory-inhibitory balance by modulating glutamate, enhances monoaminergic transmission (dopamine, serotonin, noradrenaline), and robustly upregulates hippocampal BDNF and downstream plasticity pathways (ERK/mTOR). Physical exercise emerges as a safe, accessible, and multifaceted complementary strategy in epilepsy treatment, exerting anticonvulsant and neuroprotective actions principally through neurotransmitter modulation and neurotrophic support. Integration of structured exercise into multidisciplinary care is warranted, although larger randomized trials are needed to optimize intensity, modality, and patient stratification, particularly in drug-resistant epilepsy.
The global macroeconomic burden of idiopathic epilepsy: estimates for 2021 and projections to 2050.推荐
PMID 43062887 · Neuroepidemiology · 2026 11 20
作者：Jin X, Sun Y, Wang Y, Li R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Idiopathic epilepsy imposes substantial health and economic burdens worldwide, yet evidence on welfare-based macroeconomic losses attributable specifically to idiopathic epilepsy remains limited. This study estimated the global, regional, and national welfare burden of idiopathic epilepsy across 190 countries and territories in 2021 and projected this burden to 2050. METHODS: Disability-adjusted life years (DALYs) and healthy life expectancy were obtained from the Global Burden of Disease Study 2021; gross domestic product (GDP) and GDP per capita from the World Bank's World Development Indicators database; and country-, sex-, and age-specific population estimates and medium-variant projections from the United Nations World Population Prospects 2024. VLW was estimated using the value of a statistical life and the value of a statistical life year and expressed in purchasing power parity-adjusted constant 2021 international dollars. Age- and sex-specific DALY rates were projected to 2050 using exponential smoothing state-space models and combined with the corresponding population projections. RESULTS: In 2021, the global undiscounted VLW attributable to idiopathic epilepsy was Int$1,138.013 billion, equivalent to 0.737% of global GDP. By 2050, it was projected to reach Int$1,287.730 billion, equivalent to 0.834% of the 2021 global GDP benchmark and 13.156% above the 2021 level. Absolute VLW was concentrated in high-income and populous regions, particularly the United States, China, and India, whereas the relative burden was greatest in low-SDI regions and Sub-Saharan Africa. Higher VLW was observed among adolescents, young and middle-aged adults, and males. CONCLUSIONS: Idiopathic epilepsy causes substantial and growing global welfare losses with marked inequalities. Strengthening prevention, diagnosis, affordable antiseizure medication access, primary care integration, and anti-stigma interventions is essential, especially in low-resource settings.
Rituximab-responsive epilepsia partialis continua in autoimmune Polyendocrine syndrome: A video-EEG-EMG polygraphic study.推荐
PMID 43062846 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 20
作者：Pascarella A, Rossini F, Ferrazzoli C, Leitinger M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：Epilepsia partialis continua (EPC) is a rare and challenging form of focal motor status epilepticus. Its occurrence strictly in systemic autoimmune disorders without overt encephalitis is exceptional. Corticosteroids are first-line therapy but may be limited by significant side effects and poor metabolic tolerability. We report a 54-year-old woman with Autoimmune Polyendocrine Syndrome (APS), including autoimmune hepatitis, latent autoimmune diabetes in adults, celiac disease, and hypothyroidism, presenting with continuous jerks of right III-V fingers. Neurological examination showed continuous, irregular jerks confined to the right III, IV, and V fingers. Extensive serum and CSF screenings, including neuronal antibodies, and MRI were unremarkable. Ictal HMPAO-SPECT revealed a focal hyperperfusion in the left hand-knob area of the primary motor cortex. Brain FDG-PET revealed focal hypometabolism in the same region. Video-EEG-EMG polygraphy showed intermittent spikes and polyspikes over the left central region preceding EMG bursts of the right V finger's abductor muscle with a latency of approximately 30-40 ms. These findings were consistent with EPC. Corticosteroids induced marked improvement but were discontinued due to severe glycemic worsening. Subsequent treatment with oral steroids, IVIG, and multiple antiseizure medications were ineffective. Administration of Rituximab led to complete and sustained clinical and electrophysiological remission, which persisted at the 18-month follow-up. EPC can be a severe, focal manifestation of systemic autoimmunity even without encephalitis. Rituximab represents an effective steroid-sparing option for refractory autoimmune EPC, particularly in metabolically vulnerable patients.
Clinical outcomes and EEG monitoring in absence epilepsy trials: A narrative review.推荐
PMID 43062842 · Epilepsia open · 2026 11 20
作者：Sourbron J, Japaridze G, Rotenberg A, Dlugos D et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：Absence epilepsy is characterized by typical absence seizures with distinct electroclinical signatures. The main semiological feature is sudden behavioral arrest associated with brief impairment of consciousness, and the electroencephalographic (EEG) hallmark pattern is generalized, symmetric 2.5-4 Hz spike-and-wave discharges (GSW). Absence epilepsy diagnosis relies on correlating GSW with observable clinical manifestations. Monitoring therapeutic effects based solely on reported symptoms may be misleading, as behavioral arrest can be difficult to identify. Patients often do not recall most seizures, and observable motor/behavioral signs may be subtle, such as minimal twitches or transient lapses in responsiveness, which may go unnoticed. In clinical trials for absence epilepsies, quantification of GSW activity by EEG offers an objective and accurate measure of electrical burden and therapeutic efficacy. Despite technological limitations, GSW remains a robust neurophysiological endpoint. Increasing evidence indicates that the timing and burden of GSW correlate with epilepsy comorbidities, including attention, cognition, behavior, and sleep. We reviewed the published literature regarding GSW as an outcome measure in absence epilepsy, including nonseizure outcomes and comorbidities. Our review highlights (1) nearly 100% of clinical absence seizures exhibit sustained GSW discharges on EEG; (2) >49% of electrographic seizures are asymptomatic, indicating that reliance on seizure diaries without EEG monitoring underestimates seizure frequency and burden; (3) Genetic Generalized Epilepsy is not a benign condition and is associated with a high prevalence of cognitive and behavioral comorbidities, supporting evaluation beyond clinically observable seizures and the use of prolonged (≥24-h) EEG monitoring; (4) circadian and multidien cycles further emphasize the need for prolonged EEG recordings to reduce timing-related bias; and (5) antiseizure medications effective in absence epilepsy reduce GSW, supporting EEG monitoring as an objective treatment outcome measure. We therefore recommend incorporating ≥24-h EEG in future clinical trials, especially in (young) adults, and exploring comorbidities as important outcome measures. PLAIN LANGUAGE SUMMARY: Absence epilepsy causes brief episodes in which a person suddenly stops what they are doing and may not be aware of their surroundings. Many of these seizures have no obvious signs and are often missed or forgotten, meaning seizure diaries alone do not show the full picture. Our review shows that brain recordings (electroencephalographic, EEG), especially when done over 24 h or longer, detect many unrecognized seizures and better measure how well treatments work. EEG findings are also linked to problems with attention, thinking, behavior, and sleep, highlighting the need to look beyond visible seizures when evaluating treatments in clinical trials.
Intrarater reliability of expert electroencephalographic interpretation of 100 full-length recordings.推荐
PMID 43062806 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 20
作者：Yuan D, Sheikh IS, Katyal R, Kaplan PW et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: The value of electroencephalography (EEG) relies on accurate and reliable interpretation. Prior research has demonstrated imperfect interrater reliability, but intrarater reliability remains poorly characterized. We performed a "noise audit" primarily to measure intrarater variability in EEG interpretation by epileptologists. METHODS: Experts independently rated the same 100 full-length EEGs on two separate occasions ≥60 days apart, unaware the EEGs were the same. EEGs were recorded from adult (56%) and pediatric patients in outpatient (78%) and non-critical care inpatient settings. There were 20 studies in each of five categories: normal, focal epileptiform (FE), generalized epileptiform (GE), focal nonepileptiform (FNE), and generalized nonepileptiform (GNE) abnormalities. EEGs were presented using our online platform (EEGHub) without clinical information except for age and sex. Experts rated each recording and indicated their level of confidence (LOC) for each EEG and overall. We analyzed percent as well as chance-corrected intra- and interrater agreement. RESULTS: Fourteen experts (mean of 19 years EEG reading experience, range = 4-52 years) were included in the study. Percent self-agreement was 96% for GE, 90% for FE, 88% for FNE, and 87% for GNE abnormalities; 90% for any epileptiform abnormality; and 82% for any nonepileptiform abnormality. Self-agreement was strongly correlated with interrater agreement (mean κML), which was almost perfect for GE (.90, 95% confidence interval [CI] = .87-.94) and FE (.82, 95% CI = .77-.87) and substantial for GNE (.75, 95% CI = .69-.81) and FNE (.74, 95% CI = .68-.80) abnormalities. LOC correlated with self-agreement on the presence of nonepileptiform but not epileptiform abnormalities. SIGNIFICANCE: We found generally strong within-expert agreement in EEG interpretation, especially in the identification of epileptiform abnormalities. Nevertheless, the number of flipped decisions was high enough to be clinically consequential. This undesired variability ("occasion noise") may be addressed by interventions to (1) decrease this noise directly and (2) reduce case ambiguity with longer recordings and better EEG data whenever feasible.

Brivaracetam in Genetic Generalized Epilepsies and Developmental and Epileptic Encephalopathies: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.推荐
PMID 43062785 · Neurology and therapy · 2026 11 20
作者：Mousdi M, Stavrogianni K, Poprelka K, Kotsali-Peteinelli V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to evaluate the efficacy, retention, safety, and tolerability of brivaracetam (BRV) in patients with genetic generalized epilepsies (GGEs) and developmental and/or epileptic encephalopathies (DEEs). METHODS: We systematically searched the PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, and Cochrane databases for studies published up to October 19, 2026. Methods followed PRISMA (Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses) 2020 guidelines. Random-effects meta-analyses of proportions were performed to estimate pooled proportions of patients achieving ≥ 50% reduction in seizure frequency, seizure freedom, retention rates, and safety and tolerability. Subgroup analyses were performed separately for juvenile myoclonic epilepsy (JME) and DEEs. RESULTS: Twenty studies met the inclusion criteria. The pooled ≥ 50% responder rate was 50% (95% confidence interval [CI] 35-66%), while the pooled seizure-freedom rate was 22% (95% CI 11-39%). In the GGE subgroup, pooled ≥ 50% responder and seizure-freedom rates were 61% (95% CI 41-78%) and 38% (95% CI 17-65%), respectively. In the JME subgroup, pooled ≥ 50% responder and seizure-freedom rates were 77% (95% CI 45-93%) and 67% (95% CI 57-75%), respectively. In patients with DEEs, the pooled ≥ 50% responder rate was 44% (95% CI 24-66%). The pooled 12-month retention rate was 62% (95% CI 42-79%). The pooled proportion of patients experiencing any treatment-emergent adverse events (TEAEs) was 37% (95% CI 19-60%), while 7% (95% CI 3-19%) discontinued BRV due to TEAEs. CONCLUSION: BRV appears to provide clinically meaningful seizure control with favorable retention and tolerability in GGEs, particularly in JME. Although the available evidence also suggests potential benefit in DEEs, conclusions remain limited by small, predominantly retrospective studies and substantial clinical heterogeneity.

Revascularization of Renal Artery Stenosis for "Flash Cerebral Edema" (Posterior Reversible Encephalopathy Syndrome).浏览
PMID 43062395 · JACC. Case reports · 2026 11 20
作者：Yanik AJ, Bhatnagar V, Burdick TR, Block CA
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Appropriate patient selection for renal artery stenosis (RAS) revascularization remains controversial. Consensus guidelines suggest certain high-risk groups benefit. Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) has rarely been connected to RAS and is not generally included as an indication for revascularization. CASE SUMMARY: A 69-year-old woman presented with confusion and seizures. Brain imaging demonstrated PRES. Blood pressure was 230/110 mm Hg and required nicardipine infusion, despite maximal doses of 4 oral medications. Duplex ultrasonography demonstrated RAS; angiography confirmed right renal artery high-grade ostial stenosis. Percutaneous balloon angioplasty and stent insertion were performed, resulting in immediate improvement in hypertension. At follow-up 6 months later, she was normotensive, and all antihypertensive medications had been discontinued. DISCUSSION: Clinicians must remain observant for appropriate candidates for RAS revascularization, as high-risk groups were excluded from the pivotal trials. Presentation of RAS with "flash" pulmonary edema is a supported indication for revascularization; PRES has pathophysiology akin to "flash" pulmonary edema and can respond to revascularization. PRES should be included in the list of potential indications for revascularization. TAKE-HOME MESSAGE: PRES warrants consideration of RAS and addition to the list of conditions that may benefit from revascularization of RAS.
Genetic Risk Stratification for Pediatric Fever-Associated Seizures: A Single-Center Retrospective Cohort.推荐
PMID 43062212 · Journal of visualized experiments : JoVE · 2026 11 13
作者：Deng X, Lu Q, Ma J, Wang S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Fever-associated seizures are a common pediatric emergency. Complex febrile seizures and fever-sensitive epilepsy show marked genetic heterogeneity, but real-world evidence linking genetic information to the prediction of short-term recurrence and readmission remains insufficient. This single-center real-world retrospective cohort included children with their first qualifying fever-associated seizure presentation at the center from 2019 to 2023 and at least 12 months of follow-up. Genetic variants were classified according to American College of Medical Genetics and Genomics criteria, and pathway-based genetic risk tiers were constructed. Multivariable Cox models were used to evaluate associations between pathogenic or likely pathogenic variants and 12-month recurrence and readmission. Prediction models based on clinical variables alone and models additionally incorporating genetic risk tiers were compared using the C-index, 12-month area under the curve, Brier score, net reclassification improvement, integrated discrimination improvement, bootstrap correction, and decision curve analysis. The overall pathogenic or likely pathogenic detection rate was 13.64%, with a higher rate in fever-sensitive epilepsy than in complex febrile seizures. Pathogenic or likely pathogenic variants were independently associated with recurrence and readmission, and high-risk pathways showed stronger effects. Adding genetic risk tiers modestly improved internally validated predictive performance and showed greater decision-curve net benefit. These findings suggest that genetic information may have potential value for short-term risk stratification and post-discharge management in children with fever-associated seizures; however, independent external validation is required before clinical implementation.
Successful Living Donor Liver Transplantation for Acute Intermittent Porphyria in an Adolescent: A Case Report and Review of the Literature.推荐
PMID 43062069 · Pediatric transplantation · 2026 Nov
作者：Gautam V, Chopra A, Panda K, Kumar V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Acute intermittent porphyria (AIP) is a rare metabolic disorder characterized by recurrent neurovisceral crises and significant morbidity. While liver transplantation (LT) offers definitive metabolic correction in refractory disease, pediatric experience has not been reported. METHOD: We report a 14-year-old male with recurrent neurovisceral attacks, seizures, autonomic instability, and psychiatric manifestations. Diagnosis of AIP was established by markedly elevated urinary porphobilinogen during an attack, an increased total urine porphyrins/creatinine ratio, and confirmation of a heterozygous HMBS mutation. Due to failure of medical therapy and persistent refractory symptoms, living donor liver transplantation (LDLT) was performed with meticulous perioperative planning, including avoidance of porphyrogenic drugs, continuous dextrose infusion, and anticoagulation strategies. RESULTS: The patient underwent LDLT using a modified right lobe graft with his mother as the donor and had an uneventful intraoperative course. Postoperatively, he was extubated on postoperative day (POD) 1 and showed gradual normalization of biochemical parameters including serum sodium and renal function. Serial Doppler studies confirmed satisfactory graft perfusion. There was no recurrence of porphyria attacks. Neurological and psychiatric symptoms improved progressively, and gastrointestinal issues resolved completely. He was discharged in stable condition on POD 14. CONCLUSION: Definitive metabolic correction of refractory pediatric AIP occurs with LDLT, with resolution of attacks and marked clinical improvement. Early consideration of LDLT in carefully selected patients can result in a good outcome.
Beyond the Brain-Gallbladder Imaging as an Early Clue in Metachromatic Leukodystrophy.推荐
PMID 43061783 · Journal of medical imaging and radiation oncology · 2026 11 20
作者：Virdi A, Hadian F, Suttatharn P, Fecteau A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Metachromatic leukodystrophy (MLD) is a debilitating neurological disorder caused by arylsulfatase A deficiency, leading to sulfatide accumulation within the central and peripheral nervous system. This manifests as progressive loss of developmental milestones, spasticity, seizures, gait disturbances and peripheral neuropathy. Sulfatide also accumulates in extra-neural tissues, notably the gallbladder, where children with MLD may develop polyposis and mural thickening even prior to neurological symptom onset. Gallbladder lesions require longitudinal follow-up because of their malignant potential. Sonographic features of gallbladder pathology in four children (mean age 4.1 years, range: 2.6-6.1) with MLD are shown. These include gallbladder wall thickening-with or without polyposis, a mass-like configuration of the gallbladder wall and gallbladder contraction despite adequate fasting. Only one patient underwent cholecystectomy, a neurologically asymptomatic child with abdominal pain and persistent polyposis on serial gallbladder ultrasound. Histopathology revealed mucosal papillary hyperplasia, a characteristic of MLD. The remaining patients had persistent sonographic abnormalities of the gallbladder. When detected in neurologically asymptomatic patients, recognizing gallbladder manifestations on ultrasound can alter disease trajectory by facilitating early diagnosis of MLD.
Takotsubo Syndrome in Acute and Chronic Neurological Conditions: A Systematic Review of Cardiac Phenotypes and Clinical Outcomes.推荐
PMID 43061696 · Cureus · 2026 11 19
作者：Jangirashvili T, Parthiv O, Kurtsikidze S, Srinivas S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Takotsubo syndrome (TTS) is an acute, usually reversible form of left ventricular dysfunction that mimics acute coronary syndrome and carries a long-term prognosis comparable to that of myocardial infarction. Its pathogenesis has been linked to the brain-heart axis, and TTS has been described after acute neurological events and in patients with chronic neurological disease. However, the evidence remains fragmented across individual conditions, and acute triggers are rarely distinguished from pre-existing disorders. The objective here is to systematically review primary observational evidence on TTS in adults with acute and chronic neurological conditions, with emphasis on the temporal relationship, cardiac phenotypes, complications, and clinical outcomes, and to summarize the mechanistic evidence reported by the included studies. A systematic literature search was conducted across PubMed/MEDLINE, ScienceDirect, Google Scholar, and ResearchGate (January 2016 to September 2026) in accordance with Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) 2020 guidelines, supplemented by citation searching. Eligibility criteria, data extraction, and quality appraisal with the Newcastle-Ottawa Scale were predefined. Of 3170 records, 16 primary observational studies were included: five multicenter registry analyses, four administrative database analyses, five cohort studies, one case-control study, and one cross-sectional study. Reviews and case reports provided context only, and reports from overlapping data sources were not pooled. In the International Takotsubo Registry, an acute neurological disorder preceded TTS in 6.7% of 2402 patients, most often seizures, intracranial hemorrhage, or cerebral ischemia, and TTS was diagnosed within two days of neurological symptoms in 87.3%. In the German-Italian-Spanish Takotsubo Registry, 17% of 2301 patients had a neurological disorder, mostly pre-existing. Neurologically associated TTS showed lower left ventricular ejection fraction (37.4% vs 41.0%), more cardiogenic shock (15.1% vs 8.6%), and higher in-hospital mortality (17.4% vs 4.0%; adjusted odds ratio 3.20), which reached 30.4% after intracranial hemorrhage. A neurological disorder independently predicted long-term mortality (hazard ratio 1.72). Migraine was associated with lower adjusted mortality, whereas amyotrophic lateral sclerosis was associated with a more severe in-hospital course. Ballooning patterns differed only modestly, and chest pain was reported in 29.0% of neurologically associated cases compared with 72.9% of other cases. Evidence for traumatic brain injury was limited to one strain-defined cohort and coded trauma data, and direct mechanistic measurements were scarce. Neurological disease identifies a higher-risk subgroup of patients with TTS who often present without chest pain. TTS should be considered in patients with acute neurological events who develop unexplained hemodynamic instability, electrocardiographic changes, or troponin elevation, and echocardiography should anticipate more severe systolic dysfunction. The evidence base is small, partly overlapping, and diagnostically heterogeneous, and outcomes were reported as all-cause. Prospective studies with standardized diagnostic criteria and separate reporting of cardiac and neurological outcomes are needed.
Case Report: Two cases of pediatric neurodevelopmental disorders associated with the DPYD c.1905+1G>A genetic variant.推荐
PMID 43061662 · Frontiers in genetics · 2026 11 06
作者：Bembenek BM, Offer SM
