JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Dihydropyrimidine dehydrogenase (DPD, gene: DPYD) catalyzes the first step of pyrimidine catabolism. DPD deficiency contributes to a rare inborn error of metabolism associated with pediatric neurological manifestations that show wide variability in clinical presentation with incomplete penetrance (OMIM #274270). Common symptoms include autism spectrum disorder-like behaviors, ataxia, and global developmental delay, with variable involvement of seizures. In this report, we present one case of pediatric DPD deficiency accompanied by epilepsy-like syndromes, and a second case presenting with ataxia and dystonia in carriers of the deleterious DPYD c.1905+1G>A variant (rs3918290, DPYD*2A, ClinVar #432). One subject also carried a variant in the KMT2B gene (c.3014G>A; ClinVar #1333975) that was predicted to be deleterious, which likely contributed to dystonia (OMIM #617284) independently of DPD deficiency. In Subject 1, treatments with anti-epileptic therapies were insufficient for controlling seizures. The two subjects reported herein demonstrate the heterogeneity in clinical presentation associated with pediatric DPD deficiency. Further studies are needed to determine how loss of DPD function can negatively impact neurodevelopment in some individuals, while others who carry the same genetic variants are unaffected by the pediatric condition.
Three years of RCVS and PRES cases at a single comprehensive stroke center: observations on clinical presentation, headache features, triggers, and management.推荐
PMID 43061594 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 11 02
作者：Ahmad SR, Challa SA, Suh L, Kaur S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Reversible cerebral vasoconstriction syndrome (RCVS) and posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) are rare neurovascular disorders requiring further investigation into their characteristics. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed and compared inpatient RCVS and PRES cases over a 3-year period, collecting data on demographics, medical history (including migraine), clinical presentation, diagnostics (urine drug screening and imaging), length of stay, time to diagnosis, suspected triggers (including substances), complications, and treatment. RESULTS: The sample consisted of 21 PRES and 9 RCVS cases (n = 30). Migraine (n = 5/9, 56%) and chronic pain (n = 4/9, 44%) were the predominant comorbidities in the RCVS group, whereas hypertension (n = 17/21, 81%) and chronic kidney disease (n = 8/21, 38%) were the predominant comorbidities in the PRES group. All individuals with RCVS presented with headache (n = 9/9, 100%), specifically thunderclap headache (n = 8/9, 89%), followed by headache that was different from their usual migraine, positional headache, and new migraine. PRES commonly presented with altered mental status (n = 15/21, 71%) and focal neurologic deficits (n = 10/21, 48%). Initial neurologic examination findings demonstrated a similar pattern, with altered mental status (n = 18/21, 86%) being common in PRES. Urine drug screening was completed for the majority of the RCVS group (n = 6/9, 67%) and half of the PRES group (n = 11/21, 52%). Of those tested in the RCVS group, n = 4/6 (67%) were positive for THC. The primary imaging modality was a CT angiogram (CTA) for RCVS (n = 9/9, 100%) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) for PRES (n = 18/21, 86%). Patients with PRES had a shorter median time to diagnosis (1 day [IQR 1-2] vs. 3 days [IQR 2-4] for RCVS) and a longer median length of stay (5 days [IQR 3-9] vs. 1 day [IQR 1-2] for RCVS). The most common physician-identified triggers were THC for RCVS (n = 7/9, 78%) and hypertension for PRES (n = 15/21, 71%). For PRES, the most common complications were seizures (n = 10/21, 48%) and respiratory failure (n = 4/21, 19%), while there were no complications in the RCVS group. The majority of patients with RCVS were treated with verapamil (n = 8/9, 89% of the RCVS group). For PRES, treatment with blood pressure control (n = 20/21, 95%) and antiseizure medication (n = 7/21, 33%) was most common. DISCUSSION: RCVS cases were more frequent in individuals with migraine presenting with thunderclap headache, with THC as the primary identified trigger. PRES cases were characterized by altered mental status, a hypertension trigger, faster diagnosis, longer lengths of stay, and more complications.
Pneumococcal meningitis complicated with thrombotic microangiopathy in a pediatric patient: a case report.推荐
PMID 43061531 · Frontiers in pediatrics · 2026 11 05
作者：Matsumura M, Tampo A, Okada M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Thrombotic microangiopathy (TMA) is characterized by three hallmark features: microangiopathic hemolytic anemia, thrombocytopenia, and organ injury caused by microcirculatory impairment. CASE PRESENTATION: The patient was a 3-year-7-month-old boy who visited a previous hospital complaining of fever and was observed for acute upper respiratory tract infection. However, due to persistence of high fever, the patient revisited the previous hospital three days later, was hospitalized, and treated with antibiotics for bacterial meningitis. After admission, thrombocytopenia and hemolytic anemia progressed over time, and the patient was transferred to our hospital on the fourth day of hospitalization. Subsequently, he developed status epilepticus and was admitted to the intensive care unit (ICU). Blood tests performed at the time of transfer revealed hemolytic anemia with schistocytes, thrombocytopenia, and renal dysfunction. In addition, Streptococcus pneumoniae was isolated in blood and cerebrospinal fluid cultures performed at the previous hospital, and treatment was initiated for suspected Streptococcus pneumoniae-associated hemolytic uremic syndrome (SpHUS). On day 5, hemolytic anemia progressed, and the patient received two units of red blood cells. Despite receiving medication, the patient experienced status epilepticus and required tracheal intubation and mechanical ventilation. From day 6, no further progression of hemolytic anemia occurred, and thrombocytopenia and renal dysfunction improved over the course of treatment. With a reduced sedative dosage, no recurrences of seizures were observed. On day 10, the patient was extubated and discharged from the ICU, and on day 45, he was discharged home. CONCLUSION: SpHUS has a different mechanism from other TMAs, and its treatment is unique, requiring consideration of the use of washed blood products and albumin preparations during blood transfusion and plasma exchange. Early recognition and treatment of SpHUS are crucial.
Marked Improvement in Epilepsy, Movement Disorder and Behaviour in a Child With CDKL5 Deficiency Disorder Treated With Ganaxolone and Fenfluramine: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43061504 · Cureus · 2026 11 12
作者：Neubauer BA, Spors H
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：CDKL5 deficiency disorder (CDD) is a severe developmental and epileptic encephalopathy characterised by early-onset, treatment-resistant seizures and severe neurodevelopmental impairment. Ganaxolone is approved for the treatment of seizures associated with CDD, while fenfluramine has shown potential antiseizure activity in a small series of patients with CDD. We report the case of a girl with CDD who experienced a marked and partially sustained clinical response to combination therapy with ganaxolone and fenfluramine. Ganaxolone reduced major motor seizure frequency by approximately 50-60%, with additional improvements in alertness, responsiveness, mood, communication and motor development. Following the addition of fenfluramine, motor seizures ceased for eight weeks before recurring at a lower frequency after a febrile illness. Overall, combination therapy was generally well tolerated, although dose escalation was limited by somnolence, which resolved promptly following dose reduction. Serial echocardiograms remained normal. Complete long-term seizure control and EEG normalisation were not achieved. This case suggests that combined ganaxolone and fenfluramine therapy may provide benefits beyond seizure reduction in CDD, including improvements in development, behaviour and overall quality of life, and warrants further investigation.
Vestibular Epilepsy Presenting With Isolated Recurrent Vertigo and Postictal Headache: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43061427 · Cureus · 2026 10 20
作者：Srie Setyaningrum CT, Sutarni S, Sejahtera DP, Yang N
链接：PubMed · DOI

发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Vestibular epilepsy, also referred to as epileptic vertigo syndrome, is a rare and underrecognized cause of recurrent episodic vertigo. Currently, there are no universally accepted diagnostic criteria for vestibular epilepsy. A 38-year-old woman presented with an eight-month history of recurrent spontaneous episodes of rotational vertigo lasting one to two minutes and occurring multiple times daily. Episodes were accompanied by nausea and vomiting and were followed by bilateral throbbing headaches. Neurological examination, bedside vestibular assessment, and cognitive screening were unremarkable. Brain magnetic resonance imaging demonstrated no structural epileptogenic lesion, while brainstem evoked response audiometry was normal. Electroencephalography revealed frequent generalized epileptiform discharges with a photoparoxysmal response during intermittent photic stimulation. The patient was diagnosed with vestibular epilepsy with postictal headache after exclusion of alternative causes of episodic vertigo. Treatment with extended-release divalproex sodium resulted in a marked reduction in attack frequency and improvement in symptoms during follow-up. Vestibular epilepsy represents an uncommon seizure manifestation in which vestibular symptoms predominate and may occur in isolation. In this patient, the brief stereotyped attacks, normal brain neuroimaging, absence of peripheral vestibular abnormalities, epileptiform electroencephalographic findings, and favorable response to antiseizure therapy supported the diagnosis. In cases of recurrent episodic vertigo presenting concurrently with headache, vestibular epilepsy remains a diagnostic challenge, mainly because overt neurological manifestations are often absent. Recent literature suggests that electroencephalography should be considered in patients with recurrent unexplained brief vertigo, particularly when episodes are stereotyped or resistant to conventional vestibular therapies.
Seizure-Onset Intracerebral Hemorrhage of Undetermined Etiology in a Child: Anesthetic, Intensive Care, and Rehabilitation Management.推荐
PMID 43061331 · Cureus · 2026 10 19
作者：Chahboun B, Majit AA, Kaba O, Bkiyar H
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Hemorrhagic stroke is rare in children but carries a substantial risk of mortality and long-term neurological disability. Its presentation may be nonspecific, with seizures and impaired consciousness potentially mimicking infectious or metabolic disorders. We report the case of an eight-year-old girl with no personal or family history of neurological disease and reportedly normal psychomotor development who presented with seizures, vomiting, and impaired consciousness. The initial computed tomography report described a left parietal intraparenchymal hematoma associated with intraventricular hemorrhage and mass effect. Emergency surgical evacuation was performed under general anesthesia, with intraoperative osmotherapy and invasive hemodynamic monitoring. The postoperative course was characterized by progressive recovery of consciousness, although right hemiplegia and aphasia persisted. Brain magnetic resonance imaging performed on postoperative day three showed an evolving intraparenchymal hematoma without appreciable contrast enhancement. Three-dimensional time-of-flight magnetic resonance angiography demonstrated preserved patency of the major intracranial arteries of the circle of Willis, without an apparent intracranial aneurysm or significant arterial stenosis. However, the available investigations did not establish the cause of the hemorrhage, and cerebral venous thrombosis or an occult vascular lesion could not be excluded. Multidisciplinary rehabilitation was initiated early. At six months, the patient was independent in the main activities of daily living and walked without an assistive device, with residual mild right-sided motor impairment, reduced right-hand dexterity, and mild language difficulties. This case illustrates the importance of rapid multidisciplinary management and continued rehabilitation after pediatric intracerebral hemorrhage, as well as the need for a complete etiological evaluation and appropriate vascular follow-up when the initial investigations are inconclusive.
Spikes in the night: rapid eye movement sleep maximally attenuates epileptiform spike morphology.推荐
PMID 43061224 · Clinical neurophysiology practice · 2026 11 04
作者：Toutant DB, McLeod GA, Morrison J, Ko JH et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To characterize morphological differences in interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) across sleep-wake states (rapid eye movement (REM), Non-REM1-3, wakefulness). METHODS: Prospectively analyzed 7379 IEDs from 17 adults with focal epilepsy (12 unifocal, 5 multifocal) admitted to an epilepsy monitoring unit for continuous surface video-EEG telemetry (41.8±17.2 years, 52.9% females) using custom-developed software allowing manual confirmation of IEDs and sleep-wake states. Nine morphological IED features reflecting paroxysmal depolarizing shift dynamics and magnitude were quantified across three EEG montages (raw, referential and Laplacian). Nonparametric Kruskal-Wallis and post hoc Dunn-Sidak tests with Bonferroni correction assessed state-dependent differences. Only features surviving correction across all montages were considered reproducibly significant. RESULTS: IED morphology varied significantly by state (p < 10-12-10-134). Across all montages and subgroups, REM IEDs had shallowest slopes (upslope 2.91-4.22 μV/s; downslope 2.87-4.33 μV/s), bluntest peaks (27.04°-40.24°), and shortest amplitudes and smallest area, (multifocal-driven). Awake IEDs had steepest slopes (upslope 8.97-15.23 μV/s; downslope 7.54-13.84 μV/s), and spikiest peaks (8.18°-14.44°). NREM IEDs were intermediate. CONCLUSIONS: Epileptiform morphology in REM was most attenuated. Shallower slopes, blunter peaks, shorter, and smaller-area IEDs indicate an electrobiological milieu less promoting of epileptic activity than NREM's synchronized state or wakefulness's maximally responsive state. Despite REM and wakefulness being desynchronized on surface EEG, REM's morphological IED divergence raises future directions into sui generis anti-epileptic mechanisms, including network connectivity and pH-responsive orexinergic glial dynamics. SIGNIFICANCE: The sleep-wake cycle strongly modulates epileptic activity, yet seizures rarely arise from REM (~1%). However, REM contains sufficient IEDs to characterize morphology and probe potential anti-epileptic solutions via their underlying cellular mechanisms.
Resection of Posterior Temporomesial Long-Term Epilepsy-Associated Tumors Through the Supracerebellar Transtentorial Approach: A Report of 2 Cases.推荐
PMID 43061210 · Neurosurgery practice · 2026 11 20
作者：Corbeil M, Slater A, Packirisamy S, Brunette-Clément T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND IMPORTANCE: Surgical resection of epileptogenic lesions involving the posterior temporomesial (PTM) region is challenging due to approach-related risks to functional temporal-occipital structures. Stereotactic ablation techniques, while excellent alternatives, are associated with incomplete ablation and lower efficacy in larger lesions. Success of the supracerebellar transtentorial (SCTT) approach, which avoids approach-related injury to overlying functional networks, relies on developing a good supracerebellar corridor and cerebellar bridging vein preservation, both typically reliant on cerebral relaxation through a semisitting position and favoring a paramedian rather than a more lateral hemispheric approach, respectively. We report 2 case reports to demonstrate that the SCTT approach (1) can be carried out in the park bench and prone positions and (2) is feasible even in the presence of a dominant developmental venous anomaly (DVA) involving a paravermian cerebellar bridging vein. CLINICAL PRESENTATION: Two pediatric patients with long-term epilepsy-associated tumors (LEATs) of the PTM region, including a right calcified posterior temporo-occipital and a left posterior fusiform gyrus LEAT adjacent to a dominant paravermian cerebellar bridging DVA, were resected using the SCTT approach in the lateral park bench position and prone position, respectively. Both cases have remained seizure-free (Engel Class IA) at 60 and 30 months of postoperative follow-up, respectively. CONCLUSION: The SCTT approach offers an excellent alternative to both standard open surgeries and stereotactic ablation techniques for children with LEATs in the PTM region. This approach can be used in both the lateral park bench and prone positions even in the presence of dominant paravermian cerebellar bridging DVA.
Seizure Prediction Using Channel-Optimized Intracranial EEG With Variable Interictal and Preictal Durations.推荐
PMID 43061054 · IEEE open journal of engineering in medicine and biology · 2026 06 08
作者：Wang X, Li M, Karkkainen T, Liu H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Objective: Predicting epileptic seizures using electroencephalogram (EEG) signals can provide a critical window for intervention to suppress impending seizures. However, the impact of varying interictal and preictal durations on seizure prediction remains unclear and warrants further exploration. Considering this, this study focuses on seizure prediction using intracranial EEG (iEEG) signals with variable interictal and preictal durations. Method: In this work, we discuss six pairwise combinations in total, involving two interictal durations (24 h and 48 h) and three preictal durations (30 min, 60 min and 90 min), during seizure prediction analysis. Furthermore, we also compare three classification strategies: the all-channel strategy (Strategy 1), the single-channel-based majority voting strategy (Strategy 2) and the channel-optimization-based majority voting strategy (Strategy 3), across each pairwise combination. Results: The long-term SWEC-ETHZ iEEG dataset is utilized to evaluate our method. The results reveal that our method achieves up to 87.7 sensitivity, 94.6 specificity, 92.8 accuracy, 0.912 Area Under the Curve (AUC) and 0.858 F1-score at the segment-based level, along with 95.9 sensitivity and 0.05/h false prediction rate (FPR) at the event-based level. Moreover, the overall performance of Strategy 3 surpasses that of Strategies 1 and 2 in each pairwise combination, indicating that channel optimization is beneficial for improving seizure prediction performance. Conclusion: This work shows high effectiveness in predicting seizures using iEEG signals, with the ability to forecast upcoming epileptic events up to 90 min in advance across both 24-h and 48-h interictal durations.
Wnt/β-catenin-mTOR crosstalk in TSC-associated epilepsy: pharmacological evidence and therapeutic opportunities.推荐
PMID 43060841 · Frontiers in pharmacology · 2026 11 05
作者：Alqurashi RS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜PHARMACOLOGY & PHARMACY：Q1
摘要：Tuberous sclerosis complex (TSC), caused by pathogenic variants in TSC1 or TSC2, is frequently associated with early-onset and drug-resistant epilepsy. Loss of hamartin-tuberin activity causes Rheb-dependent mTORC1 hyperactivation and provides the mechanistic basis for mTOR-directed treatment with everolimus, but mTORC1 activation alone does not fully account for variation in seizure burden or treatment response. Evidence from biochemical and disease-model studies indicates that TSC1/TSC2 also interacts with canonical Wnt/β-catenin signaling through GSK3β and Axin, while cellular metabolism may provide an additional point of convergence through AMPK-dependent regulation of TSC2. This narrative review examines the biochemical, neuronal, human-tissue, pharmacological, and clinical evidence for this Wnt-GSK3β-TSC2-mTOR framework and distinguishes established findings from context-limited, emerging, and hypothetical relationships. Everolimus remains the established mechanism-based therapy for TSC-associated refractory seizures, whereas metformin has the strongest TSC-specific clinical signal among the investigational metabolic approaches discussed. GLP-1 receptor agonists and direct GSK3β modulation remain preclinical or mechanistically speculative in this setting. Gene- and RNA-based approaches provide emerging precedents for pathway-selective intervention, but none has directly targeted the Wnt-GSK3β-TSC2 interface. The principal unresolved question is whether this signaling relationship operates in human TSC epileptogenic tissue. Direct tissue-level validation, followed by selective pathway interrogation and adequately powered therapeutic studies, is needed before Wnt-directed strategies can be considered clinically relevant in TSC-associated epilepsy.
Epilepsy Lateralization by Elastic Net Regularization of a Diffusion Tensor Imaging Biomarker using Machine Learning.推荐
PMID 43060756 · medRxiv : the preprint server for health sciences · 2026 11 04
作者：Lepping RJ, Pearl M, Chen X, Hu J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：不适用（预印本）
JCR分区：不适用（预印本）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Accurate lateralization of the epileptogenic focus is essential for presurgical evaluation of patients with drug-resistant epilepsy. This study examined whether diffusion tensor imaging (DTI) measures of white matter microstructure, combined with clinical and demographic variables, could classify epilepsy lateralization using machine learning. METHODS: We present a retrospective analysis of 334 adult patients undergoing presurgical epilepsy evaluation at the University of Kansas Health System. Fractional anisotropy (FA) and mean diffusivity (MD) were extracted from 36 bilateral white matter tracts and combined with clinical and demographic variables. Several supervised machine learning approaches were evaluated, with elastic net logistic regression selected for detailed analysis. Models classified right-sided, left-sided, and bilateral or generalized epilepsy. Performance was evaluated with area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), accuracy, sensitivity, and specificity as performance metrics. RESULTS: Elastic net models demonstrated good discrimination across classification tasks, with AUC values ranging from 0.76 to 0.88. The strongest performance distinguished left-sided epilepsy from bilateral or generalized epilepsy (AUC = 0.88; accuracy = 0.90; sensitivity = 0.90; specificity = 0.89). Discrimination between unilateral epilepsy and bilateral or generalized epilepsy was also good (AUC = 0.83). Classification of right-sided versus left-sided epilepsy demonstrated lower performance (AUC = 0.76). SIGNIFICANCE: DTI-derived white matter features analyzed using a machine learning approach may provide complementary information for epilepsy lateralization during presurgical evaluation. Further studies incorporating other pre-surgical evaluation data into the machine learning model are needed to establish the clinical utility of this approach. KEY POINTS: A machine learning model was tested using diffusion tensor imaging and clinical data to retrospectively classify epilepsy lateralization.Our model balanced sensitivity and specificity in epilepsy localization, suggesting potential as an interpretable surgical decision tool.Findings support the hypothesis that white matter tracts contain useful information about seizure propagation to distal brain regions.Further model validation is needed before this can be used as a clinical tool.
Electric and Magnetic Source Imaging in Epilepsy: Anatomical Insights from Realistic Simulations.推荐
PMID 43060707 · medRxiv : the preprint server for health sciences · 2026 11 06
作者：Sdoukopoulou G, Jahromi S, Corona L, Partamian H et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：不适用（预印本）
JCR分区：不适用（预印本）
摘要：UNLABELLED: Neurosurgery is an effective treatment for drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE), but its success depends on the accurate delineation of the epileptogenic focus, approximated noninvasively by electric and magnetic source imaging (ESI/MSI) of high-density electroencephalography (HD-EEG) and magnetoencephalography (MEG). These modalities have distinct sensitivity profiles; consequently, epileptiform activity detected by one modality may remain undetected by the other. However, the implications of these differences for spike detectability and localization accuracy, and the added value of combined electromagnetic source imaging (EMSI), remain insufficiently characterized under clinically realistic conditions. Here, we characterize HD-EEG and MEG sensitivity profiles and compare ESI, MSI, and EMSI, using realistic simulations with known ground truth. We generated HD-EEG and MEG datasets from cortical, insular, and hippocampal sources across multiple orientations, activated using patient-derived intracranial EEG spike waveforms. Spike sensitivity and localization accuracy were evaluated using dipole scanning, dynamic statistical parametric mapping (dSPM), and linearly constrained minimum variance (LCMV). EEG demonstrated higher spike detection rates and sensitivity for radially cortical sources at gyral crowns and sulcal bottoms ( p <0.001), while MEG detected more spikes from tangential cortical sources along sulcal walls ( p <0.001). MSI localized tangential sources more accurately than ESI across all methods ( p <0.001), while dipole scanning outperformed dSPM and LCMV for cortical and insular sources ( p <0.001). EMSI outperformed ESI ( p <0.001) but did not improve localization over MSI. These findings suggest that complementary anatomical sensitivities of HD-EEG and MEG may improve epileptogenic source characterization, supporting simultaneous acquisition during the presurgical evaluation of patients with DRE. KEY POINTS: EEG and MEG exhibit complementary sensitivity profiles, with EEG detecting more spikes from gyral crowns and sulcal bottoms and MEG from sulcal walls.MSI provides more focal and precise source localization than ESI, particularly for tangentially oriented sources.Dipole-based inverse method achieves higher localization accuracy than experimental dSPM and LCMV.
Quantitative deep motor phenotyping of 102 people with SCN2A, KCNQ2, CSNK2A1, CACNA1A or SCN8A -related disorders.浏览
PMID 43060692 · medRxiv : the preprint server for health sciences · 2026 11 03
作者：Bajaj S, Brünger T, Fauteux M, Fracisco R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：不适用（预印本）
JCR分区：不适用（预印本）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Motor dysfunction is a major contributor to disability in pediatric-onset monogenic neurodevelopmental disorders (NDDs), yet systematic quantifications of motor phenotypes across development using validated clinical scales remain underdeveloped. Existing studies have classified motor phenotypes rather than quantified domain-specific severity, limiting their use as outcome measures in clinical trials. Here, we systematically quantify motor dysfunction using established clinical rating scales to explore their utility as motor endpoints. METHODS: In this cross-sectional observational study, 102 individuals with NDDs (0.5-38 years) secondary to pathogenic or likely pathogenic variants in five genes ( SCN2A, KCNQ2, CSNK2A1, CACNA1A, SCN8A ) and 31 unaffected sibling controls underwent standardized motor assessments using the Scale for Assessment and Rating of Ataxia, Burke-Fahn-Marsden Dystonia Scale, and the Modified Ashworth Scale. Regional and functional categorization of parameters enabled differentiated interpretation. Gene-specific patterns were evaluated using z-score standardization across the pooled affected cohort, with gene-level averaging and nonparametric bootstrap-derived 95% confidence intervals. RESULTS: Dysfunction was present in individuals with NDDs across speech (90%), coordination (87%), dystonia (83.3%), gross motor (82.4%) and spasticity (42.2%), with minimal impairment in controls (0-12.9%). We observed substantial heterogeneity across motor domains and subdomains; ataxic dysarthria had the highest score distribution (median 0.83 [IQR, 0.17-1.0]), whereas spasticity showed near-floor scores. Individuals with SCN2A variants demonstrated higher mean z-scores across multiple subdomains than other genes, with more consistent deviations observed in coordination and speech domains (Coordination: mean z = 0.29, 95% CI 0.03-0.54; Speech: mean z = 0.3, 95% CI 0.03-0.57), whereas domain-specific or less pronounced patterns were observed in other genes. The ataxia and dystonia scales demonstrated low floor and ceiling effects. DISCUSSION: Using three clinical scales across a broad developmental and functional range, our study moves motor phenotyping in monogenic NDDs from categorical description toward quantitative, domain-resolved severity assessment. Exploratory analysis indicates marked heterogeneity in severity profiles across genes, most notably in SCN2A , suggesting potential selective motor vulnerability. These findings provide evidence for the feasibility of motor phenotyping and provide preliminary, cross-sectional benchmark severity distributions. Subsequent longitudinal validation and validation in larger, longitudinal, and variant-stratified cohorts will enable natural history studies and future therapeutic trials alongside seizure outcomes.
Early Increases in Hippocampal Synchrony Predict Chronic Epileptiform Burden in a Temporal Lobe Epilepsy Model.推荐
PMID 43060564 · bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology · 2026 11 05
作者：Gasmi N, Tobin H, Ahmadpour D, Tahmasebi G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：不适用（预印本）
JCR分区：不适用（预印本）
摘要：Temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE), the most common focal epilepsy, is often drug-resistant and arises predominantly in the hippocampus. Disease severity is conventionally assessed by seizure frequency and duration, yet epileptiform activity outside discrete seizures may also track disease progression. Whether this activity reflects hippocampal network reorganization as part of epileptogenesis, and whether such reorganization predicts chronic severity, is unknown. Here, we combined longitudinal two-photon calcium imaging of hippocampal CA1 neurons with repeated 24-hour EEG recordings that quantified epileptiform burden in the same pilocarpine-treated mice over 16 weeks. CA1 firing and synchrony rose acutely, subsided during a latent period, and increased again chronically, with synchronized firing organized into brief waves that propagated in a consistent direction. Epileptiform burden followed a similar temporal trajectory and increasingly contained electrographic seizures. CA1 synchrony correlated with epileptiform burden and stratified mice into groups with distinct neuronal and electrographic outcomes. CA1 synchrony measured within three weeks of pilocarpine induction predicted chronic epileptiform burden, identifying early hippocampal synchrony as a candidate imaging biomarker and pointing to an early window for intervention in TLE.
iDEND syndrome caused by KCNJ11 mutation unresponsive to sulfonylurea therapy: two case reports and literature review.推荐
PMID 43060535 · Frontiers in pediatrics · 2026 11 05
作者：Xue K, Li X, Zhang T, Chen C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Neonatal diabetes mellitus (NDM) is a rare monogenic form of diabetes that predominantly manifests within the first six months of life. This report presents two patients with mutations in the potassium inwardly rectifying channel subfamily J member 11 (KCNJ11) gene, accompanied by a narrative literature review. Both infants exhibited severe diabetic ketoacidosis (DKA) and growth retardation, without seizures. Following ketoacidosis resolution, Patient 1 required continuous subcutaneous insulin infusion (CSII) with continuous glucose monitoring (CGM), while Patient 2 received hospital-based point-of-care testing-guided multiple daily insulin injections supplemented with glibenclamide. However, both cases exhibited persistent non-responsiveness to sulfonylurea therapy. Genetic testing revealed a heterozygous KCNJ11 c.497G > A (p.Cys166Tyr) variant in both infants. Based on their clinical manifestations, both patients were diagnosed with iDEND. This paper presents a comprehensive account of the clinical features, varied insulin delivery methods, and glucose monitoring strategies in two patients with iDEND harboring the KCNJ11 c.497G > A variant. This finding expands the known clinical spectrum associated with this specific variant and provides critical evidence-based guidance for the therapeutic management.
A DNM1L GTPase Effector Domain Deletion Causes Intermediate Defects in Peroxisomal and Mitochondrial Dynamics and Reveals a Targetable Metabolic Vulnerability.浏览
PMID 43060450 · bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology · 2026 11 04
作者：Srivastav S, Jangam SV, Andrews JC, Theodorou E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：不适用（预印本）
JCR分区：不适用（预印本）
摘要：DNM1L encodes dynamin-related protein 1 (DRP1), a key GTPase regulating mitochondrial and peroxisomal dynamics. Pathogenic DNM1L variants are associated with a broad spectrum of neurodevelopmental and neurodegenerative disorders, with phenotypic severity varying by affected protein domain. While variants in the GTPase domain are typically associated with milder phenotypes and middle-domain variants with severe developmental and epileptic encephalopathy, the functional impact of GTPase effector domain (GED) variants remains less defined. Here, we describe four individuals harboring a recurrent heterozygous GED deletion (L700del) presenting with movement disorders and lacking severe infantile encephalopathy, consistent with an intermediate clinical phenotype. Patient-derived fibroblasts exhibited tubular peroxisomes and enlarged mitochondria, while Drosophila models demonstrated that DNM1L L700del has intermediate functional effects that were more pronounced than those of a GTPase-domain variant A192E but less severe than those of the middle-domain variant G350R. Comparison with another GED variant, Y691C, further demonstrated variant-specific differences in severity within the GED. Metabolic stress assays showed that L700del fibroblasts exhibited impaired growth under galactose conditions, consistent with increased vulnerability under conditions dependent on mitochondrial oxidative metabolism. Bezafibrate, a PPAR agonist that promotes mitochondrial biogenesis, partially rescued this growth defect. Together, these findings support a functional allelic spectrum in DNM1L -associated disease in which protein domain contributes to, but does not completely determine, functional severity. L700del represents an intermediate-strength allele associated with a movement-disorder phenotype and a pharmacologically modifiable metabolic vulnerability.
Allele-agnostic KCNH1 suppression rescues gain-of-function developmental epileptic encephalopathy.推荐
PMID 43059702 · bioRxiv : the preprint server for biology · 2026 11 09
作者：Chi W, Corbett EE, Thompson CH, Simkin D et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：不适用（预印本）
JCR分区：不适用（预印本）
摘要：Gain-of-function variants in KCNH1, which encodes the voltage-gated potassium channel Kv10.1, cause developmental and epileptic encephalopathies (DEEs) characterized by seizures, developmental delay and intellectual disability. While pathogenic variants enhance channel activity, loss of KCNH1 is tolerated in humans and mice, suggesting that allele-agnostic gene suppression may represent a viable therapeutic strategy. Here, we developed a gapmer antisense oligonucleotide (ASO) targeting KCNH1 and evaluated its efficacy in human induced pluripotent stem cell (iPSC)-derived neurons and a knock-in mouse model of KCNH1-related disorder. Neurons carrying the pathogenic KCNH1 variants I494V or G375R exhibited a shared phenotype characterized by hyperpolarized resting membrane potential and abnormalities in neuronal network activity despite largely preserved intrinsic firing properties. ASOs efficiently reduced KCNH1 expression without disrupting network activity in control neurons. In contrast, ASO treatment normalized network dysfunction across both mutant genotypes, demonstrating that distinct KCNH1 gain-of-function variants converge on a reversible disease state. To assess the efficacy of ASOs in vivo, we generated a new Kcnh1-R357Q knock-in mouse model that exhibited profound seizures and early mortality. A single ASO treatment reduced seizure burden significantly and restored survival to near-normal levels. Together, these findings establish KCNH1 suppression as a precision therapeutic strategy for KCNH1-related DEEs and provide a framework for treating gain-of-function epilepsies in which partial gene reduction is clinically tolerated.

Mesenchymal stromal cells for periventricular hemorrhagic infarction in extremely preterm infants: a double-blinded randomized controlled phase IIa clinical trial.推荐
PMID 43058895 · Stem cell research & therapy · 2026 08 24
作者：Ahn SY, Yoo K, Kim YJ, Lim CY et al.

摘要：BACKGROUND: Periventricular hemorrhagic infarction in extremely preterm infants (PVHI; the most severe form of intraventricular hemorrhage) are associated with a high risk of mortality and neurodevelopmental disability with no effective treatments. We aimed to assess the safety and potential efficacy of mesenchymal stromal cells (MSCs) for PVHI in preterm infants. METHODS: We conducted a single-center, phase 2a double-blind randomized controlled trial at the Samsung Medical Center. Twenty-two preterm infants (23-31 weeks gestation) with PVHI were double-blindly randomized (1:1) to receive intraventricular MSCs (1 × 107 cells/kg) or normal saline within 7 days post-diagnosis. Primary outcome was combined rate of ventriculo-peritoneal shunt or death. Secondary outcomes included safety, mortality, cerebral palsy (CP), epilepsy, developmental delays. RESULTS: The primary endpoint of shunt or death by 36 weeks' postmenstrual age did not differ between groups (relative risk 1.14, 95% CI 0.64 to 2.03). Among survivors assessed through 2 years' corrected age, the MSC group had lower observed rates of cerebral palsy, epilepsy, and microcephaly than the placebo group, although confidence intervals were wide; lower incidence of CP (relative risk 0.56, 95% CI 0.27 to 1.17), epilepsy (relative risk 0.38, 95% CI 0.10 to 1.36) and microcephaly (relative risk 0.30, 95% CI 0.08 to 1.07). No serious treatment-related adverse events occurred. CONCLUSIONS: Intraventricular MSC therapy did not reduce the mortality or VP shunting dependence in premature infants with PVHI. However, 2-year exploratory long-term neurodevelopmental outcomes suggested potential benefits, which warranting confirmation in larger trials. Trial registration The study protocol was registered at Clinical Trials.gov (NCT02890953) on September 6, 2016.
Brain somatic chromosome 1q gain causes focal cortical malformations and epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43058778 · Genome medicine · 2026 11 16
作者：De Meulemeester AS, Scopin M, Sami L, Doladilhe M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Brain somatic mutations are a major cause of epileptogenic focal cortical malformations. Hyaline astrocytic inclusions represent a rare neuropathological finding in epilepsy. METHODS: We analyzed eight patients undergoing surgery for drug-resistant focal epilepsy associated with astrocytic inclusions, combining bulk and single-nucleus genomic analyses, cell-type-specific genetic profiling, neuropathological characterization, and electrophysiological recordings to establish genotype-neuropathology correlations. RESULTS: Microelectrode array recordings from cortical slices from one patient revealed spontaneous abnormal network activity in regions without astrocytic inclusions. Multilayered genomic analysis of surgical brain tissue, including single-nucleus sequencing, identified mosaic chromosome 1q copy-number gains in all eight cases, with chr1q tetrasomies confirmed in six. In four cases, the supernumerary chromosome 1q was of maternal origin, and long-read haplotyping in a patient-parent trio supported a novel mechanism of meiosis II chromosome missegregation. Chr1q gains were enriched in inclusion-bearing astrocytes, linking chromosomal aneuploidy to neuropathology. Finally, we developed a targeted sequencing panel enabling the detection of chr1q gains in surgical brain samples, including samples obtained from presurgical stereo-EEG electrodes. CONCLUSIONS: Together, these findings support postzygotic rescue of a maternal meiotic error in non-neural lineages as a mechanism of pathogenic brain mosaicism and define mosaic chr1q gain as a distinct cause of epilepsy.
Clinical characteristics and inflammatory-endothelial biomarkers associated with posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome in eclampsia.推荐
PMID 43058593 · BMC pregnancy and childbirth · 2026 08 24
作者：Kandilcik M, Arslan M, Öksüz H, Çalışır F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) is an acute neurological syndrome characterized by seizures, headache, visual disturbances, and altered consciousness, and is frequently associated with vasogenic cerebral edema. Eclampsia is one of the most common obstetric conditions associated with PRES due to severe hypertension, endothelial dysfunction, and systemic inflammation. However, data regarding easily accessible inflammatory and endothelial dysfunction biomarkers associated with PRES in patients with eclampsia remain limited. The aim of this study was to evaluate the clinical characteristics, laboratory parameters, and inflammatory indices associated with PRES in patients diagnosed with eclampsia. METHODS: This retrospective observational study included a total of 62 patients diagnosed with eclampsia and followed in the intensive care unit (ICU) between January 2015 and December 2024. Patients were divided into two groups according to the presence or absence of PRES based on neurological findings and brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) results. Demographic, obstetric, clinical, and laboratory data were obtained from electronic medical records. Inflammatory biomarkers, including C-reactive protein (CRP), albumin, magnesium, C-reactive protein/albumin ratio (CAR), CRP/lymphocyte ratio (CRP/L), neutrophil/lymphocyte ratio (NLR), systemic immune-inflammation index (SII), and systemic inflammation response index (SIRI), were analyzed. Receiver operating characteristic (ROC) analysis and multivariable logistic regression analysis were performed. RESULTS: PRES was identified in 25 patients (40.3%). Parity and gravidity were significantly lower in patients with PRES. CRP, CAR, and CRP/L levels were significantly higher in the PRES group, whereas albumin and serum magnesium levels were significantly lower. Parieto-occipital involvement was the most common MRI finding. In ROC analysis, CAR (AUC: 0.852), CRP (AUC: 0.847), and serum magnesium (AUC: 0.808) demonstrated good discriminative ability between patients with and without PRES. In the parsimonious multivariable logistic regression analysis, higher CAR levels and lower serum magnesium levels and parity remained independently associated with the presence of PRES. CONCLUSIONS: The presence of PRES in patients with eclampsia was associated with lower parity, increased inflammatory burden, and markers of endothelial dysfunction. These findings add to the limited evidence regarding inflammation-related biomarkers in obstetric PRES and suggest that inflammation-related biomarkers may provide complementary information when interpreted alongside established clinical and radiological findings. Further prospective multicenter studies are warranted to confirm these exploratory findings.
Predicting post-surgical seizure outcomes in drug-resistant temporal lobe epilepsy using hippocampal radiomics from 18F-FDG hybrid PET/MRI.推荐
PMID 43058531 · BMC medical imaging · 2026 08 26
作者：Hu J, Yao C, Wang J, Zhang S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To develop multivariate hippocampal radiomics models using multimodal 18F-FDG hybrid PET/MRI and clinical variables to predict post-surgical seizure outcomes in patients with drug-resistant temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE). METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed 117 drug-resistant TLE patients from Xuanwu Hospital (August 2015-March 2023) who underwent preoperative 18F-FDG hybrid PET/MRI, surgical intervention, and one-year postoperative follow-up using Engel classification. Structural (3D-T1 and T2-FLAIR) and metabolic (18F-FDG PET) images were obtained. Hippocampal subfields were automatically segmented using FreeSurfer on 3D T1 images, with 18F-FDG PET and T2-FLAIR images registered accordingly. Radiomics features were extracted from the hippocampal subfields. Feature selection was performed using the minimum redundancy maximum relevance algorithm and the least absolute shrinkage and selection operator regression methods to calculate radscores. Radiomics-clinical and clinical models was constructed and model performance was evaluated using receiver operating characteristic analysis and decision curve analysis. Patients were divided into hippocampal sclerosis (HS) and non-HS groups based on histology and mediation analysis explored relationships among radscore, HS, and Engel classification. RESULTS: The radiomics model combining all three imaging sequences outperformed models using single or dual modalities. In the training set, the combined 18F-FDG PET/MRI radiomics-clinical model demonstrated higher discrimination than the clinical model alone (AUC = 0.915 vs. 0.716, p = 0.004). In the test set, the combined model showed a numerically higher AUC (0.819 vs. 0.743); however, this difference did not reach statistical significance (p = 0.661). Decision curve analysis shown that the combined 18F-FDG PET/MRI radiomics-clinical model provides higher clinical benefits in all risk threshold compared with the clinical model. The radscore was significantly higher in the HS group than in the non-HS group (t = 2.308, p = 0.023). Mediation analysis shows that radscore is a significant mediator between HS and Engel classification (proportion of indirect effect = 45.8%, p = 0.028). CONCLUSION: The combined 18F-FDG PET/MRI radiomics-clinical model enhances the prediction of post-surgical seizure outcomes in drug-resistant TLE patients. Radiomics features may reflect the pathological state of HS, aiding in preoperative prognostication and clinical decision-making.
KCNQ2 p.(Arg214Trp): systematic review with retrospective analysis and expanding the phenotype.推荐
PMID 43058523 · BMC neurology · 2026 08 26
作者：Christova P, Ostrozovicova M, Neupauerova J, Skorvanek M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Heterozygous pathogenic variants in the KCNQ2 gene underlie a broad phenotypic spectrum ranging from self-limited (familial) neonatal epilepsy (SeLNE) to developmental and epileptic encephalopathy or isolated intellectual disability. The KCNQ2 gene encodes subunits of a voltage-gated potassium channel involved in neuronal excitability, and its mutations are known to have both loss-of-function (LoF) and gain-of-function (GoF) effects, resulting in distinct neurological syndromes. The recurrent missense LoF variant KCNQ2 p.(Arg214Trp) has been previously reported in a single family with a presumed SeLNE phenotype, without detailed adult follow-up and with additional database-reported evidence suggesting a broader phenotype associated with this recurrent variant. METHODS: Here, we report a family with two adult carriers of the heterozygous KCNQ2 p.(Arg214Trp) variant identified through whole-exome sequencing, including long-term follow-up into late adulthood. In addition, a literature review of previously reported cases carrying the same recurrent variant was performed. RESULTS: Both individuals presented with early-onset focal epilepsy with a relapsing course, characterized by prolonged seizure-free periods followed by recurrence in adulthood. The proband demonstrated transient motor regression, developmental delay, moderate intellectual disability, ataxia and fine motor impairment. The father exhibited milder cognitive impairment and additional comorbidities in later life. CONCLUSION: These findings challenge the concept of KCNQ2 p.(Arg214Trp) as a self-limited epilepsy-associated variant and indicate a broader phenotypic spectrum, with persistence of epilepsy and associated neurological and non-neurological comorbidities into adulthood. Our report provides new insights into adult outcomes, aiding in genetic counseling and the long-term management of affected individuals. More extensive studies with larger cohorts carrying this variant are necessary to better define the full phenotypic spectrum and to distinguish comorbidities associated with different etiological factors, including perinatal factors.
Life-threatening upper gastrointestinal bleeding associated with aspirin and Salvia miltiorrhiza combination therapy presenting as seizures: a case report.推荐
PMID 43058486 · BMC gastroenterology · 2026 08 24
作者：Liu YQ, Huang PD, Zheng CB, Zhong ZY et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Gastrointestinal bleeding is a prevalent medical emergency, often presenting with symptoms such as hematemesis, melena, and dizziness. While the link between antiplatelet therapy and gastrointestinal bleeding complications is well established, atypical initial manifestations-such as seizure-like episodes in stroke patients-are exceedingly rare. This report details a case of massive gastrointestinal bleeding associated with aspirin-Salvia miltiorrhiza (Danshen) combination therapy, which initially presented with jet-like vomiting, seizures, and loss of consciousness. CASE PRESENTATION: A 68-year-old male with a history of hypertension and cerebral infarction was admitted for abdominal distension. Initial imaging did not reveal any new ischemic lesions. During hospitalization, he experienced sudden jet-like vomiting, seizures, and loss of consciousness. Despite stable blood pressure and a negative occult blood test for vomitus, laboratory results indicated a significant drop in hemoglobin levels. Emergency endoscopy revealed a large ulcer with an adherent blood clot in the duodenal bulb, leading to a diagnosis of upper gastrointestinal bleeding. Due to the risks associated with active massive hemorrhage and potential perforation from electrical injury during endoscopic clot removal-which could complicate wound management-conservative medical management was implemented. The patient recovered without recurrent bleeding, demonstrated improved hemoglobin levels, and was successfully discharged. CONCLUSIONS: This case illustrates that gastrointestinal bleeding can present atypically with neurological symptoms such as seizures and altered consciousness, which may contribute to misdiagnosis. A heightened level of suspicion is essential in patients on antithrombotic therapy, even in the absence of conventional bleeding indicators. Furthermore, this case emphasizes the importance of drug safety awareness regarding aspirin combination therapy and the necessity for clinicians to regularly assess patients and implement measures to mitigate the risk of drug-induced gastrointestinal bleeding.
Tissue and Cerebral Biofluid Remodelling of tRNA-Derived Small RNA Signatures in Experimental and Human Epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43058392 · Cellular and molecular neurobiology · 2026 08 27
作者：Zaheer S, Venø MT, Siebenbrodt K, Soukupova M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Small noncoding RNAs are increasingly recognized as important regulators of gene expression in brain diseases including epilepsy. tRNA-derived small RNAs (tsRNAs) including tRNA fragments (tRFs) and tRNA halves (tiRNAs) are emerging as stable and regulated RNA species which respond to cellular stress and accumulate in disease states. Using experimental temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE) models and human TLE samples, we here determine whether tsRNAs are localized to specific tissue and biofluid compartments in the brain and how they are altered in epilepsy. We analysed small RNA sequencing datasets from hippocampal tissue, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF), and hippocampal interstitial fluid over time from rat TLE models, and human CSF from control subjects and TLE patients. Small RNA class composition, tsRNA subclass distributions, parent tRNA origins, and differential tsRNA expression profiles were systematically compared across intracellular and extracellular compartments, experimental epilepsy models, and human TLE samples. All differential expression analyses applied Benjamini-Hochberg correction. This revealed that small RNA composition varied strongly by compartment. Hippocampal tissue was dominated by microRNAs, while CSF and interstitial fluid contained significant proportions of tRNA-derived reads. In chronic epilepsy, hippocampal tissue showed a significant increase in overall tsRNA content and in the proportion of tRNA-derived reads, without a significant shift in tsRNA class composition. The CSF and interstitial fluid of epileptic animals showed fragment-level remodelling of tsRNA profiles, although compositional shifts did not reach significance after correction. The CSF from TLE patients also showed extracellular tsRNA populations with potential disease-associated shifts in small RNA composition. Fragments derived from parent tRNAs GlyGCC and GluCTC were recurrent contributors across compartments, though their prominence was compartment-dependent. Collectively, these findings suggest that tsRNAs represent a prominent, compartment-specific, and disease-associated layer of small RNA remodelling in experimental and human epilepsy. The findings may guide the choice of biofluid source and panel of RNAs for biomarker-based diagnostics or provide targets for new therapeutic approaches in drug-resistant epilepsies.
E-Cigarette Directories: A Qualitative Study of Perspectives From State Tobacco Control Policy Implementers.浏览
PMID 43055539 · Public health reports (Washington, D.C. : 1974) · 2026 11 19
作者：Chadwick G, Lee SC, Kahl HM, Stover M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: Bills introducing electronic cigarette (e-cigarette) directories have been filed in most US states in recent years and are quickly being adopted. This study captures the perspectives of state agency staff on these emerging, controversial tobacco control laws. METHODS: Semi-structured interviews examined the implementation of 5 e-cigarette policies: taxation, licensure, flavor restrictions, minimum legal sales age, and clean indoor air. When data on e-cigarette directories emerged during interviews, key informants (KIs) were asked follow-up questions on their perceptions about the benefits and challenges associated with directories. Data were analyzed through an iterative, team-based inductive approach. A 50-state legislative scan of tobacco (cigarettes and roll-your-own tobacco products) and e-cigarette directories was also conducted. RESULTS: Of 74 interviews conducted during April 2023-July 2024, directories were discussed in 12 (14 KIs, 10 states). The legislative scan (conducted from August through December 2025) identified 16 states with e-cigarette directories and 46 states with tobacco directories. KIs described e-cigarette directories as emerging tools to improve product tracking, compliance, and enforcement through fines, product seizure, and license suspension. Early implementers reported variable listing criteria, fees, and oversight. Limited staff, funding, and access to US Food and Drug Administration (FDA) data hindered enforcement. KIs expressed concerns about industry support for directories and the possibility that e-cigarette directories could advantage companies with FDA-authorized products while marginalizing smaller competitors. CONCLUSIONS: State regulators view e-cigarette directories as a tool to curb an unfettered market, although not without disadvantages. Inclusion criteria for products are key to aligning the policies with public health goals.
Optimising Tuberculosis Treatment in Low- and Middle-Income Countries: A Narrative Review of the Role, Challenges, and Innovations in Therapeutic Drug Monitoring.浏览
PMID 43057609 · International journal of infectious diseases : IJID : official publication of the International Society for Infectious Diseases · 2026 11 19
作者：Nabisere-Arinaitwe R, Kengo A, Sharma S, Riezk A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Therapeutic drug monitoring (TDM) may improve anti-tuberculosis (TB) therapy by optimising dosing based on patient characteristics. This is especially relevant in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) where TB treatment is largely standardized despite frequent treatment failure and drug-related toxicity. METHODS: This narrative review summarizes current evidence on the role, barriers, and future directions of TDM for TB treatment in LMICs, including emerging point-of-care and artificial intelligence (AI)-supported technologies. RESULTS: TDM may improve treatment outcomes for key TB drugs like rifampicin, isoniazid, linezolid, and aminoglycosides. However, its implementation in LMICs is hindered by limited laboratory capacity, high assay costs, and lack of standardized protocols. Emerging approaches like dried blood spot sampling, electrochemical biosensors, wearable monitoring devices, and AI-guided dosing models may facilitate implementation and integration of TDM into routine TB care. Most of this evidence is drawn from observational cohorts, non-TB disease areas (e.g., sepsis, HIV, epilepsy), or early-stage device studies rather than TB-specific randomised or implementation trials. CONCLUSION: TDM has strong biological rationale and promising observational evidence for improving TB treatment outcomes in LMICs, but this remains to be confirmed by TB-specific clinical and cost-effectiveness studies. Realising its potential will require affordable technologies, robust implementation frameworks, and enabling policy support, with validation of low-cost, point-of-care tools essential for scaling deployment in resource-limited settings.
Clinical Course of Depressed Skull Fractures Managed Without Elevation During Armed Conflict: A Retrospective Series From Gaza.浏览

JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3；SURGERY：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To describe the recorded clinical course of depressed skull fractures treated without elevation during wartime disruption of neurosurgical services in Gaza. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed a two-hospital series of 169 CT-confirmed depressed skull fractures managed without initial elevation (December 2024-June 2026). Fracture status was coded from clinical descriptions, including an assumption of closed injury when no overlying wound was recorded. We reassessed the originally specified infection-related intravenous antibiotic escalation criterion and the narrower criterion introduced during revision. RESULTS: Median age was 15 years (135 recorded). Admission Glasgow Coma Scale was 13-15 in 122/128 (95%), 9-12 in four (3.1%), and 3-8 in two (1.6%); 87/125 injuries (70%) were blast-associated. Two wound events received escalation of intravenous antibiotics, corresponding to 2/169 failures (1.2%; 95% Wilson CI, 0.3%-4.2%) under the originally specified endpoint. No patient required operative wound debridement (0/169; 95% Wilson CI, 0.0%-2.2%) under the narrower post hoc definition. A transient cerebrospinal fluid leak and a post-admission seizure were separately documented. Telephone contact was reported for all patients, but patient-linked contact dates and a minimum follow-up interval could not be recovered. CONCLUSIONS: The clinical record describes few treatment events, but the originally defined endpoint was inconsistently counted at first submission. Incomplete antimicrobial records, operational fracture classification, nonstandardized follow-up, and absence of a surgical comparator preclude conclusions about comparative safety.
A microcircuit model of astrocytic potassium buffering and neural synchronization.推荐
PMID 43057562 · Brain research · 2026 11 19
作者：Cafiso M, Casagrande G, Angiolleli M, Paradisi P et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Neural synchronization is fundamental to brain function and, when it becomes excessive, underlies pathological conditions such as epilepsy. Among brain regions, the temporal lobes, and the hippocampus in particular, exhibit the highest epileptogenic potential, with mesial temporal lobe epilepsy representing the most prevalent form of the condition in humans. Within the hippocampus, extracellular potassium dynamics are central to non-synaptic epileptiform activity, and astrocytic potassium buffering mechanisms have emerged as key regulators of network excitability. Yet the specific contributions of astrocytic gap-junction coupling and potassium spatial buffering to neuronal synchronization across different spatial scales remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we developed a microcircuit biophysical model consisting of two astrocyte-neuron modules, each comprising one astrocyte coupled to five neurons. Astrocyte-neuron interactions are mediated exclusively through shared extracellular potassium dynamics. Using a reduced astrocyte model that captures both local membrane and syncytial potassium buffering, we systematically investigated how astrocytic potassium handling shapes neuronal activity patterns and inter-module synchronization. Our results demonstrate that astrocytes prevent the emergence of pathological states - such as sustained ictal activity and depolarization block, by stabilizing extracellular potassium levels. Furthermore, we show that astrocytic gap-junction coupling strength critically regulates phase synchronization between neuronal modules: stronger coupling increases inter-module phase synchronization in lower-excitability model regimes, whereas impaired astrocytic buffering is associated with strongly synchronized seizure-like activity when extracellular potassium is elevated. These findings support the hypothesis that astrocytic networks could enhance modularity on hippocampal neuronal assemblies, and suggest that astrocytic connexins may represent a relevant therapeutic target in epilepsy and other disorders characterized by aberrant neural synchronization.
The Comparative Safety of Tramadol versus Oxycodone after Major Procedures, in Patients with Cirrhosis.浏览
PMID 43057554 · The American journal of medicine · 2026 11 19
作者：Simon TG, Pritchard KT, Chen Q, Kehoe AR et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: In patients with cirrhosis undergoing surgical procedures, the optimal post-operative pain management strategy is unclear. While oxycodone and tramadol are frequently prescribed, some guidelines caution against tramadol use in cirrhosis, given variable hepatic metabolism and anticholinergic effects. DESIGN: Within two U.S. datasets (MarketScan [2012-2023]; Optum's de-identified Clinformatics® Data Mart Database [2004-2025]), we constructed a 1:1 propensity score (PS)-matched cohort with cirrhosis initiating either short-acting tramadol or oxycodone within 30 days of a major operative procedure. Patients were followed for 90-days in an intention-to-treat approach for the primary outcome of liver decompensation (i.e., hospitalization for hepatic encephalopathy [HE], ascites, spontaneous bacterial peritonitis [SBP], variceal hemorrhage or hepatorenal syndrome [HRS]); secondary outcomes included all-cause hospitalizations, fractures, hyponatremia, hypoglycemia, seizures, and mortality. We estimated pooled PS-matched hazard ratios (HRs) and 95% confidence intervals (CIs), controlling for 141 pre-treatment characteristics. RESULTS: After PS matching, initiators of tramadol versus oxycodone demonstrated no significant differences in rates of liver decompensation (303.9 per 1000 person-years [PY] vs. 288.0/1000PY; HR=1.06 [95%CI=0.93-1.21]; n=6,818 matched pairs). This included non-significant differences in rates of hospitalization for HE (HR=1.07, 95%CI=0.92-1.04), or for ascites, SBP or HRS (HR=1.02, 95%CI=0.87-1.19). We also found no significant differences for the secondary outcomes, between groups. Findings persisted across all subgroup and sensitivity analyses. CONCLUSION: Among patients with cirrhosis initiating post-procedure tramadol or oxycodone, no significant differences were observed in rates of liver decompensation. Given the caution necessary when prescribing opioids in cirrhosis, future studies will need to assess safety across opioid formulations and dosing ranges.
Epilepsy Surgery in Patients with Non-Lesional Magnetic Resonance Imaging Evaluated with Stereoelectroencephalography: Retrospective Analysis in a High-Complexity Center and Literature Review.推荐
PMID 43057519 · Neurocirugia · 2026 11 19
作者：Minghinelli FE, Verdier E, Seoane P, Giagante B et al.

发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Background and objective When epilepsy is associated with non-lesional magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and there is no anatomical-electro-clinical correlation of seizures, invasive studies, such as stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG), are necessary to determine the epileptogenic zone (EZ) and, subsequently, the type of surgery to be performed. The aim of this work is to report the results of epilepsy surgery in patients with non-lesional MRI evaluated with SEEG and to conduct a literature review. Materials and methods Retrospective descriptive observational study. Non-invasive pre-surgical studies included neuropsychological and psychiatric evaluation, MRI with epilepsy protocol, positron emission tomography, and video-electroencephalography. Invasive studies included SEEG. Surgery was performed with curative intent in all patients. The Engel criteria were used to evaluate post-surgical results. Pathological anatomy and its correlation with surgical outcomes were considered. Results Eleven patients met the inclusion criteria for our analysis. All presented with inconsistent non-invasive preoperative examinations that did not allow for localization of the epileptic zone (EZ). SEEG was performed, successfully theorizing the ZE according to the electroclinical hypothesis in all patients. Four patients underwent anterior temporal lobectomy and seven underwent extratemporal corticectomy. We obtained a 63.6% rate of good postoperative results. Conclusion The SEEG allowed theorizing the ZE in all patients. The overall postoperative results were consistent with the literature. However, in the subgroup analysis, unlike previous studies, in our case series, extratemporal non-lesional epilepsy showed better postoperative results than temporal non-lesional epilepsy.
Prediction of surgical outcome using cyclic spectral coherent networks of intracranial EEG signals.推荐
PMID 43057485 · Journal of neuroscience methods · 2026 11 19
作者：Krishnamani DB, Manuskandan SR, Menon RN, Karthick PA
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜BIOCHEMICAL RESEARCH METHODS：Q3；NEUROSCIENCES：Q3
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy is a network disorder characterized by abnormal brain connectivity. Identifying network-level biomarkers is critical for improving surgical outcome prediction and understanding seizure dynamics. NEW METHODS: This study proposes a novel framework to assess the brain network organization of epileptic patients using cyclostationary-based connectivity matrices derived from intracranial EEG (iEEG) signals. The iEEG recordings of epileptic patients with surgical outcomes classified as Engel I (n=19) and Engel II+ (n=19) groups are considered for this study. Cyclic Spectral Coherence (CSC) based connectivity matrices are estimated from the preictal, ictal and postictal seizure phases using Welch's method. Further, network hubs are identified using centrality measures and the Resection Hub Alignment Degree (RHAD) metric is computed to quantify the alignment between the hubs and surgically resected nodes. RESULTS: The results of the cyclostationary spectrum reveal non-zero spectral correlation density, confirming the presence of rhythmic patterns in iEEG signals with dominant peaks observed in the SOZ regions. Furthermore, preictal RHAD-EVC differentiates the seizure-free and non-seizure-free patient groups (p < 0.01) and achieves an AUC of 0.64 for postoperative surgical outcome prediction. COMPARISON WITH EXISTING METHODS: The results of the proposed method are in good agreement with those reported in previous studies. CONCLUSIONS: Overall, the cyclic spectral coherent network-based RHAD metric may serve as a robust index that captures the network disruptions beyond geometric overlap. The proposed framework demonstrates potential to serve as an ancillary tool for characterizing network alterations rather than as a standalone predictive indicator.
Serum lactate distribution across neurologic emergencies: implications for seizure identification.推荐
PMID 43057152 · The American journal of emergency medicine · 2026 11 12
作者：Osawa SI, Miyata S, Fujita K, Kawamura T et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Serum lactate is routinely measured in emergency departments (EDs) as a marker of systemic metabolic stress, but its distribution across neurologic emergencies is poorly characterized. We evaluated serum lactate distributions among patients with suspected neurologic emergencies and its utility for seizure identification. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed 22,430 consecutive ED visits and identified 637 ambulance-transported patients with suspected neurologic emergencies. All underwent venous blood gas analysis and non-contrast computed tomography on arrival. Serum lactate levels were compared among seizure (SZ), cerebral infarction (INF), transient ischemic attack (TIA), subarachnoid hemorrhage (SAH), intracerebral hemorrhage (ICH), and other diagnoses. Multivariable logistic regression, receiver operating characteristic (ROC), and classification and regression tree (CART) analyses were performed. RESULTS: Serum lactate differed significantly across diagnostic groups (P < 0.001) and was highest in patients with SZ and SAH. Among patients evaluated for ischemic stroke, age and serum lactate independently predicted SZ. ROC analysis showed moderate discrimination between SZ and ischemic stroke (INF/TIA) (area under the curve, 0.80; 95% confidence interval, 0.73-0.90). CART identified 4.05 mmol/L as the primary lactate threshold, with age providing additional stratification. Patients with lactate ≥4.05 mmol/L and age < 59.5 years had a 100% probability of SZ. CONCLUSIONS: Serum lactate shows distinct distributions across neurologic emergencies encountered in the ED. Although not disease-specific, age-adjusted interpretation of serum lactate may improve seizure identification in patients presenting with suspected stroke. Because lactate measurement is rapid and routinely available, it may provide practical support for early diagnostic decision-making.
Focal impaired-consciousness seizures show greater thalamocortical coupling than preserved-consciousness seizures at matched windows.推荐
PMID 43057029 · Clinical neurophysiology : official journal of the International Federation of Clinical Neurophysiology · 2026 11 16
作者：Oluigbo CO, Wittanayakorn N, Xie H, Buraniqi E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To test whether seizure type (focal impaired consciousness seizures [FICS] vs focal preserved consciousness seizures [FPCS]) predicts seizure-onset-zone (SOZ) thalamocortical coupling at matched ictal windows. METHODS: 74 focal seizures (62 FICS, 12 FPCS) from 26 patients undergoing stereoelectroencephalography were analyzed. Imaginary coherence (iCoh) between SOZ and thalamic or cortical contacts was computed in the gamma (30-80 Hz) and high-gamma (80-150 Hz) bands as pre-specified primary bands. Primary tests compared FICS and FPCS at three fixed 16-second ictal windows (onset, mid-ictal, offset) using linear mixed-effects (LME) models. RESULTS: At all three fixed 16-s ictal windows, focal impaired consciousness seizures (FICS) showed greater SOZ-thalamocortical coupling than focal preserved consciousness seizures (FPCS) (onset P = 0.005, mid-ictal P = 0.043, offset P = 0.002), with no independent effect of ictal duration in any window. The full-seizure average showed a strong apparent duration effect (P < 0.001) and null seizure-type effect (P = 0.40), consistent with mechanical dilution of the early-ictal signal by longer post-onset averaging. Per-epoch matched-position analysis confirmed FICS > FPCS at 19/19 positions. CONCLUSIONS: When compared at matched ictal windows, FICS exceeds FPCS in thalamocortical coupling consistently. The full-seizure average's dominant duration effect is a modeling artefact of averaging unequal window lengths, not a physiological property of longer seizures. SIGNIFICANCE: Greater ictal SOZ-thalamocortical coupling in seizures with impaired consciousness identifies a candidate objective physiological correlate of awareness impairment in focal epilepsy, with implications for thalamic-targeted neuromodulation aimed at preserving or restoring awareness.
Seizure freedom in a regional epilepsy cohort: associations with age, income, and education.推荐
PMID 43057026 · Seizure · 2026 11 13
作者：Rosendahl H, Zelano J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q3
摘要：PURPOSE: Seizure freedom is one of the most important outcomes in epilepsy. Historically, two-thirds of epilepsy patients achieve seizure freedom, but shifts in etiologies, ages, treatments, and care provision limit extrapolation to present conditions. Low socioeconomic position is associated with epilepsy severity; suggested bidirectional mechanisms include disparities in access, health literacy, or adherence and seizure control allowing employment. We asked what proportion of epilepsy patients achieve seizure freedom and aimed to describe associations with relative socioeconomic position. METHODS: Regional study of adult patients enrolled between 2020-2024 (n = 575, median age 47) with follow-up for at least one year. Individual and household income and education level were obtained from nationwide registers for 2023. Seizure freedom for one year at last follow-up was determined from medical records or a participant survey. Cross-sectional associations between seizure freedom and age, epilepsy type, income and education were assessed using binary logistic regression. RESULTS: 65.7% (95%CI:61.7-69.4) were seizure-free at last follow-up. Higher age and generalized epilepsy were associated with seizure freedom. Normalized household income was not associated with seizure freedom, but individual total and earned income and higher education were. Reported adherence to prescribed drugs was lower among seizure-free patients. CONCLUSIONS: The proportion attaining seizure freedom was similar to previous reports. The associations with individual income and education were complex and the limitation of our cohort and the cross-sectional method preclude causal inference. Longitudinal studies are needed to assess whether socioeconomic position can identify patients who would benefit from closer clinical follow-up.

摘要：BACKGROUND: Inappropriate use of diagnostic tests is associated with a range of adverse effects, including increased healthcare costs, use of emergency department resources and cumulative exposure to ionising radiation . The aim of this study was to determine the frequency, yield and influence of neuroimaging among patients that present to the emergency department (ED) with recurrent seizures. METHODS: This retrospective cohort study was conducted in an adult ED in Australia and included patients with recurrent seizures without suspicion of acute traumatic brain injury. The primary outcome was the frequency of neuroimaging. Secondary outcomes were the proportion of abnormal imaging results, the influence of imaging results on acute patient management and the correlation between clinical factors and neuroimaging requests and results. RESULTS: Among 398 eligible patient presentations, 84 (21.1%) underwent computed tomography of the brain (CTB) scans. Postsurgical seizure occurrence and cognitive outcome between temporal and non-temporal lobe epilepsy: MRI-guided laser interstitial thermal therapy for drug-resistant epilepsy.推荐

摘要：OBJECTIVE: MRI-guided laser interstitial thermal therapy (MRgLITT) is a novel minimally invasive intervention for drug-resistant epilepsy. We assess the surgical outcome and cognitive outcome of MRgLITT for temporal and non-temporal epilepsy. METHODS: 27 patients with refractory epilepsy scheduled for MRgLITT from 2023 to 2024 at the First Affiliated Hospital of Fujian Medical University were enrolled. Epileptogenic zones were located with non-invasive evaluation system ± stereoelectroencephalography (sEEG). Demographic data, surgical and cognitive outcome were compared between temporal and non-temporal lobe epilepsy. RESULTS: 10 patients had temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE), all involving mesial temporal targets, and 17 patients had non-temporal lobe epilepsy (nTLE). All patients were followed up for seizure occurrence after surgery. 18 patients completed MMSE, 12 patients completed WAB and AVLT.
