SIGNIFICANCE: Patients with TLE achieved better seizure control than those with nTLE, whereas cognitive decline was observed mainly in TLE patients after MRgLITT. These preliminary findings suggest that MRgLITT could be an effective option for well-localized TLE, and a reasonable alternative for nTLE patients with relatively stable preservation of cognitive function.
Large Language Models in Clinical Neurology: A Scoping Review.浏览
PMID 43056665 · Neurology. Clinical practice · 2026 11 19
作者：Gorenshtein A, Shihada K, Omar M, Barash Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：PURPOSE OF REVIEW: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being evaluated for clinical neurology applications, yet their real-world utility, safety, and deployment readiness remain unclear. This review maps the literature on LLMs applied to text-based and text-derived neurology tasks, characterizing current use cases, evaluation strategies, performance metrics, and reported safety concerns. We identify areas with relatively robust evidence, highlight important evidence gaps, and outline key methodological and clinical barriers that must be addressed before responsible clinical adoption. RECENT FINDINGS: Following  Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses Extension for Scoping Reviews (PRISMA-ScR), we searched 4 databases (January 2022 to February 2026) and included 32 studies (2023-2026) spanning 7 neurology subspecialties; 9 were simulation or feasibility studies, 17 analyzed retrospective clinical data, and 6 reported prospective evaluation. Proprietary models predominated; 5 studies used retrieval-augmented generation and 1 used a multiagent framework. Performance was highest for constrained tasks, including binary diagnostic classification (area under the curve 0.75-0.94), information extraction (F1 0.89-0.90), patient-education question answering (accuracy 68%-97%), and ischemic stroke thrombectomy decision support (area under the curve [AUC] 0.92) and lowest for open-ended case-based reasoning (accuracy 42%-54%). Recurrent safety signals included hallucinated and fabricated citations, poor calibration with overconfidence (for example, 100% sensitivity with 27% specificity for epilepsy diagnosis), and a systematic bias toward intervention. On adapted Quality Assessment of Diagnostic Accuracy Studies Using Artificial Intelligence (QUADAS-AI) charting, overall risk of bias was low in 1 study (3%), moderate in 16 (50%), and high in 15 (47%), driven chiefly by absent external validation (31/32, 97%). Benchmarked against high-reliability and 6 Sigma standards, reported error rates remain orders of magnitude too high for autonomous use. SUMMARY: Evidence for LLMs in neurology is early, heterogeneous, and limited by scarce external and prospective validation. Current data support only narrow, clinician-supervised uses such as documentation and education, while cautioning against autonomous diagnostic or therapeutic use. Rigorous, prospective, externally validated studies with standardized reporting and explicit safety and ethical evaluation are needed before broader adoption. REGISTRATION: PROSPERO CRD420251082465.
Long-term complication profiles following transsphenoidal and transcranial resection of adult craniopharyngioma: A retrospective cohort study.推荐

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Craniopharyngiomas arise in the sellar/suprasellar region and are managed with maximal safe resection via transsphenoidal (TSR) or transcranial (TCR) approaches. The optimal approach remains contested, as treatment must balance tumor control against long-term visual, endocrine, neurologic, and cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) leak-related morbidity. Longitudinal complication and morbidity profiles of craniopharyngioma resection in adults remain limited. We evaluated approach-specific long-term outcomes in a large multi-institutional adult cohort. METHODS: In a retrospective cohort study using the TriNetX network, adult craniopharyngioma patients undergoing TSR or TCR were compared using unmatched analyses and propensity score matching (PSM). Matching characteristics included demographics, comorbidities, presenting features, and prior radiation. Cox models estimated hazard ratios (HR) at 1, 5, 10, and 15 years from surgery. RESULTS: We identified 2,558 (41.0%) patients who underwent TSR and 1,452 (23.3%) who underwent TCR. Patients selected for TCR had more preoperative hydrocephalus and diabetes insipidus (DI), whereas TSR patients had more hypopituitarism. After PSM (1,199 pairs), TSR carried higher early CSF-leak risk (1-year HR:1.65, 95% confidence interval [CI]: 1.25-2.19, p < 0.001) but lower rates of 1-year mortality (HR:0.54, 95%CI:0.33-0.91, p = 0.02), hydrocephalus (HR:0.35, 95%CI:0.18-0.71, p = 0.002), blindness (HR:0.54, 95%CI:0.33-0.86, p = 0.009), and adjuvant radiation (HR:0.70, 95%CI:0.52-0.94, p = 0.02). At 10 years, TSR was associated with lower hydrocephalus (HR:0.57, 95%CI:0.35-0.93, p = 0.02), DI (HR:0.69, 95%CI:0.52-0.91, p = 0.01), and blindness (HR:0.61, 95%CI:0.43-0.86, p = 0.01), but higher hypopituitarism (HR:1.24, 95%CI:1.06-1.46, p = 0.01), higher composite endocrine morbidity (HR:1.23, 95%CI:1.03-1.46, p = 0.02), and persistently elevated CSF leak (HR:1.32, 95%CI:1.02-1.72, p = 0.04). Findings at 5 and 15 years were concordant. CONCLUSIONS: Adults with craniopharyngioma experience substantial long-term visual, endocrine, hydrocephalus-related, and treatment-related morbidity. TSR and TCR were associated with distinct long-term complication profiles, with TSR generally associated with lower rates of hydrocephalus, blindness, epilepsy, and DI but higher rates of CSF leak and hypopituitarism. These findings provide long-term population-level estimates that may inform surgical counseling and postoperative surveillance.
Data-driven clinical decision trees and generative topographic mapping to guide stroke rehabilitation program allocation.推荐
PMID 43056318 · PloS one · 2026 11 19
作者：Komaris DS, Anagnostou P, Nikolopoulos S, Tsaopoulos D et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Early and timely admission to post-stroke rehabilitation remains a key determinant of optimal functional recovery. In this study, we present a structured framework for the development and evaluation of multivariable prediction models and Classification and Regression Tree (CART) decision-support tools to guide patient allocation across diverse subacute rehabilitation settings. METHODS: We have constructed a heterogeneous synthetic dataset of 700 stroke patients characterized by 49 demographic, clinical, and functional variables derived from multiple, literature-reported, statistical distributions of patient populations. Subsequently, the dataset was independently annotated by an expert panel of clinicians, who assigned each stroke patient to one of seven rehabilitation programs ranging from home-based to high-intensity hospital-based care (target outcome). Feature importance analysis identified cognitive function status, National Institutes of Health Stroke Scale (NIHSS) score, age, presence of early seizures, and alcohol consumption as the most influential predictors of rehabilitation program allocation. Six supervised classifiers were then trained to predict rehabilitation program allocation, along with shallow CARTs of different depths. Internal evaluation used 200 repetitions of stratified ten-fold cross-validation, with metrics calculated from pooled out-of-fold predictions and summarized as mean and standard deviation. RESULTS: In internal evaluation against the expert-assigned labels, the XGBoost and the best-performing CART model achieved mean accuracies of 0.832 and 0.847 and balanced accuracies of 0.381 and 0.393, respectively. Both exceeded the institutional benchmark, which achieved accuracy of 0.753 and balanced accuracy of 0.325. Key predictors included cognitive function status, NIHSS score, age, presence of early seizures, and alcohol consumption. In addition, Generative Topographic Mapping (GTM) facilitated the exploration of patient relationships within different prediction models that may help distinguish clusters with similar phenotypes, i.e., patient groups with shared clinical characteristics. It further enabled the visual comparison of stroke rehabilitation program predictions across models (e.g., baseline versus CART model). This allowed for assessment of program recommendations by the developed models in transparent and meaningful ways, and the identification of patient characteristics influencing divergent model decisions. CONCLUSIONS: This study demonstrates the feasibility of developing and evaluating multivariable prediction models for rehabilitation program allocation using an openly shared and expert-annotated dataset. This framework offers a transparent and adaptable foundation for developing bespoke, data-driven decision-support systems in stroke rehabilitation. Ultimately, the proposed approach promotes interpretable, context-sensitive, and individualized rehabilitation planning adaptable to varying clinical resources and practices.

摘要：Many dynamical systems in nature, such as brains and weather systems, are highly nonlinear and complex. Determining information flow among the components that make up these dynamical systems is challenging. If the components are the result of a common process or become synchronized, causality measures typically fail. We previously introduced Cross-Dynamical Delay Differential Analysis, a nonlinear method for assessing causal influence, along with a complementary approach for dynamical similarity between time series data, Dynamical Ergodicity Delay Differential Analysis. Adapting Tensor Kernel Machines to Enable Efficient Transfer Learning for Seizure Detection.推荐
PMID 43056265 · IEEE transactions on neural systems and rehabilitation engineering : a publication of the IEEE Engineering in Medicine and Biology Society · 2026 11 19
作者：De Rooij SJS, Hunyadi B
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜2区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜ENGINEERING, BIOMEDICAL：Q1；REHABILITATION：Q1
摘要：Transfer learning aims to optimize performance in a target task by learning from a related source problem. In this work, we propose an efficient transfer learning method using a tensor kernel machine. Our method takes inspiration from the adaptive SVM and hence transfers 'knowledge' from the source to the 'adapted' model via regularization. The main advantage of using tensor kernel machines is that they leverage low-rank tensor networks to learn a compact non-linear model in the primal domain. This allows for a more efficient adaptation without adding more parameters to the model. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, we apply the adaptive tensor kernel machine (Adapt-TKM) to seizure detection on behind-the-ear EEG. By personalizing patient-independent models with a small amount of patient-specific data, the patient-adapted model (which utilizes the Adapt-TKM), improves the detection performance for the majority of the patients and significantly outperforms the fully patient-specific model. Notably, it is able to do so while requiring around 100 times fewer parameters than the adaptive SVM model, leading to a correspondingly faster inference speed. This makes the Adapt-TKM especially useful for resource-constrained wearable devices.
Neurocognitive, Neurological, and Neuropsychiatric Manifestations of Chronic Hypoparathyroidism: A Narrative Review.推荐
PMID 43055938 · Calcified tissue international · 2026 11 19
作者：Mineo MI, Guarnotta V, Arnaldi G
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Hypoparathyroidism (HPT) is characterized by hypocalcemia and inappropriately low or absent parathyroid hormone (PTH) levels. Although skeletal and renal complications are well recognized, neurological, neurocognitive, and neuropsychiatric manifestations remain underappreciated despite their impact on quality of life. This narrative review focuses on their pathophysiology, clinical presentation, assessment, and therapeutic implications. A narrative review was conducted using a structured search of PubMed/MEDLINE from database inception through July 2026, supplemented by reference-list screening. Search terms combined hypoparathyroidism and hypocalcemia with neurological, neurocognitive, neuropsychiatric, seizure, basal ganglia calcification, brain fog, fatigue, and quality-of-life terms. Guidelines, cohort studies, original mechanistic studies, and informative case reports were included. Manifestations include acute hypocalcemia-related neuromuscular irritability and seizures, as well as chronic intracranial calcifications, movement disorders, cognitive dysfunction, fatigue, and psychiatric symptoms. Persistent manifestations may reflect irreversible mineral deposition, cumulative exposure to calcium-phosphate disturbances, structural brain changes, and incomplete restoration of PTH-dependent physiology despite target serum calcium concentrations. Neurocognitive, neurological, and neuropsychiatric manifestations are clinically important components of chronic hypoparathyroidism. Their recognition supports timely biochemical correction, focused neurological and psychiatric assessment, and individualized treatment when symptoms persist despite conventional therapy.

摘要：Children with a chronic physical illness (CPI), such as diabetes, asthma, and epilepsy, experience significant stress and are at a greater risk of psychopathology. Parent distress strongly influences child psychopathology, and understanding stable and dynamic paths of transmission are critical for developing effective interventions. This study examined the between-dyad (stable) and within-dyad (dynamic) effects of parent internalizing symptoms and parenting stress on psychopathology in children with a CPI. Additionally, we examined whether these associations were moderated by child hair cortisol concentration (HCC). Data come from 244 children (2-16 years at baseline) with a CPI, and their parents, who were followed for 48-months (assessments at baseline, 6, 12, 24, and 48 months). Parent internalizing symptoms and parenting stress were assessed using self-report measures. Hair samples were collected from children, and parents reported child psychopathology symptoms. Previous work identified three HCC trajectories among children in this sample: Hypersecretion (68.0%), Hyposecretion (8.6%), and Hyper-to-Hypo (23.4%). There were significant between- and within-dyad effects of parent internalizing symptoms and parenting stress on child internalizing and externalizing symptoms. Child HCC moderated the between-dyad effects of parent internalizing symptoms and parenting stress on child internalizing symptoms. The combination of high average levels of parent internalizing symptoms or parenting stress and cortisol hypersecretion predicted greater child symptoms. Findings highlight the importance of considering parent distress, paired with child cortisol, to understand psychopathology risk in children with a CPI. Overall, HCC may be a promising biomarker for understanding heterogeneity in associations between parent distress and child psychopathology.
A nonsense variant in KCNH7 (c.1324 C>T) is associated with a variable phenotypic spectrum of epilepsy.推荐

摘要：KCNH7 is a voltage-gated potassium (K+) channel (Kv) that regulates membrane excitability and K+ flux across tissues. Studies using mouse models have suggested that Kcnh7 may play an important role in epilepsy susceptibility and pathogenesis. However, clinical and experimental evidence implicating KCNH7 variants in human epilepsy, as well as the genotype-phenotype association, remains unclear. Here, we aimed to refine the clinical, genetic, and functional electrophysiological features of a p.R442* variant identified in three individuals to elucidate potential genotype-phenotype association that may guide anticipatory management. We generated Kcnh7 mutants​ using CRISPR/Cas9-mediated genome editing. Heterozygous mutants exhibited increased neuronal hyperexcitability and increased seizure susceptibility due to reduced Kcnh7-encoded protein levels. Functional analyses established the KCNH7 p.R442* variant as a loss-of-function allele, with haploinsufficiency identified as a primary pathogenic mechanism. Our results underscore the significance of functional characterization for the KCNH7 p.R442* variant within the context of clinical interpretation, although larger cohorts are required to further validate these observations.
Association between neonatal phototherapy and childhood epilepsy and febrile seizures: a systematic review and meta-analysis.推荐
PMID 43055793 · European journal of pediatrics · 2026 11 19
作者：Aldemerdash MA, Hamouda N, Abbas OF, Abdelaziz AB et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：Meta分析
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：UNLABELLED: Phototherapy has been widely used for the treatment of neonatal hyperbilirubinemia. However, concerns persist about its long-term neurological effects. While some studies report an increased risk of epilepsy after phototherapy, others found no association. This systematic review and meta-analysis aim to examine the association between phototherapy and childhood epilepsy and febrile seizures. We followed the PRISMA guidelines for our meta-analysis. We searched six databases for studies investigating the association between phototherapy and childhood epilepsy and febrile seizures. The Newcastle-Ottawa Scale was used for quality assessment. Pooled hazard ratios (HRs) and risk ratios (RRs) with 95% CIs were calculated using random-effect model in R software. Three studies with 632,227 participants met our inclusion criteria. Phototherapy was associated with a 37% higher risk of childhood epilepsy (HR = 1.37, 95% CI = 1.13-1.67, p < 0.01; I2 = 30%). Subgroup analysis revealed a significant risk in boys (HR = 1.62, 95% CI = 1.17-2.24, p < 0.01) but not girls (HR = 1.14, 95% CI = 0.92-1.41, p = 0.23). No association was found with febrile seizures (HR = 1.07, 95% CI = 0.99-1.15, p = 0.09). CONCLUSION: In this meta-analysis of three observational cohorts, neonatal phototherapy was associated with a possible increase in the risk of childhood epilepsy, particularly in boys; this finding depends on a small and methodologically heterogeneous evidence base, and residual confounding by indication cannot be excluded. Given the limited number of studies, the conflicting result from the most methodologically rigorous study, and the exploratory nature of the sex-stratified analysis, these findings should be regarded as hypothesis-generating rather than as grounds to alter current phototherapy practice. Further well-designed studies controlling for confounders are needed. WHAT IS KNOWN: • Phototherapy is standard treatment for neonatal hyperbilirubinemia and is generally considered safe. • Prior studies on phototherapy and childhood epilepsy have shown conflicting results. WHAT IS NEW: • Pooled analysis of 632,227 infants show an association between phototherapy and childhood epilepsy, particularlyin boys, but not febrile seizures. • The association may reflect residual confounding rather than a causal effect, and does not currently justify changingphototherapy practice.
Long-Term Neurologic Complication and Healthcare Burden in Infants Receiving Extracorporeal Life Support: A Nationwide Population-Based Cohort Study.浏览
PMID 43055590 · Pediatric critical care medicine : a journal of the Society of Critical Care Medicine and the World Federation of Pediatric Intensive and Critical Care Societies · 2026 11 19
作者：Kang M, Lee D, Yu H, Choi I et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: To evaluate survival, neurologic complications, and healthcare burden in infant extracorporeal life support (ECLS) recipients using nationally representative administrative data from a single-payer healthcare system. DESIGN: Retrospective, population-based cohort study. SETTING: South Korea's National Health Insurance Service, covering 97% of the population. PATIENTS: Infants under 1 year who received ECLS between 2009 and 2022. INTERVENTIONS: None. MEASUREMENTS AND MAIN RESULTS: Of 698 infants who underwent ECLS, 212 infants (30.4%) survived during follow-up (estimated 1-yr mortality: 67.1%). Among survivors, 128 (60.4%) developed neurologic injuries, including acute events such as ischemic stroke, hemorrhagic stroke, and hypoxic-ischemic brain injury (HIBI), as well as long-term sequelae such as cerebral palsy, epilepsy, hearing loss, and developmental delay. Most acute neurologic injuries were diagnosed within the first year, whereas developmental delay showed prolonged diagnostic windows extending up to 12 years. Survivors with neurologic injury had significantly longer hospital stays (432.1 vs. 182.3 d; p < 0.001) and higher cumulative healthcare costs during long-term follow-up. Median index hospitalization costs were USD 36,000-37,000. The burden analysis showed distinct profiles across neurologic complications: developmental delay was the most prevalent sequela, whereas HIBI was associated with the highest cumulative healthcare cost despite lower prevalence. CONCLUSIONS: Neurologic complications following ECLS in infants are common and impose substantial long-term healthcare burden. These findings highlight the need for early risk stratification, structured neurologic and neurodevelopmental monitoring, and sustained rehabilitation planning in this high-risk population. This study provides a foundation for international benchmarking and health policy planning in pediatric critical care.
[Medication dose adjustment in obesity: lessons from practice].浏览
PMID 43055323 · Nederlands tijdschrift voor geneeskunde · 2026 10 06
作者：Stolk RF, Stunnenberg BC, Ten Napel X, de Maat MR et al.
链接：PubMed
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The increasing prevalence of obesity complicates appropriate drug dosing. We describe patient with obesity who developed aciclovir-induced renal and neurotoxicity, culminating in status epilepticus for which he required ICU admission. In this case, dosing was based on total body weight, which likely resulted in overdosing of this hydrophilic, renally cleared drug. In contrast, we present a patient with obesity who was successfully treated with voriconazole for invasive aspergillosis following septic shock. Given the drug's interindividual variability, dosing was based on the adjusted body weight and therapeutic drug monitoring was performed. Obesity induces clinically relevant physiological changes affecting drug absorption, distribution, metabolism, and elimination. However, these effects are heterogeneous and drug-specific, limiting the utility of simple weight-based dosing strategies. We conclude that drug dosing in patients with obesity requires an individualized approach based on pharmacokinetic principles. Early involvement of a clinical pharmacist and, where available, therapeutic drug monitoring are strongly recommended.
Synaptic Transmission Onto Somatostatin Interneurons Is Enhanced Under Acidic Conditions in an Elfn1-Dependent Manner.推荐
PMID 43055155 · Journal of neurophysiology · 2026 11 19
作者：Bobbitt MR, Evans MA, Stachniak TJ
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜NEUROSCIENCES：Q3；PHYSIOLOGY：Q3
摘要：Somatostatin (SOM) interneurons regulate hyperexcitability by producing inhibitory feedback when brain activity becomes excessive. SOM inhibitory feedback depends on the cooperative activity of two synaptic proteins: Elfn1 (extracellular leucine rich repeat and fibronectin type III domain containing 1) and mGluR7 (metabotropic glutamate receptor 7). Elfn1 knockout (KO) mice experience seizures, and humans with ELFN1 mutations are at risk of epilepsy. Seizures can increase brain acidity, and mGluR7 can be inhibited by acidity. Elfn1 and mGluR7 normally act together to transiently suppress SOM interneuron recruitment and previous work showed that inhibiting mGluR7 increases excitatory transmission onto SOM interneurons. We hypothesized that acidity would disrupt mGluR7 activity to increase excitatory transmission onto SOM interneurons. Whole-cell recordings in cortical slices showed that acidity increased putative excitatory input onto SOM interneurons and decreased paired-pulse ratio (PPR), an effect that is absent in Elfn1 KO mice. Conversely, putative excitatory transmission onto excitatory cells in wild-type mice was decreased under acidic conditions, without changes in PPR. Overall, we show how Elfn1/mGluR7 change SOM interneuron responses to acidic environments to enhance inhibition while reducing excitation.

摘要：Autism Spectrum Disorder is a neurological and developmental condition impacting 1 in 31 children. Children, identified on the autism spectrum, are typically diagnosed by the age of 8; however, research indicates that there could be as many as 25% of undiagnosed adolescents. Autistic adolescents have a significantly higher prevalence of mental-health and medical conditions than their neurotypical peers including gastrointestinal disorders (e.g., chronic constipation), seizure disorders, sleep disturbances, and nutritional disorders (e.g., overeating). Part-1, "Interventions for Adolescent Students with Autism and Co-Occurring Mental Health Disorders," of this three-part series discussed interventions for the mental health comorbidities often experienced by autistic adolescents. Caregiver Burden in Highly Complex Pediatric Drug-Resistant Epilepsy: Associations With Functional Status and Developmental and Epileptic Encephalopathy.推荐
PMID 43055056 · Cureus · 2026 10 19
作者：Arteaga-Huiracocha AB, Venta-Sobero JA, Delaflor-Wagner CA, Avila-Cervantes EM et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Background Caregiver burden is an important consequence of caring for children with highly complex drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE). Although greater functional impairment may increase caregiving demands, burden may also be influenced by developmental and epileptic encephalopathy (DEE) and caregiver characteristics. We aimed to estimate caregiver burden and determine whether child functional status remained associated with burden after accounting for DEE and caregiver characteristics. Methods We conducted a cross-sectional analytical study of 59 child-caregiver dyads with highly complex DRE attending a national high-specialty medical center in Mexico City between November 2025 and February 2026. Caregiver burden was assessed using the 22-item Zarit Burden Interview (ZBI), with scores >46 defining overburden. Child functional status was assessed using the Functional Status Scale (FSS). DEE was classified according to an operational definition incorporating frequent seizures and/or epileptiform discharges, developmental slowing or regression, and an underlying etiology contributing to developmental impairment. Logistic regression models evaluated threshold-defined overburden, and a complementary linear model examined continuous ZBI scores. Results Overburden was present in 40/59 caregivers (67.8%; 95% CI, 54.4%-79.4%). Among 54 children with available FSS assessments, FSS scores were higher in dyads with overburden than in those without (median 11 (IQR, 9-20) vs 8 (IQR, 7-11.5); p = 0.021). However, the correlation between FSS and continuous ZBI scores was weak and not significant (rho = 0.165; p = 0.233). FSS was associated with overburden in the univariable model (OR per point, 1.14; 95% CI, 1.01-1.29), but this association attenuated after inclusion of DEE and caregiver characteristics and was not significant in the combined model (OR, 1.07; 95% CI, 0.93-1.22). In the combined model, DEE (OR, 8.38; 95% CI, 1.14-61.85) and caregiver age per 10 years (OR, 4.51; 95% CI, 1.26-16.12) were associated with overburden. In the continuous ZBI model, caregiver age was associated with higher burden (B = 6.49 points per 10 years; 95% CI, 1.59-11.39), as was parental caregiver status (B = 13.68; 95% CI, 2.19-25.16), whereas FSS and DEE were not independently associated with continuous ZBI scores. Individual FSS domains showed no consistent association with burden. Conclusions Caregiver overburden was frequent in this Mexican cohort of children with highly complex DRE. Although poorer functional status distinguished caregivers with overburden before adjustment, its association weakened after consideration of DEE and caregiver characteristics and was not observed when burden was analyzed continuously. DEE showed a large but imprecise association with threshold-defined overburden, whereas caregiver age was consistently associated with both categorical and continuous measures of burden. These findings suggest that caregiver burden should be understood as a distinct caregiver-level outcome within the broader clinical and family context of highly complex pediatric DRE, rather than solely as a consequence of the child's functional impairment.
Predictors of Anesthetic Augmentation in Electroconvulsive Therapy: A Retrospective Cohort Study.推荐
PMID 43054751 · Psychiatry and clinical psychopharmacology · 2026 10 11
作者：İpekçioğlu D, Oflazer C, Konuk N, Atay Ö et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Background: Anesthetic augmentation with aminophylline and/or remifentanil is commonly used to prolong seizure duration when propofol-based electroconvulsive therapy (ECT) results in inadequate seizures. We aimed to identify independent predictors of subsequent augmentation use and develop an exploratory risk score. Methods: This retrospective cohort study (January-December 2015, single center) included 527 patients (4,016 ECT sessions) who received propofol anesthesia. Patients were categorized as propofol-only (n=357) or augmentation (n=170) group. Multiple logistic regression was used to identify predictors of augmentation. ANCOVA compared the effect of different adjunctive agents on post-adjunct seizure duration. Results: Shorter first-session electroencephalography (EEG) seizure duration (OR = 0.968, P < .001) and higher first-session seizure-inducing energy (OR = 1.044, P < .001) independently predicted augmentation, while age was not significant (P = .120). All three augmentation strategies significantly prolonged seizure duration; although, the sample sizes for individual agents (aminophylline n=85, remifentanil n=44, combination n=41) precluded a definitive comparative analysis; the ANCOVA revealed no significant differences between agents (F=0.967, P = .382). CGI scores were comparable between groups at baseline (P = .412) and discharge (P = .321). An exploratory risk score based on the two significant predictors showed good discriminative ability (AUC = 0.786). Conclusion: First-session EEG seizure duration and stimulation energy are practical predictors of subsequent augmentation use, whereas age is not independently predictive. No statistically significant difference in post-adjunct seizure duration was detected among the three adjunctive strategies and augmentation is associated with comparable clinical improvement. External validation of the proposed risk score is required before clinical implementation.

Point-of-Care Ultrasound Detection of Acute Myocardial Dysfunction Following Bupropion Overdose: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43054333 · Cureus · 2026 10 19
作者：Cheatwood IR, Sterndorf HP, Guthrie A, Atti S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Bupropion overdose is associated with significant morbidity and mortality due to severe neurologic and cardiovascular toxicity, including seizures and life-threatening arrhythmias. Early recognition of electrocardiographic, clinical, and echocardiographic abnormalities is critical for prompt intervention and prevention of clinical deterioration. We present a case of a 21-year-old female who presented to the emergency department following an intentional polysubstance overdose involving suspected bupropion ingestion. The initial electrocardiogram (EKG) demonstrated sinus tachycardia. However, during continued monitoring, the patient developed QRS widening and seizures. Treatment included benzodiazepines, calcium, and sodium bicarbonate. Point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS) revealed a reduced ejection fraction (EF) of less than 30%, which was later confirmed on formal transthoracic echocardiography. These findings prompted escalation of care, intubation for airway protection and deep sedation, and transfer to the intensive care unit (ICU). During admission, she developed torsades de pointes (TdP), resulting in cardiac arrest. Despite her critical presentation, repeat echocardiography upon resolution of overdose toxicity demonstrated recovery of left ventricular systolic function, with improvement of EF to 50-55% during hospitalization. This case demonstrates the potential role of POCUS in the early recognition of myocardial dysfunction associated with bupropion toxicity. Together with serial EKG monitoring, these findings identified a patient at high risk for clinical deterioration and supported early escalation of care.

Association between immediate and early seizures and pre-injury alcohol and marijuana use: A traumatic brain injury model systems national database analysis.推荐
PMID 43054266 · Neurotrauma reports · 2026 10 30
作者：Acosta O, Mathews R, Nguyen N, Al-Lawati Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：This study examined the role that pre-injury alcohol and marijuana use have on the incidence of post-traumatic seizures, as well as their relationship with their known risk factors. We hypothesized that pre-injury alcohol and marijuana use would independently predict post-traumatic seizures at 3 separate time points, categorized as immediate, early, and both immediate and early seizures. 3,875 participants were identified using the Traumatic Brain Injury Model Systems National Database. The primary outcome was the occurrence of a post-traumatic seizure. The primary exposure variables of interest included pre-injury alcohol and marijuana use. Covariates included presence of pre-injury seizures, age and sex at injury, and injury-related characteristics. The relationship between pre-injury alcohol and marijuana use, and each seizure outcome was examined using separate logistic regression models, with results reported as odds ratios with 95% confidence intervals. From the total number of participants 88.8% did not experience seizures, with immediate seizures observed in 269 participants, early seizures observed in 111 participants, and both immediate and early seizures observed in 53 participants. For the immediate seizure group pre-injury alcohol use was not significantly associated with immediate seizures. In the early seizure group, however, alcohol use in the month prior to injury was significantly associated with increased odds, while pre-injury marijuana use was associated with decreased odds. In the combined immediate and early group, alcohol use was not significantly associated, but pre-injury marijuana use was associated with an increased odds of seizure. This study illustrated a possible priming effect of pre-injury alcohol use on post-injury brains, showing a stronger association between the development of early compared to immediate seizures. The effect of marijuana on seizure risk was less clear, nevertheless reinforcing the importance of strong counseling for this vulnerable population, particularly for those with a pre-morbid history of alcohol/substance use.

Corpora amylacea and synaptic organization in the human brain analyzed by volume electron microscopy.浏览
PMID 43054091 · Frontiers in neuroanatomy · 2026 11 04
作者：Cano-Astorga N, Merchan-Perez A, DeFelipe J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The presence of corpora amylacea in the brain is a frequent finding in normal aging and in a broad spectrum of neurological disorders, yet the significance of these deposits for the organization of human cortical circuits remains unclear. Here, we combined complementary light and electron microscopy approaches to analyze temporal lobe epilepsy biopsy specimens with and without corpora amylacea. Samples containing corpora amylacea showed marked alterations in synaptic circuit microorganization, which were not restricted to areas of corpora amylacea accumulation but extended throughout the cortical tissue. These changes were characterized by neuronal loss, gliosis, reduced synaptic density, an increased proportion of inhibitory synapses, and larger synapses. Integration of these structural findings with clinical data from the same patients further supports the view that corpora amylacea are associated with disease-related pathological processes. Together, these findings provide a basis for understanding the potential significance of corpora amylacea in human brain circuit dysfunction and may help to inform future research studies focusing on disease mechanisms.
EGFR-mutant lung adenocarcinoma with leptomeningeal metastasis after osimertinib treatment: intrathecal chemotherapy combined with targeted therapy supported by cerebrospinal fluid next generation sequencing - a case report.推荐
PMID 43054042 · Frontiers in oncology · 2026 11 04
作者：Gan X, Jin X, Pan M, Guo Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Leptomeningeal metastasis (LM) is a severe complication of advanced non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC). Conventional cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) cytology has limited sensitivity and often delays diagnosis. We report a case of LM molecularly supported by CSF next-generation sequencing (NGS) after three negative cytological examinations. NGS also revealed a potential bypass resistance alteration that informed subsequent treatment considerations. CASE: A 57-year-old female never-smoker had EGFR exon 21 p.L858R-mutant lung adenocarcinoma. After 18 months of first-line osimertinib (80 mg once daily), she developed progressive nausea, vomiting, and dizziness. Contrast-enhanced cranial magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) showed linear leptomeningeal enhancement. Lumbar puncture showed markedly elevated intracranial pressure (>300 mmH2O) and increased CSF protein. However, three CSF cytological examinations (including cell block sections) found no malignant cells. CSF NGS detected the EGFR p.L858R mutation identical to the primary tumor, together with CDK4 amplification. Integrated with the clinical and MRI presentation, these findings supported a diagnosis of probable LM and suggested cell-cycle bypass activation as a potential mechanism associated with osimertinib resistance. After intrathecal methotrexate plus continued oral osimertinib, her neurological symptoms improved markedly, and intracranial pressure returned to normal. The first seizure occurred on March 19, 2026, and MRI on April 8, 2026 confirmed new brain parenchymal metastases. Whole-brain radiotherapy with a local boost to the metastases, antiepileptic treatment, and continued osimertinib led to obvious shrinkage of the intracranial lesions. Grade 3 thrombocytopenia and severe malnutrition occurred during multimodal therapy and improved with supportive care. At the last follow-up, the patient was clinically stable with an Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group performance status (ECOG PS) of 1. CONCLUSIONS: CSF NGS can overcome the diagnostic bottleneck of cytology-negative LM. It can also identify potential resistance alterations and actionable targets (such as CDK4/6 inhibitors) at the same time, providing direction for subsequent targeted strategies. As part of multimodal therapy, intrathecal chemotherapy combined with an EGFR tyrosine kinase inhibitor (TKI) can contribute to disease control in the CSF compartment, whereas parenchymal progression still requires salvage radiotherapy. Cumulative hematological and nutritional toxicity should be monitored during sequential multimodal therapy.
Twenty Years of the Concept of the Privileged Extracellular Region: From Early Computations to Modern Anatomical Evidence.浏览
PMID 43053328 · Journal of neurochemistry · 2026 Nov
作者：Chvátal A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The brain extracellular space (ECS) is a heterogeneous and plastic compartment. The concept of the privileged extracellular region (PER) was introduced in 2004 as a computationally derived, spatially restricted microdomain of the ECS immediately surrounding glial cells, within which potassium (K+) accumulates preferentially and independently from the broader extracellular milieu. Using mathematical modeling of membrane currents in astrocytes and oligodendrocytes, the seminal paper demonstrated that the volume of the ECS accessible for K+ accumulation during depolarization was 10-100-fold smaller around oligodendrocytes than around astrocytes, establishing the first quantitative evidence for spatial heterogeneity of the periglial ECS. Over the following two decades, converging lines of structural, functional, and clinical evidence have dramatically expanded this concept. Perisynaptic astrocytic regions and extracellular geometry have now been visualized at nanoscale resolution using STED, dSTORM, and super-resolution shadow imaging (SUSHI), revealing a spongiform architecture with fine leaflets and reflexive loops that delineate functionally distinct microdomains at the synaptic interface. EAAT2/GLT-1 glutamate transporters and Kir4.1 K+ channels concentrated within these processes provide the molecular infrastructure for localized neurotransmitter clearance and K+ siphoning. Disruption of PER geometry-through reactive gliosis, astrocytic atrophy during aging, or leaflet retraction-contributes to the pathophysiology of epilepsy, Alzheimer's disease, ischemia, and cognitive decline. The present review traces the intellectual lineage of the PER concept, integrates two decades of anatomical and functional evidence, and highlights the therapeutic implications of understanding periglial microdomains as active regulators of brain homeostasis.
When elimination leaves patients behind: clinical, serologic and molecular evidence of a human African trypanosomiasis case highlighting diagnostic, surveillance and treatment gaps in Nigeria.推荐
PMID 43053212 · Infectious diseases of poverty · 2026 11 18
作者：Chukwudi C, Chinwendu J, Adenekan O, Opara O et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Despite substantial progress toward elimination, human African trypanosomiasis (HAT) remains vulnerable to under-recognition in low-surveillance settings. Nigeria, historically endemic for HAT, has reported very few recent cases, which raises questions about whether declining incidence reflects true interruption of transmission or limitations in surveillance and diagnostic systems. Here, we report a case that illustrates the unique challenges of case detection and surveillance, confirmation and treatment in Nigeria, which resulted in poor patient outcomes and ongoing transmission that may hinder sustainable HAT elimination. CASE PRESENTATION: A 17-year-old male from Imo State, Nigeria, first developed recurrent seizures in 2020 at the age of 12 and received treatment for epilepsy for approximately 4 years before progressive neurological deterioration prompted further investigations for HAT. Serological and molecular testing supported infection with Trypanosoma brucei, and cerebrospinal fluid findings were consistent with second-stage HAT. Tested family members also showed positive serological and molecular results, suggesting possible undetected transmission within the household. Despite the cumulative clinical, serologic and molecular evidence, treatment was not initiated owing to prevailing operational practices in the country. Patient's symptoms remain unabated with continued anti-epilepsy treatment and no access to HAT medications. CONCLUSIONS: This case demonstrates how prolonged clinical misdiagnosis, gaps in surveillance, and barriers to accessing treatment can delay recognition and management of HAT in low-prevalence settings, especially in the absence of parasitological confirmation. It also highlights broader challenges facing elimination programmes, illustrating how the gap between international diagnostic/treatment guidelines and operational practice affects patient care. The detection of infection markers among family members further raises concern about under-recognized transmissions in Nigeria. Strengthening clinician awareness, enhancing surveillance systems, conducting family-based case investigations, and ensuring timely access to adequate diagnosis and treatment will be essential for achieving equitable HAT elimination.
Clinical spectrum of intracellular cobalamin metabolism disorders: a single-center retrospective study from the United Arab Emirates.浏览
PMID 43053060 · Orphanet journal of rare diseases · 2026 08 22
作者：Shamsi AA, Abuhaltem O
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Cobalamin is essential for DNA synthesis and for methylmalonyl-CoA mutase and methionine synthase activity. Inherited disorders of intracellular cobalamin (Cbl) metabolism are single-gene defects affecting absorption, transport, or intracellular processing, with variable phenotypes depending on the severity and location of the defect. Data from high consanguinity populations, such as the UAE, are limited. AIM: To describe the clinical, biochemical, and molecular characteristics of patients with intracellular Cbl metabolism disorders at Tawam Hospital, UAE. METHODS: Retrospective review of patients followed between January 2010 and December 2025. Data included demographics, clinical features, laboratory and radiological findings, molecular results, treatment, and outcomes. RESULTS: Twelve patients were identified: 8 CblC, 1 CblE, 1 CblG, and 2 unclassified. Consanguinity was present in 75%. The earliest diagnosis was at 8 days; 91.6% were symptomatic. Presentations included seizures (58%), apparent life-threatening events (33%), hydrocephalus and cytopenia (25% each), global developmental delay, osteoporosis, hypothyroidism, hypogonadism, multicystic kidney, and psychiatric symptoms. Homocysteine was elevated in all. The MMACHC c.271dupA variant was common; a novel variant in the MTRR gene was identified. All received parenteral hydroxocobalamin, betaine, and carnitine with partial biochemical improvement. The newborn-screened patient had a favorable outcome. CONCLUSION: This cohort broadens the phenotypic spectrum to endocrine, renal, and psychiatric manifestations. Early diagnosis and hydroxocobalamin treatment improve outcomes, though neurocognitive and ophthalmologic complications persist.
Glymphatic system dysfunction in anti-NMDAR encephalitis: a comparative DTI-ALPS study with healthy controls and epilepsy patients.推荐
PMID 43052821 · BMC neurology · 2026 08 25
作者：Xu Z, Wang D, Zhou F, Zhong J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Anti-N-methyl-D-aspartate receptor (NMDAR) encephalitis is characterized by pathogenic autoantibody-mediated synaptic dysfunction, yet the mechanisms facilitating antibody access to the brain parenchyma remain incompletely understood. The glymphatic system, which supports cerebrospinal fluid-interstitial fluid exchange along perivascular spaces, has been implicated in immune surveillance and waste clearance; however, its involvement in anti-NMDAR encephalitis has not been fully investigated in vivo. METHODS: Glymphatic system-related function was assessed using diffusion tensor imaging along the perivascular space (DTI-ALPS) in 15 patients with anti-NMDAR encephalitis, 17 patients with epilepsy, and 18 healthy controls. Group differences in ALPS-index values were analyzed with adjustment for age and sex. Associations between ALPS-indexes, clinical severity, and regional brain volumes were further examined. RESULTS: ALPS-index values were significantly reduced in both anti-NMDAR encephalitis and epilepsy compared with healthy controls. Across all groups, ALPS-indexes demonstrated strong bilateral correlations, with consistently lower values in the right hemisphere. In anti-NMDAR encephalitis, lower ALPS-index values were associated with longer hospitalization and higher modified Rankin Scale scores, whereas no associations were observed with cerebrospinal fluid anti-NMDAR antibody titers. In epilepsy, ALPS-indexes showed a distinct pattern of clinical and structural correlations, including associations with regional brain volumes that were absent in the NMDAR group. Receiver operating characteristic analysis indicated moderate-to-good discriminative performance of ALPS-indexes for both disease groups. CONCLUSION: DTI-ALPS reveals altered perivascular water diffusivity in anti-NMDAR encephalitis, which correlates with clinical severity independently of antibody titers. These findings suggest glymphatic-related dysfunction in anti-NMDAR encephalitis, and indicate that the ALPS-index captures disease-relevant pathophysiological processes beyond antibody burden, with potential utility as a noninvasive imaging marker for clinical assessment and prognosis in immune-mediated encephalitis.
Ephaptic coupling between neurons: a systematic review of mathematical models.推荐
PMID 43052716 · BMC bioinformatics · 2026 08 25
作者：Kondrakhin PY, Dubrovin SV, Chupov EA, Kolpakov FA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Ephaptic coupling is a relatively underexplored mechanism of neuronal interaction mediated by perturbations of the extracellular electric potential without the formation of conventional chemical or electrical synapses. Although the first theoretical accounts of ephaptic interactions appeared as early as the 1940s, computational approaches to this phenomenon have been actively developed only since the late 1990s and early 2000s, based on classical models of neuronal activity and cable theory. Both simplified phenomenological and biophysically detailed models have been developed. The former capture only essential features of large-scale neuronal dynamics and offer high computational efficiency, whereas the latter incorporate more complex characteristics, such as the properties of myelinated and unmyelinated fibers, the spatial distribution of nodes of Ranvier, axonal diameter heterogeneity, and the dynamics of extracellular ion concentrations. Recent studies highlight a growing interest in ephaptic interactions, particularly in their potential roles in neuronal synchronization, sensory coding, the propagation of epileptic seizures, and the development of neurodegenerative as well as demyelinating disorders. OBJECTIVE AND METHODS: This work presents the systematic review dedicated to mathematical models of ephaptic coupling. The review aims to consolidate the diverse computational approaches used to study ephaptic coupling and to outline the main mechanisms of this phenomenon explored in modeling studies. The reviewed models were classified by type, tested hypotheses, and availability of open-source implementations. RESULTS: We surveyed nearly seventy mathematical models of ephaptic coupling. The review highlights how ephaptic interactions may contribute not only to normal brain function-such as synchronization and sensory processing-but also to pathological conditions, including epilepsy, demyelination, neuropathic pain, and neurodegeneration. By bringing together disparate studies, we aim to make the modeling of ephaptic coupling comprehensible to a broad scientific audience, including researchers with little prior experience in this area, stimulate further integration of computational and experimental approaches, and promote investigation of ephaptic mechanisms in both health and disease. CONCLUSION: This review serves both as a reference point for researchers developing new computational models and as a resource for those seeking to understand how ephaptic interactions may shape neuronal dynamics.
Development and Validation of an Early Clinical Prediction Model for Post-Encephalitic Epilepsy in Children With Viral Encephalitis: A Single-Center Retrospective Cohort Study.推荐
PMID 43052691 · Clinical pediatrics · 2026 11 18
作者：Xu B, Xie J, Liu J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Post-encephalitic epilepsy (PEE) is a serious long-term sequela of pediatric viral encephalitis, yet early risk stratification tools are lacking. In this single-center retrospective cohort study of 206 children with viral encephalitis, 43 (20.87%) developed PEE during follow-up. A multivariable logistic regression model incorporating 4 early clinical predictors-acute seizure count, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) white blood cell count, consciousness disturbance, and cortical involvement-demonstrated good discrimination and acceptable calibration. Decision curve analysis showed higher net benefit than treat-all and treat-none strategies across threshold probabilities of approximately 0.06 to 0.99. Sensitivity analyses, including false-discovery rate correction, adjustment for follow-up duration, pathogen-stratified analyses, and a time-to-event (Cox) model, did not materially alter the principal findings. This model, built from routinely available variables, showed promise for early identification of children at elevated risk for PEE in internal validation. External validation in independent multicenter cohorts is warranted before clinical implementation.
On-Premises Open-Weight Large Language Models for Classifying Epilepsy Brain MRI Reports: A Retrospective Study.推荐
PMID 43052637 · Journal of imaging informatics in medicine · 2026 11 18
作者：Kang SM, Kim S, Choi Y, Koo YS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Free-text reports limit the use of imaging data for research cohort screening. This study evaluated whether locally deployed open-weight large language models (LLMs) can classify brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reports in epilepsy without external data transfer, and which forms of domain knowledge improve classification. In this single-center retrospective study, 1210 epilepsy-protocol brain MRI reports were labeled by a board-certified neuroradiologist for abnormality status and primary imaging finding (eight classes). A held-out test set of 206 reports was classified by a biomedical (OpenBioLLM-8B) and a general-purpose (Qwen3.5-9B) LLM on a single 24-GB graphics processing unit, with and without a rule set and example retrieval, and compared with a fine-tuned radiology-specific BERT model, an independent neuroradiology fellow, and a same-base control model. The primary outcome was the macro-averaged F1 score for the primary finding. Without task-specific training, the general-purpose model achieved a finding macro-F1 of 0.63 (95% confidence interval (CI), 0.53-0.70), comparable to the fine-tuned BERT model (0.59; difference, - 0.04; 95% CI, - 0.13 to 0.07) and to inter-reader agreement (0.61; κ = 0.59). A rule set stating institutional category definitions improved the biomedical model (difference, 0.23; 95% CI, 0.11-0.33) without significantly reducing performance in any model, whereas biomedical fine-tuning conferred no advantage over the control model and example retrieval did not help. Residual errors concentrated where the readers disagreed. Locally deployed LLMs performed comparably to inter-reader agreement, which may support human-verified cohort screening; in this institution-specific task, explicit category definitions were more useful than biomedical specialization.
Neurological manifestations in pediatric COVID-19: a comparative single-center study.推荐
PMID 43052557 · Acta neurologica Belgica · 2026 11 19
作者：Ibrahim H, Mansour M, Ahmed AR, Rushdy M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To compare clinical characteristics, investigations, management, and outcomes of hospitalized children with SARS-CoV-2 infection with and without neurological involvement, and to determine whether neurological disease reflects systemic severity or a distinct clinical pattern. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective observational study of children aged 1 month-16 years with laboratory-confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection admitted to a tertiary pediatric center between June 2022 and June 2023. Patients were divided into those with neurological manifestations (ND group, n = 24) and those without neurological involvement (non-ND group, n = 23). Demographic features, clinical presentation, laboratory and neuroimaging findings, treatment, and outcomes were compared. RESULTS: Children with neurological involvement were significantly older (median 5.75 vs. 3.00 years, p = 0.022) and less likely to present with fever or systemic inflammatory features. They demonstrated lower inflammatory markers and significantly less cardiac involvement, including dilated cardiomyopathy. Common neurological presentations included encephalopathy (50%), headache (45.8%), and seizures (20.8%). 37% met MIS-C criteria. Neuroimaging revealed lacunar infarctions, demyelinating white matter lesions, longitudinally extensive transverse myelitis, and cauda equina nerve root enhancement. Despite similar mortality rates, children with neurological involvement had a significantly higher rate of incomplete recovery, with more than a quarter experiencing persistent neurological sequelae (29.2% vs. 4.3%, p = 0.048). CONCLUSION: Neurological manifestations of pediatric COVID-19 represent a clinically distinct pattern characterized by limited systemic inflammation but an increased risk of persistent neurological sequelae. Early neurological evaluation, appropriate neuroimaging, and structured long-term follow-up are warranted even in the absence of severe systemic illness.
IMPACT: an open-source workflow for unified variant interpretation using phenotype-driven filtering.浏览
PMID 43052306 · NPJ genomic medicine · 2026 08 02
作者：Boehler NA, Cheng HM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Comprehensive interpretation of whole-genome sequencing data for phenotypes with complex genetic architecture requires integrating diverse variant classes, but most workflows remain focused on single-nucleotide changes and small indels. We present IMPACT, an open-source, phenotype-configurable pipeline that harmonizes preprocessing, applies variant-specific annotation, and consolidates single-nucleotide variants, indels, structural variants, and copy-number variants within an interactive R Shiny interface. Applied to the UK Biobank cohorts with deafness (n = 126) and epilepsy (n = 41), IMPACT prioritized 565 and 126 variants, respectively. Of these, 512 and 103 were non-incidental, phenotype-relevant candidates, which were subsequently reviewed using ACMG-aligned evidence and classified as pathogenic or candidate variants of uncertain significance where supported. All deafness participants and 87.8% of epilepsy participants carried at least one candidate variant, with configurations ranging from single high-impact variants to compound heterozygotes spanning variant classes. By coupling phenotype-aware filtering with cross-type visualization, IMPACT addresses critical gaps in genome interpretation and offers a scalable foundation for research-driven curation and precision medicine.
Differential Expression and Therapeutic Targeting of K+ Channels in Neurological Disorders: An Integrative Approach.推荐
PMID 43052235 · Omics : a journal of integrative biology · 2026 11 18
作者：Maqsood L, Azeem F, Zubair M, Azmat S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Potassium (K+) channels regulate cellular homeostasis and electrical signaling, particularly in the nervous system, and their dysfunction is linked to neurological disorders such as epilepsy, Alzheimer's disease (AD), and Parkinson's disease (PD). In this study, Gene Expression Omnibus (GEO) datasets (GSE252132, GSE190451, and GSE160299) were analyzed using GEO2R to identify differentially expressed K+ channel genes. A total of 2741, 3681, and 861 differentially expressed genes were identified in AD, epilepsy, and PD, respectively, including the common K+ channel genes KCNJ5, KCNJ6, and KCNJ12. Functional enrichment using Enrichr highlighted potassium ion transport, potassium channel complexes, voltage-gated potassium channel activity, and gonadotropin-releasing hormone secretion. Virtual screening of 2315 Food and Drug Administration-approved drugs and 2390 Traditional Chinese Medicine compounds identified Sorafenib, Hinokiflavone, Posaconazole, and 1,3,6-Tri-O-galloyl-beta-D-glucose as promising KCNJ6-binding candidates. These findings highlight K+ channels, particularly KCNJ6, as biomarkers and therapeutic targets in neurological disorders.

摘要：A 73-year-old man with conservatively managed bilateral chronic subdural hematomas presented with fever and vomiting, followed by a seizure and impaired consciousness. 摘要：Psychosis in children and adolescents caused by medical illnesses/conditions is rare. Visual hallucinations, confusion, and change of baseline mentation support this diagnosis. Diagnosis and genetic analysis of a case of glycosylphosphatidylinositol biosynthesis deficiency 15 caused by compound heterozygous mutations of GPAA1.推荐

作者：Zhong WJ, Chen YJ, Liu JN, Xiong F et al.

摘要：Glycosylphosphatidylinositol biosynthesis deficiency 15 (GPIBD15, MIM: 617810) is an autosomal recessive inherited metabolic disorder caused by mutations in the GPAA1 gene. It is primarily characterized by global developmental delay, intellectual disability, early-onset epileptic seizures, hypotonia, and cerebellar atrophy. In this study, we reported a child diagnosed with GPIBD15 whose main clinical manifestations included dysplasia and cerebral dysfunction. We performed whole-exome sequencing on the patient and his parents. The patient was found to harbor compound heterozygous mutations c.1288C>T(p.P430S) and c.1621C>T(p.R541W) in the GPAA1 gene. The c.1288C>T(p.P430S) variant was inherited from the father, while the c.1621C>T(p.R541W) variant was inherited from the mother. Pathogenicity prediction, evolutionary conservation analysis and protein structure prediction analysis of these mutation sites showed that the variants are deleterious and located at highly conserved residues. These mutations lead to obvious changes in the secondary and tertiary structures of the protein, suggesting that they may affect protein function. To further investigate their functional impact, we constructed plasmid expression vectors harboring the c.1288C>T(p.P430S) and c.1621C> T(p.R541W) mutations and transfected them into HEK293T cells. We observed that co-transfection of the two mutant constructs significantly increased GPAA1 mRNA expression levels, while the corresponding protein expression levels were markedly decreased. Subcellular localization analysis showed no apparent difference between the wild-type and mutant proteins. In conclusion, we report a case of GPIBD15 caused by two novel compound heterozygous mutations in the GPAA1 gene. The pathogenic mechanism may be associated with mutation-induced structural alterations and reduced protein expression. Our findings expand the mutational spectrum of GPAA1 and provide valuable insights for early diagnosis and genetic counseling of patients with GPIBD15.
Dravet syndrome: clinical features across the lifespan, molecular findings, and therapeutic approaches.推荐
PMID 43051189 · The Lancet. Neurology · 2026 Dec
作者：Scheffer IE
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：Dravet syndrome is a prototypical genetic developmental and epileptic encephalopathy, caused by pathogenic variants in the sodium channel alpha-1 subunit gene (SCN1A) in 95% of individuals. The classic presentation of Dravet syndrome is a baby aged about 6 months, with typical development, who presents with a prolonged febrile generalised tonic-clonic or hemiclonic seizure, and frequent seizures ensue. Individuals with Dravet syndrome usually develop multiple seizure types alongside developmental slowing, and sometimes regression, and a range of morbidities emerge. Dravet syndrome is associated with a poor prognosis and a high risk of mortality. De novo SCN1A pathogenic variants typically cause Dravet syndrome, but parental mosaicism might occur, with implications for reproductive counselling. Early diagnosis is essential to guide prompt, optimal selection of antiseizure medications to control seizures and improve long-term outcomes. Natural history studies are crucial for interpreting the outcomes of trials of novel therapies. Newer antiseizure medications show improved efficacy in controlling seizures, often associated with developmental gains. Pivotal antisense oligonucleotide and genetic therapy trials show promise to transform this devastating disease.
Epigenetic control of neural homeostasis by REST.推荐
PMID 43051157 · Cell chemical biology · 2026 11 18
作者：Baldelli P, Valente P
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Neural network stability relies on homeostatic plasticity mechanisms acting across intrinsic, synaptic, and glial levels to counter persistent deviations in activity. In hyperexcitable or chronically stressed brain states, including epilepsy, neuroinflammation, ageing, and early Alzheimer's disease, sustained stress can destabilize these compensatory programs and network function. Alongside its established roles in neuronal differentiation and neurological disease when dysregulated, repressor element-1 silencing transcription (REST), also known as neuron-restrictive silencer factor (NRSF) is emerging as a multilevel, long-timescale epigenetic hub linking neuronal activity to coordinated homeostatic transcriptional programs. This review examines this less explored homeostatic dimension of REST biology. Rather than acting as a binary repressor, REST functions as a tunable chromatin-regulatory platform whose output depends on RE1 architecture, chromatin context, cellular state, and activation dynamics. We discuss how REST-dependent programs regulate intrinsic homeostasis, excitatory and inhibitory synaptic scaling, and astrocyte-mediated homeostatic support. Finally, we propose that neuronal energy metabolism may bias REST signaling toward adaptive compensation rather than maladaptive repression.
Beyond sensitivity: Performance, methodological heterogeneity, and real-time translation of wrist-worn convulsive seizure detection devices - A systematic review and meta-analysis.推荐
PMID 43051057 · Seizure · 2026 11 16
作者：Fouda MA, Freglette CA, Bender HA, Still M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q3
摘要：Wrist-worn seizure-detection devices can detect convulsive seizures, but pooled performance estimates may obscure methodological differences between studies. We evaluated the performance, heterogeneity, and translational readiness of wrist-worn non-EEG systems for automated detection of convulsive and generalized tonic-clonic (GTC) seizures. We systematically searched MEDLINE/PubMed, Embase, Scopus, Web of Science, and Google Scholar. Event-level sensitivity for convulsive and GTC seizures was pooled using random-effects generalized linear mixed models; false-alarm rates (FARs) were pooled as incidence rates per 24 h. We evaluated heterogeneity, prespecified subgroups, methodological variability, detection latency, and progression to real-time intervention. Twenty studies comprising 1598 participants and 71,310 monitoring hours were included. Pooled random-effects sensitivity was approximately 93% for convulsive seizures and 96% for GTC seizures, with heterogeneity exceeding 75% for both outcomes. The pooled FAR was approximately 0.39/24 h, with heterogeneity approaching 99%. Neither sensing modality, study phase, population, algorithm locking, nor offline versus real-time processing explained performance differences under random-effects modeling. Methodological variability extended to seizure eligibility, amplitude and duration thresholds, temporal windowing, classifier architecture, operating-point selection, validation strategy, and FAR denominator construction. Mean detection latency was 30.04 s across nine reporting studies. Only one study directly evaluated translation from detection to real-time caregiver notification and intervention. Wrist-worn devices demonstrate high sensitivity for convulsive and GTC seizure detection, but performance is not yet methodologically uniform or consistently generalizable. Standardized evaluation and independent validation are needed, with future studies extending beyond detection accuracy to reliable real-time alert delivery and actionable intervention.
Intranasal dexmedetomidine sedation in pediatric electroencephalography: dose optimization, safety, and EEG characteristics.推荐
PMID 43051056 · Seizure · 2026 10 27
作者：Yun HJ, Youn I, Lee NY, Kook M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q3
摘要：PURPOSE: To evaluate the efficacy and safety of intranasal dexmedetomidine (DEX) for sedation during pediatric EEG and to explore whether EEG spectral characteristics under DEX differ from those under chloral hydrate. METHODS: In this retrospective cohort study, 224 children (0-18 years) who underwent EEG under intranasal DEX sedation at a single tertiary center (January 2024-April 2025) were analyzed. Patients were stratified into weight-based dosing (Group 1, <50 kg; median 2 μg/kg) and fixed dosing (Group 2, ≥50 kg; median 100 μg). Sedation success, onset time, total sedation time, and adverse events were assessed. In a within-subject subgroup of 11 patients with a prior chloral hydrate EEG, relative power spectral density (rPSD) in the delta, theta, alpha, and beta bands during N1 and N2 sleep was compared using patient-level paired analyses, with mean paired differences, 95% confidence intervals (CIs), and correction for multiple comparisons. RESULTS: Initial sedation success was 94.6% in Group 1 and 94.7% in Group 2; all patients achieved adequate sedation after rescue dosing. Median onset time was 10 minutes in both groups. Adverse events were mild and self-limited (3.2% and 2.6%). No statistically significant differences in rPSD between DEX and chloral hydrate were detected in either sleep stage after correction for multiple comparisons; however, the CIs were wide, and equivalence could not be established. Interictal epileptiform discharges were visually unchanged. CONCLUSION: Intranasal DEX is an effective, well-tolerated sedative for pediatric EEG, and a weight-stratified dosing strategy appears practical. In a small paired subgroup, no significant spectral differences from chloral hydrate were detected; these preliminary quantitative findings warrant confirmation in larger studies.
Perceived stigma, social support, and associated factors among adults with epilepsy: A cross-sectional study.推荐

作者：Havlioglu S, Ersin F, Demir TG
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜BEHAVIORAL SCIENCES：Q2；CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3；PSYCHIATRY：Q3
摘要：Epilepsy is frequently associated with perceived stigma and inadequate social support, both of which adversely affect patients' psychosocial well-being and quality of life. This study aimed to investigate perceived stigma, perceived social support, the relationship between these variables, and factors associated with both among adults with epilepsy. A descriptive, cross-sectional, correlational study was conducted with 147 adults diagnosed with epilepsy who attended the Neurology Outpatient Clinic of Harran University Hospital between June 2025-June 2026. Data were collected using a sociodemographic questionnaire, the Perceived Stigma Scale, and the Multidimensional Scale of Perceived Social Support. The mean perceived stigma score was 26.58 ± 9.34, while the mean total perceived social support score was 51.91 ± 18.87. Higher perceived stigma was significantly associated with lower educational level, unemployment, work impairment due to epilepsy, lower income, the presence of another chronic disease, concealing the epilepsy diagnosis, and perceiving that others avoided them because of epilepsy (p < 0.05). Higher educational level and not perceiving social rejection were associated with greater perceived social support (p < 0.05). Perceived stigma demonstrated a significant weak negative correlation with total perceived social support (r = -0.405, p = 0.001). These findings highlight the importance of considering stigma and social support in the psychosocial care of people with epilepsy. Healthcare professionals should assess these needs and facilitate access to appropriate psychosocial resources, including peer support and self-management programmes. In addition, public education initiatives and targeted support programmes may help address misconceptions about epilepsy, reduce stigma, and enhance social acceptance.
Towards a national drug checking database to monitor the drug supply and prevent drug-related harms: A narrative review.浏览
PMID 43050841 · Drug and alcohol dependence · 2026 11 07
作者：Park JN, Figgatt M, Carroll JJ, Davis CS et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: The changing drug supply poses challenges for preventing drug-related harms. Timely drug supply information is critical for public health and clinical responses, yet existing surveillance systems lack the nuance and speed to detect emerging trends. Drug checking services may help address these gaps by enhancing monitoring of drug supplies, informing public health and clinical interventions, and supporting evidence-based policymaking. This review examines the evolution of drug checking in the United States, evaluates the strengths and limitations of these data relative to existing surveillance systems, and provides recommendations for integrating drug checking into national drug surveillance efforts. METHODS: We conducted a narrative review to synthesize the current evidence on drug checking with an emphasis on the U.S. CONTEXT: Findings from published studies were supplemented by the expertise of subject matter experts actively engaged in drug checking research, practice, and policy. RESULTS: Compared to traditional drug surveillance, drug checking data offer several advantages, including face validity (representing drugs at the level of consumption rather than from upstream seizures), timeliness (days to weeks rather than months to years), breadth (inclusion of novel psychoactive substances) and depth (inclusion of contextual cues). Four recommendations emerged from the review: expanding sustainable funding; advancing drug checking technologies; strengthening legal protections for programs and clients; and establishing data governance and stewardship frameworks to support data sharing, quality, and privacy. CONCLUSIONS: Greater investment in drug checking services, supported by coordinated data systems and enabling policies, could strengthen surveillance and advance efforts to prevent and reduce drug-related harms.
Preferential responses to spectrally degraded sentences in the human cortex revealed by intracranial electrophysiology.推荐
PMID 43050754 · Hearing research · 2026 11 12
作者：Nourski KV, Steinschneider M, Rhone AE, Billig AJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Despite progress in cochlear implant (CI) technology, there is considerable variability in outcomes, in part attributed to the function of central auditory pathways. Spectrally degraded sounds presented to normal-hearing listeners can be used to approximate cortical sound processing by CI users. Prior intracranial electroencephalography (iEEG) work identified "clear-preferred" and less common "vocoded-preferred" differential responses to clear and noise-vocoded non-word speech tokens. Better identification of vocoded consonants was associated with activation of the cortical dorsal auditory stream. The present study further characterized these differential responses using sentence stimuli that are expected to engage the ventral processing stream. Participants were neurosurgical epilepsy patients undergoing iEEG monitoring. Sentences were degraded using a 3-band noise vocoder. Analysis of iEEG data focused on event-related band power (ERBP) between 4-150 Hz. Differential responses (ERBP augmentation or suppression) to clear and vocoded sentences were observed both in early and late portions of the sentence, suggesting a sustained, rather than transient, nature of the preferential responses. Clear-preferred responses are likely driven by spectral complexity and intelligibility. Vocoded-preferred responses may reflect increased processing demands associated with challenging listening conditions. These responses occurred primarily in core auditory cortex and regions along the dorsal auditory stream but were virtually absent in prefrontal cortex. Identifying regions involved in the processing of degraded speech has potential clinical utility and may present candidate targets for neuromodulation-based CI rehabilitation strategies.

摘要：OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study is to determine the coexistence of elevated depressive symptoms and epilepsy in children and adolescents and to ascertain if the symptoms are related to a specific localization. METHODS: Patients between ages 9-14 with temporal lobe epilepsy (TLE), extratemporal lobe epilepsy (ETLE), and generalized epilepsy were followed up, and healthy siblings of these patients in the same age group as the control group were included in the study. RESULTS: The average age of the patients was 12.2 ± 1.8, 50.6% of them were female. Prospective multicenter studies are warranted to clarify the relationship between epileptic focus localization and elevated depressive symptoms.
