摘要：A 33-year-old woman with a medical history of polycystic ovary syndrome on combined oral contraceptive pills, insulin resistance, and bilateral silicone breast implants (SBIs) placed 10 years prior presented with a 2-month history of intermittent myoclonic jerks involving the head, trunk, and lower limbs; polyarthralgia; and cognitive and functional decline (compared with being previously independent in her activities of daily living). Oligonucleotide-mediated AASS silencing as a potential therapeutic strategy for ALDH7A1 deficiency and glutaric aciduria type I.浏览
PMID 43050502 · Science translational medicine · 2026 11 18
作者：Vootukuri RS, Dyjach K, Khalil Y, Toporowska J et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

摘要：α-Aminoadipic semialdehyde synthase (AASS) catalyzes the first two steps of lysine catabolism. Inherited metabolic disorders affecting enzymes downstream of AASS, including α-aminoadipic semialdehyde dehydrogenase (ALDH7A1) deficiency, which causes pyridoxine-dependent epilepsy, and glutaric aciduria type I, cause accumulation of potentially neurotoxic lysine-derived metabolites. Current therapies incompletely prevent metabolite accumulation and neurological sequelae. We evaluated whether hepatic AASS silencing could reduce conversion of lysine into downstream metabolites. Antisense oligonucleotides and small interfering RNAs targeting AASS reduced disease-associated metabolites in patient-derived cells from individuals with ALDH7A1 deficiency and glutaric aciduria type I. These findings support further evaluation of oligonucleotide-mediated AASS silencing, including liver-directed delivery, as a substrate reduction strategy for ALDH7A1 deficiency and glutaric aciduria type I.
Duration and intensity of chronic treadmill exercise differentially modulate hippocampal neuronal apoptosis in PTZ-kindled rats.推荐
PMID 43050302 · Science progress · 2026 11 18
作者：Kayacan Y, Cerit G, Kabak YB, İnal S et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：ObjectiveThe objective of this study was to evaluate how chronic treadmill exercise-varying in intensity and duration-influences hippocampal neuronal apoptosis within a pentylenetetrazol (PTZ)-induced kindling model.MethodsA total of 96 male Wistar rats were divided into eight experimental cohorts: a control group (C), a PTZ-only group (PC), and groups subjected to short-term (SE), moderate (ME), and high-intensity (HE) exercise, both with and without PTZ administration (PSE, PME, and PHE, respectively). Kindling was induced by PTZ (35 mg per kg, i.p., three days per week). The exercise groups were subject to specific treadmill protocols according to their intensity. Neuronal apoptosis in the hippocampus was analysed by immunohistochemistry for Bcl-2, Bax and Caspase-3 and by TUNEL staining.ResultsNo significant statistical difference in anti-apoptotic Bcl-2 or Bax levels was observed between the treatment groups (P>0.05). However, there were significant differences in the scores for Caspase-3 (P <0.001) and TUNEL (P = 0.001). Low-intensity exercise (PSE) did not significantly induce activation of Caspase-3 compared with control. Conversely, moderate (PME) and high (PHE) exercise significantly increased the number of apoptotic cells in the hippocampus. In particular, the PHE group had the highest Caspase-3 activity (10.3 ± 3.61) while the PME group had the highest TUNEL positivity (15.50 ± 22.1).ConclusionsIn this PTZ-induced rat model, hippocampal apoptosis-related markers differed across exercise conditions. Moderate- and high-intensity exercise combined with PTZ were associated with increased Caspase-3 activity and TUNEL positivity, whereas the PSE group showed no detectable Caspase-3 activity. These findings require cautious interpretation because of the experimental animal model and the study's methodological limitations. Further research is needed to clarify the effects of exercise intensity on neuronal apoptosis in epilepsy.
Prevalence of Chronic Traumatic Encephalopathy Without Contact Sport Repetitive Head Impact Exposure: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis.推荐
PMID 43050191 · JAMA network open · 2026 11 02
作者：Mastrodicasa MJ, Olson GH, Guo RC, Luster CB et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：Meta分析
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：IMPORTANCE: A pooled baseline prevalence of chronic traumatic encephalopathy (CTE) in populations without repetitive head impact (RHI) exposure is needed to contextualize prevalence in other cohorts. OBJECTIVE: To estimate the pooled prevalence of CTE in populations without documented contact sport-based RHI exposure. DATA SOURCES: PubMed and Embase were searched from inception through February 23, 2026, using terms for CTE, prevalence, epidemiology, and neuropathology. STUDY SELECTION: Studies reporting CTE prevalence in cohorts without documented contact sport RHI history were included and classified by ascertainment rigor into a primary group with verified absence of contact sport RHI or a secondary unscreened reference cohort. Studies were excluded for cohort overlap or for RHI-linked comorbid conditions (eg, epilepsy). DATA EXTRACTION AND SYNTHESIS: Data were independently extracted by 2 authors, with discrepancy resolved by a third. Risk of bias and quality were assessed using the Joanna Briggs Institute checklist. Pooled prevalence was calculated using a random-effects model with restricted maximum likelihood estimation. Heterogeneity (I2 and τ2) and robustness (leave-one-out sensitivity analysis) were assessed. MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: Confirmed CTE in individuals without documented contact sport RHI history was estimated using pooled prevalence and 95% CIs. RESULTS: Twelve cohort studies (4421 individuals; weighted mean age at death, 75.1 years; 2481 male [56.1%]) were identified. In the primary analysis of 7 cohorts with verified absence of contact sport RHI (1604 individuals), 12 cases of CTE were identified (crude prevalence, 0.75% [95% CI, 0.43%-1.30%]); pooled prevalence was 1.06% (95% CI, 0.10%-10.79%), reflecting substantial heterogeneity (I2 = 87.0%) and limited precision from few cases and presence of an outlier with 5 of 7 individuals with CTE. Excluding the outlier yielded a pooled prevalence of 0.70% (95% CI, 0.28%-1.74%) and reduced heterogeneity (I2 = 6.6%). In the 5 unscreened reference cohorts (2817 individuals), 17 cases of CTE were identified (crude prevalence, 0.60% [95% CI, 0.38%-0.96%]). Across all 12 cohorts, the crude prevalence was 0.66% (95% CI, 0.46%-0.94%), and the pooled prevalence was 0.85% (95% CI, 0.24%-2.89%). CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: This systematic review and meta-analysis of 12 cohort studies not selected for contact sport-based RHI exposure found that CTE pathology was rare, with a baseline prevalence of approximately 1%. These findings provide a reference point for interpreting CTE prevalence in other populations, including cohorts with contact sport exposure.
Longitudinal quantitative MRI feature analysis in paediatric focal cortical dysplasia: a retrospective longitudinal cohort study.推荐
PMID 43049883 · Neuroradiology · 2026 11 18
作者：Burr P, Ripart M, Adler S, Piper RJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：PURPOSE: Focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) is a common cause of treatment-resistant epilepsy and can be difficult to detect on MRI. Although traditionally considered static, lesion visibility may vary across scans. We assessed whether MELD-derived lesional imaging features differ across serial MRI in histologically confirmed paediatric FCD type II. METHODS: Twenty-one patients with histopathologically confirmed FCD type II underwent paired T1-weighted MRI; 13 also had paired FLAIR imaging. Images were processed using FastSurfer and a modified MELD feature-extraction pipeline. Lesion-mask feature values were compared with anatomically matched contralateral regions, and time-corrected longitudinal lesion-contralateral differences were calculated. Blinded qualitative neuroradiological review was available for 20/21 subjects. RESULTS: FCD lesions demonstrated quantitative feature differences from contralateral cortex. In the FLAIR cohort, longitudinal lesion-contralateral differences were consistently positive across sampled grey and white matter depths and remained significant after false discovery rate correction (mean paired differences 0.607-0.737; all pFDR = 0.009). Protocol-restricted FLAIR sensitivity analyses preserved the direction of effect, although restricted subgroups were underpowered. In T1-weighted MRI, longitudinal lesion-contralateral differences were positive across grey matter intensity features, greatest at 75% cortical thickness (mean paired difference 0.185, 95% CI 0.060-0.309, p = 0.006), but no T1 feature survived false discovery rate correction. Neuroradiological review identified possible visually appreciable interval change in a minority of cases, with most apparent change attributed to acquisition-related variation or artefact. CONCLUSION: Quantitative MRI-derived FCD features can vary across serial paediatric MRI. These findings demonstrate longitudinal imaging feature variability, most consistently in FLAIR-derived measures, with implications for repeat imaging and machine learning derived lesion detection.
Influenza A-Associated Encephalitis and Encephalopathy in Adults: An Exploratory Criteria-Based Analysis.推荐
PMID 43049832 · Neurology and therapy · 2026 11 18
作者：Gómez-Porro Sánchez P, Trebolle Banet J, de la Puente Garrido B, Ruiz Molina Á et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Influenza A can cause encephalopathy or encephalitis, yet adult data are scarce and the two entities are seldom distinguished systematically. This small retrospective study of five adults explored the feasibility and limitations of classifying these complications with the International Encephalitis Consortium (IEC) 2013 criteria and describes their clinical, radiological, cerebrospinal fluid (CSF), and treatment characteristics. METHODS: We retrospectively reviewed five consecutive adults admitted to a tertiary center with polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-confirmed influenza A and acute neurological symptoms, all evaluated with brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), lumbar puncture, and electroencephalography (EEG). Encephalitis was defined by the IEC 2013 criteria (altered mental status > 24 h plus ≥ 2 minor criteria of central nervous system (CNS) inflammation). Data were analyzed descriptively. RESULTS: Three patients met criteria for encephalitis and two for encephalopathy (four women; age 44-68 years). Respiratory influenza A PCR was positive and CSF PCR negative in all. CSF pleocytosis occurred in two patients, bilateral thalamic and subcortical white matter MRI hyperintensities in two, generalized EEG slowing in four, and focal status epilepticus in one. All received oseltamivir; three received high-dose methylprednisolone, two of whom also required intravenous immunoglobulin and plasma exchange. The categories overlapped substantially: EEG slowing and negative CSF PCR occurred in both, and treatment escalation followed clinical course rather than category, with the most intensive regimen required by one patient in each. At 3 months, three patients were asymptomatic and two, both with encephalitis and structural MRI lesions, had persistent deficits. CONCLUSION: In this small, hypothesis-generating study, IEC-based classification was feasible, but encephalitis and encephalopathy overlapped substantially and the distinction did not determine treatment. The main contribution lies in illustrating both the feasibility and limitations of criteria-based classification in adults with influenza A, rather than in establishing its clinical utility.
Rasmussen's encephalitis in Turner syndrome needing a functional hemispherotomy-coincidence or connection?推荐
PMID 43049779 · Child's nervous system : ChNS : official journal of the International Society for Pediatric Neurosurgery · 2026 11 18
作者：Malgaonkar S, Choudhary P, Vilanilam GC, Menon RN et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Rasmussen's encephalitis (RE) is a rare, chronic, immune-mediated epileptic encephalitis characterized by drug-resistant focal seizures, progressive unilateral cortical dysfunction, and hemispheric atrophy. Turner syndrome (TS), caused by complete or partial monosomy X, is associated with increased autoimmune susceptibility. The coexistence of RE and TS has not, to our knowledge, been previously reported; however, a single case can only raise the question of chance co-occurrence versus a biologically plausible immunogenetic connection. An eight-year-old girl with karyotype-proven TS presented with a two-year history of progressively worsening drug-resistant focal seizures. Serial MRI demonstrated progressive right hemispheric cortical atrophy, most pronounced in the frontal, insular, and mesial temporal regions, with right hemispheric hypoperfusion on arterial spin labelling. Video-EEG confirmed right frontocentral ictal onset without asynchronous contralateral onset. Despite immunotherapy, seizures persisted, and she underwent right functional hemispherotomy with seizure freedom at 8 months follow-up (Engel class I). We believe this to be the first reported coexistence of TS and RE. Although causality cannot be inferred from a single case, the observation supports discussion of X-linked immune dysregulation as a possible susceptibility factor in rare focal neuroinflammatory disease.
Cognition in older adults with epilepsy: A review of existing interventions and a proposed conceptual framework for integrated care.推荐
PMID 43049694 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 18
作者：Reyes A, Jhanji S, Sankari R, Saleh MA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：Older adults with epilepsy (OAE) represent one of the fastest growing yet least studied subgroups within the epilepsy population, encompassing both individuals with chronic epilepsy persisting into later life and those diagnosed with late onset epilepsy after ages 50-60 years. This population faces a convergence of risk; epilepsy is associated with substantial cognitive comorbidity, with up to 80% of OAE demonstrating cognitive impairment and more than half meeting criteria for mild cognitive impairment (MCI), alongside 1.8-4-fold increased odds of developing dementia. Cognitive dysfunction in OAE is typically multidomain, affecting memory, executive function, and language, and has been associated with demographic, lifestyle, epilepsy, medication, comorbidity, genetic, and neuroimaging factors. Despite this convergence of risk, we identified no adequately tested intervention specifically developed for OAE. This narrative review synthesizes evidence from two historically parallel literatures, epilepsy self-management/rehabilitation programs and cognitive interventions developed for individuals with MCI, to identify strategies that could be merged or adapted for OAE. Epilepsy-focused programs, including foundational self-management models and cognitive interventions, have shown improvements in self-efficacy, quality of life, and select cognitive outcomes, but were evaluated largely in younger and middle-aged adults and have not been developed for or tested in OAE. Similarly, MCI-focused compensatory, restorative, and multimodal interventions offer adaptable strategies for preserving cognitive function but do not account for the complexity of epilepsy management, including antiseizure medication effects, polypharmacy, and seizure-related cognitive fluctuations. Building on this synthesis, we propose a conceptual, not yet tested, integrated intervention framework for OAE that combines epilepsy self-management, comorbidity care, cognition-focused strategies, and lifestyle modification within a unified, multidomain model, alongside recommendations for implementation, outcome assessment, and future trial design. Advancing this field will require deliberate cross-disciplinary integration and a broader conceptualization of successful epilepsy care in later life, one that treats cognitive health as a central therapeutic target alongside seizure control.
Extreme delta brush as an ictal pattern in anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis.推荐
PMID 43049564 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 18
作者：Casson NE, Moser Filho HL, Van Winssen CM, Hirsch LJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：Anti-N-methyl-D-aspartate receptor (NMDAR) encephalitis frequently presents with psychiatric symptoms, seizures, dyskinesias, autonomic instability, and characteristic electroencephalographic (EEG) abnormalities including extreme delta brush (EDB). Differentiating epileptic from non-epileptic dyskinetic movements remains challenging, and the ictal versus interictal nature of EDB remains incompletely understood. In this case, a 22-year-old man developed neuropsychiatric symptoms progressing to hallucinations, confusion, abnormal speech, dyskinetic movements, and generalized tonic-clonic seizures. Brain MRI was unremarkable. Cerebrospinal fluid demonstrated positive anti-NMDAR antibodies. Continuous EEG initially showed diffuse slowing without correlation to dyskinetic movements of grimacing, jaw thrusting, and limb posturing. During treatment, abundant stereotyped episodes of facial pulling with rhythmic lip twitching emerged and were found on EEG to have an ictal correlate consistent with focal motor status epilepticus. Following reduction in ictal burden, EEG demonstrated a striking pattern of highly stereotyped right jaw movement and right arm internal rotation synchronized to complexes of midline beta-gamma activity overriding each delta wave peak, qualifying as a definite EDB pattern with clinical correlate to each EDB complex. Thus, in this case, EDB was an ictal pattern. This case illustrates the overlap between seizures and dyskinesias in anti-NMDAR encephalitis and highlights characteristic EEG findings including the EDB pattern. Unlike classic descriptions of EDB as a non-ictal EEG biomarker of severe anti-NMDAR encephalitis, this patient demonstrated clinical motor events time-locked to each EDB complex, supporting the possibility that EDB patterns represent ictal phenomena, at least in some cases such as this one. Continuous EEG monitoring is essential to distinguish electroclinical seizures from dyskinesias and may provide further insight into the potentially ictal significance of EDB.
Validation of drug-resistant epilepsy identification algorithms in US Veterans across diverse care settings.推荐
PMID 43049542 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 18
作者：Haneef Z, Nagabhirava RS, Hildebrand AD, Zhou R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Early recognition of drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE) is critical for timely referral to comprehensive epilepsy centers (CECs). Our prior pilot study in the Veterans Health Administration (VHA) demonstrated the feasibility of an algorithm-based approach to identify DRE within selected CECs. In this study, we validated and refined these algorithms in a broader, national cohort, including CEC and non-CEC care, to optimize algorithms for different care settings. METHODS: We conducted a multicenter, retrospective study of randomly sampled veterans with epilepsy on ≥2 antiseizure medications (ASMs) from 1999 to 2024. Chart review by 26 epilepsy providers rated DRE status. Ninety-six candidate algorithms using combinations of six criteria-number of ASMs, treatment duration, dose thresholds, pregabalin exclusion, intractable International Classification of Diseases (ICD) codes, and electroencephalography (EEG)/magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) utilization-were evaluated. Performance for DRE classification was evaluated among all veterans, and separately for CEC or non-CEC care. RESULTS: Among 970 veterans on ≥2 lifetime ASMs, 518 (53.4%) were rated as DRE, 363 (37.4%) as non-DRE, and 89 (9.2%) as undetermined. Veterans with DRE were younger (mean age = 68.9 vs. 72.0 years) and more frequently had at least one intractable ICD code (46.1% vs. 18.2%), EEG (77.8% vs. 66.7%), or MRI (78.0% vs. 65.8%) compared with non-DRE (all p-values <.001). Among all veterans, the best overall algorithm (area under the curve [AUC] = .701) identified DRE as either (1) exposure to 3+ ASMs or (2) exposure to two ASMs for at least 1 year, with the requirement of past EEG/MRI and intractable ICD code documentation. This algorithm was also highly effective among veterans who received CEC (AUC = .756) or only non-CEC care (AUC = .678). SIGNIFICANCE: This national validation study confirms that medication-based algorithms can identify DRE within VHA administrative data across care settings (CECs and non-CECs). This supports the implementation of a standardized DRE definition for quality improvement, surveillance, and referral optimization within the VHA.

摘要：Co-occurrence of primary brain tumors and schizophrenia presents diagnostic and therapeutic challenges. Electroconvulsive therapy (ECT) has historically been considered contraindicated in patients with intracranial lesions. A 27-year-old female with an 11-year history of schizophrenia presented with worsening psychosis, irritability, and aggression. She was previously diagnosed with diffuse midline glioma, which was inoperable and treated with temozolomide. Repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation with concomitant pharmacotherapy for addictive disorders: a review of current evidence.推荐
PMID 43049125 · Expert review of clinical pharmacology · 2026 11 18
作者：Wang VX, Le Foll B
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Substance use disorders (SUDs) impose a substantial public health burden. In addition to behavioral therapies, pharmacological options are available for some SUDs. Repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) is emerging as a therapeutic modality, but evidence on its benefits and tolerability when combined with pharmacotherapy remains limited. AREAS COVERED: This narrative review examines therapeutic rTMS alongside pharmacotherapy for addictive disorders. MEDLINE, Embase, PsycINFO, PubMed, and CENTRAL were searched from inception to 6 September 2026; PsycArticles, Web of Science, and Scopus were searched on 4 November 2026. Twenty-one studies, including 12 controlled comparisons, were synthesized descriptively. EXPERT OPINION: Integration into addiction services is promising but requires evidence of durable behavioral benefit. rTMS after medical stabilization during the transition from detoxification to rehabilitation, followed by continuing pharmacotherapy, warrants investigation. Future directions include optimizing medication regimens, stimulation protocols, maintenance, and targeting while evaluating substance use outcomes, treatment attendance, and safety to establish which combinations benefit which patients.

From sleep complaints to clinical phenotypes: A tiered assessment and integrated management framework for sleep comorbidities in Epilepsy-A narrative review.推荐
PMID 43048528 · Sage open chronic disease · 2026 10 27
作者：Ao WL, Liu Z, Luo AD, Wang J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Sleep disorders are among the most common but frequently underestimated comorbidities in patients with epilepsy, with major manifestations including insomnia, obstructive sleep apnea, excessive daytime sleepiness, restless legs syndrome, and periodic limb movements of sleep. A complex bidirectional interaction exists between epilepsy and sleep disorders: sleep deprivation, disrupted sleep architecture, and sleep-disordered breathing may increase seizure susceptibility, whereas nocturnal seizures, interictal epileptiform discharges, antiseizure medications, and mood comorbidities may further impair sleep continuity and daytime alertness. Although mechanistic links and individual sleep disorders have received increasing attention, a practical outpatient pathway that connects sleep-related complaints with screening, phenotype recognition, objective testing, and subsequent intervention remains insufficiently defined. From a clinical phenotyping perspective, this review proposes a complaint-based framework for the identification, tiered assessment, and integrated management of sleep comorbidities in epilepsy. The framework translates common sleep complaints into major actionable phenotypes, including insomnia, obstructive sleep apnea, restless legs syndrome/periodic limb movements of sleep, excessive daytime sleepiness, and overlap phenotypes, while incorporating life-course and syndrome-specific considerations for children and adolescents, young adults, middle-aged adults, patients with juvenile myoclonic epilepsy, and older adults with epilepsy. This review organizes dispersed evidence on sleep and epilepsy into a clinically oriented conceptual framework linking complaint-based entry, tiered assessment, phenotype-specific intervention, and follow-up reassessment. The framework is intended to facilitate recognition and structured clinical reasoning, while its feasibility and effects on patient outcomes require prospective evaluation.
IRF5 drives pro-inflammatory microglial polarization and aggravates brain injury after status epilepticus in experimental rats.推荐

摘要：OBJECTIVES: Status epilepticus (SE) can induce sustained neuroinflammation and microglia/macrophage activation, contributing to brain injury. METHODS: A lithium chloride-pilocarpine-induced SE model in postnatal day 20 rats and an LPS-stimulated primary rat microglial model were established, and IRF5 was silenced using siRNA. Non-ischemic cerebral enhancing (NICE) lesions after extracranial carotid stenting: A case report.推荐
PMID 43048245 · Therapeutic advances in neurological disorders · 2026 10 23
作者：von Itter J, von Itter MN, Härtel L, Pawlowski M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜2区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：Non-ischemic cerebral enhancing (NICE) lesions are exceptionally rare complications of neuroendovascular procedures and have been reported predominantly after intracranial interventions, with only one previous case following extracranial carotid stenting. We report a second such case. A woman in her mid-thirties with inactive Takayasu arteritis underwent long-segment extracranial stenting of asymptomatic stenoses of the left common (CCA) and internal carotid (ICA) arteries using three overlapping stents. Eighteen days after the procedure, she developed new-onset headache; MRI showed a small left parieto-occipital hemorrhage and multiple ipsilateral NICE lesions. Serial MRI over 10 months demonstrated asynchronous NICE lesion behavior, with simultaneous emergence, regression, and persistence of ipsilateral and contralateral foci. Their non-ischemic morphology and the absence of clinical, laboratory, CSF, or vascular imaging evidence of active vasculitis argued against a Takayasu-related origin. Despite fluctuating lesion burden, she developed no persistent neurological deficits and required no long-term immunosuppressive therapy. Mild headaches and a one-day cluster of focal sensory seizures occurred, with no recurrence under low-dose lamotrigine. This case provides further evidence that NICE lesions are not confined to intracranial procedures and may also follow extracranial carotid stenting, showing highly variable lesion dynamics. The continued appearance of new lesions up to 308 days after stenting suggests persistent microembolization or delayed inflammatory manifestation of initially occult embolic deposits rather than a purely one-time periprocedural event. The divergence between radiological lesion burden and clinical severity further suggests that management should be guided primarily by the clinical course and that conservative management may be appropriate in clinically stable patients.
From a surgeon's stroke to a third hemisphere-The auxilium architecture for brain restoration and extension.推荐
PMID 43048172 · Frontiers in human neuroscience · 2026 10 21
作者：Serruya MD
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：A surgeon was exercising on a stationary bicycle when he had a large ischemic left middle cerebral artery territory stroke, complicated by hemorrhagic transformation requiring hemicraniectomy and cranioplasty. He developed right-sided spastic hemiplegia and a mixed aphasia. This paper takes that case as its anchor to pose the question: beyond improving residual function, what would it mean to restore cognitive function that was lost so that the person fully returns to their pre-stroke baseline? Existing brain-computer interfaces rely on the person having an intact cortical substrate for intended movement and speech. Here we propose the Auxilium, a brain restoration architecture for the situation where that substrate is no longer present: an extracranial auxiliary brain system, agnostic to its substrate, that connects to a person's residual cortex through whatever input-output technology proves most appropriate, and that supplies, in parallel and in real time, new substrate, whether biological or synthetic. We address an assumption of brain-computer interface development, that throughput is the binding constraint. Linking a kHz brain to MHz digital does not impart the higher clock speed, because the limitation lies not in the port but in the architecture of the entire brain and body imparted by evolution. We posit that the substrate of canonical circuits and their integration areas is essential, and the Auxilium can be understood as restoring both connectivity (linkage between disconnected areas) and computation (information generation capability of a volume of neural tissue). We propose a near-term de-risking pipeline using stereoelectroencephalography during surgical epilepsy evaluation, comparing extracranial modules spanning threshold rules, PID controllers, expert systems, artificial neural networks, neuromorphic devices, and biological specimens, each linked in real time to a participant serving as their own control on naturalistic tasks. If ports alone restore the lost function, the requirement is routing connectivity. If performance improves only when the module supplies its own dynamics, the requirement is computational substrate. We expect the answer to depend on how much tissue was lost, how quickly, and on the individual's reserve, and we treat routing and substrate as endpoints of a continuum to be mapped empirically.
Drug Reaction with Eosinophilia and Systemic Symptoms (DRESS) Syndrome in an Adolescent Girl With Epilepsy: A Case Report of Severe Multorgan Involvement.推荐
PMID 43048144 · Cureus · 2026 10 18
作者：Cherrat A
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Drug reaction with eosinophilia and systemic symptoms (DRESS) syndrome is a rare but potentially life-threatening severe cutaneous adverse drug reaction characterized by a delayed onset of fever, extensive skin eruption, hematologic abnormalities, and internal organ involvement. Antiepileptic drugs are among the most frequently implicated medications. Early recognition and prompt withdrawal of the culprit drug with appropriate treatment are essential to prevent severe complications such as multiorgan failure. We report the case of a 15-year-old girl with autism spectrum disorder treated for uncontrolled epilepsy requiring multiple antiepileptic medications who developed severe DRESS syndrome seven to eight weeks after initiation of lamotrigine. The patient presented with mucocutaneous lesions with fever, eosinophilia, acute kidney injury, severe hepatocellular damage, respiratory manifestations, and a marked inflammatory response. DRESS was diagnosed using the RegiSCAR criteria. Carbamazepine and lamotrigine were immediately discontinued and replaced with levetiracetam. Systemic corticosteroid therapy at 1 mg/kg/day was initiated with supportive management. Despite an initial improvement, the patient's clinical condition deteriorated again, followed by fatal respiratory failure. This case highlights the diagnostic complexity and potentially fulminant course of pediatric DRESS, particularly when multiple systemic organs are involved, and urges clinicians to maintain a high index of suspicion for DRESS in children receiving aromatic antiepileptic drugs who develop fever and a generalized rash several weeks after treatment initiation.
Hippocampal circuit remodeling in post-traumatic epileptogenesis: inhibitory dysfunction, glial homeostatic failure, and therapeutic opportunities.推荐
PMID 43048100 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 11 03
作者：Delgado A, Yasmine S, Tang S, Madden G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：Traumatic brain injury (TBI) is a major cause of acquired epilepsy, yet no established therapy prevents the transition from brain injury to post-traumatic epilepsy (PTE). Although the primary lesion frequently involves neocortex, the hippocampus undergoes rapid and persistent cellular and circuit remodeling and is a major candidate substrate for epileptogenesis. This review evaluates hippocampal PTE through a dentate-gate framework in which three interacting dimensions determine network stability: inhibitory control, structural circuit topology, and extracellular/glial homeostasis. TBI can selectively disrupt hippocampal interneuron populations and GABAergic signaling, generate recurrent excitatory connectivity and mossy-fiber sprouting, alter the production and integration of adult-born granule cells, and induce persistent astrocytic, inflammatory, and blood-brain barrier responses. Not all of these observations carry the same weight as evidence for epileptogenesis. We distinguish injury-associated changes from abnormalities enriched in animals that later develop spontaneous PTE and from mechanisms whose targeted manipulation alters spontaneous seizure development. Direct intervention evidence includes suppression of dentate granule-cell mechanistic target of rapamycin (mTOR) signaling and reconstruction of hippocampal inhibitory circuitry with medial ganglionic eminence-derived interneuron precursors, whereas genetic loss of astrocytic aquaporin-4 worsens post-traumatic spontaneous seizures, supporting a causal contribution of extracellular homeostasis. By contrast, several compelling pathways - including altered glutamate transport, connexin signaling, and astrocytic TGF-β responses to blood-brain barrier dysfunction - still require direct testing against spontaneous PTE endpoints. Hippocampal electrophysiological and imaging abnormalities also offer candidate biomarkers for stratifying risk during the latent period. We propose that PTE emerges not from a single lesion but from time-dependent failure of coordinated dentate homeostasis, with different mechanisms dominating at different stages after injury. Organizing the literature in this way highlights testable mechanistic gaps and opportunities for biomarker-guided disease-modifying therapy.

摘要：Acute transverse myelitis (ATM) is an inflammatory condition of the spinal cord that can cause weakness, sensory changes, and bladder dysfunction. Although viral infections can trigger them, it is very rare that herpes simplex virus type 1 (HSV-1) causes it in toddlers. We report a 2-year-2-month-old previously healthy girl who presented with one week of fever and diarrhea followed by progressive weakness in her lower limbs, more on the left side. Magnitude and burden of neurological disorders in Ethiopia, from 1990 to 2023.浏览
PMID 43047934 · The journal of medicine access · 2026 10 12
作者：Tadesse S, Gebremariam AG, Jemal M, Areru HA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurological disorders are diseases or conditions that affect the brain, spinal cord, and nerves. Without understanding the magnitude and burden, it will be difficult to design evidence-based interventions. Therefore, this study will fill the information gap by analyzing the magnitude and burden of neurological disorders in Ethiopia, from 1990 to 2023. METHODS: A retrospective, systematic analysis of secondary data was conducted. Estimates of age-standardized incidence, prevalence, disability-adjusted life years (DALYs), years of life lost (YLLs), and years lived with disability (YLDs) were extracted. Percentage change was also used to assess trends in all metrics between 1990 and 2023. Each metric was estimated to be per 100,000 populations with a 95% uncertainty interval. The analysis included Level 2 and specific Level 3 cause categories, adhering to the GATHER guidelines for transparency. RESULTS: In 2023, the overall age-standardized incidence rate of neurological disorders in Ethiopia was 7,588 per 100,000 population (95% UI: 6,659, 8,490) and the prevalence rate was 25,346 per 100,000 population (95%UI: 23,005, 27,886). While overall rates showed a slight, statistically significant decline, Parkinson's disease showed a significant increase in incidence of 14% (95% UI: 7%, 21%) and prevalence of 35% (95% UI: 25%, 45%). Headache disorders (tension-type headache and migraine) were the main drivers of YLDs, whereas idiopathic epilepsy and Alzheimer's disease were the leading contributors to YLLs and DALYs. The burden increases sharply after age 55. Notably, Parkinson's, motor neuron, and other neurological disorders exhibited significant increments across all primary burden metrics (DALYs, YLLs, and YLDs). Gender disparities were observed, with females bearing a higher burden of Alzheimer's and migraine, while males were more affected by Parkinson's disease. CONCLUSION: The observed trends reflect a demographic transition toward an aging population. The slight decline in magnitude and insignificant change in burden likely reflect underdiagnosis and limited healthcare access rather than true epidemiological stability. These findings underscore the urgent need for targeted public health programs, including integrating neurological care into primary healthcare, developing age-specific and gender-responsive care strategies, and improving surveillance systems to monitor the rising burden in Ethiopia.
Kcnq3 channels shape active membrane properties and firing signatures of thalamocortical neurons during postnatal development.推荐
PMID 43047766 · Frontiers in molecular neuroscience · 2026 11 03
作者：Fazio L, Cerina M, Vinnenberg L, Karabatak E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Ascending somatosensory pathways are formed before birth and refined in the early postnatal stages through activity-dependent mechanisms. In this context, the electrophysiological maturation of thalamocortical (TC) neurons is crucial to tune growing circuitry and ensure the fidelity of information processing. Accordingly, perturbations in ionic homeostasis during this vulnerable period might result in neurodevelopmental disorders. Encoded by Kcnq2 and Kcnq3, Kv7 or M-channels primarily assemble into hetero-tetramers under physiological conditions to mediate a subthreshold potassium current, termed M-current (IM), that regulates intrinsic excitability and neuronal firing signatures. Yet, how partial or complete absence of Kcnq3 subunits affects active and passive properties of developing TC neurons has hitherto not been fully elucidated. METHODS: Here, by performing patch-clamp recordings in the mouse ventrobasal thalamic complex (VB), we provide a comprehensive electrophysiological characterization of the IM during postnatal development. We used transgenic mice carrying Kcnq3 mutations, corresponding to human orthologous variants associated with childhood forms of epilepsy, to investigate the impact of Kcnq3 full deletion and haploinsufficiency on the membrane properties and specific firing pattern of TC neurons. Furthermore, we determined the differential contribution of KCNQ2 and KCNQ3 channels to IM generation by expressing the human gene plasmids in Chinese hamster ovary (CHO) cells at defined subunit ratios. RESULTS: Pharmacological modulation with the Kcnq opener retigabine (RTG) revealed a gradual increase in IM magnitude during the first 30 postnatal days, whereas relative Kcnq2 and Kcnq3 transcripts remained unchanged across development. In the partial or complete absence of Kcnq3 channels, TC neurons exhibited hyperexcitability, characterized by enhanced burst firing and altered single action potential waveform. In accordance with these findings, equimolar KCNQ2/KCNQ3 expression evoked larger currents compared to either KCNQ2-dominant or KCNQ3-dominant subunit compositions. CONCLUSION: Our results suggest a central role for the Kcnq3 channels in stabilizing the membrane properties and firing mode of developing TC neurons, thus ensuring the faithful transmission of peripheral input to the cerebral cortex.
De novo POLR2A p.(L1127F) variant presenting with neurodevelopmental regression and dystonia, with favorable response to low-dose dopamine therapy: a case report and literature review.推荐
PMID 43047756 · Frontiers in neuroscience · 2026 10 28
作者：Cao Z, Shen Q, Tangzhu C, Guo X et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurodevelopmental disorder with hypotonia and variable intellectual and behavioral abnormalities (NEDHIB) is a rare autosomal-dominant condition caused by pathogenic POLR2A variants. It typically presents in infancy with hypotonia, developmental delay, and multisystem manifestations. Fewer than 40 patients have been reported worldwide, suggesting frequent misdiagnosis and underdiagnosis. To our knowledge, neurodevelopmental regression has not been documented in confirmed NEDHIB cases, and no standardized therapeutic regimens are established for clinical management. CASE DESCRIPTION: The patient presented with neonatal hypotonia and developmental delay. At 2 years and 4 months, she developed sleep-associated dystonia-like nodding and limb tremors that were initially suspected to represent epileptic seizures. By 3 years of age, she exhibited marked neurodevelopmental regression: loss of the ability to walk and sit independently, together with deterioration of language function. Electroencephalography (EEG) demonstrated background slowing accompanied by epileptiform discharges. Whole-exome sequencing identified a de novo heterozygous POLR2A variant c.3379C > T (p. L1127F), which confirmed the diagnosis of NEDHIB. RESULTS: Long-term rehabilitation training yielded only modest clinical benefits. After initiation of the dopamine agent levodopa-benserazide hydrochloride, marked improvements in motor and cognitive performance were observed, accompanied by the complete resolution of nodding episodes. With dose titration during follow-up, her clinical status remained stable without symptom relapse. CONCLUSION: This case expands the phenotypic spectrum associated with POLR2A-related NEDHIB and suggests that neurodevelopmental regression may occur within the phenotypic spectrum of this disorder. Our findings also highlight the potential benefit of low-dose dopamine therapy, offering novel insights into the clinical diagnosis, differential diagnosis, and therapeutic management of NEDHIB.
The Wide Phenotypic Spectrum of Adult-Onset Single Large-Scale Mitochondrial DNA Deletion Syndromes.浏览
PMID 43046863 · Journal of inherited metabolic disease · 2026 Nov
作者：Björkman K, de Coo I, Vissing J, Hikmat O et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Single large-scale mitochondrial DNA deletions (SLSMDs) give rise to a wide spectrum of clinical features, including Kearns-Sayre syndrome (KSS) and chronic progressive external ophthalmoplegia (CPEO). Because longitudinal data are scarce, the natural history remains poorly defined. This study was undertaken to describe the clinical spectrum in a large cohort of patients with clinical onset after 16 years of age. A retrospective multicenter study was performed in patients diagnosed and followed in seven European mitochondrial disease centers. Seventy patients were included. The median age at disease onset was 27 years, and the median age at last evaluation was 52 years, with a median follow-up duration of 19 years. Median survival from disease onset was 34 years. KSS spectrum disorder was present in 36% of the patients, while CPEO was identified in the remaining 64%. Among those with CPEO at onset, 27% progressed to KSS spectrum disorder. Multisystem involvement was present in 59%. The most frequently reported symptoms or signs were ptosis and/or ophthalmoplegia (100%), skeletal muscle weakness (56%), perceived fatigue (34%), visual impairment (30%), exercise intolerance (33%), pigmentary retinopathy (20%), hearing impairment (19%), cardiac conduction block (15%), ataxia (11%), epilepsy (14%), and cognitive impairment (11%). Our study gives further insight into the highly heterogenous clinical presentation and natural history associated with SLSMDs. The relevance of categorizing patients into classical syndromic phenotypes may be questioned, as the observed overlap in clinical manifestations and the frequent evolution of phenotype over time suggest a continuum of disease expression rather than clearly distinct syndromic entities.
Advances in Fluorescent Probes to Illuminate Chloride Dynamics in the Brain.浏览
PMID 43046854 · ACS chemical neuroscience · 2026 Nov 18
作者：Rahmani C, Lim HH, Alamudi SH
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Chloride ions (Cl-) play an important role in regulating neuronal excitability, inhibitory neurotransmission, cell volume control, and ionic homeostasis in the brain. Precise control of the intracellular chloride concentration is essential for normal neuronal and glial function, while its dysregulation has been linked to neurological disorders such as epilepsy, neurodevelopmental disorders, schizophrenia, and neurodegenerative diseases. Therefore, understanding chloride dynamics has become an essential step in neuroscience research. To further support the observation of chloride dynamics, fluorescence imaging has emerged as a powerful approach for monitoring intracellular chloride in living cells, offering high spatial and temporal resolution that complements conventional electrophysiological techniques. This review provides an overview of the significance of chloride dynamics in the nervous system and discusses fluorescence-based strategies for investigating chloride homeostasis. The development of fluorescent chloride probes is summarized, from early small-molecule dyes to genetically encoded fluorescent proteins with emphasis on their sensing mechanisms, advantages, limitations, and applications. Finally, current challenges and future directions in probe development, including improved chloride selectivity, reduced pH sensitivity, and subcellular targeting, are discussed.
Spreading voltage buildup in high density EEG during migraine aura: A longitudinal single-patient study.推荐
PMID 43046351 · Headache · 2026 11 17
作者：Riederer F, Klein A, Scutelnic A, Slavova N et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to investigate spreading features in surface high-density electroencephalogram (HD-EEG) during migraine aura. BACKGROUND: Approximately 20%-30% of people with migraine experience migraine with aura (MwA). Migraine aura consists of transient neurological deficits such as visual, sensory, speech, motor, brainstem, or retinal symptoms. Clear features of cortical spreading depolarization (CSD) during migraine aura have recently been demonstrated using intracranial depth electrodes in a patient with MwA and comorbid epilepsy; however, in patients with MwA without comorbid epilepsy, direct detection of CSD in a non-invasive setting is still lacking. METHODS: We report a longitudinal 256-channel HD-EEG study with a direct coupled amplifier set-up in a 45-year-old patient with MwA, in whom repeated recordings could be acquired during aura including early phases and interictally. Electrodes over the forehead, cheeks, periauricular, and nuchal regions were removed to reduce muscle artifacts, resulting in 172 electrodes. RESULTS: In total, 14 electroencephalograms (EEGs) were acquired, thereof eight during the ictal phase. In two out of four aura recordings with predominantly right homonymous scintillating scotoma, recorded soon after onset, we observed a spreading voltage buildup with contralateral maximum. This was associated with a spreading power increase in the delta frequency band. Voltage increases between neighboring electrodes showed a spread with velocity of estimated at 3.8 mm/min. During auras, increased voltage was observed, predominantly in occipital and temporal electrodes. CONCLUSIONS: We suggest that topographically related amplitude buildup and delta spread in temporal relation with aura progression reflects CSD. Since invasive CSD detection has been suggested as a useful neuromonitoring tool, with prognostic and therapeutic relevance, further confirmatory studies for non-invasive CSD detections are warranted.
Clinical characteristics, energy intake patterns, and factors associated with severity of refeeding syndrome in pediatric patients: a single-center retrospective observational study.浏览
PMID 43046252 · BMC nutrition · 2026 08 20
作者：Paphatsinh L, Saengnipanthkul S, Tran LC, Sengchanh S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Refeeding syndrome (RFS) is a critical condition marked by metabolic and electrolyte imbalances during nutritional rehabilitation, particularly in malnourished patients. Data on pediatric RFS remains scarce. This study examined clinical characteristics, energy intake patterns, and factors linked to RFS severity. METHODS: A retrospective study included patients aged 1 month to 18 years diagnosed with RFS at Srinagarind Hospital, Thailand, between January 2018 and December 2019. Patients were categorized as high-risk or low-risk based on ASPEN consensus criteria. Data on demographics, clinical features, energy intake, and outcomes were analyzed. Multivariable logistic regression examined factors linked to severe RFS. RESULTS: Of 1,677 hospitalized pediatric patients, 135 (8.1%) were diagnosed with RFS, of whom 22.2% were classified as high-risk for RFS. Among patients with RFS, high-risk patients had significantly higher rates of severe malnutrition (BMIZ <-2: 73.3% vs. 15.2%, p < 0.001), prolonged fasting, and ICU admission (30.0% vs. 12.4%, p = 0.046) compared to low-risk patients. Clinical signs of RFS were identified in 16.3% of patients with RFS, with abnormal electrocardiograms (4.4%), heart failure (3.7%), and seizures (2.9%) being the most common. Median energy intake as a percentage of target was slightly higher in high-risk patients (21.0% vs. 14.6%, p = 0.133). High-risk patients more frequently received parenteral or combined nutritional support (24.0% vs. 10.0%, p = 0.019). High-risk RFS status was associated with prolonged fasting and surgical interventions, with a higher prevalence of severe disease (86.7% vs. 62.9%, p = 0.014). Multivariable analysis identified autoimmune diseases, surgical interventions, and hepatobiliary and pancreatic diseases were independently associated with severe RFS. CONCLUSIONS: These findings highlight the importance of systematic RFS risk stratification in hospitalized children, particularly for those undergoing surgery or with autoimmune diseases. Proactive identification, close electrolyte monitoring, and timely thiamine supplementation are key to preventing severe complications. Future prospective studies and pediatric-specific risk assessment tools are needed to validate and translate these findings into clinical practice.
Prehospital tranexamic acid in trauma and traumatic brain injury: a systematic review with meta-analysis of randomized comparative evidence.推荐
PMID 43046131 · BMC emergency medicine · 2026 08 22
作者：Almulhim KN, Almulhim M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：Meta分析
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Tranexamic acid (TXA) is commonly used in trauma care to decrease bleeding, but its effectiveness and safety in prehospital settings, notably in traumatic brain injury (TBI), remain controversial. This review aimed to systematically evaluate the evidence on prehospital tranexamic acid (TXA) use in trauma and traumatic brain injury (TBI), with quantitative meta-analysis restricted to randomized comparative evidence and non-randomized evidence summarized narratively. METHODS: We conducted a systematic review with quantitative meta-analysis restricted to independent randomized comparative studies evaluating prehospital TXA versus placebo, usual care, or no TXA. Observational studies, secondary analyses, and survey studies were summarized separately and were not pooled with randomized trials in the primary efficacy or safety meta-analysis. outcomes included mortality (28/30-day, in-hospital), thromboembolic events, seizures, and neurological consequences. Risk of bias was evaluated using Cochrane risk of bias tools for randomized controlled trials (RoB2) and non-randomized studies by Risk Of Bias In Non-randomized Studies of Interventions (ROBINS-I). RESULTS: Twenty-one reports met the broad review criteria. The RCT-only analysis of 28- or 30-day mortality included three independent parent randomized trials: PATCH-Trauma/Gruen et al. 2023, STAAMP/Guyette et al. 2021, and ROC-TXA/Rowell et al. 2020. Prehospital TXA was associated with lower 28- or 30-day mortality (RR 0.81, 95% CI 0.68-0.95). The TBI mortality result was derived solely from ROC-TXA/Rowell et al. 2020 and was therefore a single-study estimate rather than a pooled estimate (RR 0.87, 95% CI 0.64-1.20). In-hospital mortality was reported only by STAAMP/Guyette et al. 2021 and was also a single-study estimate (RR 0.87, 95% CI 0.57-1.33). No statistically significant increase in thromboembolic events or seizures was demonstrated. However, the DVT and PE point estimates were above the null, and their wide confidence intervals did not exclude a clinically relevant increase in thromboembolic risk. CONCLUSION: Prehospital TXA may reduce 28- or 30-day mortality in selected broader trauma populations, particularly among patients at risk of hemorrhage. No statistically significant increase in thromboembolic events or seizures was demonstrated; however, clinically relevant thromboembolic harm cannot be excluded because the DVT and PE point estimates were above the null, and the confidence intervals were wide.

摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy affects multiple aspects of daily life beyond seizure control, yet evidence regarding its psychosocial impact remains scarce in conflict-affected settings such as Syria. This study assessed the self-reported impact of epilepsy on key life domains and explored associated clinical and psychosocial factors among people with epilepsy in Damascus, Syria. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted among patients with neurologist-confirmed epilepsy attending three private neurology clinics and an outpatient neurology clinic at a governmental hospital in Damascus. 摘要：BACKGROUND: Biallelic loss-of-function variants in PCDHGC4 cause a rare autosomal recessive neurodevelopmental disorder characterized by developmental delay or intellectual disability, severe speech impairment, gait abnormalities, and variably microcephaly, seizures, and joint anomalies. CONCLUSIONS: This additional family expands the phenotypic and allelic spectrum of PCDHGC4-related neurodevelopmental disorder. Urolithin A mitigates behavioural and synaptic deficits associated with epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43045993 · BMC neuroscience · 2026 08 21
作者：Mishra S, Verma V, Pradhan S, Dwivedi MK et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: The persistence of drug-resistant epilepsy highlights the need for new therapies that target novel mechanisms while maintaining synaptic strength and minimizing side effects. Small molecules of natural origin with anti-epileptogenic potential can influence altered synaptic deficits and reducing seizure threshold, offering a strategy to restore cellular energy homeostasis and slow the progression of epilepsy and associated comorbidities. METHODS: We used a combination of approaches including behavioural assays, immunostaining and electrophysiological recordings to study the synaptic contributions to reduced seizure-threshold in both Drosophila and mouse models of epilepsy. In addition, we used affinity-purification with synthesized beads specific for our small molecule to identify its cellular partners. RESULTS: We found that administration of Urolithin-A (UA), a small molecule of natural origin, mitigated behavioural and synaptic dysfunctions under chemically and genetically induced epileptic conditions in Drosophila. Additionally, chronic treatment with UA attenuated the altered single-cell and network synaptic transmission in PTZ-induced chronic kindling of mice. However, UA exerted no seizure-suppressive effects in in vitro epileptic conditions, suggesting the absence of acute anti-epileptic effects. In addition, we identified the direct interactor of UA as the complex of tubulin dimer and VDAC1. Molecular docking analyses also predicted that urolithin A interacts with VDAC1. Consistent with the neuroprotective role of VDAC1 downregulation reported in previous studies, neuron-specific knockdown of VDAC1 in Drosophila phenocopied the effects of UA. CONCLUSIONS: Collectively, our data show that UA exerts disease-modifying anti-epileptic effects in both Drosophila and mouse models with VDAC1 identified as a critical functional mediator; however, direct functional modulation of VDAC1 and associated partners by UA will require further experimental validation. These findings provide the blueprint for future drug discovery targeting VDAC1-dependent pathways.

发表形式：Meta分析
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Invasive extra-operative monitoring for drug-resistant epilepsy is primarily conducted using stereo-electroencephalography (SEEG) and subdural grids (SDG). This study aims to provide a comprehensive comparison of the two methods. METHODS: This systematic review was conducted in accordance with PRISMA guidelines. A comprehensive literature search was performed using PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Database. The primary outcomes assessed included postoperative seizure outcome, complications, neurological deficits, and pain. All original English-language studies published up until January 2025 were reviewed, and the risk of bias was evaluated using the Newcastle-Ottawa Scale. RESULTS: Seven studies involving a total of 1,156 patients who underwent SEEG and 737 patients who underwent SDG were included. The pooled analysis demonstrated higher odds of seizure freedom in the SEEG group compared with the SDG group (OR = 1.72, 95% CI: 1.33-2.22). However, the included studies compared clinically distinct patient populations with different indications for invasive monitoring and surgical pathways, which may have influenced seizure outcomes. Across the included studies, SEEG was consistently associated with lower infection rates and reduced postoperative analgesic requirements, while rates of hemorrhage and neurological deficits were generally comparable between groups, with some studies reporting slightly higher hemorrhage rates in SEEG cohorts. CONCLUSION: SEEG was associated with higher odds of seizure freedom following epilepsy surgery; however, this finding should be interpreted with caution because the included studies compared clinically distinct patient populations with different indications for invasive monitoring, surgical pathways, and resection rates. The available evidence suggests lower infection rates and reduced postoperative analgesic requirements with SEEG, whereas findings regarding hemorrhage and neurological deficits were variable across studies. Further studies with larger, more comparable patient populations are needed to better define procedure-specific risks and outcomes.
Feasibility of Simulated Low-Count [18F]FDG PET/MR for Patients with Pediatric Epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43045482 · Journal of nuclear medicine technology · 2026 11 17
作者：Li R, Wang F, Yang R, Luo R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：To minimize radiation risks in pediatric PET imaging, injected dose should be kept as low as reasonably achievable. The aim of this study was to explore whether simulated low-count [18F]FDG PET/MR could preserve diagnostic quality in pediatric epilepsy, thereby supporting the design of future lower-activity validation studies. Methods: Twenty-seven pediatric patients with epilepsy were enrolled. List-mode PET data acquired over 20 min were truncated to 10, 7, and 4 min to simulate count reductions of 50%, 65%, and 80%, respectively. Image quality of the PET images was assessed visually using a 5-point Likert scale and quantitatively using contrast-to-noise ratio and coefficient of variation. The intraclass correlation coefficient was used to evaluate SUV ratio (SUVR) consistency across the simulated count reduction levels in different brain regions. Two computer-assisted methods, region of interest (ROI)-based methods and voxel-based methods, were used for epileptogenic zone (EZ) lateralization. For the ROI-based method, a machine learning model using regional SUVRs as features was applied, and performance was evaluated via receiver-operating-characteristic curve analysis and the area under the curve, with statistical significance assessed by the DeLong test. For the voxel-based method, EZ lateralization was determined on the basis of the hemisphere of hypometabolic clusters identified through asymmetry analysis. Lateralization agreement with the clinical consensus was compared across simulated levels of count reduction. Results: Quantitative analysis showed that the contrast-to-noise ratio decreased and the coefficient of variation increased significantly with simulated count reduction. Visual assessment indicated that, although overall image quality declined, count reduction up to 65% remained clinically acceptable under these simulated conditions. High consistency in mean SUVRs was observed between full-count and simulated low-count PET images across brain regions. Both ROI-based and voxel-based methods demonstrated that EZ lateralization agreement with the clinical consensus did not decline with simulated count reduction. Conclusion: Our study in pediatric epilepsy suggests that a 65% simulated count reduction preserved clinically acceptable [18F]FDG PET image quality, stable SUVR quantification, and concordance of EZ lateralization with the clinical consensus. These findings support the need for prospective validation of a low-activity protocol in pediatric epilepsy.
Persistent Hypersomnia in Neurological Disease: Mechanisms, Diagnosis, and Management.推荐
PMID 43045441 · Neurologic clinics · 2026 09 03
作者：Roberts JM, Auger RR, Lipford MC
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Hypersomnia in neurological disease is characterized by excessive sleep quantity in a day, distinct from excessive daytime sleepiness. It arises from the disruption of brainstem-hypothalamic-thalamic arousal circuits and neurotransmitter systems. Hypersomnia significantly impacts quality of life, safety, cognition, rehabilitation, and caregiver burden across conditions, including Parkinson's disease, stroke, traumatic brain injury, and multiple sclerosis. Systematic evaluation with polysomnography/multiple sleep latency testing distinguishes hypersomnia from fatigue and apathy. Treatment involves mechanism-guided approaches: addressing reversible causes (medications, sleep disorders), then pharmacotherapy with wake-promoting agents or stimulants, with special considerations for epilepsy, autonomic dysfunction, and neurodegenerative disease.

Case report: A worm's eye view: Fatal intraocular Dirofilaria immitis in a domestic cat from Georgia, USA.浏览
PMID 43045350 · Veterinary parasitology, regional studies and reports · 2026 09 12
作者：Jones H, Yared K, Belknap E, Pulaski C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Dirofilaria immitis (canine heartworm) is a mosquito-transmitted nematode for which the domestic cat (Felis catus) is an aberrant host. Feline infections typically involve low worm burdens, unisex or developmentally arrested specimens, absent or transient microfilaremia, and unreliable antigen serology. Ectopic larval migration to the ocular anterior chamber is rarely reported. We describe a fatal case of intraocular D. immitis in a 6-year-old male neutered indoor/outdoor domestic shorthair from Walton County, Georgia, USA. The cat presented to a local veterinary hospital with lethargy, hyporexia, and an owner-observed organism in the right eye (OD); examination revealed anterior chamber opacity, and a presumptive diagnosis of ophthalmomyiasis interna likely due to a Cuterebra species. Prior to referral, the cat received selamectin/sarolaner (Revolution® Plus), epsiprantel (Cestex), and a rabies vaccine. At the referral center, the cat developed recurrent seizure-like episodes and died overnight despite supportive care. A SNAP Feline Triple Test (IDEXX) was negative for heartworm antigen, FeLV antigen, and FIV antibody. Post-mortem enucleation of OD revealed a single nematode (10.74 cm) within the anterior chamber. The worm was morphologically consistent with Dirofilaria and was confirmed to be D. immitis by sequence analysis of the COI and 18S rDNA gene targets. This case highlights the limitations of feline heartworm testing, initial clinical misidentification, morphologic limitations in aberrant host-tissue combinations, and the importance of pre-treatment microfilaremia assessment.
Glymphatic dysfunction in epilepsy: imaging assessment, comorbidities, pathophysiology, and emerging therapeutic perspectives.推荐
PMID 43045006 · Neuroscience · 2026 11 17
作者：Chen Y, Xu K, Zhou Z, Zhao X
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Epilepsy is a prevalent neurological disorder driven by complex, multifactorial mechanisms, yet seizures remain uncontrolled in a substantial proportion of patients despite treatment. The glymphatic system, a brain-wide cerebrospinal fluid-interstitial fluid exchange pathway that facilitates metabolic waste clearance, has emerged as an important regulator of cerebral homeostasis. Accumulating experimental and neuroimaging evidence suggests that glymphatic-related processes may be involved in epileptogenesis and disease progression, although their precise roles remain to be established. This narrative review summarizes advances in the assessment of glymphatic-related processes and synthesizes findings across epilepsy subtypes. We examine how these findings relate to cognitive, sleep, and psychiatric comorbidities, as well as treatment outcomes. We interpret findings from complementary imaging approaches in their clinical context, considering epilepsy phenotype and disease course alongside accompanying white matter and network changes. This synthesis highlights the potential clinical significance of these findings and the limitations of current evidence while distinguishing observed associations from proposed mechanisms. We further discuss emerging therapeutic strategies as hypotheses for testing and outline considerations for initial proof-of-concept studies. Because most clinical studies rely on indirect imaging markers and include small patient cohorts, causal relationships and the clinical utility of these markers remain uncertain. Future studies combining standardized multimodal imaging, experimental models, and prospective clinical cohorts are needed to clarify underlying mechanisms and establish the clinical value of glymphatic-related findings in epilepsy.

JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：RANBP2/Nup358 is the largest human nucleoporin and a major structural and functional component of the cytoplasmic filaments of the nuclear pore complex. Heterozygous missense variants in RANBP2 are associated with Acute Necrotizing Encephalopathy type 1 (ANE1), a rare autosomal dominant pediatric encephalopathy often triggered by viral infection and characterized by rapid neurological deterioration and symmetric thalamic lesions. In this study, we report a 5-year-old girl who presented with status epilepticus following fever and viral nasopharyngitis. Brain MRI revealed bilateral thalamic and brainstem lesions consistent with acute necrotizing encephalopathy initially suggestive of Leigh syndrome. Full mitochondrial DNA sequencing, long-range PCR, and quantification of mitochondrial DNA copy number revealed no pathogenic mtDNA variants, no detectable large-scale mtDNA rearrangement, and no mtDNA depletion. Whole-exome sequencing identified a novel heterozygous missense variant in RANBP2, c.3163C > T (p.Pro1055Ser), affecting a highly conserved proline residue located in the FG repeat region in the inter-domain linker between the N-terminal domain and the first Ran-binding domain (RanBD1). The variant was not detected in either tested parent. "Residue 1055 was predicted to lie within a low-confidence, intrinsically disordered region. Therefore, the structural models were considered descriptive and were not interpreted as evidence of mutation-induced structural or interaction changes. The clinicoradiological phenotype and genetic findings support p.Pro1055Ser as a candidate ANE1-associated variant, although its pathogenic role remains unproven due to the absence of variant-specific functional validation. This report expands the spectrum of RANBP2 variants observed in ANE-compatible phenotypes.

摘要：NMDA receptors (NMDARs) are expressed throughout the brain and, in addition to their central role in synaptic transmission, are essential mediators of many forms of synaptic plasticity. Given their widespread expression, it is not surprising that NMDAR dysfunction has been implicated in numerous neurological and neuropsychiatric disorders, including epilepsy, autism spectrum disorders and depression. NMDARs are localized at both pre- and postsynaptic sites, but presynaptic NMDARs are poorly understood. Here, by combining the use of wild-type and NMDAR subunit-specific knockout mice, a series of highly selective subunit-specific pharmacological modulators, and electron microscopy analysis of immunostained brain sections, we describe GluN2D-containing NMDARs located presynaptically on CA3 pyramidal neuron axon terminals. We show that presynaptic NMDARs at the Schaffer collateral terminals control the probability of glutamate release onto connecting CA1 pyramidal neurons, but not Schaffer innervation of radiatum interneurons. Thus, GluN2D-containing NMDARs could be involved in various forms of plasticity. A micro-nano electrode array-based glioma-neuronal interaction model for modulating glutamate-mediated glioma-associated epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43044445 · Biosensors & bioelectronics · 2026 11 05
作者：Yin S, Fang S, Shen M, Yin P et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Glioma-associated epilepsy (GAE) is characterized by excessive glutamate (Glu) release from glioma cells. This process is largely attributed to the activity of the cystine/glutamate antiporter (system xc-) and is associated with neuronal hyperexcitability. Current treatments mainly rely on broad-spectrum antiepileptic drugs that suppress neuronal activity without targeting tumor-derived mechanisms and may impair normal neuronal function. To investigate glioma-neuron interactions and evaluate mechanism-oriented interventions, we developed an in vitro glioma-neuronal interaction model (GNI model) based on a micro-nano electrode array (MNEA) integrated with removable polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) chambers, enabling spatially controlled compartmentalized culture and subsequent co-culture. This platform allows real-time, multimodal monitoring of electrophysiological activity and metabolites, including choline (Cho), creatine (Cr), and Glu. Within this system, migration of glioma cells toward the neuronal region was accompanied by elevated extracellular Glu levels and increased neuronal firing, consistent with Glu-related neuronal hyperexcitability and partially recapitulating features relevant to GAE. Notably, pharmacological inhibition of system xc- using sulfasalazine (SAS), erastin (Era), and sorafenib (Sora) reduced extracellular Glu levels and modulated neuronal activity. Overall, this platform provides a robust tool for probing glioma-neuron interactions and suggests that targeting system xc- may mitigate tumor-associated neuronal hyperexcitability.
Development and validation of a LC-MS/MS method for the quantification of 22 antiepileptic drugs in saliva.推荐
PMID 43044172 · Journal of chromatography. B, Analytical technologies in the biomedical and life sciences · 2026 11 15
作者：Almeida C, Dubertrand C, Achard A, Pannier F et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Worldwide, 50 million people of all ages are affected by epileptic seizures. Drug therapy is the cornerstone of epilepsy management, but treatments are difficult to equilibrate due to the wide variability in plasma concentrations of antiepileptic drugs (AED), which results in unpredictable dosing. However, target plasma concentrations are known, and therefore therapy monitoring is carried out by measuring drug concentrations to guide dose adjustment. Increasing accessibility to therapeutic drug monitoring (TDM) is therefore a major issue. Saliva offers an attractive alternative to blood due to its non-invasive collection method, particularly in children and the elderly. However, standardization of saliva collection is mandatory to study the relationship between saliva and plasma concentrations properly, and to explore the feasibility of TDM in saliva. The assay reported here has been validated according to the European Medicine Agency guidelines for 22 AED in saliva collected using the Quantisal® sampling device. The results demonstrate the reliability of the method which offers reproducible saliva collection and ensures analyte stability up to 7 days before sample processing. This assay will be used as part of a clinical study designed to demonstrate its clinical applicability and investigate the relationship between saliva and plasma concentrations of AED, to verify whether saliva could be used as an alternative to blood for monitoring antiepileptic treatment.
