Validation of the BASED Score as a Prognostic Biomarker for Refractory Epilepsy Following Treatment of Infantile Epileptic Spasms Syndrome.推荐
PMID 43044079 · Neurology · 2026 11 17
作者：Mytinger JR, Lo WD, Weber AB, Vidaurre J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Refractory epilepsy is common among children with a history of infantile epileptic spasms syndrome (IESS), yet no validated prognostic biomarkers exist to guide early risk stratification. The Burden of Amplitudes and Epileptiform Discharges (BASED) score is an interictal EEG rating scale for children with IESS, with scores of 4 or 5 suggesting epileptic encephalopathy. The objective of this study was to determine whether a post-treatment BASED score of 4/5 is a prognostic biomarker for refractory epilepsy after IESS treatment. METHODS: This retrospective validation study of children with IESS was conducted at a Level 4 pediatric epilepsy center. Children with tuberous sclerosis complex (TSC) were excluded. Three blinded reviewers assigned 2021 BASED scores to full-length post-treatment EEGs. Refractory epilepsy was defined as persistent seizures despite treatment with at least 2 antiseizure medications or completion of epilepsy surgery. Prespecified performance benchmarks for BASED 4/5 as a rule-in test required ≥80% specificity and a positive likelihood ratio of ≥4. RESULTS: The cohort included 123 children (42.3% female), with a median first treatment age of 8.0 months (IQR 5.9-10.3) and follow-up duration after first treatment of 58.8 months (IQR 42.2-80.7). Only 11.4% had a favorable etiology. EEGs (median 19 hours) were completed at a median of 47 days (32-72) after starting treatment. The inter-rater agreement for epileptic encephalopathy was excellent (κfree = 0.78). Half (52%) of the children had refractory epilepsy. BASED 4/5 demonstrated high specificity (88.1%, 95% CI 77.1-95.1) for refractory epilepsy, with a positive likelihood ratio of 4.4 (2.1-9.1). The sensitivity, positive predictive value, and negative predictive value were 51.6% (38.7-64.3), 82.5% (69.3-90.8), and 62.7% (56.1-68.7), respectively. An exploratory composite indicator comprising BASED 4/5, persistent epileptic spasms, and/or epileptic spasms relapse demonstrated higher sensitivity (79.7%, 67.8-88.7) with modestly reduced specificity (81.4%, 69.1-90.3). DISCUSSION: Study limitations include the single-center design, exclusion of children with TSC, variable EEG timing, and shorter follow-up in some children. Nevertheless, in this independent validation cohort, a post-treatment BASED 4/5 demonstrated favorable prognostic performance consistent with its proposed use as a rule-in test for refractory epilepsy. These findings support a post-treatment BASED score of 4/5 as a prognostic biomarker for refractory epilepsy after IESS treatment.
An Integrated Approach for Early In Vitro Sedation Prediction Utilizing hiPSC Neurons and Human Ion Channel Assays.浏览
PMID 43044067 · International journal of toxicology · 2026 11 17
作者：Zolkiewski LAK, Rockley KL, Jones K, Jennings HG et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Central nervous system (CNS) liabilities remain a significant cause of failure in drug development. Two complementary in vitro safety assays were previously developed, combining automated patch-clamp electrophysiology and multielectrode array techniques, to detect and avoid seizurogenic compounds. Further to seizure risk, sedation and fatigue are commonly encountered CNS side effects during drug safety testing. Here, the aim was to extend the capability of the in vitro safety assay to enable earlier detection of sedative potential. Fourteen compounds with reported sedative activity and five non-sedative controls were screened in human-induced pluripotent stem cell-derived neurons using multielectrode array. These compounds were further screened with automated patch-clamp electrophysiology using cells expressing CNS ion channels implicated in sedation: KV1.1, KV2.1, KV3.1, KV7.2/7.3, NaV1.2, K2P2.1, or the α1β2γ2 GABAA, and α4β2 nicotinic receptors. Known sedative compounds reduced neural firing and network burst activity in human-induced pluripotent stem cell-neurons, indicating sedative effects, whilst negative control compounds did not significantly change multielectrode array parameters. Of the 19 compounds screened, 94% were accurately classified. Automated patch-clamp electrophysiology confirmed that several sedative compounds interacted with CNS ion channels within a 30-fold margin to their total therapeutic blood plasma concentration. These results support the use of the combined in vitro safety models for detecting sedative as well as seizurogenic effects, offering valuable human-based tools for early hazard identification and prioritization of drug candidates. These platforms are also useful for interpretation of CNS-related findings in vivo supporting the principles of 3Rs to minimize and replace the use of animals in scientific testing.

SCN1B intron 3 retention results in a nonsense-mediated decay-sensitive mRNA linked to developmental and epileptic encephalopathy.推荐
PMID 43043717 · Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences of the United States of America · 2026 11 17
作者：Bakshi S, Catalfio AM, Chen C, Yuan Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Biallelic variants in the voltage-gated sodium channel (VGSC) gene SCN1B are linked to developmental and epileptic encephalopathy type 52 (DEE52). SCN1B encodes two isoforms: transmembrane β1, generated by all 6 coding exons, and β1B, generated by exons 1 to 3 followed by retention of intron 3 (SCN1B-IR). Transmembrane β1 subunits are multifunctional proteins that regulate sodium current and channel cell-surface expression, participate in cell-cell and cell-matrix adhesion, and modulate transcription. Several DEE52 variants in the transmembrane β1 coding region have been shown to result in mixed loss-of-function. DEE52 variants are also identified in SCN1B-IR, yet their functional impacts remain unknown. Here, we provide evidence that SCN1B-IR is a cell type-specific, nonsense-mediated decay (NMD)-sensitive transcript. We investigate a severe biallelic DEE52 patient variant in intron 3, SCN1B c.449-2A>G, which results in expression of SCN1B-IR, but not transmembrane β1 transcripts. A transgenic mouse model that expresses SCN1B-IR, but not transmembrane β1, transcript phenocopies Scn1b null mice with seizures and early death. Thus, SCN1B-IR functions as an NMD-sensitive transcript that cannot compensate for the loss of transmembrane β1. We propose that future work may reveal SCN1B-IR to be a therapeutic target similar to other NMD-targeted transcripts.
Females with Epilepsy Show Menstrual Cycle-linked Alterations in Sensory Induced Plasticity.推荐
PMID 43043672 · Neuroendocrinology · 2026 11 17
作者：Alshakhouri M, Sharpe C, Bergin P, Shaw AD et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Catamenial epilepsy describes menstrual cycle-linked exacerbation of seizure frequency and/or intensity. To investigate its neurophysiological mechanisms, we assessed visual long-term potentiation (LTP) across the menstrual cycle using EEG and photic stimulation. Oestradiol can enhance NMDA receptor-mediated excitation and facilitate LTP, whereas allopregnanolone, a progesterone metabolite, promotes GABAergic inhibition and suppresses LTP. METHODS: We recruited 28 females with epilepsy and 25 controls (mean age: 27 ± 8.52; 30 ± 7.06 years, respectively). Twenty participants with epilepsy and all controls successfully completed the visual LTP task across three menstrual phases: mid-follicular, mid-luteal, and perimenstrual. Cycle phase was verified via blood samples, and session order was counterbalanced. Standard evoked-related potential (ERP) analysis was paired with generative modelling of thalamocortical circuits. RESULTS: In controls, P2 potentiation, a positive-going component of the ERP, was enhanced by LTP induction during the mid-follicular phase compared to the perimenstrual phase (F(2, 144) =11.54, p = 0.004 FWE-c). In contrast, females with epilepsy exhibited greater P2 potentiation during the perimenstrual and mid-luteal phases than the mid-follicular phase (F(2,228) = 11.76, p = .003 FWE-c), with only two participants showing control-like pattern of peak mid-follicular potentiation. Computational modelling indicated loss of inhibitory control and breakthrough excitability during the perimenstrual and mid-luteal phases in epilepsy. CONCLUSION: Our findings reveal aberrant menstrual cycle modulation of sensory-induced plasticity in epilepsy, suggesting altered inhibitory gating as well as thalamocortical excitatory drive in the mid-luteal and perimenstrual phases. While GABAergic mechanisms are often emphasised in catamenial epilepsy, our results warrant considering altered excitatory mechanisms.
Acute multifocal myoclonus as a rare central nervous system manifestation of propoxur (carbamate) poisoning.推荐
PMID 43043615 · Acta neurologica Belgica · 2026 11 17
作者：Tayade K
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：来信
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Carbamates cause reversible acetylcholinesterase (AChE) inhibition producing a cholinergic toxidrome similar to organophosphate poisoning. Myoclonus is an exceedingly rare CNS complication, with very few indexed cases reported. CASE PRESENTATION: A 24-year-old male presented four hours after intentional ingestion of ~ 30 mL of 100% propoxur, with bradycardia, hypotension, miosis, and garlic-like breath odour. Pseudocholinesterase was critically suppressed (< 200 U/L). After intubation and atropinisation, he developed sudden multifocal, arrhythmic, non-stimulus-sensitive myoclonic jerks within six hours of ingestion - earlier than the typical post-hypoxic window. EEG showed mild slowing without epileptiform discharges; MRI was normal. Lance-Adams syndrome, post-hypoxic myoclonus, seizures, hypoxic-ischaemic encephalopathy, and metabolic causes were systematically excluded. With clonazepam, myoclonus resolved within 24-48 h; the patient was discharged neurologically intact. CONCLUSION: This case documents unusually early-onset multifocal myoclonus in propoxur poisoning, expanding the sparse carbamate-specific literature and illustrating a structured exclusion approach in a resource-limited setting.
ADAM10 in Epilepsy: Synaptic, Inflammatory, and Neuroprotective Mechanisms and Evidence Gaps.推荐
PMID 43043579 · Molecular neurobiology · 2026 11 17
作者：Leo A, De Caro C, Roberti R, Pirozzi C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Epilepsy involves molecular and circuit abnormalities that may contribute to seizures and associated comorbidities. A disintegrin and metalloproteinase 10 (ADAM10) regulates synaptic adhesion, amyloid precursor protein (APP) processing, developmental signaling, and inflammatory substrate shedding. This narrative review examines evidence linking these functions to epilepsy and distinguishes direct experimental findings from mechanistic extrapolation. Postnatal neuronal Adam10 deletion causes spontaneous seizures, synaptic abnormalities, and cognitive deficits in mice. In selected experimental models, interventions that increase ADAM10 expression have reduced either the frequency of spontaneous seizures or susceptibility to experimentally induced seizures. These outcomes alone do not establish prevention of epileptogenesis or disease modification. Human genetic evidence is limited to a promoter-variant association study, and the functional consequences of the associated variants remain unresolved. Neither the proposed link between ADAM10-dependent receptor shedding and inflammasome signaling, nor the antiseizure effects reported for indirect ADAM10 modulators, has been validated as ADAM10-dependent in epilepsy. Therapeutic interpretation must consider substrate, cell type, developmental stage, and network context because increased ADAM10 activity cannot be assumed to be uniformly protective. The available evidence supports further investigation of ADAM10 in epilepsy, prioritizing substrate-specific causal studies, validation across etiologies, and prospective assessment of ADAM10-related biomarkers in well-characterized patient cohorts.
Molecular Heterogeneity of WNT and mTOR Signaling Pathways in Mild Malformation of Cortical Development with Oligodendroglial Hyperplasia and Epilepsy (MOGHE).推荐
PMID 43043571 · Journal of molecular neuroscience : MN · 2026 11 17
作者：Damacena Kunrath V, Neuhaus Lignati B, Rosinha PFP, Canani Sommer R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Mild Malformation of Cortical Development with Oligodendroglial Hyperplasia and Epilepsy (MOGHE) is a recently recognized developmental lesion associated with drug-resistant epilepsy. Although its clinical and neuropathological features are increasingly understood, the molecular mechanisms underlying MOGHE remain largely unknown. Given the roles of mTOR and WNT signaling in cortical development and epileptogenesis, we investigated the expression of selected genes from these pathways in surgically resected MOGHE tissue and explored their biological associations through computational network analysis. Cortical samples from 12 patients with histopathologically confirmed MOGHE were analyzed by quantitative real-time PCR. Gene expression levels were compared with control cortical tissue from patients with temporal lobe epilepsy associated with hippocampal sclerosis. Bioinformatic analyses included MalaCards-derived gene-disease associations, molecular network modeling, community detection, and heuristic disease-prioritization approaches. Gene expression analysis revealed marked molecular heterogeneity among MOGHE samples. Despite substantial interindividual variability, several genes showed consistently reduced expression, including RICTOR, RPTOR, TSC2, LRP6, and WNT5A. Stratification by cortical location identified region-specific expression patterns, particularly for WNT5A, which was upregulated in right temporal lesions and markedly downregulated in frontal lesions. Network analyses identified two major functional modules: one centered on RICTOR, RPTOR, and TSC2, associated with epilepsy and neurodevelopmental disorders, and another centered on LRP6 and WNT5A. TSC2 occupied a central position connecting both modules, suggesting biological interaction between mTOR and WNT signaling. These findings indicate that MOGHE is a molecularly heterogeneous developmental disorder involving coordinated alterations in mTOR and WNT pathways, providing new insights into its pathogenesis and potential therapeutic targets.

Extracellular Vesicles in Epilepsy: Molecular Mechanisms, Neuronal Signaling Pathways, and Biomarker Potential.推荐
PMID 43043527 · Molecular neurobiology · 2026 11 17
作者：Alabbas A, Afzal M, Alqahtani SM, Afzal O et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Extracellular vesicles (EVs) are membrane-bound vesicles secreted by cells (including neurons and glia). The biofluid profiles of EV-associated microRNAs, proteins, and glycans are not fixed but rather can change in response to the state of the releasing cells; moreover, the biofluid profile is dependent on the mixtures of cells and processing of the sample. Plasma EV-enriched microRNAs, coagulation factor IX and thrombospondin-1 in EV preparations from serum, and EV-associated N-glycan signatures have been reported to be candidates. Results from total serum, total CSF, brain tissue, and/or whole plasma validation are distinguished from EV-specific measurements. Activity-dependent EV release, activity-regulated cytoskeleton-associated protein (ARC)-mediated RNA transfer, and context-dependent effects on neuronal and glial signaling are supported by experimental studies. Circulating candidates are not proven to be brain-originating and/or causal. The majority of human results are left at the discovery or the initial validation, and estimations of discrimination do not prove clinical utility. Need for reproducible matrix-specific separation, assessment of non-EV contaminants and putative cellular enrichment, appropriate clinical comparators, and independent prospective validation.
Efficacy and Safety of Bumetanide (NKCC1 Inhibitor) for Core Symptoms in Childrenwith Autism Spectrum Disorder: An Updated Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis(2020-2026).推荐
PMID 43043338 · Pharmacology · 2026 11 17
作者：Choudhary AK
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Bumetanide, a selective inhibitor of the Na⁺-K⁺-2Cl⁻ co-transporter 1 (NKCC1), is hypothesised to restore hyperpolarising γ-aminobutyric acid (GABA)-mediated inhibition and has been investigated as a disease-modifying therapy for the core symptoms of autism spectrum disorder (ASD). Early small randomised controlled trials (RCTs) reported benefits on the Childhood Autism Rating Scale (CARS); however, two large Phase III trials (SIGN-1 and SIGN-2; Fuentes et al., 2023) failed on their primary endpoints. An updated systematic review and meta-analysis of RCTs, with the search extended from January 2020 to May 2026, was conducted to clarify the efficacy, safety and moderators of effect of bumetanide for core ASD symptoms in children. METHODS: Seven databases (PubMed/MEDLINE, Embase, Web of Science, Cochrane CENTRAL, PsycINFO, ClinicalTrials.gov and the WHO ICTRP) were searched from January 2020 to 9 May 2026. A single investigator screened records, extracted data and assessed risk of bias using the Cochrane Risk of Bias 2 tool, each step being carried out twice at a separation of several weeks with the second pass made blind to the first. The primary outcome was change in CARS total score. Secondary outcomes included the Social Responsiveness Scale, second edition (SRS-2), the Autism Diagnostic Observation Schedule (ADOS), Clinical Global Impression scales and pre-specified safety endpoints. Mean differences (MD) and standardised mean differences (SMD; Hedges' g) were pooled using a DerSimonian-Laird random-effects model, and absolute effects and numbers needed to harm (NNH) were derived for safety outcomes. All numerical data were verified against the primary reports and trial registries. Post hoc analyses examined trial design features, mean change within each arm, and how the trials reported co-occurring conditions and excitation/inhibition-relevant behavioural phenotypes. The Grading of Recommendations Assessment, Development and Evaluation (GRADE) approach was used to assess certainty of evidence. RESULTS: Ten RCTs (about 1,020 children) were included, of which six (710 children analysed) provided arm-level CARS data. The pooled MD favoured bumetanide (MD -1.27; 95% CI -2.05 to -0.49; I² = 63%; SMD -0.47, 95% CI -0.80 to -0.14) but was well below the minimal clinically important difference of approximately 4.5 points. The four earlier trials gave MD -1.83 (95% CI -2.85 to -0.82; SMD -0.74), whereas the two Phase III trials, whose primary endpoint was the CARS2, gave MD -0.19 (95% CI -1.12 to +0.73; SMD -0.04). Children receiving bumetanide improved by a similar amount in both trial generations (3.43 and 3.57 points); children in the control arms improved by 1.28 points in the earlier trials and 3.38 points in the Phase III trials. Larger trials found smaller mean differences, and age did not significantly modify the effect (p = 0.11). No trial pre-specified attention-deficit/hyperactivity symptoms, impulsivity or seizure frequency as an outcome. Bumetanide increased hypokalaemia (RR 7.77; 95% CI 3.59 to 16.83; NNH 7), polyuria (RR 3.31; 1.47 to 7.43; NNH 4) and serious adverse events (RR 2.25; 1.03 to 4.91; NNH 24). Certainty of evidence was Low for CARS and ranged from Low to High for safety outcomes. CONCLUSIONS: Bumetanide produces, at most, a small CARS reduction that falls below clinical meaningfulness in unselected children, is absent in the Phase III trials, and is accompanied by an unfavourable tolerability profile. The difference between trial generations lies in how much the control arms improved rather than in the response to bumetanide, so the earlier positive estimates are not a reliable guide to efficacy. The available data do not, however, allow a clean separation of 'the drug does not work' from 'the trials were not built to detect what the drug does': the endpoint used across the programme is a diagnostic severity instrument with limited sensitivity to the excitation/inhibition-linked behaviours that the mechanism should most plausibly modify, and a machine-learning reanalysis of the Phase III data has since identified cross-validated responder subgroups comprising up to 40% of participants. Current evidence does not support routine clinical use. Future trials should prioritise biomarker-stratified designs with blinded or objective outcome assessment, pre-defined responder criteria and endpoints matched to the proposed mechanism.
Whole-exome sequencing reveals the neurogenetic spectrum of pediatric epilepsy in Türkiye.推荐
PMID 43043129 · Journal of neurogenetics · 2026 11 17
作者：Mutlu MB, Öz Ö, Sanrı A, Yaralı O et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Epilepsy is a clinically and genetically heterogeneous neurodevelopmental disorder, and diagnostic yield from next-generation sequencing varies substantially across cohorts and health-care settings. Population-specific data remain particularly limited for pediatric patients from underrepresented regions. In this retrospective two-center study, we evaluated 690 individuals with epilepsy onset at 18 years of age or younger who underwent whole-exome sequencing between January 2020 and April 2025. Patients with congenital infection, brain trauma, stroke, or perinatal asphyxia as the primary cause of epilepsy were excluded. Pathogenic or likely pathogenic findings were identified in 205 of 690 patients (29.7%). Single-nucleotide variants (SNVs) and insertions/deletions (indels) accounted for 195 patients (28.2%), whereas clinically relevant copy number variants accounted for 10 patients (1.4%). The cohort included 370 males (53.6%) and 320 females (46.4%). We identified 68 rare SNVs and indels, of which 48 were classified as novel because they were absent from ClinVar, gnomAD, and dbSNP and had not been reported in the published literature at the time of analysis. In addition, four variants with rs identifiers lacked ClinVar records and prior case-based publications, and 15 variants had previously been reported only once. Eleven copy number variants were detected in 10 patients; all but one were confirmed by orthogonal testing. Several findings were considered of particular neurogenetic interest, including variants in genes with synaptic or ion-channel relevance and rare recurrent findings that may broaden the recognized phenotypic spectrum of pediatric epilepsy. Overall, this study supports the clinical utility of whole-exome sequencing in pediatric epilepsy and provides population-specific neurogenetic data from Türkiye, while also highlighting a subset of rare findings that require replication and functional validation.
Expanding the Phenotypic Spectrum of COG3-Related Congenital Disorder of Glycosylation: Report of Two Novel Cases.推荐
PMID 43042966 · International journal of developmental neuroscience : the official journal of the International Society for Developmental Neuroscience · 2026 Nov
作者：Yoldaş Çelik M, Üçtepe E, Uçar HK, Köşeci B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: COG3-congenital disorder of glycosylation (COG3-CDG) is an ultra-rare Golgi trafficking disorder caused by biallelic pathogenic variants in COG3, encoding a lobe A subunit of the conserved oligomeric Golgi (COG) complex. To date, only four patients from two consanguineous families have been reported, presenting with a severe neurodevelopmental phenotype characterised by progressive microcephaly, early-onset epilepsy, profound intellectual disability and early mortality. Consequently, the phenotypic and biochemical spectrum of COG3-CDG remains incompletely defined. CASE REPORT: We report two previously undescribed patients harbouring novel homozygous COG3 variants. Case 1 is a 3.5-year-old boy born to consanguineous parents, presenting with global developmental delay, autism spectrum disorder and macrocephaly, without epilepsy, neuroregression or loss of ambulation, carrying a canonical splice-site variant (c.1810-2A>G). Case 2 is a 15-month-old boy born at 28 weeks of gestation to consanguineous parents, presenting with severe global developmental delay, infantile spasms with hypsarrhythmia and progressive microcephaly, carrying a homozygous missense variant (c.1550A>G). In both patients, serum transferrin isoform analysis by capillary electrophoresis showed individual isoform percentages within or near the laboratory reference ranges. DISCUSSION: These cases substantially expand the clinical spectrum of COG3-CDG. Case 1 represents the first reported patient with macrocephaly and preserved ambulation beyond early childhood, challenging the previously assumed uniformly severe and progressive phenotype. Interpretation of Case 2 is confounded by extreme prematurity and Grade IV intraventricular haemorrhage, necessitating caution in phenotype attribution. With these reports, the total number of published COG3-CDG patients increases to six. These observations suggest a broader clinical spectrum associated with COG3 variants and highlight the need for additional biochemical and functional characterisation when routine transferrin screening is inconclusive. Continued international case reporting will be critical to refining genotype-phenotype correlations and improving recognition of this ultra-rare disorder.
WNK-SPAK/OSR1 signaling pathway facilitates ictal activity via reduced neuronal chloride extrusion rate.推荐
PMID 43042931 · eLife · 2026 11 17
作者：Dzhala VI, Shiu FH, Rahmati N, Carroll A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜生物学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区
关联文献（UpdateOf）：PMID 42327210
摘要：Seizures upregulate Na+-K+-2Cl- (NKCC1)-mediated Cl- influx and downregulate K+-Cl- (KCC2)-mediated Cl- efflux via the WNK-SPAK/OSR1 kinases, leading to cytoplasmic chloride ([Cl-]i) accumulation, reduced GABAergic inhibition, and anticonvulsant failure. Early studies found that inhibiting WNK-kinase reduced baseline [Cl-]i (ECl) and seizures via increased KCC2 activity. However, increased KCC2 activity alone should not affect ECl, whose determinants are more complex. We determined the net effects of the WNK-SPAK/OSR1 pathway inhibitor WNK463 on ECl and [Cl-]i transients during spontaneous ictal-like discharges (ILDs) in organotypic hippocampal slice cultures from mice. We found that WNK463 reduced interictal [Cl-]i, but did not change baseline [Cl-]i measured in the presence of TTX. WNK463 enhanced neuronal Cl- extrusion during and after ILDs before abolishing ILDs. Pharmacological inhibition and targeted siRNA silencing demonstrated that the anti-ictal effects of WNK463 involved both NKCC1 and KCC2. Our data support a mechanism whereby mutual NKCC1 inhibition and KCC2 activation via the WNK-SPAK/OSR1 pathway exert powerful anti-ictal effects by facilitating [Cl-]i extrusion during ILDs.
Real-world safety profiles of newer antiseizure medications: A pharmacovigilance study based on FDA Adverse Event Reporting System database.推荐
PMID 43042732 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 17
作者：He Z, Li T, Chang Y, Liu Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to systematically evaluate and compare the post-marketing safety of four newer antiseizure medications (ASMs)-brivaracetam (BRV), cannabidiol (CBD), cenobamate (CNB), and fenfluramine (FFA). METHODS: A pharmacovigilance study was conducted using data from the FAERS database (Q1 2016-Q4 2024). Disproportionality analyses were performed to detect adverse event (AE) signals using three algorithms. Subgroup analyses were stratified by age, sex, reporter, and treatment strategy. We employed multivariable logistic regression, adjusted for covariates including age, sex, weight, dose, and concomitant medications, to validate associations between ASMs and specific AEs and to identify potential influencing factors. Bayesian network analysis was conducted to explore conditional dependencies among variables. Hospitalization risk was evaluated based on system organ classes. RESULTS: After data cleaning, 13 763 reports were included. We identified 308 positive preferred term (PT) signals. Seizure-related AEs were frequently reported. All four ASMs had the PT of seizure cluster, with each exhibiting distinct AE profiles: BRV with psychiatric disorders, CBD with gastrointestinal events, CNB with visual dysfunction, and FFA with valvular heart disease (VHD). Children had higher reporting odds of seizure with BRV and CBD, while reporting odds of FFA-related VHD were lower in children; polytherapy was associated with higher reporting odds of somnolence, and AE reporting patterns varied by reporter source. Both CBD-related gastrointestinal events and FFA-related cardiac events exhibited dose-dependent effects. Infection-related AEs were associated with higher odds of hospitalization for multiple ASMs. SIGNIFICANCE: This study comprehensively characterizes the safety profiles of newer ASMs and highlights the importance of tailored AE monitoring based on specific ASM and patient characteristics.
Adapting the international classification of cognitive disorders in epilepsy to Shwachman diamond syndrome: a feasibility study.推荐
PMID 43042571 · Archives of clinical neuropsychology : the official journal of the National Academy of Neuropsychologists · 2026 Nov 06
作者：Kerr EN, Rommens JM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Shwachman Diamond syndrome (SDS; OMIM#260400) is a rare multisystemic ribosomopathy associated with prevalent inherent neuropsychological issues. Understanding cognitive phenotypes remains limited by disease rarity and substantial heterogeneity in neuropsychological performance. The International Classification of Cognitive Disorders in Epilepsy (IC-CoDE), provides an innovative approach to harmonize data using universal cognitive domains. We evaluated its adaptability for SDS (CoD-SDS) to better characterized cognitive phenotypes and support future international collaborative research and ultimately enable more precise patient care. METHODS: IC-CoDE was adapted to address SDS-specific concerns. Using established IC-CoDE guidelines, we: determined impairment thresholds of -1 and -1.5 standard deviations for tests scores; defined domain disruption as impairment on two or more tests within each domain; and classified cognitive phenotypes based on the number of impaired domains. Neuropsychological data from 32 children with SDS were analyzed. RESULTS: -1.0 and -1.5 SD thresholds revealed intact phenotypes in 1 and 9 patients. Single-, bi-domain, and generalized vulnerabilities were evident in 5 and 9, 8 and 4, and 18 and 10 patients, respectively. All patients with impairments showed vulnerabilities in executive functioning and/or attention/processing speed. CONCLUSIONS: Applying a cognitive classification methodology to children with SDS accentuated individual performance and underscored the cognitive morbidity hidden by group means. This feasibility study supports the development of a consensus-based SDS classification system for larger international cohorts that could be harmonized through the SDS registries. Its implementation will be critical for defining the nature, frequency, severity, and correlates of cognitive vulnerabilities to inform precision health and achieve optimized outcomes.
Drug-resistant epilepsy: A retrospective observational study at a tertiary care hospital.推荐
PMID 43041957 · Pakistan journal of medical sciences · 2026 Oct
作者：Zaidi S, Barry U, Ali M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To determine the prevalence and predictors of drug-resistant epilepsy among patients with epilepsy. METHODOLOGY: A retrospective observational study was conducted at the Neurology Department of Liaquat National Hospital, Karachi, for the period from November, 2024 to October, 2025. Patients with epilepsy were enrolled, excluding neonates and those with psychiatric comorbidities or active substance abuse. Participants were recruited consecutively. Descriptive statistics summarized patient characteristics, while chi-square tests evaluated associations between variables such as drug adherence, epilepsy type, and antiepileptic drug (ASM) selection. Odds ratios (ORs) with 95% confidence intervals were calculated to quantify the strength of these associations. RESULTS: A total of 382 participants were included (49.5% male, 50.5% female), with a mean age of 31.73 +/- 16.82 years and a mean seizure onset age of 25.3 years. The majority were urban residents with higher educational levels and 19.4% had refractory epilepsy. On multivariate logistic regression analysis, normal MRI brain findings (a OR = 0.29, 95% CI: 0.142-0.570; p < 0.001), normal EEG findings (a OR = 0.50, 95% CI: 0.256-0.974; p = 0.042) and adherence to the given treatment (a OR= 0.516, 95% CI: 0.234-1.138; p=0.101) have lower odds of having drug resistance. Lower level of education (a OR = 3.496, 95% CI: 1.687-7.245; p < 0.001), and a history of status epilepticus (a OR = 8.029, 95% CI: 4.186-15.400: p <0.001) have higher odds of refractory epilepsy. CONCLUSION: By identifying key predictors of drug-resistant epilepsy, our study contributes to a better understanding of factors associated with treatment resistance and may inform future interventions.
Reduced penetrance and phenotypic heterogeneity of 16p13.11 copy number variations: Insights from a Turkish cohort.推荐

摘要：OBJECTIVE: Copy number variations (CNVs) involving the 16p13.11 locus have been increasingly recognized as important contributors to the etiology of neurodevelopmental and multisystem disorders. In this study, we characterized the clinical and molecular spectrum of 16p13.11 CNVs in a Turkish cohort and evaluated the associated phenotypic variability. METHODS: 16p13.11 CNVs identified in 10 unrelated families were evaluated. Clinical, neurodevelopmental, and dysmorphic features were systematically assessed. RESULTS: Among cases with microdeletions (1 female, 3 males), the most frequent findings included language delay, motor delay, learning difficulties, and epilepsy, accompanied by autism spectrum disorder, anxiety, and structural brain anomalies. In the microduplication group (9 females, 4 males, and 1 fetus), motor and language delay, intellectual disability, and epilepsy were observed, together with behavioral abnormalities such as hyperactivity and self-injurious behavior. Segregation analysis showed that one patient with a 16p13.11 deletion inherited the CNV from an affected parent, whereas the remaining patients were de novo. In contrast, all patients with 16p13.11 duplications inherited the rearrangement from asymptomatic parents. CONCLUSION: Our findings highlight reduced penetrance in 16p13.11 duplications, supporting the concept of this region as a susceptibility locus for neurodevelopmental disorders. In contrast, the predominantly de novo occurrence of deletions and the presence of subtle findings in inherited cases emphasize the need for detailed familial evaluation.
First-trimester maternal exposures and offspring congenital heart defects: a Hungarian population-based case-control study, 1980-2009.浏览
PMID 43041305 · Frontiers in public health · 2026 11 02
作者：Suo Z, Váradi A, Hegyi P, Rancz A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Congenital heart defects (CHDs) are a major cause of infant morbidity and mortality. Identifying modifiable maternal exposures during early pregnancy is important for CHD prevention and maternal and child health. OBJECTIVES: To examine associations between first-trimester maternal active smoking and alcohol consumption and offspring CHDs, and to explore associations of selected maternal diseases and related treatments with CHD outcomes. METHODS: This population-based case-control study used data from the Hungarian Case-Control Surveillance of Congenital Abnormalities (1980-2009), including 7,362 CHD cases and 56,894 controls. Overall CHDs were the primary outcome, with ventricular septal defect (VSD) and atrial septal defect (ASD) examined as secondary outcomes. Complete-case multivariable logistic regression estimated odds ratios (ORs) for smoking and alcohol consumption, with mutual adjustment and additional adjustment for maternal age, socioeconomic status, and offspring sex. Multiple imputation was performed as a sensitivity analysis. Maternal disease and treatment analyses were crude and exploratory. RESULTS: First-trimester maternal smoking was associated with higher odds of overall CHDs (adjusted OR 1.50, 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.30-1.71), VSD (1.36, 1.11-1.66) and ASD (1.62, 1.35-1.93). Multiple-imputation estimates were similar to the complete-case estimates. No statistically significant associations were observed between maternal alcohol consumption and CHD outcomes in either the adjusted complete-case analysis or the multiple-imputation analysis. Maternal epilepsy, migraine, anxiety disorder and anemia showed positive crude associations with one or more CHD outcomes. CONCLUSION: Maternal smoking reported during the first trimester was associated with higher odds of overall CHDs, VSD, and ASD, supporting smoking cessation before conception and during pregnancy as a prevention priority. Exploratory associations involving maternal diseases and treatments require further investigation and should not be used to infer treatment effects or guide treatment decisions.
Performance of the NightWatch seizure detection device on the leg compared to the standard arm placement: A pilot study.推荐
PMID 43041259 · Epilepsy & behavior reports · 2026 10 29
作者：Krom M, Lemmen EM, Evers SMAA, Lazeron RHC et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Nighttime monitoring is crucial for individuals with epilepsy, both to reduce the risk of injuries and to help prevent sudden unexpected death in epilepsy. NightWatch, a seizure detection device that can detect nocturnal seizures by using heart rate (HR) and movement parameters, has been shown to be effective when worn on the upper arm. This study piloted whether wearing NightWatch on the lower leg provides comparable seizure detection sensitivity. In this prospective pilot study, NightWatch users wore a second device on the lower leg for four weeks. The positive percent agreement (PPA) was determined for the leg-worn NightWatch compared to the arm module. Differences in signal quality and time spent in rest state (sensor in horizontal position) were evaluated both per participant and across the population. Ten participants (mean age: 12.5 years, range = 5-25) were included in the study. In total, 245 nights were analyzed. PPA ranged from 11.1% to 100%, yielding an overall mean PPA of 76.8% and median of 100%. The quality of HR signal for the leg module was comparable to the arm module (p = 0.1602). The leg module showed variability across patients and, overall, registered the rest state significantly less often than the arm module (p = 0.0059). This pilot study demonstrated that NightWatch performance on the leg varies across participants, with comparable results to arm use in some participants but reduced accuracy in others. This study has been registered in the Overview of Medical Research in the Netherlands (OMON) (NL-OMON57506).
Report-derived interictal epileptiform discharge burden is associated with 7-day antiseizure medication decisions after pediatric video electroencephalography.推荐
PMID 43041243 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 11 02
作者：Zhu F, Xu W, Gao Y, Kang S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To determine whether a three-level interictal epileptiform discharge (IED) burden abstracted from qualitative wording in issued pediatric video electroencephalography (VEEG) reports, rather than directly measured spike frequency, was associated with antiseizure medication (ASM) decisions within 7 days. METHODS: This single-center retrospective cohort included 216 VEEG report episodes from 181 children. Two reviewers independently classified each report as showing no, low, or moderate-to-high IED burden while unaware of the standardized 7-day outcome; disagreements were adjudicated. We separately analyzed a diagnostic/initiation cohort for new ASM initiation and an established-epilepsy cohort for exploratory treatment intensification. Associations were assessed using exact and trend tests and logistic regression with patient-clustered robust standard errors. RESULTS: Agreement was 211/216 (97.7%); linear-weighted Cohen κ was 0.975 (patient-clustered bootstrap 95% CI 0.947-0.995). The diagnostic/initiation cohort comprised 113 reports from 112 children, with 36 new ASM initiations. Initiation rates were 13.0%, 42.9%, and 70.0% for no, low, and moderate-to-high burden, respectively (trend p < 0.001). The adjusted odds ratio per higher burden grade was 2.89 (95% CI 1.58-5.30; p < 0.001). The established-epilepsy cohort comprised 103 reports from 76 children, with 13 intensification events. Moderate-to-high burden versus none/low yielded an odds ratio of 4.73 (95% CI 1.31-17.08; p = 0.018), but complementary analyses were not statistically significant. CONCLUSION: Report-derived IED burden showed a strong graded association with new ASM initiation within 7 days. The treatment-intensification finding was exploratory. These data describe prescribing context and do not establish causality, treatment benefit, or an IED-based treatment threshold.
Forced normalization presenting with explosive aggressive behavior following bilateral deep brain stimulation of the anterior nucleus of the thalamus: A case report.推荐
PMID 43041216 · Epilepsy & behavior reports · 2026 11 06
作者：Matsunaga S, Kanno A, Enatsu R, Ishida K et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Forced normalization (FN) is a rare phenomenon characterized by the paradoxical emergence of psychiatric symptoms following successful seizure control and electroencephalographic improvement. Herein, we report a rare case of FN that predominantly presented with explosive aggressive behavior following bilateral deep brain stimulation of the anterior nucleus of the thalamus (ANT-DBS) for drug-resistant epilepsy. A 10-year-old boy with drug-resistant epilepsy underwent ANT-DBS after receiving multiple antiseizure medications; vagus nerve stimulation failed to achieve seizure control. Progressive escalation in stimulation intensity achieved complete seizure remission. However, the patient subsequently developed acute psychiatric and behavioral deterioration, including severe agitation, emotional dysregulation, and aggressive outbursts. These psychiatric symptoms partially improved with a reduction in the stimulation intensity. This case highlights that atypical psychiatric manifestations of FN may occur following successful seizure suppression using ANT-DBS. Clinicians should recognize that aggressive behavioral symptoms may be a rare manifestation of FN associated with limbic neuromodulation therapy.

Screening of residual solvents in cocaine seizures by HS-SPME-GC-MS: Application to multi-class solvent profiling.推荐
PMID 43040781 · Journal of forensic sciences · 2026 11 16
作者：Góngora J
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：A headspace solid-phase microextraction coupled to gas chromatography-mass spectrometry (HS-SPME-GC-MS) method was developed for the screening of 50 residual solvents (RS), including alcohols, ethers, esters, ketones, aliphatics, aromatics, and chlorinated solvents. Given the chemical diversity of the compounds, a diluent selection step was critically addressed to ensure analyte compatibility. Some key GC parameters were optimized using a one-variable-at-a-time (OVAT) approach, whereas HS-SPME extraction parameters (extraction time, temperature, desorption time) were optimized using a design of experiments (DoE) strategy. A semi-quantitative assessment using model solvent mixtures supported method optimization and characterization of the target compounds. Quantitative validation was performed for the most frequently encountered residual solvents, demonstrating good selectivity, coefficients of determination (R2 = 0.981-0.998), low detection limits (0.07-0.29 mg L-1), satisfactory recoveries (70.2%-117.4%), and acceptable intra- and inter-day precision (RSD <15%). The proposed approach shows adequate performance for screening volatile residues in complex drug matrices such as cocaine hydrochloride. However, because the extraction strategy is based on saturated aqueous NaCl and does not require organic solvents, it can potentially be extended to other drug matrices that are soluble in aqueous media. This characteristic makes the approach particularly suitable for routine forensic applications. A kinetic study further revealed compound-dependent profiles, with rapid initial sorption followed by stabilization (e.g., aliphatics and ethers), whereas aromatics and several ketones showed a more progressive increase, indicating that equilibrium was not generally reached under the evaluated HS-SPME conditions.
Polypharmacy and use of prescription medication in children and adolescents with life-limiting conditions-a nationwide cohort study.浏览
PMID 43040175 · European journal of pediatrics · 2026 11 16
作者：Høeg BL, Grell K, Neergaard MA, Hain R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：UNLABELLED: Children and adolescents (CA) with life-limiting conditions (LLC) are living longer thanks to better treatment but face substantial medication burden. We investigated polypharmacy and use of prescription medication in CAs with LLC compared to a comparison population of CAs with no LLC up to 25 years after diagnosis. This retrospective nationwide cohort study included all patients aged 0-21 years born in Denmark and diagnosed with an LLC between January 1, 1996, and December 31, 2021. Sociodemographic and prescription data were obtained from national registries. We assessed polypharmacy and the mean cumulative number of prescriptions at 1, 5, 10 and 25 years after diagnosis. We assessed concurrent polypharmacy (≥ 5 different prescriptions within 120 days before a follow-up time) and sequential polypharmacy (≥ 4 regularly dispensed prescriptions, defined as at least three prescriptions within 12 months before a follow-up time). We identified 98,508 CAs with LLC and 492,540 CAs with no LLC matched 1:5 on sex and age at diagnosis. Median follow-up was 9.4 years (interquartile range 4.8, 15.5) with 3608 deaths. One year after diagnosis, 4.53% of CAs with LLC were using ≥ 5 unique medications compared to 1.57% of population controls, while 2.1% of CAs with LLC were using ≥ 4 medications regularly compared to only 0.1% of population controls. CAs with a neurological or neuromuscular diagnosis and from families with lower education levels had the highest medication use. CONCLUSIONS: Healthcare providers of CAs with LLCs must be aware of the impact of long-term polypharmacy and balance symptom control with the complexity of medication regiments, especially in children from families with lower educational levels. WHAT IS KNOWN: • Children and adolescents (CA) with life-limiting conditions (LLC) are living longer and often receive multiple medications for long-term symptom management. • However, the prevalence of polypharmacy and patterns of prescription medication use over time across diagnostic groups and sociodemograpic factors in this population compared to the general pediatric population are unknown. WHAT IS NEW: • The prevalence of polypharmacy was almost threefold higher among CAs with LLC compared to the general pediatric population at one year after diagnosis, with smaller yet elevated differences up to 25 years after diagnosis. • Anti-infectives, anti-epileptics, respiratory drugs, and anxiolytics/sedatives were most prescribed, and the highest overall medication use was observed among CAs with a neurological or neuromuscular diagnosis and those from families with lower parental educational attainment.

Low carbohydrate high fat diet curbs glioblastoma by shifting lipids and limiting cancer stem cell metabolic flexibility.浏览
PMID 43039977 · Communications biology · 2026 07 27
作者：Sarkar S, Ashokan A, Surnar B, Banik B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Metabolic rewiring fuels tumorigenicity of cancer stem cells contributing to universal glioblastoma (GBM) recurrence. Invasive glioblastoma stem cells (GSCs), already unresectable, acquire tumorigenic properties that confer therapy resistance. Using samples from both recurrent and newly diagnosed GBM patients, we identified fatty acid oxidation (FAO) as a key pathway supporting GSC tumorigenicity. Transcriptomic profiling, compared against non-malignant human astrocytes from epilepsy patients, highlighted an FAO-enriched signature in GSCs. In vivo studies showed that tumor formation by these GSCs could be modulated by altering the availability of metabolizable lipids in the brain, with beneficial effects observed under a low carbohydrate high fat diet (LCHFD). The lipid metabolism contributing to GSC mediated tumor formation was confirmed using metabolomic and lipidomic analyses. Gut microbiome analyses revealed that LCHFD shapes the microbiome and modulates the circadian network. By targeting FAO in the postoperative period before chemoradiation, LCHFD offers a non-pharmacologic strategy to delay GBM recurrence. This study suggests a mechanistic framework for dietary modulation as a therapeutic approach to improve GBM outcomes.
SCN8A-Related Epilepsy and/or Neurodevelopmental Disorders推荐
PMID 27559564 · GeneReviews® · 2016-8-25（更新 2026-11-17）
作者：Hammer MF, Hack J, Schreiber JM
链接：PubMed
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：图书章节/更新
中科院分区：不适用（图书章节）
JCR分区：不适用（图书章节）
摘要：CLINICAL CHARACTERISTICS: SCN8A-related epilepsy and/or neurodevelopmental disorders (SCN8A-E/NDD) encompass a spectrum of phenotypes. Epilepsy phenotypes include developmental and epileptic encephalopathy (DEE) associated with severe developmental delays and usually pharmacoresistant epilepsy with multiple seizure types; mild-to-moderate developmental and epileptic encephalopathy (mild/modDEE) with partially treatable epilepsy; benign familial infantile epilepsy (BFIE, or self-limited familial infantile epilepsy, SeLFIE), with typically normal cognition and medically treatable seizures; neurodevelopmental delays with generalized epilepsy (NDDwGE); and neurodevelopmental disorder without epilepsy (NDDwoE) with mild-to-moderate intellectual disability (though it can be severe in ~10% of affected individuals). Hypotonia and movement disorders including dystonia, ataxia, and choreoathetosis are common in some phenotypes. Sudden unexpected death in epilepsy (SUDEP) has been reported. DIAGNOSIS/TESTING: The diagnosis of SCN8A-E/NDD is established in a proband with suggestive findings and a heterozygous pathogenic variant in SCN8A identified by molecular genetic testing. MANAGEMENT: Targeted therapies: Sodium channel blockers represent targeted therapies for some SCN8A-related epilepsy phenotypes, including SCN8A-BFIE, SCN8A-mild/modDEE, and SCN8A-DEE with onset in the first year of life. Oxcarbazepine and carbamazepine are considered first-line treatments for these early-onset epilepsy phenotypes as well. Valproate, ethosuximide, or lamotrigine have been favored for later-onset SCN8A-NDDwGE. Supportive care: Seizure control should be managed by a pediatric neurologist with expertise in epilepsy management who is ideally familiar with the pharmacotherapy for SCN8A-related epilepsy. Vigorous attempts to control seizures are warranted, and counseling on SUDEP risk is recommended. Supportive care to improve quality of life, maximize function, and reduce complications is recommended, ideally involving multidisciplinary care by specialists in relevant fields. Surveillance: Periodic evaluations for neurologic, developmental, musculoskeletal, and/or behavioral complications. Monitoring with EEG and other modalities such as video EEG telemetry or ambulatory EEG when new or different seizure types are suspected. Because of the increased risk of SUDEP, monitoring of seizures in individuals at higher risk, including those with generalized tonic-clonic seizures and/or nighttime seizures, is warranted. Assessment of sleep issues over time is recommended. Evaluation of relatives at risk: It is appropriate to clarify the genetic status of apparently asymptomatic older and younger at-risk relatives of an affected individual in order to identify those who are at risk for developing seizures. This typically entails targeted molecular genetic testing for the known pathogenic variant(s) in the family. Pregnancy management: Pregnant women should receive counseling regarding the risks and benefits of using anti-seizure medications during pregnancy; the advantages and disadvantages of increasing maternal periconceptional folic acid supplementation to 4,000 µg daily; the effects of pregnancy on anti-seizure medication metabolism; and the effect of pregnancy on maternal seizure control. Agents/circumstances to avoid: Several families have reported worsening of seizures with levetiracetam. GENETIC COUNSELING: SCN8A-E/NDD are inherited in an autosomal dominant manner. Individuals with more severe SCN8A-related phenotypes are more likely to have a de novo pathogenic variant than individuals with milder SCN8A-related phenotypes. Each child of an individual with SCN8A-related epilepsy and/or neurodevelopmental disorders has a 50% chance of inheriting the SCN8A pathogenic variant. Once the SCN8A pathogenic variant has been identified in an affected family member, prenatal and preimplantation genetic testing are possible.
Coincidence detection in aspiny interneuron dendrites.浏览
PMID 43039696 · eNeuro · 2026 11 16
作者：Griesius S, Richardson A, Mercier M, Kullmann DM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Recent evidence for long-term potentiation (LTP) at glutamatergic synapses on GABAergic interneurons calls for close attention to the sub-cellular phenomena that underlie the induction of plasticity in aspiny dendrites. Neurogliaform interneurons exhibit large NMDA receptor-mediated currents, Hebbian LTP and supralinear summation of glutamatergic signals converging on dendritic compartments. Dendritic supralinearity in interneurons does not require sodium channels. Here we investigate the principles governing supralinear synaptic integration and its interaction with backpropagating action potentials in the synaptic plasticity context. We developed a biophysically realistic, multi-compartmental model of a murine (Mus musculus, male and female) hippocampal neurogliaform interneuron, with dendritic architecture and ion channel densities tuned to match ex vivo recordings. The interaction between synaptic inputs and action potentials was tested with patch-clamp electrophysiology and two-photon imaging of dendritic calcium-dependent fluorescence transients. Whether simulated glutamatergic excitatory postsynaptic potentials are clustered on a dendritic fragment or dispersed across the entire arbor, they summate supralinearly as assessed from the calculated somatic voltage response. Clustered inputs induce more pronounced local depolarizations and calcium transients than dispersed inputs. Action potentials generated at the soma backpropagate throughout the dendritic arbor but are attenuated at branch points. Coincident synaptic input and backpropagating action potentials relieve this attenuation, further enhance dendritic depolarizations, and greatly potentiate calcium influx via NMDA receptors. Despite differences in dendritic anatomy and in the roles of sodium channels, the interplay of orthodromic and antidromic voltage propagation and resultant calcium signal amplification exhibits remarkable similarities between neurogliaform interneurons and principal cells.Significance Statement Neurogliaform interneurons are key regulators of brain circuit activity, yet the dendritic mechanisms that enable these inhibitory cells to undergo synaptic plasticity remain poorly understood. Combining biophysically realistic computer modeling with electrophysiology and two-photon calcium imaging, we show that coincident synaptic input and backpropagating action potentials strongly amplify dendritic depolarization and calcium influx in these interneurons. Although their dendrites lack spines and do not rely on sodium-channel-dependent dendritic spikes, neurogliaform interneurons nonetheless use computational principles remarkably similar to those of excitatory principal neurons. These findings advance the understanding of how inhibitory circuits support learning-related plasticity and may provide foundational insight into disorders such as epilepsy and schizophrenia, in which abnormal dendritic signalling and inhibitory dysfunction are implicated.

Long-Term Treatment Outcomes and Remission Durability in Absence Epilepsy: A Nationwide Claims-Based Cohort Study.推荐
PMID 43039412 · Journal of clinical neurology (Seoul, Korea) · 2026 Nov
作者：Lee J, Choi A, Yoo IH

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

摘要：BACKGROUND AND PURPOSE: Population-level evidence comparing long-term treatment trajectories in childhood absence epilepsy (CAE) and juvenile absence epilepsy (JAE) remains limited. We evaluated claims-defined remission patterns and treatment persistence in a nationwide cohort. METHODS: Using the South Korean Health Insurance Review and Assessment Service database (2011-2025), we identified newly treated probable absence epilepsy and operationally categorized patients as CAE (index age, 0-9 years) or JAE (index age, 10-17 years). Outcomes were claims-defined first remission, antiseizure medication (ASM) reinitiation after claims-defined first remission, claims-defined final remission, and severe post-index outcomes. A multicenter chart review assessed case identification and age-based CAE/JAE concordance. RESULTS: The cohort included 1,775 patients (1,279 CAE; 496 JAE). Case identification had 78.0% sensitivity, 99.6% specificity, and 94.1% positive predictive value; age-based classification matched clinician-adjudicated CAE/JAE diagnoses in all 32 confirmed cases. In the 2013-2016 cohort with mature follow-up, claims-defined final remission occurred in 67.0% and 34.1% of CAE and JAE cases, respectively, and the median time to claims-defined final remission was 5.89 years in CAE but was not reached in JAE. JAE showed lower claims-defined first remission, more frequent ASM reinitiation after claims-defined first remission, and lower claims-defined final remission than CAE. No significant between-group difference was detected in claims-defined status epilepticus or drug-resistant epilepsy. CONCLUSIONS: This study provides population-level benchmarks for long-term outcomes in absence epilepsy. Notably, durable claims-defined ASM-free remission was not universal or rapid in CAE, and JAE showed lower remission durability and greater treatment persistence.

Vagus nerve stimulation in a patient with bilateral cochlear implants: potential device interactions and clinical considerations.推荐
PMID 43039383 · BMJ case reports · 2026 11 16
作者：Tsuchimoto K, Nagata K, Kashio A, Fujitani S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Vagus nerve stimulation (VNS) is one of the standard treatments for drug-resistant epilepsy, and cochlear implants (CIs) are one of the standard treatments for profound sensorineural hearing loss. However, case reports combining both CIs and VNS devices are extremely rare, and there is limited knowledge regarding interference between these devices and their safety. We report the case of a 13-year-old boy with drug-resistant epilepsy associated with congenital cytomegalovirus infection, who had previously undergone bilateral cochlear implantation. He presented with multiple seizure types and multifocal epileptogenic discharges on long-term video-electroencephalography. Despite polytherapy, the seizures persisted and VNS was introduced. Postoperative recovery was uneventful, with no device interference between the CIs and VNS. This case demonstrates that patients with bilateral CIs can safely undergo VNS implantation with appropriate perioperative management and postoperative follow-up.
Exploring anaesthetic strategies in rare disorders: a case of molybdenum cofactor deficiency.推荐
PMID 43039315 · Revista espanola de anestesiologia y reanimacion · 2026 11 16
作者：Silva A, Araújo G, Duque M
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Molybdenum cofactor deficiency (MoCD) is a rare metabolic disorder that presents significant challenges for anaesthesiologists due to intractable seizures, neurotoxicity, and systemic complications. We describe the sedation management of a 15-month-old child with MoCD Type A undergoing magnetic resonance imaging. Adequate depth of sedation was achieved with sevoflurane via face mask under spontaneous ventilation. The procedure lasted 37 min and was uneventful. Anaesthesia in patients with MoCD must be carefully planned to ensure the airway remains patent and seizures are controlled. Sevoflurane was chosen for its GABA-mediated effects, and proved effective. This case demonstrates the safety of sevoflurane for short, non-invasive procedures in patients with MoCD.
Convergent mechanisms between GLP-1 and adenosine in epilepsy: Opportunities for therapeutic exploitation?推荐
PMID 43039199 · Brain research bulletin · 2026 11 16
作者：Cho H, Boison D
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Epilepsy is a serious neurological condition characterized by spontaneous recurrent seizures affecting approximately 83 million people worldwide. Type 2 diabetes (T2D) and obesity are highly prevalent in persons with epilepsy, significantly influencing long-term outcomes, quality of life, and mortality. Traditionally, glucagon-like peptide-1 receptor agonists (GLP-1 RAs) are used to treat T2D and obesity, and they have recently been highlighted as a therapeutic intervention for neurological disorders, including epilepsy. In epilepsy, adenosine signaling plays a crucial role, as it not only controls seizures but also affects epileptogenesis. As a metabolite and metabolic regulator adenosine is also implicated in the pathophysiology of T2D and obesity. The involvement of adenosine in both conditions raises the possibility of convergent mechanisms implicated in the antiepileptic effects of GLP-1 RAs. This review provides a comprehensive overview of the effects of GLP-1 RAs and adenosine in epilepsy and their convergent mechanisms, which could serve as a basis for synergistic therapeutic applications and insight into their underlying mechanisms. GLP-1 RAs affect several mechanisms shared with adenosine signaling. These include reduction of astrogliosis, neuroinflammation, and mTOR activity, and restoration of the excitation and inhibition balance by suppressing the glutamatergic pathway and upregulating the GABAergic pathway. In peripheral organs, direct convergent mechanisms between adenosine signaling and GLP-1 RAs include interactions between adenosine deaminase and dipeptidylpeptidase-4, lipid metabolism, and pancreatic β-cell homeostasis. These convergent mechanisms highlight the need to further investigate possible bidirectional relationships with the involvement of adenosine in both conditions.
Bimetallic Silver-Gold Nanoparticle-Decorated Nitrogen-doped Graphite Platform for Sensitive Electrochemical Therapeutic Monitoring of an Antiepileptic Drug.推荐
PMID 43039165 · Analytical biochemistry · 2026 11 16
作者：Soliman SS, Mahmoud AM, Hussein OG
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Carbamazepine is a cornerstone therapy for epilepsy and several neurological disorders; however, its narrow therapeutic range and pronounced pharmacokinetic variability necessitate reliable quantification. Herein, a nanostructured electrochemical sensing platform based on a silver-gold/nitrogen-doped graphite-modified carbon paste electrode (Ag-Au/N-doped graphite/CPE) was developed for sensitive and selective carbamazepine determination. Incorporation of the Ag-Au/N-doped graphite nanocomposite enhanced the voltammetric response compared with the bare carbon paste electrode indicating improved interfacial electrochemical properties and charge-transfer behaviour. Electrode composition, supporting-electrolyte pH, and differential pulse voltammetric parameters were systematically optimized. Complementary cyclic voltammetry and electrochemical impedance spectroscopy confirmed improved interfacial charge-transfer characteristics while scan-rate investigations indicated predominantly diffusion-controlled oxidation. Under optimized conditions, differential pulse voltammetry exhibited a linear response over 1.0-100.0 μM with limits of detection and quantification of 0.31 and 0.95 μM, respectively. The method demonstrated satisfactory precision with repeatability and intermediate precision relative standard deviations of 1.901% and 1.995%, respectively. Selectivity was evaluated using individual pharmaceutical compounds and carbamazepine containing mixtures showing interference values ranging from 0.2% to 9.4% under the investigated conditions. Application to commercial Tegretol® tablets yielded 100.21 ± 1.371% of the labeled content. Importantly, the sensor maintained satisfactory quantitative performance in spiked human plasma over 10.0-900.0 μM achieving a limit of detection of 7.45 μM and recoveries of 96.55-99.58%. These findings highlight the synergistic contribution of bimetallic Ag-Au nanoparticles and nitrogen-doped graphite establishing a simple, sensitive, rapid, reproducible, and cost-effective electrochemical approach for carbamazepine determination in pharmaceutical formulations and complex biological matrices.
DNA methylation profiling of the RELN gene using MSP in Iraqi pediatric patients with epilepsy: A case-control study.推荐
PMID 43038919 · Epilepsy research · 2026 11 13
作者：Jumaah SS, Khalaf AF, Naeem NT, Hassan MJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy is one of the most common neurological disorders in children and is influenced by both genetic and epigenetic mechanisms. Altered DNA methylation may contribute to disease susceptibility through changes in gene regulation. The RELN gene is involved in neuronal migration, synaptic plasticity, and brain development, making it a potential target for epigenetic investigation. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to evaluate the association between RELN methylation levels and pediatric epilepsy and to investigate its potential diagnostic value. METHODS: A case-control study was conducted involving 47 pediatric epilepsy patients and 39 healthy controls. Genomic DNA was extracted and validated prior to methylation analysis. Quantitative real-time PCR using methylation-specific primers was performed to determine RELN methylation percentage. Statistical analyses included Mann-Whitney testing, logistic regression analysis, receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis, and Spearman correlation analysis. RESULTS: RELN methylation percentage was significantly higher in patients compared with controls (11.60% [8.88-14.61] vs. 7.77% [6.87-10.73], P < 0.0001). ROC analysis demonstrated good discriminatory performance with an AUC of 0.811 (95% CI: 0.712-0.887), sensitivity of 80.85%, and specificity of 74.36% at a cutoff value > 8.545%. Logistic regression analysis identified RELN methylation as an independent predictor of epilepsy (adjusted OR=1.537, 95% CI: 1.240-1.906, P < 0.001). No significant correlations were observed between RELN methylation and age, age at first seizure, or days since the last seizure. CONCLUSION: Increased RELN methylation was significantly associated with pediatric epilepsy and independently predicted disease status, suggesting a potential role for RELN methylation as an epigenetic biomarker in epilepsy.
Advancing neuropsychological and neurodevelopmental outcome measurement in pediatric epilepsy surgery.推荐
PMID 43038918 · Epilepsy research · 2026 11 14
作者：Davalan W
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：Epilepsy surgery is the most effective treatment for appropriately selected children with drug-resistant epilepsy, however, surgical success continues to be defined primarily by seizure outcomes. Neuropsychological and neurodevelopmental outcomes are inconsistently assessed, and children with substantial developmental or neurological impairment may be systematically underrepresented in postoperative outcome data. We conducted an overview of systematic reviews published from 2015 to 2025 that reported outcomes after pediatric epilepsy surgery, with particular emphasis on cognitive, developmental, adaptive, behavioral, and quality-of-life domains. Among 50 eligible systematic reviews, only 22 (44%) reported neuropsychological or cognitive outcomes. Global cognitive measures predominated, whereas language, memory, executive function, attention, and processing speed were substantially less frequently assessed. Only nine reviews quantitatively synthesized neurocognitive or developmental outcomes, and substantial heterogeneity in assessment instruments, baseline testing, and postoperative follow-up limited cross-study comparability. These findings highlight the need to move beyond whether neuropsychological testing is performed toward standardized measurement of meaningful longitudinal change. We propose a framework incorporating common core outcome domains, prespecified assessment intervals, developmentally appropriate and floor-sensitive measures, and inclusion of children unable to complete conventional norm-referenced testing. Prospective integration of harmonized neuropsychological trajectories with structural and functional connectomic measures may further clarify how seizure control, surgical network disruption, and neuroplasticity shape postoperative development. Pediatric epilepsy surgery outcomes should therefore encompass both seizure control and preservation or improvement of cognitive, developmental, adaptive, and functional trajectories.
