JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: The traditional Bugis medicinal manuscript, Lontaraq Pabbura, preserves traditional ethnopharmacological knowledge, yet many of its remedies and medicinal plants remain underexplored in modern scientific and historical research. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to explore medicinal plants and remedies recorded in Lontaraq Pabbura, identify traditional disease terms and evaluate their ethnopharmacological relevance. MATERIALS AND METHODS: The manuscript (MS 12915.(d.)) from the SOAS Library, University of London, was analyzed for provenance, content, and historical context. Vernacular plant names and disease terms were transliterated and evaluated using the Historical-Pharmacognostic-Ethnobotanical-Comparative (HPEC) framework. Traditional disease terms were correlated with modern biomedical concepts through linguistic and ethnomedical perspectives and cross-checked against regional ethnobotanical literature. Candidate plant species were identified and validated based on historical sources and comparative ethnobotanical evidence to assess regional continuity. RESULTS: 21 plant names and 14 disease terms were analyzed. Remedies addressed general malaise, seizure-like conditions, menstrual disorders, weakness, skin infections, eye disorders, inflammation, and neurological disorders. Plants identified with high ethnobotanical confidence, including Cocos nucifera, Tamarindus indica, Piper nigrum, and Syzygium aromaticum, demonstrated correspondence with traditional uses recorded in regional ethnobotanical sources. Comparative analysis revealed continuity with other Austronesian and Southeast Asian ethnobotanical traditions, while several plants remain underexplored, highlighting opportunities for further chemical and pharmacological investigation. CONCLUSIONS: Lontaraq Pabbura provides a detailed record of Bugis medicinal knowledge, combining historically attested remedies with regional ethnobotanical parallels and offering candidate plants for further phytochemical and pharmacological investigation relevant to integrative medicine.
Automated extraction of seizures and ictal-interictal continuum patterns from EEG reports to enable large-scale neurophysiology and neurocritical care research.推荐
PMID 43038589 · International journal of medical informatics · 2026 11 13
作者：Sartipi S, Godfrey DS, Tauțan AM, Fernandes MP et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Critical clinical information in EEG reports is often locked in free text, limiting large-scale research. This work develops and validates an automated, cross-institutional pipeline to extract seizures, rhythmic patterns, and ictal-interictal continuum features for population-level neurophysiology studies. METHODS: We developed a five-stage hybrid pipeline comprising: (1) text normalization, (2) rule-based pattern filtering to focus the analysis on relevant report segments, (3) segment-focused prompt generation, (4) large language model (LLM) inference for structured attribute extraction, and (5) post-processing. This hybrid design combines the consistency of rule-based screening with the flexibility of LLM-based extraction from variable free-text EEG reports. The pipeline was applied to 156,582 EEG reports from three health systems (two adult, one pediatric). Performance was evaluated using a balanced set of 1,500 expert-reviewed samples, which extracted seizure presence, count, burden, and timing, as well as the frequency and prevalence of periodic and rhythmic patterns. Patterns included lateralized periodic discharges (LPD), generalized periodic discharges (GPD), and generalized/lateralized rhythmic delta activity (GRDA/LRDA). RESULTS: The pipeline achieved high performance across health systems for seizure (average accuracy 0.94) and periodic and rhythmic pattern detection (LPD: 0.97 [95 % CI: 0.94-0.99], GPD: 0.96 [0.92-0.98], GRDA/LRDA: 0.97 [0.94-0.99]). Seizure specificity was 0.72 at two adult institutions (95 % CI: 0.66-0.77) and 0.99 at the other two (one adult and one pediatric). Frequency and prevalence extraction showed strong agreement with mean values 0.94 and 0.93, respectively. High-frequency discharges (≥2 Hz) were associated with higher seizure occurrence within 24 and 48 h in descriptive analysis. CONCLUSIONS: This study introduces a scalable method for EEG analysis using clinical narratives, linking rhythmic patterns to seizure progression and supporting critical care risk stratification and large-scale EEG outcome prediction research.
Health-Related Quality of Life Across Neurologic Conditions: A Cross-Sectional Cohort Study Using Real-World Patient-Reported Outcomes.浏览
PMID 43038501 · Neurology. Clinical practice · 2026 11 13
作者：Lapin B, Li Y, Miller DM, Katzan I
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: Health-related quality of life (HRQOL) is an important outcome within value-based care for patients with neurologic conditions. The American Academy of Neurology recommends the use of Patient-Reported Outcomes Measurement Information System (PROMIS) Global Health as a patient-reported outcome performance measure to assess stability or improvement over 1 year. However, interpretation of this measure requires an understanding of HRQOL across neurologic populations. Our study leveraged real-world data collected through the electronic health record to characterize differences in HRQOL across common neurologic conditions. METHODS: This is a cross-sectional study of adult patients with 1 of 10 conditions (amyotrophic lateral sclerosis [ALS], back pain, brain tumor, dementia, major depression, epilepsy, migraine, multiple sclerosis, Parkinson disease, and stroke) who completed patient-reported outcomes as standard care between October 2015 and March 2023 at a large Neurologic Institute. Patients completed PROMIS-GH, from which physical and mental summary scores (Global Mental Health [GMH], Global Physical Health [GPH]) were calculated, PHQ-9 depression screen, and condition-specific measures. EQ-5D utility index was estimated from PROMIS-GH. These were summarized across conditions after adjustment for demographics, clinical characteristics, and self-reported condition-specific severity. RESULTS: There were 133,636 patients included in this analysis (mean age 51.5 [SD ± 18] years, 64% female, 81% White race). Overall adjusted average scores for GPH, GMH, PHQ-9, and EQ-5D were 42.7 ± 5.0, 43.6 ± 5.0, 7.9 ± 3.7, and 0.68 ± 0.11, respectively. Scores were significantly different across condition, with GPH ranging from 39.6 ± 5.5 for back pain to 45.9 ± 3.9 for brain tumor. Patients with brain tumor, back pain, stroke, ALS, and multiple sclerosis had more favorable GMH and PHQ-9 scores, while patients with depression, epilepsy, and dementia had the most affected GMH and PHQ-9 scores. Patients with back pain and headache had the lowest EQ-5D scores (0.63 ± 0.13 and 0.67 ± 0.09), while those with brain tumor had the highest (0.77 ± 0.08). DISCUSSION: This study provides condition-specific and severity-specific benchmarks for PROMIS-GH and related HRQOL measures that can support interpretation of patient-reported outcomes across neurologic populations. These data inform clinical care, quality measurement, research, and the implementation of the AAN-recommended performance measure.
Frequency-Specific Thalamocortical Network Dynamics During Focal Seizures in Humans.推荐
PMID 43038493 · Neurology · 2026 11 16
作者：Panchavati S, Daida A, Kanai S, Oana S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVES: The thalamus is an emerging neuromodulation target in drug-resistant focal epilepsy because of its frequent ictal involvement. However, frequency-specific and directional thalamocortical dynamics remain poorly understood. We sought to characterize thalamocortical network dynamics across seizure evolution using stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG). METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed SEEG recordings from patients with pediatric-onset drug-resistant focal epilepsy with simultaneous cortical and thalamic sampling focusing on the anterior nucleus (AN) and centromedian nucleus (CM). We applied visual analysis with quantitative measures of spectral power, imaginary coherence, and spectral Granger causality across slow (1-12 Hz), β (13-30 Hz), and γ (30-70 Hz) bands to quantify thalamocortical and cortico-cortical interactions from seizure onset to termination. Random forest models were trained to classify seizure state using thalamic EEG features as an exploratory analysis. RESULTS: The final cohort comprised 19 patients (median age, 16.9 years; 31.6% female), with 66 focal seizures analyzed. Thalamic ictal activity was present at seizure onset in 81% of sampled thalamic contacts and became nearly universal by seizure termination. Both the thalamus and seizure onset zone showed increased spectral power during seizures, with prominent slow-band augmentation at seizure termination in the thalamus. Similarly, thalamocortical imaginary coherence increased during seizures, with the largest changes near seizure termination in the slow band (AN-SOZ: 11% [IQR, 2 to 20], rank-biserial correlations (rrb) = 0.84; CM-SOZ: 12% [IQR, 3 to 23], rrb = 0.84) and β band (AN-SOZ: 5% [IQR, 0 to 12], rrb = 0.74; CM-SOZ: 8% [IQR, 2 to 15], rrb = 0.86). Directed connectivity analyses demonstrated bidirectional thalamocortical interactions, with large slow-frequency thalamus-to-SOZ outflow near seizure termination from both AN and CM, exceeding propagation-zone-to-SOZ cortico-cortical flow (both p < 0.01). Seizure state was classified using thalamic EEG features, with area under the receiver operating characteristic curve values of 0.825 for AN and 0.839 for CM. DISCUSSION: Human focal seizures engage frequency-specific and bidirectional thalamocortical interactions that evolve from onset to termination. These findings support the thalamus as a source of clinically relevant seizure-state information and may inform thalamic biomarker development for neuromodulation in drug-resistant epilepsy.
Localization mechanisms and function of postsynaptic Kv7 (KCNQ) channels and their reduction in KCNQ2 epileptic encephalopathy.推荐
PMID 43038459 · Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences of the United States of America · 2026 11 16
作者：Malpotra S, Zhao H, Tracy GC, Wilton AR et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Kv7 channels composed of Kv7.2 and Kv7.3 subunits are enriched in neuronal axons where their hyperpolarizing potassium current suppresses repetitive and burst firing of action potentials. Mutations in their genes, KCNQ2 and KCNQ3, cause neonatal epilepsy, underscoring their critical role in regulating neuronal excitability. In this study, we provide evidence that Kv7 channels are present at and near the postsynaptic density within dendritic spines of hippocampal neurons, where their current decreases calcium influx through NMDA receptors. This spine localization is promoted by heteromerization of Kv7.2 and Kv7.3 and their binding to AnkyrinG-190, which is the only AnkyrinG isoform present in dendritic spines. Lastly, spine localization of Kv7 channels and their ability to suppress NMDA receptor activity are markedly reduced by select variants of KCNQ2 that cause developmental and epileptic encephalopathy (DEE). These findings reveal a subcellular population of Kv7 channels that can dampen excitatory synaptic transmission and implicate their dysregulation in KCNQ2-associated DEE.
Concomitant Psychotropic Medication Is Associated with Reduced Outcomes of Trauma-Focused Psychotherapy for Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder.推荐
PMID 43038311 · Psychotherapy and psychosomatics · 2026 Nov 16
作者：Steenen SA, van Westrhenen R, Steenen CC, Klausch LT et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Psychotropic medications are frequently prescribed alongside trauma-focused psychotherapy for post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD), yet their impact on treatment response remains uncertain. This study emulated target trials to examine the association between psychotropic co-medication at treatment onset and psychotherapy outcomes in a real-world PTSD cohort. METHODS: A prospective cohort of 6,125 adults with chronic or delayed-onset PTSD received a standardized 2-8 day trauma-focused psychotherapy program, including daily prolonged exposure and eye movement desensitization and reprocessing (EMDR) therapy, at a Dutch psychotrauma center (2021-2024). Target trial emulation with double machine learning with inverse probability of treatment weighting estimated the effects of specific psychotropic co-medications versus non-use on changes in Clinician-Administered PTSD Scale for DSM-5 (CAPS-5) scores (range, 0-80) from pre-to post-treatment. RESULTS: Mean CAPS-5 scores improved by 25.7 points (Cohen's d = -2.30). This effect persisted at 6-month follow-up (d = -0.42). Antidepressants overall (d = -0.28; follow-up d = -0.56), amitriptyline (d = -0.51), and mirtazapine (d = -0.29) were consistently associated with poorer outcomes across sensitivity analyses. Similar patterns were observed for anticonvulsants, mood-stabilizing anticonvulsants, antipsychotics, fluoxetine, zolpidem, and zopiclone. Sensitivity analyses and E-values indicated robustness to unmeasured confounding. CONCLUSION: Several psychotropic co-medications were associated with reduced outcomes of evidence-based trauma-focused psychotherapy for PTSD. By identifying this as a potentially modifiable factor, psychotherapy outcomes may be optimized. Trials are warranted to evaluate whether tapering or substituting these agents improves outcomes.

Suspected partial arginine vasopressin deficiency during herpes simplex virus type 1 meningoencephalitis: a case report.推荐

摘要：Herpes simplex virus type 1 (HSV-1) encephalitis can be accompanied by complex disturbances of water and sodium balance. A 55-year-old woman presented with fever, recurrent seizures and profound encephalopathy. Cerebrospinal fluid DNA-based metagenomic sequencing yielded 13,426 HSV-1 reads with 94.46% genomic coverage; RNA sequencing identified 18 reads. Severe polyuria (maximum complete nursing-day output, 7,525 mL), hypernatremia (maximum 149.7 mmol/L) and increased plasma osmolality prompted suspicion of partial arginine vasopressin deficiency. Interpretation was limited by concurrent mannitol, hypokalemia, variable fluid therapy and overlapping enteral and intravenous desmopressin. Because of critical illness, acyclovir and foscarnet were empirically initiated together; acyclovir continued for 34 days. Creatinine-based renal function remained preserved on available monitoring. No standardized desmopressin challenge, paired pretreatment blood and urine osmolality, or definitive hypothalamic-neurohypophyseal lesion was documented. Desmopressin tablets were discontinued by June 2026. Serum sodium in August was 133 mmol/L, and neurological recovery was incomplete despite regained ambulation and independence in basic daily activities. The case emphasizes careful differential diagnosis of polyuria in severe viral encephalitis rather than confirmation of a direct HSV-related posterior pituitary injury.
Neurocognitive profiles structure heterogeneous treatment response to bumetanide in neurodevelopmental disorders: secondary analysis of a multiple baseline single-case experimental design.浏览
PMID 43037653 · European child & adolescent psychiatry · 2026 11 16
作者：Geertjens L, Kooper CC, Königs M, Oosterlaan J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：We performed a secondary analysis of the Bumetanide for Developmental Disorders (BUDDI;EUDRA-CT-2020-002196-35) trial to examine whether baseline neurocognitive profiles are associated with differential behavioral and cognitive treatment response. Participants (7-19 years) with atypical sensory reactivity and ASD, ADHD, epilepsy, or TSC diagnoses received 6-months of bumetanide treatment (max 3.0 mg/day) within a multiple-baseline SCED. Neurocognitive functioning was assessed across multiple domains and used to derive subgroups via cluster analysis. Behavioral outcomes were captured using a parent-reported PROMset and standardized questionnaires. Linear mixed models and subgroup comparisons were used to evaluate change over time. Of 119 participants enrolled, 105 completed baseline and 85 follow-up assessments At the group level, memory improved after six months (B = 0.65, 95%CI = 0.45to0.85, pFDR < .001, d = 1.07), while processing speed decreased (B = - 0.25, 95%CI = - 0.42to - 0.07, pFDR < .001, d = - 0.49). Cluster analysis identified four neurocognitive subgroups with distinct baseline profiles and differential treatment responses: individuals with weaker functioning showed the largest memory improvements, whereas those with preserved functioning showed greater behavioral improvement alongside declines in processing speed and visuomotor integration. Across subgroups, neurocognitive and behavioral changes did not align, and correlations were small (r < 0.30). In this study, neurocognitive profiling based on a standardized test battery was associated with distinct patterns of change during treatment. The dissociation between neurocognitive and behavioral outcomes indicated domain-specific and non-linear treatment effects. These findings provide a basis for linking variability in real-world change to individual neurocognitive characteristics within a defined clinical context.Trial registration: EudraCT (2020-002196-35) registration date 24-04-2020. Knockdown of KCNQ2 in CA1 reshapes epilepsy-related limbic network rhythms and reduces spontaneous exploratory behavior in mice.推荐
PMID 43037638 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 16
作者：Li J, He T, Zhang Y, Li J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Circuit-level effects of localized cornu ammonis 1 (CA1) potassium voltage-gated channel subfamily Q member 2 (KCNQ2) loss-of-function on epileptogenesis remain poorly understood, despite the well-established association of KCNQ2 deficiency with epilepsy and neuropsychiatric comorbidities. METHODS: To examine how CA1-specific KCNQ2 deficiency modulates neural network activity during acquired epileptogenesis, we selectively knocked down KCNQ2 in mouse CA1 via adeno-associated virus (AAV)-short hairpin RNA (shRNA) and explored limbic network alterations. Calcium activity of glutamatergic neurons, gamma-aminobutyric acid (GABA) ergic neurons, somatostatin-positive (SST+) interneurons, and parvalbumin-positive (PV+) interneurons was recorded using cell type-specific GCaMP expression with simultaneous CA1 electroencephalography (EEG). Multi-site EEG recordings from CA1, cornu ammonis 3 (CA3), retrosplenial agranular cortex (RSA), infralimbic cortex (IL), cingulate cortex area 1 (CG1), primary motor cortex (M1), and basolateral amygdala (BLA) were analyzed for spectral power, functional connectivity (FC), and phase-amplitude coupling (PAC). We observed spontaneous activity, novel object recognition, and social behaviors of the model mice. RESULTS: CA1 KCNQ2 knockdown increased calcium activity in all neuronal populations during the latency, with greater activation of SST+ than PV+ interneurons. Excitatory-inhibitory imbalance correlated with seizure severity. EEG analyses revealed a biphasic pattern: reduced low-frequency power, phase synchrony, and FC during the latency, followed by widespread hypersynchronization of power spectral density, mutual information, and phase synchrony during severe seizures. In the non-epileptic state, PAC analysis revealed that knockdown group developed a biased network configuration characterized by enhanced PAC directed toward the BLA and weakened CA3-M1 and M1-related coupling. This pattern was consistent with their behavioral phenotypes, including reduced central exploration, fewer open-arm entries, impaired novel object exploration, and decreased social preference. During generalized seizures, PAC underwent extensive reorganization, with strengthened coupling between the BLA and multiple brain regions and reduced M1-directed coupling. Intraperitoneally administered retigabine (RTG), a pan-KCNQ channel opener, partially alleviated these behavioral deficits and attenuated the abnormally potentiated long-range network activity. SIGNIFICANCE: CA1 KCNQ2 knockdown not only disrupts the local excitatory/inhibitory balance but also promotes epileptogenesis by reshaping limbic-cortical network coupling patterns.
Antiseizure medication use in self-limited epilepsies of childhood: A scoping review by the ILAE Pediatric Medical Therapies Task Force.推荐
PMID 43037621 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 16
作者：Coan AC, Jacobs J, Wanigasinghe J, Wirrell E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Self-limited focal epilepsies (SeLFEs) of childhood, including self-limited epilepsy with centrotemporal spikes (SeLECTS), self-limited epilepsy with autonomic seizures (SeLEAS), childhood occipital visual epilepsy (COVE), and photosensitive occipital lobe epilepsy (POLE), are common pediatric epilepsy syndromes with heterogeneous clinical management. We performed a scoping review to synthesize evidence regarding the use, effectiveness, safety, and discontinuation of antiseizure medications (ASMs) in children with SeLFEs. METHODS: Nine databases were searched from inception to January 2026. Eligible studies included original research reporting ASM-related outcomes in children with SeLFEs. PRISMA-ScR (Preferred Reporting Items of Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses, extension for scoping reviews) guidance was followed. RESULTS: Sixty-two studies met eligibility criteria, including 11 randomized controlled trials (RCTs). Most studies evaluated SeLECTS (6171 participants), whereas substantially fewer investigated SeLEAS (n = 348), COVE (n = 91), or POLE (n = 26). Evidence was highly heterogeneous and consisted predominantly of retrospective observational studies. In SeLECTS, reported 12-month seizure freedom rates after ASM treatment were approximately 72% across studies, although estimates varied considerably. Evidence for SeLEAS, COVE, and POLE was limited and derived mainly from small retrospective cohorts with heterogeneous methodologies and outcome definitions. Failure of first-line ASM therapy occurred in approximately 30% of patients. Adverse events were reported in approximately 22% of treated children and infrequently led to treatment discontinuation. RCTs generally reported similar seizure outcomes across multiple ASMs, although many trials were limited by small sample sizes and open-label designs. SIGNIFICANCE: Current evidence suggests that children with SeLFEs often achieve seizure control with ASM treatment and that adverse effects are generally manageable. However, the overall certainty of evidence remains limited because of methodological heterogeneity, predominance of retrospective studies, and limited randomized data. Important evidence gaps remain regarding the need for ASM treatment, optimal treatment duration, and timing of medication withdrawal.
Cenobamate in brain tumor-related epilepsy: A multicenter real-world experience.推荐
PMID 43037523 · Epilepsia open · 2026 11 16
作者：Romozzi M, Mazzeo A, Antonucci M, Assenza G et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Cenobamate (CNB) is a novel antiseizure medication with a dual mechanism of action that has demonstrated high efficacy in drug-resistant focal epilepsy; however, evidence in brain tumor-related epilepsy (BTRE) remains limited. The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness, safety, and tolerability of CNB as add-on therapy in a multicenter cohort of patients with BTRE. METHODS: We conducted a multicenter, retrospective observational study across tertiary epilepsy centers in the Lazio region, Italy. Adult patients with drug-resistant BTRE treated with CNB as adjunctive therapy were included. Seizure frequency was assessed at baseline and during follow-up. Primary outcomes were changes in monthly seizure frequency and responder rates. Effectiveness was analyzed in both the overall population and in a homogeneous cohort of patients with 6-month follow-up. RESULTS: Twenty patients (10 females, median age 48 years) were included. Tumor histology included meningioma (n = 7, 35%), astrocytoma (n = 4, 20%), glioblastoma (n = 3, 15%), ganglioglioma (n = 3, 15%), oligodendroglioma (n = 2, 10%), and dysembryoplastic neuroepithelial tumor (n = 1, 5%). In the overall analysis population (n = 20), after a median 12-month follow-up, median seizure frequency significantly decreased from 6 to 1 per month (p < 0.001), with a median reduction of 85.8% (IQR 51.4-96.7). Responder rates were 80% (≥50% seizure reduction), 60% (≥75%), and 20% achieved seizure freedom. Adverse events occurred in 12 patients (60%), including sedation (n = 8, 40%), postural instability (n = 3, 15%), diarrhea, and mild cutaneous rash (n = 1, 5% each). CNB was discontinued in one patient (5%). SIGNIFICANCE: In this multicenter real-world cohort of patients with drug-resistant BTRE and heterogeneous tumor histology, CNB was associated with a reduction in seizure frequency and was well tolerated. PLAIN LANGUAGE SUMMARY: Seizures caused by brain tumors can be difficult to control. We reviewed records from 20 adults who received cenobamate alongside their existing seizure medicines. At their last follow-up, 16 had at least half as many seizures, and four were seizure-free. Twelve patients experienced side effects, most commonly sleepiness; one stopped cenobamate because of a rash. These findings suggest that cenobamate may help some people with brain tumor-related epilepsy. However, the study was small, had no comparison group, and included patients with different tumors and follow-up durations. Larger studies are needed.
Invasive monitoring and mapping in epilepsy surgery: Acute and chronic intracranial recording from surface to depth.推荐
PMID 43037480 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 16
作者：Englot DJ, Dorfer C, McKhann GM, Rydenhag B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：Invasive intracranial recording remains central to the surgical evaluation of drug-resistant focal epilepsy when noninvasive data are insufficient to define the epileptogenic zone or its relationship to eloquent cortex. This review, part of an International League Against Epilepsy (ILAE) Surgical Therapies Commission education series aligned with the epilepsy surgery curriculum, reviews invasive monitoring and mapping for the practicing and trainee epilepsy surgeon. Consistent with the competency-based ILAE epilepsy surgery curriculum, in which the surgeon is expected to understand and actively contribute to electrode selection, safe implantation, and the interpretation of invasive recordings within the multidisciplinary team, we give particular emphasis to the surgical execution of these methods alongside their electrophysiological rationale. We outline the historical development of the field from intraoperative electrocorticography and subdural recording to stereoelectroencephalography (SEEG), describe acute and chronic recording strategies using both surface and depth approaches, including the operative techniques of implantation and post-implantation electrode localization, and summarize the techniques of functional mapping. We compare subdural and stereotactic methods with respect to coverage, mapping capability, diagnostic yield, seizure outcome, and morbidity, and we review the complication profiles of each. Finally, we discuss future directions, including high-density and chronically implantable probes that combine macro-contacts with microelectrode recording throughout the electrode shaft. We emphasize that the choice of method should follow a clearly stated anatomo-electro-clinical hypothesis, balancing the completeness of sampling against procedural risk.

Laser interstitial thermal therapy in older patients with drug-resistant mesial temporal lobe epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43037447 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 16
作者：Lau J, Von Stein E, Hartmann D, Le S et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Laser interstitial thermal therapy (LITT) is a minimally invasive treatment for focal drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE). This study sought to examine the efficacy of LITT for a cohort of older adults with mesial temporal lobe epilepsy (MTLE) and evaluate predictors of safety. METHODS: We examined 24 patients aged ≥50 years, with 10/24 (42%) ≥60 years. Primary outcomes were Engel classification and reduction from baseline seizure frequency. We additionally reviewed post-ablation complications, hospital length of stay (LOS), neurocognitive and psychiatric worsening, and anti-seizure medication (ASM) reduction. The Charlson Comorbidity Index (CCI) was calculated to assess its utility in prediction of postoperative complications. RESULTS: 22/24 (92%) had ≥50% reduction in seizure frequency, and 17/24 (71%) had ≥90% reduction at last follow-up, with median follow-up of 5.2 years (range, 1.5-10.4 years). Engel Class I outcome was achieved in 19/24 (79%) of patients by 6 months post-ablation and maintained in 12/24 (50%) of patients by last follow-up. Immediate postoperative complications were mostly transient, though worse cognitive symptoms were reported in 5/24 (21%), and worse mood was reported in 3/24 (13%) at 6 months post-ablation. Higher CCI did not correlate with complications but was associated with prolonged LOS. ASM reduction occurred in half of patients. SIGNIFICANCE: In our cohort, LITT offered favorable seizure and safety outcomes in an older population with focal DRE. Perioperative risk assessments such as the CCI may be useful in older patient selection for LITT.
Preoperative hippocampal volume, resection extent, and 5-year stability of seizure freedom after temporal lobe epilepsy surgery.推荐
PMID 43037442 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 16
作者：Bozkurt B, Bozkurt BE
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To examine whether preoperative ipsilateral hippocampal volume modifies the association between hippocampal resection extent and durable 5-year seizure freedom after temporal lobe epilepsy surgery. METHODS: We performed a retrospective secondary analysis of the public Imaging Database for Epilepsy And Surgery. Patients undergoing temporal lobectomy or temporal lesionectomy were eligible. The exposure was ipsilateral hippocampal resection fraction, the modifier was preoperative ipsilateral hippocampal volume z-score, and the outcome was International League Against Epilepsy class 1 at every annual assessment from years 1 through 5. Multivariable logistic regression included their interaction and pathology, procedure type, preoperative focal-to-bilateral tonic-clonic seizures, age, epilepsy duration, antiseizure medication count, sex, and operative side. RESULTS: Among 353 patients undergoing temporal surgery, 220 had all five annual outcomes and 210 had complete primary-model data. Durable seizure freedom occurred in 82 of 210 patients (39.0%). The resection-by-volume interaction odds ratio was 1.49 (95% confidence interval, 1.01-2.21; p = .045). Model-based probabilities increased with greater resection when hippocampal volume was preserved but not with marked atrophy. The estimate was similar in temporal lobectomy alone (odds ratio, 1.74; 95% confidence interval, 1.07-2.83) and attenuated after inverse-probability weighting (odds ratio, 1.43; 95% confidence interval, .95-2.13). SIGNIFICANCE: In this cohort, the association between measured hippocampal resection overlap and seizure outcome varied across preoperative hippocampal volumes. The pattern was most apparent when seizure freedom was assessed across 5 years rather than at a single postoperative visit.
Extracellular vesicles enable rotavirus and astrovirus infection of low-susceptibility cell lines.浏览
PMID 43037418 · Journal of virology · 2026 11 16
作者：Hernández Bustos AM, Murrieta-Coxca JM, Fonseca Z, Granillo-Luna ON et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Rotaviruses (RVA) and human astroviruses (HAstV) are classical gastrointestinal pathogens, but increasing evidence indicates that they are also involved in a range of extraintestinal diseases. These include seizures and diabetes in RVA infections, and central nervous system infections in HAstV infections. However, the mechanisms by which these viruses disseminate beyond the intestine remain unclear. Given the previously described association of RVA and HAstV with extracellular vesicles (EVs), we investigated whether purified EV-virus complexes could mediate infection of cells with low susceptibility to these viruses. We found that the RVA strain RRV, as well as two HAstV strains (the classical HAstV-8, Yuc8 strain, and the divergent non-classical VA1 strain), could infect low-susceptibility cell lines (BHK, SK-N-SH, F11, and CHO) when associated with EVs. This infectivity was dependent on EV integrity, since disruption of vesicles by detergent treatment reduced infection. Furthermore, a fraction of viral particles was protected from neutralizing antibodies, most likely because they were enclosed within EVs. Together, these findings suggest that EVs may act as vehicles facilitating the dissemination of RVA and HAstV beyond the gastrointestinal tract.IMPORTANCEGastrointestinal viruses typically infect specific intestinal cell types; however, rotaviruses (RVA) and human astroviruses (HAstV) have been associated with a range of extraintestinal pathologies. To better understand how these viruses disseminate beyond the intestine, we investigated the potential role of their interactions with extracellular vesicles (EVs). We found that the simian RVA strain RRV and HAstV strains Yuc8 and VA1, when associated with EVs, could infect low-susceptibility cells. A fraction of the EV-associated viral particles was resistant to antibody-mediated neutralization, suggesting that EVs protected them. These observations support the possibility that EVs could serve as vehicles enabling RVA and HAstV to disseminate beyond the gastrointestinal tract.
Prospective genotype-guided drug selection and dosing in alcohol withdrawal syndrome complicated by seizures: a randomized clinical trial.推荐
PMID 43037191 · Drug metabolism and personalized therapy · 2026 11 17
作者：Skryabin VY, Sokolova SI, Ivanchenko VA, Matskevich AA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVES: To determine whether prospective genotype-guided drug selection and dosing can reduce cumulative withdrawal severity and improve treatment tolerability in alcohol withdrawal syndrome (AWS) complicated by seizures. METHODS: In this single-center, parallel-group randomized clinical trial, 100 inpatients with AWS and seizures were allocated 1:1 to a genotype-guided prescribing strategy or control. All patients were genotyped on admission for CYP2D6*4, CYP2C19*2, CYP2C19*17, CYP3A4*1B, CYP3A5*3, and ABCB1 3435C>T. Intervention-group physicians received an individualized report with recommendations on benzodiazepine and anticonvulsant selection and starting dose; control-group physicians received a format-matched report without individualized recommendations. The primary outcome was area under the CIWA-Ar curve over 0-72 h, assessed by a blinded rater. RESULTS: AUC CIWA-Ar was 774.3 ± 175.9 vs. 903.6 ± 199.5 score-hours (mean difference -129.3; 95 % CI -204.0 to -54.6; p<0.001). Confirmed stabilization by 48 h occurred in 31/50 vs. 20/50 patients (log-rank p=0.020; Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted p=0.051, not significant after multiplicity correction). UKU side-effect scores were lower on days 3 and 5 (both p<0.001), and excessive sedation occurred in 1/50 vs. 9/50 patients (p=0.016). Total benzodiazepine load was lower with genotype guidance (25.0 vs. 27.5 mg diazepam equivalents; p=0.016). The composite rate of clinically significant adverse events and the rates of delirium and unplanned treatment modification did not differ significantly. CONCLUSIONS: A prospective genotype-informed prescribing strategy was associated with lower cumulative withdrawal severity and less excessive sedation while using a lower benzodiazepine load. Because the intervention also changed anticonvulsant selection and drug concentrations were not measured, these findings support the clinical strategy as a whole rather than any single genotype-to-dose rule. Multicenter, prospectively registered trials with measured drug exposure are warranted.
First Seizure Services: Status and Healthcare Professional Attitudes and Perspectives. Results From a Survey of the European Academy of Neurology.推荐
PMID 43037185 · European journal of neurology · 2026 Nov
作者：Mazzeo A, Meletti S, Rüegg S, Steinhoff BJ et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Dedicated first seizure services (FSSs) may improve the early assessment, diagnosis, and management of people presenting with a suspected first seizure, but little is known regarding their current status in Europe. In this study, we aimed to explore the availability of FSSs throughout Europe, as well as to investigate attitudes towards their implementation among healthcare professionals involved in epilepsy care. METHODS: We designed a web-based survey that was distributed through the European Academy of Neurology. The survey assessed respondent characteristics, local FSS availability, service organization, perceived utility, and barriers to implementation. RESULTS: A total of 441 participants from 72 countries completed the survey; 379 unique centers were included in center-specific analyses. Overall, 171/331 centers in geographical Europe (51.7%) reported an available FSS. Availability varied significantly by region, being highest in Northern Europe (74.0%) and lowest in Eastern Europe (35.5%). FSS availability correlated positively with Gross Domestic Product and current health expenditure per capita of the respondent's country. Participants reported a positive attitude towards FSSs: 90.9% believed that they should be a standard of care, 93.2% believed they improve outcomes, and 83.0% believed that they are cost-effective. The main reported barriers to implementation were limited infrastructure, financial constraints, organizational barriers, and a shortage of specialized personnel. CONCLUSIONS: FSS availability across Europe remains heterogeneous, with substantial variation according to geographical area and a possible association with economic factors. Despite implementation barriers, healthcare professionals expressed overall positive support for standardized first seizure pathways.
Blood Pressure Variability in Brain Arteriovenous Malformations: a Review of Pathophysiological Mechanisms, Clinical Relevance, and Therapeutic Insights.浏览
PMID 43037016 · Blood pressure · 2026 11 16
作者：Jiang X, Liang Z, Liang R, Wang Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Brain arteriovenous malformations (bAVMs) are high-flow vascular lesions linked to substantial hemorrhage risk and neurological impairment. Traditional risk stratification centers on static hemodynamic parameters, but blood pressure variability (BPV)-fluctuations in blood pressure over time-has emerged as a dynamic, modifiable risk factor. This review explores how pathophysiological pathways link BPV to bAVMs progression and rupture, with specific focus on how aberrant hemodynamics, endothelial dysfunction, compromised cerebral autoregulation, and blood steal syndrome interact. We look at how BPV can worsen wall stress, spark inflammatory responses, and drive vascular restructuring-all of which raise rupture risk. We discuss BPV's clinical impacts in both unruptured and ruptured bAVMs, including how it affects perioperative outcomes and long-term functional recovery. We also assess BPV as a therapeutic target, highlighting pharmacological and monitoring strategies built to stabilize blood pressure fluctuations. Finally, we point out critical knowledge gaps and suggest future research directions to fold BPV into personalized risk assessment and management protocols for bAVMs.
Adult phenotype of genetically confirmed Dravet syndrome: diagnostic challenges and clinical red flags from a multicenter Polish cohort.推荐

作者：Winczewska-Wiktor A, Zawadzka M, Nagańska E, Mazurkiewicz-Bełdzińska M et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2
摘要：AIM OF THE STUDY: To characterize the clinical phenotype of adults with clinically diagnosed and genetically confirmed Dravet syndrome (DS), with particular emphasis on seizure evolution, cognitive and motor impairment, and diagnostic delay. CLINICAL RATIONALE FOR THE STUDY: An increasing number of individuals with DS survive into adulthood and require long-term neurological care. However, the adult phenotype differs from the classical childhood presentation and remains underrecognized in adult neurological practice. Recognition of an age-evolving seizure phenotype, together with intellectual disability, gait and postural abnormalities, and extrapyramidal features, may provide important diagnostic clues and prompt molecular testing in previously undiagnosed adults. MATERIAL AND METHODS: We conducted a multicenter retrospective case series of nine adults (six females and three males) fulfilling clinical diagnostic criteria for DS and carrying a pathogenic or likely pathogenic SCN1A variant. Clinical data were obtained from medical records, neurological examinations, seizure diaries, and psychological assessments performed as part of routine care. Seizure history and evolution, status epilepticus, cognitive and communication impairment, motor phenotype, and treatment history were analyzed. RESULTS: The mean age at evaluation was 27.8 years (range 21-37 years). Seizure onset occurred at a mean age of 6 months, whereas the mean age at molecular diagnosis was 13.2 years, corresponding to a mean diagnostic delay of 12.7 years. Febrile seizures occurred in eight of nine patients and persisted beyond the age of 18 years in three. Generalized tonic-clonic seizures persisted into adulthood in all patients, whereas status epilepticus occurred after the age of 18 years in only one. Intellectual disability was present in all patients and was severe or profound in six; communication impairment was observed in eight. Motor abnormalities were prominent, including gait impairment (9/9), ataxia (8/9), postural abnormalities (8/9), bradykinesia (5/9), hypomimia (5/9), and parkinsonian features (3/9). All patients had been exposed to sodium channel-blocking antiseizure medications before molecular diagnosis, with seizure worsening documented in eight of nine patients. CONCLUSIONS AND CLINICAL IMPLICATIONS: The adult phenotype of DS combines persistent drug-resistant epilepsy with intellectual and communication impairment and prominent motor dysfunction. Although generalized tonic-clonic seizures may persist, other features of the epileptic phenotype, particularly status epilepticus, become less prominent with age. Gait, postural, cerebellar, and extrapyramidal abnormalities may provide important diagnostic clues in adults with infantile-onset epilepsy and intellectual disability. Recognition of this age-evolving phenotype may facilitate molecular diagnosis and support syndrome-specific management in adult neurological practice.
[Comparison of prehospital management of selected emergencies between a paramedical team and a physician-led team : a retrospective study in Belgium].推荐
PMID 43036968 · Revue medicale de Liege · 2026 Nov
作者：Moury J, Vandevelde V, Soetewey A, Michaux I et al.
链接：PubMed
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：In Belgium, the emergency medical system (EMS) coordinates mobile emergency and resuscitation units (SMUR) and paramedical intervention teams (PIT). As demand increases, greater use of PITs could help relieve SMUR workload, although comparative data remain limited. This retrospective study compared prehospital care by analysing 2,325 prehospital interventions (PIT = 1,377; SMUR = 948) involving cardiac arrest, trauma, seizures, chest pain, and acute respiratory disorders. Patient characteristics, response times, and management strategies were analysing. Management of cardiac arrest and seizures was comparable between the two intervention systems. In trauma cases, both patient characteristics and therapeutic interventions differed. In patients with chest pain, SMUR teams performed more electrocardiograms. For respiratory conditions, PIT teams more frequently administered oxygen therapy and aerosol treatment, whereas SMUR teams more often performed intubation. No difference was observed in response times. Overall, PIT appears to be a useful alternative when SMUR is unavailable; however, prospective studies are needed before confirming comparable performance between the two systems.

摘要：18F-FDG PET/MR demonstrated extensive right cerebral cortical hypometabolism in a 52-year-old man with Sturge-Weber syndrome undergoing presurgical evaluation of drug-resistant epilepsy. Clinical photographs showed capillary malformations involving the face and both hands. CT revealed prominent right hemispheric gyriform calcifications, while integrated 18F-FDG PET/MR demonstrated ipsilateral cerebral atrophy and reduced cortical uptake in the frontoparietal and temporal lobes. These images illustrate the complementary roles of CT and 18F-FDG PET/MR in characterizing structural abnormalities and regional metabolic impairment in longstanding Sturge-Weber syndrome.
Hidden in Plain Sight: An Unusual Case of Neurocysticercosis.推荐
PMID 43036777 · IDCases · 2026 11 05
作者：Brown JA, David DM, Elsayad C
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Neurocysticercosis (NCC), caused by the larval stage of Taenia solium, is a leading cause of acquired epilepsy in endemic regions and is increasingly encountered in non-endemic countries due to migration and travel [1,4]. NCC results from fecal-oral ingestion of T. solium eggs from a human tapeworm carrier rather than from pork consumption [4]. Intraventricular NCC may present without seizures and can mimic primary headache syndromes or cranial neuralgias while causing obstructive hydrocephalus [1,2]. We report a 68-year-old woman with hypertension, hyperlipidemia, prior right V1 herpes zoster, and a reported cerebrovascular event who presented with years of severe right-sided headache and shooting facial pain (V1-V3). Initial diagnoses favored zoster-related neuralgia, and neuropathic medications provided minimal relief. Brain MRI revealed a predominantly cystic, non-enhancing lesion filling the fourth ventricle with ventriculomegaly consistent with chronic obstructive hydrocephalus. Progressive ventricular enlargement prompted neurosurgical intervention. Suboccipital craniotomy with cyst excision and external ventricular drain placement was performed. Pathology demonstrated calcified cysticercus structures, confirming NCC, and serum cysticercus IgG was positive. The patient was treated with albendazole and corticosteroids, with improvement in headache. Fourth ventricular NCC may masquerade as chronic unilateral headache or trigeminal neuralgia, and progressive ventriculomegaly should prompt consideration of intraventricular NCC in at-risk patients. Early recognition is essential to prevent decompensated hydrocephalus and guide appropriate neurosurgical and antiparasitic management [1,3].
A Homozygous SLC39A8 p.Asn369Thr Variant in an Infant With Leigh-Like Encephalopathy.推荐
PMID 43036732 · Case reports in genetics · 2026 11 15
作者：Sagri MN, Akbar A, Rehman B, Hasan Z et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Biallelic variants of SLC39A8 cause congenital disorders of glycosylation Type IIn (CDG-IIn, OMIM #616721), a rare autosomal recessive condition characterized by impaired manganese (Mn) transport, defective glycosylation, and secondary mitochondrial dysfunction. Affected infants present with developmental delays, hypotonia, seizures, and symmetric basal ganglia lesions on brain MRI. CASE PRESENTATION: An 11-month-old girl, previously developmentally appropriate, presented with new-onset seizures and regression of motor and social milestones. Examination revealed global hypotonia, poor feeding, and faltered growth. Brain MRI demonstrated bilateral, symmetric T2/FLAIR hyperintensities with restricted diffusion in the caudate nuclei, putamin, globi pallidi, subthalamic nuclei, and cerebral peduncles, with sparing of the dentate nuclei and no contrast enhancement. Whole exome sequencing identified a novel homozygous SLC39A8 variant, NM_001,135,146.2:c.1106A > C (p.Asn369Thr). Each parent was a heterozygous carrier, confirming autosomal recessive inheritance. This variant is absent from ClinVar and gnomAD and is currently classified as a variant of uncertain significance (VUS). The clinical phenotype and neuroimaging pattern are compatible with SLC39A8-CDG; however, the identified variant remains a VUS, and biochemical confirmation could not be obtained because the relevant tests were unavailable. Confirmation would require functional studies. Supportive testing would include transferrin glycosylation analysis and blood manganese measurement; a normal conventional transferrin result alone would not exclude the diagnosis. If SLC39A8-CDG is confirmed, manganese and galactose supplementation should be considered only under specialist metabolic supervision with appropriate biochemical monitoring. This case suggests a possible phenotype-genotype association but does not establish the pathogenicity of p.Asn369Thr.
Exertional syncope caused by acquired obstruction of the pulmonary artery due to an organized thrombus in a dog.浏览
PMID 43036678 · Veterinary and animal science · 2026 11 06
作者：Szatmári V, Bergmann W, Grinwis GCM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：An 8.5-year-old Boxer was referred for evaluation of frequently occurring exercise-induced seizure-like episodes for 2.5 months. Cardiac auscultation revealed a left basilar systolic murmur with an intensity of IV/VI. Echocardiography showed severe pulmonary arterial obstruction with a pressure gradient of 90 mmHg due to a suspected intraluminal mass. Cardiac catheterization was performed, where a right ventriculogram revealed a large rounded filling defect in the pulmonary artery trunk. No balloon angioplasty, biopsy or stenting were attempted. After euthanasia, post-mortem examination revealed an organized thrombus of unknown etiology attached to the wall of the pulmonary artery trunk. The adjacent arterial wall showed a markedly thickened intimal layer. The etiology remains unclear, primarily due to the chronic nature of the condition. Antemortem blood tests and postmortem examination failed to reveal any prothrombotic conditions. Acquired causes of pulmonary arterial obstruction are extremely rare in dogs, and an organized thrombus has not been reported yet.
From Prevalence to Prediction: A Cross-Sectional Study to Develop a Novel Risk Score for Hearing Loss in High-Risk Neonates in South India.浏览
PMID 43036653 · Cureus · 2026 10 16
作者：Sachithanandam ST, Baskaradass R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND AIM: Neonates admitted to the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) with multiple risk factors are more likely to develop hearing loss. This study aimed to determine the prevalence of hearing loss, identify its predictive factors, and develop a novel clinical risk score for hearing loss among high-risk neonates. MATERIALS AND METHODS: This cross-sectional study included 105 high-risk neonates admitted to the NICU from July 2024 to June 2025. The hearing assessment protocol included transient-evoked otoacoustic emissions at discharge and at six weeks of corrected age, followed by diagnostic brainstem-evoked response audiometry at three months. Associations between risk factors and hearing loss were analyzed using logistic regression. A novel clinical risk score was developed using the factors significantly associated with hearing loss. RESULTS: The prevalence of hearing loss among high-risk neonates was 4.8% (95% CI, 1.6%-10.8%). Hearing loss was significantly associated with intrauterine toxoplasmosis, perinatal asphyxia, neonatal hyperbilirubinemia (NNH) requiring exchange transfusion, stage II or higher hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy, neonatal sepsis, neonatal seizures, syndromic or craniofacial anomalies, and ototoxic drug exposure (p < 0.05). The clinical risk prediction model, developed using significant predictive factors and assigning double weight to intrauterine toxoplasmosis and NNH requiring exchange transfusion, achieved a sensitivity of 60% and a specificity of 96% at a cutoff score of ≥3. CONCLUSIONS: The prevalence of hearing loss among high-risk neonates was 4.8%. The novel clinical risk score developed in this study may help guide targeted screening, timely diagnosis, and early intervention for hearing loss.
Posterior Reversible Encephalopathy Syndrome Presenting as Late Postpartum Eclampsia in a Previously Normotensive Woman: A Case Report.推荐
PMID 43036598 · Cureus · 2026 10 16
作者：Aung KZ, Saw NEL
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜无JIF分区（已移出WoS）
摘要：Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) is a clinico-radiological syndrome characterized by acute neurological manifestations, including headache and seizures, with neuroimaging evidence of predominantly reversible vasogenic cerebral edema. It is commonly associated with hypertensive disorders of pregnancy, particularly preeclampsia and eclampsia. A 27-year-old primigravida with known Hashimoto thyroiditis, treated with levothyroxine 50 µg daily, presented on postpartum day seven with a severe headache followed by two generalized tonic-clonic seizures. Her blood pressure was 160/110 mmHg, and the urine protein-to-creatinine ratio was 6.96 g/g creatinine. Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) of the brain demonstrated bilateral parieto-occipital cortical and subcortical T2-weighted/fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) hyperintensities consistent with PRES. Magnetic resonance venography excluded cerebral venous sinus thrombosis. Autoimmune investigations for alternative systemic autoimmune disorders, including antinuclear antibody, anti-double-stranded DNA antibody, antineutrophil cytoplasmic antibody, and complement levels, were unremarkable. She was treated with magnesium sulfate and antihypertensive therapy, resulting in complete clinical recovery and resolution of the radiological abnormalities. This case highlights late postpartum eclampsia presenting as PRES in a previously normotensive woman and emphasizes the importance of maintaining a high index of suspicion for postpartum hypertensive disorders in women presenting with new-onset headache or seizures, even after an apparently uncomplicated pregnancy and delivery. Early recognition and prompt treatment are essential to prevent potentially life-threatening complications and achieve favorable clinical outcomes.

A Novel DHX9 Variant Was Identified in a Patient With Developmental Delay, Facial Dysmorphism and Seizure.推荐
PMID 43036232 · International journal of developmental neuroscience : the official journal of the International Society for Developmental Neuroscience · 2026 Nov
作者：Li X, Chen Y, Sun X
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The DHX9 gene encodes an RNA helicase belonging to the DExD/H-box family, which is highly expressed in the brain and nervous system. Pathogenic variants in the DHX9 gene are associated with neurodevelopmental disorder (NDD) and Charcot-Marie-Tooth disease (CMT). Both loss-of-function (LoF) and missense variants can cause NDD, whereas CMT is associated with specific missense variants. Patients with DHX9-related NDD typically present with intellectual disability, language and motor developmental delay, and autism spectrum disorder (ASD). CMT patients primarily present with distal weakness, sensory deficits, variable muscle wasting, foot deformities and painful sensory neuropathy. Here, we report a female patient with severe DHX9-related NDD phenotypes, including global developmental delay, ASD, facial dysmorphism, epilepsy, microcephaly, abnormal brain MRI findings and clinodactyly of the fifth finger. She experienced her first febrile seizure at 1 year and 2 months of age. She developed frequent myoclonic seizures characterized by rapid nodding and upper body tremors lasting 3-10 s at the age of 3 years. Simultaneous electroencephalogram (EEG) revealed paroxysmal bursts of high- to very-high-amplitude sharp-slow waves, spike-slow waves and polyspike-wave complexes diffusely over bilateral leads. Clinical exome sequencing (CES) identified a likely de novo missense variant in the DHX9 gene in the patient (NM_001357.5, c.1540C>T, p.(His514Tyr)). Notably, this variant resides within the highly conserved DEIH motif (Motif II) of the helicase ATP-binding domain. This critical region plays an essential role in ATP hydrolysis and helicase function. Three-dimensional protein structure modelling suggested that the p.(His514Tyr) substitution may compromise local protein stability by altering hydrogen bond networks. Our study expands the genetic and phenotypic spectrum of DHX9 variant-associated disorders.
Optimal Timing of Cranioplasty Following Decompressive Craniectomy: An 8-Year Retrospective Single-Center Analysis of 184 Patients.推荐
PMID 43036190 · Polski przeglad chirurgiczny · 2026 Jun 11
作者：Slavkov D, Troyanova-Slavkova S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Decompressive craniectomy (DC) is an established life-saving procedure for patients with refractory intracranial hypertension. After stabilization, cranioplasty (CP) is performed to restore cranial integrity, improve cerebral hemodynamics, and protect the brain. However, CP is associated with considerable complication rates, and the optimal timing of reconstruction remains controversial. AIM: To evaluate the impact of cranioplasty timing on postoperative complications and functional outcomes following decompressive craniectomy, and to identify independent predictors of adverse events after reconstruction. MATERIALS AND METHODS: We conducted a retrospective single-center cohort study including 184 adult patients who underwent cranioplasty after decompressive craniectomy between January 2017 and December 2024. Patients were categorized according to the interval between DC and CP into early (≤3 months), intermediate (3-6 months), and delayed (>6 months) groups. Demographic, clinical, and surgical variables were analyzed, including age, sex, indication for DC, Charlson Comorbidity Index (CCI), cranial defect size, implant material, and ventriculoperitoneal shunt dependency. The primary outcome was the occurrence of postoperative complications. Secondary outcomes included surgical site infection, postoperative hematoma, hydrocephalus requiring CSF diversion, seizures, bone flap resorption, and reoperation. Functional outcome was assessed using the modified Rankin Scale (mRS) at six months. Kaplan-Meier analysis was used to evaluate complication-free survival, and multivariable Cox proportional hazards regression was performed to identify independent predictors of complications. RESULTS: The cohort consisted of 184 patients with a mean age of 52.4 16.8 years; 64% were male. The most common indication for decompressive craniectomy was traumatic brain injury (55%), followed by ischemic stroke (42%) and intracerebral hemorrhage (2%). Early cranioplasty was performed in 37% of patients, intermediate in 34%, and delayed in 29%, with a median interval of 121 days between DC and CP. Autologous bone flap reimplantation was used in 86% of cases, whereas 14% received patient-specific synthetic implants. The overall postoperative complication rate was 26.6%. The most frequent complications were surgical site infection (13.0%), postoperative hematoma (8.2%), hydrocephalus (1.6%), and implant-related complications (3.8%). Complication rates did not significantly differ among timing groups (early 28%, intermediate 25%, delayed 26%; p = 0.61). Kaplan-Meier analysis demonstrated similar complication-free survival across groups (p = 0.64). Multivariate analysis identified higher comorbidity burden (CCI ≥4) as an independent predictor of postoperative complications (HR 2.1; 95% CI 1.2-3.7; p = 0.02), whereas timing of cranioplasty, cranial defect size, and implant material were not significant predictors. CONCLUSIONS: Cranioplasty after decompressive craniectomy is generally safe, with complication rates comparable to those reported in the literature. In this cohort, the timing of cranioplasty did not independently influence postoperative complication rates. Instead, patient-related factors, particularly comorbidity burden, played a more significant role in determining outcomes. These findings support an individualized approach to cranioplasty timing based on patient condition and clinical factors rather than rigid temporal thresholds.

ICFinder: ion channel identification and ion permeation residue prediction using protein language models.浏览
PMID 43035683 · BMC bioinformatics · 2026 08 19
作者：Wang J, Zhang X, Fan X, Xiao B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Ion channel dysfunction underlies many diseases (e.g., arrhythmias, epilepsy, cystic fibrosis), and uncharacterized channels may also contribute to pathology. Identifying such channels and their residues directly contacting the permeation pathway (i.e., ion permeation residues) is key to elucidating transport mechanisms and developing targeted therapies. RESULTS: We developed BLAPE and CLAPE frameworks using the protein language model ESM-2 and curated datasets for high-throughput ion channel identification and permeation residue prediction. Our models achieved competitive performance, with relative improvements of 78% in Matthews correlation coefficients (MCC) on the ICIdentification dataset and 59% on the ICPermeation dataset compared with existing methods. Analysis of amino acid composition revealed enrichment for hydrophobic residues among ion permeation sites. Case studies on four diverse ion channels highlighted that CLAPE generally performs better than existing predictors and remains applicable to proteins lacking experimental structures, while also complementing structure-based pipelines such as AlphaFold3. We further applied our models to UniRef50 to predict potential ion channels, and made these results publicly available through the ICFinder webserver ( https://tianlab-tsinghua.cn/icfinder/ ), providing a ready-to-use resource for the research community. All source code is available at https://github.com/JueWangTHU/ICFinder . CONCLUSIONS: BLAPE and CLAPE achieve competitive performance in ion channel identification and permeation residue prediction. ICFinder provides a practical resource for discovering potential ion channels, guiding target identification, and supporting drug design.
Postoperative hemispheric hyperperfusion and status epilepticus after chronic subdural hematoma evacuation: a case report.推荐
PMID 43035663 · BMC neurology · 2026 08 20
作者：Chen R, Huyan M, Yu Y, Xu L et al.

命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）

摘要：Cerebral hyperperfusion syndrome (CHS) is rarely reported after chronic subdural hematoma (CSDH) evacuation. We describe a 40-year-old man who developed right hemispheric swelling, neurological deterioration, generalized seizures, and ipsilateral hyperperfusion on computed tomography perfusion after burr-hole drainage of a right CSDH. The overall clinical and radiological course was most consistent with probable CHS complicated by refractory status epilepticus, although seizure-related ictal or postictal hyperperfusion could not be fully excluded. He required strict blood pressure control, antiseizure therapy, deep intravenous anesthesia, and continuous electroencephalographic monitoring. Serial perfusion imaging showed gradual resolution of the hyperperfusion. He was discharged without neurological deficits and had returned to work with a modified Rankin Scale score of 0 by postoperative day 45. At 1 year, he remained seizure-free after withdrawal of all antiseizure medications, and an outside-hospital 24-hour ambulatory electroencephalography report showed no epileptiform abnormalities.
The prevalence of dementia and cognitive impairment in patients with neurocysticercosis: a systematic review and meta-analysis.浏览
PMID 43035640 · BMC infectious diseases · 2026 08 18
作者：Bayatani SS, Ghasemikhah R
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：Meta分析
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Neurocysticercosis (NCC) is a leading parasitic infection of the central nervous system and an important cause of neurological morbidity in endemic regions. Although seizures are well-characterized manifestations, the burden of cognitive impairment and dementia in NCC remains poorly quantified. This systematic review and meta-analysis aimed to estimate the prevalence of cognitive impairment and dementia among patients with NCC and to explore sources of between-study variability. METHODS: Following PRISMA guidelines, we searched PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, and Google Scholar (June-July 2025) and registered the protocol in PROSPERO (CRD420251106614). Observational studies reporting dementia or cognitive impairment in imaging-confirmed NCC were eligible. Data extraction and quality assessment were independently performed, and risk of bias was evaluated using the Hoy tool. Pooled prevalence estimates were generated using random-effects models with REML and Hartung-Knapp adjustments. Exact binomial (Clopper-Pearson) confidence intervals and logit-transformations were applied. Prespecified subgroup, meta-regression, and sensitivity analyses assessed heterogeneity. RESULTS: Nine studies met inclusion criteria (N = 798 for cognitive impairment; N = 296 for dementia). The pooled prevalence of cognitive impairment was 50% (95% CI: 0.23-0.76; I²=97.87%) and dementia 10% (95% CI: 0.05-0.15; I²=49.11%). Subgroup analyses showed significant regional variation, with highest cognitive impairment prevalence in South America (68%). Cognitive assessment method did not account for heterogeneity. Sensitivity analyses confirmed the robustness of pooled estimates, and no significant small-study effects were detected. CONCLUSION: Cognitive impairment affects approximately half of patients with NCC, while one in ten develop dementia. Geographic context and methodological factors largely drive between-study variability. These findings underscore NCC as an important contributor to neurocognitive morbidity in endemic regions and highlight the need for standardized cognitive assessment, harmonized diagnostic criteria, and longitudinal studies to clarify disease mechanisms and progression.
Effectiveness and cost-effectiveness of epilepsy surgery in patients with refractory epilepsy: A systematic review of decision-analytic models.推荐
PMID 43035593 · Neurosurgical review · 2026 11 16
作者：Alva-Diaz C, Rimachi VAV, Grados-Espinoza P, Cabanillas-Lazo M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Our objective was to evaluate the effectiveness and cost-effectiveness of epilepsy surgery for the treatment of patients with DRE. We carried out a systematic review of Decision Analytic Models (DAMs) in the main international databases regarding the comparison between epilepsy surgery versus medical treatment. We evaluated the relevance and credibility of the DAMs with the ISPOR-AMCP-NPC tool. We synthesized the results qualitatively. The certainty of the body of evidence was determined using the GRADE approach proposed for modeling studies. Clinical and economic outcomes were synthesized narratively because substantial heterogeneity existed across studies in epilepsy etiology, patient populations, model structures, perspectives, time horizons, and outcome definitions, precluding formal meta-analysis and quantitative pooling of cost-effectiveness estimates. Surgery was associated with higher reported rates of seizure freedom and seizure reduction than medical treatment, with seizure freedom ranging from 37.1% to 64.0% and seizure reduction from 24.6% to 42.2%. Reported QALY gains ranged from 0.3 to 3.0. Incremental costs varied substantially, ranging from savings of I$116,642 to additional costs of I$227,146 per patient. ICERs ranged from dominant (less costly and more effective) to I$291,091 per QALY gained. For seizure-related outcomes, ICERs ranged from I$454 to I$1,268 per 1% increase in seizure reduction and from I$5.83 to I$1,782 per 1% increase in seizure freedom, reflecting substantial variation in healthcare settings, perspectives, time horizons, and economic models. Epilepsy surgery is considered an effective and cost-effective option in patients with DRE, although the evidence is still low and very low certainty. So future studies of higher methodological quality are needed to better assess the picture and corroborate these results.Clinical trial number: Not applicable.
Status epilepticus in mTORopathies: a systematic review of clinical features, management, and outcomes.推荐
PMID 43035524 · Neurological sciences : official journal of the Italian Neurological Society and of the Italian Society of Clinical Neurophysiology · 2026 11 16
作者：Kim M, Kaminski M, Goldberg M, Young W et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：系统综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: The mechanistic target of rapamycin (mTOR) pathway plays a critical role in epileptogenesis; however, its involvement in human status epilepticus (SE) remains poorly understood. mTORopathies, genetic disorders defined by mutations that disrupt mTOR signaling, frequently manifest severe neurological complications, among which SE is particularly consequential. METHODS: A systematic search of PubMed, Embase, and Web of Science was conducted for studies on mTORopathies and SE. We analyzed the clinical presentation, treatment strategies, and outcomes of all cases. RESULTS: Eight hundred ninety-seven records were initially identified, of which 31 were included in this review. SE may manifest in different mTORopathies. SE often presents early, recurs frequently, and can be catastrophic. Refractory SE (RSE) predominantly affects neonates and infants and frequently occurs in the context of MCD. SE can be terminated with multiple anti-seizure medications (ASMs), alone or in combination with mTOR inhibitors. However, managing RSE is challenging. Current treatment strategies include novel ASMs, anesthetics, mTOR inhibitors, the ketogenic diet, immunotherapy, epilepsy surgery, and neuromodulation. A multifaceted approach is preferred to achieve seizure control and to improve developmental outcomes. CONCLUSIONS: SE remains a significant clinical challenge in patients with mTORopathies. mTOR signaling may contribute to the pathogenesis of SE and could represent a potential therapeutic target. Further investigation in large cohort studies is needed to clarify the role of mTOR signaling in SE and to evaluate the effects of mTOR inhibitors and immunotherapies on outcomes in patients with SE/RSE.
Refractory ventricular tachyarrhythmia following a suicidal massive caffeine overdose: a case report and review of emergency management.推荐
PMID 43035483 · Journal of medical case reports · 2026 08 20
作者：Negahdari T, Zadehbagheri F, Bornapour S, Yazdanpanah M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Caffeine is one of the most widely consumed psychoactive substances worldwide and is readily accessible in tablet formulations and caffeinated beverages. Although moderate caffeine intake is generally considered safe, massive acute ingestion can result in severe toxicity involving the central nervous and cardiovascular systems. Life-threatening manifestations include seizures, profound electrolyte disturbances, metabolic derangements, and malignant ventricular arrhythmias. Because caffeine has favorable dialyzable properties, including low molecular weight, low protein binding, and a relatively small volume of distribution, extracorporeal toxin removal may play a critical therapeutic role in severe intoxication refractory to conventional supportive and antiarrhythmic management. CASE PRESENTATION: We report the case of a previously healthy 16-year-old Iranian adolescent girl who intentionally ingested 10.8 g of caffeine in a suicide attempt. Approximately two hours after ingestion, she presented with markedly reduced consciousness, agitation, generalized tremor, intermittent tonic/myoclonic seizure-like activity, sinus tachycardia (180-190 beats/min), hypokalemia (serum potassium 2.7 mEq/L), primary metabolic acidosis, significant rhabdomyolysis, and electrocardiographic abnormalities including mild QRS widening and QTc prolongation (~ 500 ms). Urine toxicology screening was positive for caffeine, supporting the diagnosis in conjunction with the clear ingestion history and subsequent patient confirmation. Despite early airway protection, gastrointestinal decontamination, electrolyte and acid-base correction, dual antiarrhythmic therapy with amiodarone and lidocaine, and repeated synchronized electrical cardioversion, she developed recurrent refractory sustained ventricular tachycardia, transient progression to asystole requiring cardiopulmonary resuscitation, and persistent severe metabolic instability. Emergency intermittent hemodialysis was initiated approximately 3-4 h after emergency department arrival, resulting in rapid correction of metabolic acidosis, complete cessation of malignant ventricular arrhythmias, and sustained restoration of stable sinus rhythm. The patient subsequently demonstrated progressive neurological recovery without persistent cardiac or neurological sequelae. CONCLUSION: This case highlights the potentially catastrophic cardiovascular consequences of massive caffeine intoxication and emphasizes the importance of early recognition, aggressive supportive care, rapid correction of metabolic abnormalities, and timely escalation to extracorporeal toxin removal in refractory cases. In patients with persistent malignant ventricular arrhythmias despite optimized pharmacologic and electrical management, intermittent hemodialysis may represent a life-saving therapeutic intervention capable of rapidly reversing toxicity and improving clinical outcomes.
Nursing care for a patient with a twin pregnancy with complete hydatidiform mole and a coexisting fetus: a case report.浏览
PMID 43035417 · BMC pregnancy and childbirth · 2026 08 19
作者：Gu H, Zhang Y, Sun S, Zhou L et al.
