JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: A twin pregnancy with a complete hydatidiform mole and a coexisting fetus is a rare and high-risk pregnancy that occurs in 1/22,000 to 100,000 pregnancies, the risk of which may increase as a result of ovulation induction and assisted fertility techniques. These pregnant women are prone to sudden heavy bleeding, heart failure, and seizures during labor. Most of the patients choose to terminate their pregnancies after diagnosis. The best way in which to standardize clinical and nursing management for pregnant women who choose to continue their pregnancy is debatable. CASE PRESENTATION: This is a report of a patient with lung metastasis after thyroid cancer surgery who underwent intrauterine insemination after administration of human menopausal gonadotrophin (HMG) to promote ovulation.After further counseling regarding maternal risks, the patient elected to continue the pregnancy, and the fetal heartbeat disappeared at 22+ 5 weeks of gestation. Through multidisciplinary treatment and specialized nursing care, maternal stabilization and preservation of future fertility potential were achieved. CONCLUSION: This report describes the clinical management of a twin pregnancy with complete hydatidiform mole and a coexisting fetus (CHM&CF).This report explores the nursing experience associated with this rare condition. It thus provides a reasoned and effective framework to guide nursing practice in comparable clinical scenarios.
Toll-Like Receptor-Driven Neuroinflammation in Neurological Disorders: Signaling Crosstalk and Therapeutic Opportunities.浏览
PMID 43035261 · Journal of neuroscience research · 2026 Nov
作者：Sharma A, Singh G, Mannan A, Subramaniyan V et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Toll-like receptors (TLRs) serve as pivotal mediators bridging innate immunity and neuroinflammation, orchestrating pathological cascades that drive neurological disorders. Beyond their sentinel role in pathogen recognition, aberrant TLR activation triggers intricate and destructive signaling networks, including activation of NF-κB, MAPK, JAK/STAT, and PI3K/AKT, alongside IRFs and type I interferon responses that converge on chronic neuroinflammation and subsequent neuronal damage. This review dissects TLRs' multilayered contribution to neuropathology, emphasizing their regulation of secondary inflammatory mechanisms, including NLRP3 inflammasome priming and activation, mTOR-driven metabolic dysregulation, SOCS-mediated feedback inhibition, inflammasome activation, and pyroptotic cell death. Critically, these signaling pathways create a pathological intersection with oxidative stress and mitochondrial dysfunction, amplifying a self-perpetuating cycle of neuronal injury that underlies conditions like Alzheimer's disease (AD), Parkinson's disease (PD), Huntington's disease (HD), and Amyotrophic lateral sclerosis (ALS), Stroke, Multiple sclerosis (MS), Epilepsy and Traumatic brain injury (TBI). Against this neuropathological landscape, this review systematically evaluates emerging therapeutic strategies targeting TLR-mediated neuroinflammation, encompassing small molecule TLR antagonists, intracellular adaptor protein inhibitors targeting myeloid differentiation primary response 88 (MyD88) and TIR-domain-containing adapter-inducing interferon-β (TRIF), biologic therapies including monoclonal antibodies and fusion proteins, natural phytochemicals and nutraceuticals, Clustered regularly interspaced short palindromic repeats (CRISPR)-based genomic interventions, and cell-based immunomodulatory therapies, collectively representing a mechanistically diverse arsenal aimed at recalibrating the dysregulated innate immune response to achieve durable neuroprotection across the full spectrum of TLR-mediated neurological disorders.
Adult Care in a Pediatric Hospital: Patterns and Implications of an Internal Medicine Consult Service at a Children's Hospital.浏览
PMID 43035111 · Hospital pediatrics · 2026 11 16
作者：Goodman A, Herbst B, Kinnear B, Caldwell A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Adults with childhood-onset conditions are increasingly admitted to children's hospitals, yet many pediatric systems lack the infrastructure and expertise to manage adult-related medical needs. One approach to bridge this gap is the use of an adult medicine consult service. We aim to describe the demographics, clinical complexity, and consultation patterns of patients admitted to a freestanding children's hospital and evaluated by an adult medicine consult service, which is a current gap in the literature. PATIENTS AND METHODS: A retrospective descriptive cohort study was performed of admitted patients with a Hospital Medicine Adult Care (HMAC) service consult from July 2014 through June 2023. RESULTS: Annual adult admissions increased over the study period, from 7.4% to 10.2% of all admissions. HMAC consulted on 1666 admissions for 1008 unique patients (median age, 27 years [range, 14-71 years]). The most frequent reasons for consult were venous thromboembolism prophylaxis (33.7%), age older than 25 years (21.2%), transition of care (7.6%), and hypertension (7.6%). HMAC physicians addressed a mean of 5.5 diagnoses per encounter. Patients averaged 3.6 admissions (range, 1-22) and 4.9 comorbidities (range, 0-21) each. The most common comorbidities were technology dependence (47%), solid tumor malignancy (22.5%), genetic conditions (20.2%), epilepsy (19.7%), congenital heart disease (17.4%), and cerebral palsy (15.7%). CONCLUSIONS: Adult consult services in children's hospitals support a medically complex population with diverse childhood-onset and adult conditions and function as longitudinal clinical partners. These findings support the role of adult medicine expertise in children's hospitals to help recognize and manage adult-specific care needs and transitions.
Symptom Impact in Angelman Syndrome: Results from the Hope in Action Caregiver Survey.浏览
PMID 43034975 · The patient · 2026 11 15
作者：Peay HL, Potter SN, Wheeler AC, Panagoulias J et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND AND OBJECTIVE: Angelman syndrome is a rare neurological disorder with no approved disease-modifying therapies. First-generation treatments are expected to be disease modifying but not curative, highlighting the importance of understanding symptoms with the greatest impact on individuals with Angelman syndrome and their caregivers to support patient-focused drug development. The Hope in Action survey was designed to capture lived experiences and inform an externally led, patient-focused drug development meeting. METHODS: A global online survey was conducted among primary caregivers of individuals with Angelman syndrome. The survey was developed by a multidisciplinary team with input from advocates, caregivers, and representatives of the US Food and Drug Administration. Caregivers ranked top-3 domains and rated related symptoms affecting individuals with Angelman syndrome and those impacting caregivers; and top-5 priority symptoms to address with a new non-curative therapeutic. Analyses were descriptive. RESULTS: When ranking and rating from lists of high-impact domains and symptoms, 370 caregiver participants most frequently identified communication, daily living skills, gross motor function, and cognition as impactful to individuals living with Angelman syndrome, and patients' independence and communication challenges as impactful to their own caregiver functioning, social participation, and mental health. Priority symptoms to treat with a new therapy included expressive communication, seizures, mobility, sleep, toileting, aggression, balance, and sustained attention/engagement. CONCLUSIONS: Through the largest caregiver-reported study on Angelman syndrome symptom impact, we demonstrate an unmet treatment need and a profound impact on individuals and caregivers across a multitude of domains. These findings directly inform patient-focused drug development and reflect alignment of caregiver priorities and outcome measure selection for trials.
Expression of nuclear factor kappa B-associated long non-coding RNAs in drug-naïve and valproic acid-treated epilepsy patients.推荐
PMID 43034957 · Molecular biology reports · 2026 11 15
作者：Harby SA, Gaber DE, Osman NA, Samy DM et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy has a complex etiopathogenesis and frequently shows resistance to conventional antiseizure medications, underscoring the need for a deeper mechanistic understanding of the disease. Accumulating evidence implicates NF-κB-mediated neuroinflammation in epileptogenesis, and several long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs) are known to regulate this pathway. However, the expression patterns of the NF-κB-associated lncRNAs UCA1, ANRIL, and NKILA in epilepsy, and whether antiseizure medications can modulate them, remain largely unknown. This study therefore assessed these lncRNAs, together with the inflammatory cytokines IL-1β and IL-6, in drug-naïve and valproic acid (VPA)-treated epileptic patients compared with healthy controls. METHODS: In a prospective, multicenter study with 120 adults, participants were divided into three groups: drug-naïve patients, VPA-treated patients, and healthy volunteers. Early morning blood samples were collected to measure serum levels of the selected lncRNAs and cytokines. Correlations between molecular markers and seizure parameters were examined. RESULTS: Serum IL-1β, IL-6, UCA1, and ANRIL were significantly higher in drug-naïve patients than in healthy controls (p < 0.001, p < 0.001, p = 0.003, and p = 0.014, respectively), whereas NKILA showed no significant difference (p = 0.484). Compared with drug-naïve patients, VPA-treated patients showed no significant difference in IL-1β (p = 0.056) or IL-6 (p = 1.00); in the age- and sex-adjusted Bayesian model, UCA1 was significantly lower in the VPA group compared to the drug-naive group (p = 0.015) and no longer differed from controls (p = 0.424), whereas ANRIL remained largely unchanged. Serum IL-1β had a significant positive correlation with IL-6 (ρ = 0.58, p < 0.001) and ANRIL (ρ = 0.20, p = 0.028), and in drug-naïve patients seizure severity significantly correlate positively with UCA1 (ρ = 0.41, p = 0.009). On ROC analysis, IL-1β and IL-6 best discriminated untreated patients from controls (AUC = 0.84 and 0.96, respectively). These findings indicate heightened systemic inflammation in untreated epilepsy and support IL-1β and IL-6 as potential candidate biomarkers, with UCA1 as a potentially treatment-responsive lncRNA. CONCLUSION: Our results identify serum UCA1 as the most notably altered lncRNA, significantly elevated in untreated epilepsy and potentially responsive to valproic acid. While IL-1β and IL-6 reflected the accompanying inflammation. These findings warrant further validation as candidate biomarker.
Cesarean Myomectomy for Refractory Uterine Atony in an Eclamptic Woman with Multiple Fibroids: A Case Report from the Democratic Republic of Congo.推荐
PMID 43034789 · International journal of women's health · 2026 11 10
作者：Kahinga Mukwiza G, Isango Idi Y, Munan Mwazaz R, Kaki Bal'olam B et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Cesarean myomectomy remains a controversial procedure because of concerns regarding severe hemorrhage and peripartum hysterectomy. Recent reports suggest that it may be performed safely in carefully selected patients; however, the certainty of the available evidence remains very low, and reports describing its use for refractory uterine atony in women with eclampsia are scarce. CASE PRESENTATION: We report the case of a 38-year-old Congolese woman, gravida 3 para 0 abortus 2, admitted to the University Clinics of Lubumbashi with generalized tonic-clonic seizures, severe hypertension (180/120 mmHg), and marked proteinuria, leading to a diagnosis of eclampsia. No ultrasound examination was performed before surgery because of the maternal emergency. After stabilization with magnesium sulfate and nicardipine, an emergency cesarean section was performed. A live female infant weighing 2030 g was delivered but died 30 minutes after birth in the neonatal intensive care unit. OPERATIVE FINDINGS AND MANAGEMENT: Extensive uterine fibromyomatosis was identified, including nine fibroids, the largest measuring 16 × 10 × 8 cm and weighing approximately 1050 g. After hysterorrhaphy, severe uterine atony persisted despite uterine massage and oxytocin administration. Considering the patient's nulliparity and the wish to preserve the uterus, a conservative cesarean myomectomy was undertaken. Five major fibroids were removed, including the giant lower-segment fibroid. Progressive restoration of uterine tone was observed after myomectomy, with satisfactory hemostasis. Estimated blood loss was 900 mL, and total operative time was 90 minutes. Postoperatively, the patient received blood transfusion and recovered without complications. She was discharged in stable condition on postoperative day five. CONCLUSION: In this patient, removal of the dominant fibroids was temporally associated with restoration of uterine tone and allowed emergency hysterectomy to be avoided. This single observation does not establish the safety or efficacy of the procedure, but suggests that cesarean myomectomy may be considered as a uterus-preserving option in carefully selected patients with refractory uterine atony related to extensive fibromyomatosis, provided that surgical expertise and blood transfusion capacity are available.

摘要：BACKGROUND: Hypertensive encephalopathy (HE) in pediatric patients is a life-threatening condition requiring prompt and controlled blood pressure reduction to prevent neurological complications. CASE ILLUSTRATION: We report a 7-year-old child with chronic kidney disease on continuous ambulatory peritoneal dialysis who presented with HE complicated by seizures and persistent hypertension despite intravenous nicardipine and multiple oral antihypertensive agents. Adjunctive manual scalp acupuncture was initiated using GV20 (Baihui), EX-HN1 (Sishencong), and additional ashi points. RESULTS: Following treatment, the patient demonstrated a progressive reduction in blood pressure over seven sessions, accompanied by resolution of seizures, improvement in sleep, and reduction of associated symptoms such as neck pain. CONCLUSIONS: The application of scalp acupuncture for pediatric HE may be a promising therapeutic adjuvant in controlling blood pressure. Nevertheless, causality cannot be established from a single case, particularly given concurrent antihypertensive therapy and dialysis-based volume management. Further studies are needed to evaluate its efficacy and safety in this setting.

Impact of PPAR agonists on seizure suppression to disease modification using status-epilepticus experimental model: Role of the PPAR in metabolic-inflammatory pathway.推荐
PMID 43033961 · Brain research · 2026 11 14
作者：Sinha S, Sarangi SC, Sharma D, Pattnaik SS et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：PURPOSE: This study explored the potential of peroxisome proliferator-activated receptor (α/γ) (PPAR modulators) for seizure control and the consequences of status epilepticus (SE) due to their anti-inflammatory and neuro-modulatory properties. METHOD: A lithium-pilocarpine-induced status epilepticus (SE) model in Wistar rats determined the optimal dose of PPAR modulators (fenofibrate, pemafibrate, and saroglitazar) for seizure suppression. Based on seizure parameters, an efficacy study examined PPAR modulators alone and in combination with ASMs (valproate and perampanel). Evaluations of disease-modifying effects included neurobehavioral tests, electron microscopy for neurodegeneration, immunohistochemistry (NeuN for neuronal viability and GFAP for astrocytosis), Western blotting for PPARα, HMGCS2, NLRP3, and mTOR, and ELISA for antioxidant capacity and neuroinflammatory biomarkers. RESULTS: The optimal doses of fenofibrate, pemafibrate, and saroglitazar were 200, 1.5, and 3 mg/kg, respectively. Compared with the SE-control group, drug-treated groups had higher latency (p<0.001), a lower number and percentage of rats with stage 3-4 seizures (p=0.003, for both), better memory retention (p=0.001), less myelin-sheath/axonal damage (p<0.001), significant differences in protein expressions [NeuN, PPARα, HMGCS2, mTOR, and NLRP3 (p<0.001)], and less neuroinflammation. The PPAR modulators + perampanel group had no stage 3-4 seizures. Compared to valproate alone, fenofibrate+valproate exhibited less neurodegeneration and astrocytosis in the hippocampus and cerebral cortex (p<0.001, for all). All PPAR modulator combinations with ASM groups had significantly higher PPARα and HMGCS2 expression. CONCLUSION: PPAR modulators fenofibrate, pemafibrate, and saroglitazar have similar seizure-control potential. In the SE model, PPAR modulators with ASM showed significant neuroprotection and increased PPARα expression compared to ASM alone.
Dopamine D2 receptor signaling alleviates seizure-related neuronal ferroptosis via MARCH8-mediated P2RX7 ubiquitination.推荐
PMID 43033946 · Brain, behavior, and immunity · 2026 11 14
作者：Lyu Y, Sun J, Huang M, Du C et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜IMMUNOLOGY：Q1；NEUROSCIENCES：Q1；PSYCHIATRY：Q1
摘要：Epilepsy affects over 70 million people worldwide and remains a major cause of morbidity and premature death. Although dopaminergic signaling dysfunction has been implicated in this disorder, its protective mechanisms are not fully understood. Here, we demonstrated that dopamine D2 receptor (D2R) expression is downregulated during seizures and that D2R signaling protects against seizure. We identified the purinergic receptor P2RX7- a known mediator of neuronal injury - as a downstream target of D2R. Activation of D2R signaling alleviated P2RX7-dependent neuronal ferroptosis, an iron-dependent form of cell death, and attenuated seizure-related neuronal injury by promoting ubiquitination and degradation of P2RX7. Mechanistically, D2R signaling upregulated the E3 ubiquitin ligase MARCH8 in an AMPK-dependent manner. MARCH8 promoted K48-linked polyubiquitination of P2RX7, thereby targeting it for proteasomal degradation in neurons. The protective effect of D2R was attenuated in March8 knockout mice, supporting the involvement of MARCH8 in this pathway. Together, our findings identify a neuroprotective mechanism wherein D2R signaling promotes MARCH8-mediated ubiquitination and degradation of P2RX7, alleviating seizure-related neuronal ferroptosis. This work provides new insights into the pathogenesis of seizure-related neuronal injury and identifies potential therapeutic targets.
EEG microstate alterations and their association with cognitive performance in late-onset epilepsy of unknown etiology: Comparison with Alzheimer's disease.推荐
PMID 43033935 · Brain research bulletin · 2026 11 14
作者：Huang J, Wan H, Jiang W, Chen Y et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Cognitive impairment is common in late-onset epilepsy of unknown etiology (LOEU), but its electrophysiological correlates and relationship to Alzheimer's disease (AD) remain unclear. We compared resting-state electroencephalogram (EEG) microstate dynamics among LOEU, AD, and cognitively normal (CN) groups and examined their associations with cognitive performance. METHODS: Eyes-closed 32-channel resting-state EEG recordings were analyzed from 60 patients with LOEU, 65 with AD, and 60 CN participants. Four canonical microstate classes (A-D) were identified. Group differences in microstate parameters were tested with false discovery rate correction. Associations with Montreal Cognitive Assessment (MoCA) and Mini-Mental State Examination (MMSE) scores were examined using multivariable interaction models adjusted for age, sex, education, and cerebral small vessel disease burden. RESULTS: Microstate B duration was longer in LOEU and AD than in CN (Holm-adjusted p = 0.011 and 0.016), whereas microstate C occurrence was lower in both patient groups (p = 0.001 and 0.018). Microstate D duration was longer in LOEU than in CN and AD (both p = 0.038). For microstate A duration, the group interaction survived false discovery rate correction for MMSE (q = 0.0255) but not MoCA (q = 0.122). In LOEU, longer microstate A duration was associated with lower MoCA and MMSE scores. Pairwise slope comparisons were more negative in LOEU than in CN for both outcomes, whereas LOEU-AD slope differences were not significant. CONCLUSIONS: LOEU showed altered resting-state EEG microstate dynamics that partly overlapped with those observed in AD, alongside a group difference in microstate D duration. Microstate A duration was associated with cognitive performance in LOEU, although this association did not significantly differ from that observed in AD. These findings support further investigation of EEG microstates as electrophysiological correlates of cognitive dysfunction in LOEU while also highlighting overlap with AD.

Mechanisms and Functional Outcomes of Astrocytic Ca2+ Signaling.浏览
PMID 43033470 · ASN neuro · 2026 11 14
作者：Cheli VT, Angeliu CG, Paez PM
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Astrocytic calcium (Ca2+) signaling is a central regulator of brain function, enabling astrocytes to integrate neuronal, vascular, and metabolic information across multiple spatial and temporal scales. Rather than passive support cells, astrocytes generate dynamic Ca2+ signals ranging from localized microdomain transients in perisynaptic processes to propagating intercellular waves. These signals arise from coordinated activity of plasma membrane channels, ligand-gated and G protein-coupled receptors, and intracellular Ca2+ handling systems. Through these mechanisms, astrocytic Ca2+ dynamics regulate essential processes including gliotransmitter release, synaptic modulation and plasticity, neurovascular coupling, and metabolic support. This multiscale signaling framework positions astrocytes as active hubs within the tripartite synapse and neurovascular unit, shaping both local circuit activity and global brain states. Notably, distinct signaling modalities-particularly microdomain events-function as key computational units that enable astrocytes to decode synaptic inputs. Disruption of Ca2+ homeostasis transforms this system into a driver of pathology. Dysregulated signaling promotes astrogliosis, neuroinflammation, excitotoxicity, and impaired neurovascular and metabolic coupling, and is implicated across disorders including Alzheimer's disease, Parkinson's disease, multiple sclerosis, epilepsy, stroke, and chronic pain. In this review, we highlight key knowledge gaps, including how microdomain and global Ca2+ signals are integrated, how Ca2+ dynamics couple to astrocytic metabolism and gliotransmission, and how these processes are reorganized across physiological and disease states. Addressing these challenges will be essential for deciphering astrocytic signaling logic and identifying precise pharmacological targets.
Potter-Optimized Multiscale Bayesian Gated Axial Quantized Attention Network for Robust Multichannel EEG-ECG-Based Seizure Prediction.推荐
PMID 43033403 · Developmental neurobiology · 2027 Jan
作者：Shobana G, Baskaran KR
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Accurate seizure prediction from physiological signals remains challenging because electroencephalography (EEG)-ECG recordings contain substantial noise, nonlinear temporal patterns, inter-subject variability, and complex cross-modal dependencies. To address these challenges, this study proposes a Potter-Optimized Multiscale Bayesian Gated Axial Quantized Attention Network (PM-BGAQAN) for robust three-state seizure prediction using multichannel EEG-ECG signals. The framework first employs adaptive weighted mean filter (AWMF) to suppress physiological noise while preserving relevant signal characteristics, followed by Convolutional Swin-Transformer Network (CSTN) for hierarchical multiscale spatiotemporal feature extraction and multimodal EEG-ECG representation learning. Inclusion of Bayesian Asymmetric Quantized Neural Network (BAQNN) is followed next for uncertainty-aware quantized features, whereas multiscale gated axial reverse attention (MGARA) exploits the discriminatory power of multiscale temporal dependencies using gated axial attention. Finally, Potter Optimization Algorithm (POA) optimizes the network parameters to improve classification performance and convergence. The proposed framework is evaluated using the Siena Scalp EEG dataset with timestamp metadata and the SeizeIT2 EEG-ECG wearable dataset for classification of preictal, ictal, and interictal states. Experimental results demonstrate an overall classification accuracy of 99.86%, whereas subject-independent validation achieves 98.06% average accuracy across the evaluated datasets. Ablation analysis further confirms the contribution of the individual components to the integrated framework. The results indicate that PM-BGAQAN provides an effective multimodal representation-learning strategy for robust seizure-state prediction and establishes a basis for future real-time, wearable, and clinically validated seizure monitoring systems.
Untargeted Plasma Metabolomics Reveals Metabolic Alterations Associated With Idiopathic Epilepsy in Dogs.推荐
PMID 43033402 · Veterinary medicine and science · 2026 Nov
作者：Nakorn PN, Chantong B, Chatchaisak D, Phochantachinda S
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：BACKGROUND: Canine idiopathic epilepsy (IE) is one of the most common chronic neurological disorders in dogs, however, its underlying pathophysiology remains incompletely understood. OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to characterise plasma metabolomic alterations associated with canine IE at two clinical points using an untargeted metabolomic approach. METHODS: Plasma samples were collected from nine client-owned healthy dogs (Normal), and nine dogs with idiopathic epilepsy evaluated at acute seizure state (Epilepsy_D0) and after a 14-day seizure-free interval following antiepileptic treatment (Epilepsy_D14). For metabolomic analysis, plasma from three dogs was pooled to generate three biological pools per condition. Untargeted plasma metabolomic profiling was performed using LC-QTOF-MS in positive ionisation mode. RESULTS: A total of 491 metabolites were retained for downstream analysis. Exploratory heatmap and principal component analyses showed differences in overall metabolic patterns among three groups. Differential abundance analysis identified distinct metabolite changes across the pairwise comparison. Pathway enrichment analysis of the significantly altered metabolites using the study-specific metabolite set as a custom background did not identify any pathway reaching statistical significance. Six metabolites of interest, namely pyridoxal, methylimidazoleacetic acid, 2-methylbutyroylcarnitine, indolelactic acid, homocitrulline and DL-Normetanephrine, showed distinct abundance patterns across the study conditions. CONCLUSIONS: These findings provide preliminary evidence of plasma metabolic alterations associated with canine IE and differences between the acute seizure state and the 14-day seizure-free interval. Given the pooled biological design and limited effective sample size, these findings should be considered exploratory and require validation in larger cohorts using individual biological samples.
Reduced Cytokine Production in a CYFIP1-Mediated Model of Autism Spectrum Disorder.浏览
PMID 43033393 · Bulletin of experimental biology and medicine · 2026 11 14
作者：Kolomina ES, Kuzmina DM, Chernova MA, Vasilyeva EA et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：CYFIP1 (Cytoplasmic FMR1 Interacting Protein 1) is a key component of the regulatory complex controlling actin polymerization, which is critically important for neuronal morphogenesis, synapse formation, and the maintenance of synaptic plasticity. Reduced CYFIP1 gene expression is associated with autism spectrum disorder, schizophrenia, epilepsy, and developmental delay. Cyfip1 haploinsufficient (Cyfip1+/-) mice demonstrate not only behavioral deficiency, but also diminished production of cytokines, key regulators of the immune response. This CYFIP1-dependent imbalance of immune system regulators may result in an inadequate response to proinflammatory stimuli in patients with neuropsychiatric symptoms. This phenomenon should be taken into account when developing therapeutic strategies for these patients.
Long-term Electroclinical Outcomes After Herpes Simplex Virus Encephalitis: A Single-Center Case Series.推荐
PMID 43033067 · Clinical EEG and neuroscience · 2026 11 14
作者：Özüm T, Arsava EM, Topcuoglu MA, Dericioglu N
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：ObjectiveHerpes simplex virus encephalitis (HSVE) remains an important cause of severe sporadic encephalitis. Electrographic seizures and status epilepticus may be underrecognized during the acute phase, and their relationship with long-term outcome is incompletely defined. We aimed to describe acute EEG abnormalities and long-term seizure and cognitive outcomes in adults with HSVE.MethodsWe retrospectively reviewed adults hospitalized with HSVE in the neurology ward or neurological intensive care unit between 2013 and 2022. Demographic and clinical characteristics, MRI findings, routine or continuous video-EEG findings, antiseizure treatments, and long-term outcomes were obtained from electronic health records and the EEG archive.ResultsNine patients (7 men; age range: 30-77 years) were included. Altered mental status was the most frequent presenting feature, and 3 patients had clinical seizures at presentation. MRI abnormalities were bilateral in 4 patients. EEG showed lateralized periodic discharges in 5 patients, electrographic status epilepticus in 4, and electrographic seizures not meeting status criteria in 1. Median follow-up was 8 years (range: 4-13 years). No patient with available follow-up reported recurrent clinical seizures after discharge. Mood and/or cognitive complaints were present in 6 of 8 patients with evaluable long-term clinical data.ConclusionIn this neurologically selected HSVE cohort, electrographic seizures and status epilepticus were frequent despite clinical seizures being documented in only one-third of patients at presentation. Long-term clinical seizure control was favorable under continued antiseizure treatment, whereas cognitive and mood complaints remained common. Larger studies using systematic continuous EEG and standardized neuropsychological assessment are needed.
Real-world antiseizure medication prescribing and treatment pathways in a pediatric health care system.推荐
PMID 43033003 · Epilepsia open · 2026 11 14
作者：Abend JE, Gonzalez AK, Kaufman MC, Fitzgerald MP
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: To characterize antiseizure medication (ASM) selection, subsequent ASM prescribing, and treatment pathways among patients with epilepsy receiving care within a pediatric health care system. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective electronic health record study over 17 years. Each unique ASM was counted once per patient, and ASMs were ordered by the date of their first observed prescription. For each observed ASM, we determined whether a subsequent unique ASM was prescribed during available follow-up. Prescription of a third unique ASM was used as a proxy for potential drug-resistant epilepsy. RESULTS: Among 20 009 patients, 27 ASMs and 3507 treatment pathways were identified. The most common first-observed ASMs were levetiracetam (35.1%), oxcarbazepine (17.2%), and valproic acid (12.3%). From 2010 to 2026, the proportion of first-observed prescriptions for levetiracetam increased from 23.1% to 41.5%. The proportion of all distinct ASMs first recorded for individual patients that were newer-generation ASMs increased from 0% in 2010 to 9.1% in 2026. The proportion of patients without a subsequent ASM ranged from 16.2% to 69.6% across commonly prescribed first-observed ASMs. Overall, 55.6% of patients had one unique ASM recorded, 22.4% had two, and 22.0% had at least three, thereby meeting the study's prescription-based proxy for potential drug-resistant epilepsy. Beyond the most common single- and two-ASM pathways, each remaining pathway accounted for less than 1% of the cohort. SIGNIFICANCE: Prescribing remained concentrated among established ASMs, although their relative use changed substantially. The proportion of patients without a subsequent ASM varied widely across medications, and treatment pathways beyond first-observed therapy were highly heterogeneous. These findings characterize real-world treatment patterns and identify a need for comparative effectiveness studies of ASM sequencing. PLAIN LANGUAGE SUMMARY: We examined antiseizure medication prescribing over 17 years among more than 20 000 patients with epilepsy receiving care in a pediatric health care system. More than half had only one medication recorded, whereas 22% had at least three medications; however, the available records could not determine whether these medications failed to control seizures. Levetiracetam became more commonly prescribed, newer medications remained a small proportion of overall use, and medication sequences varied widely after the first observed treatment. These findings highlight the need for better evidence to guide medication selection after initial treatment.
Etiology and geographic variation in acute symptomatic seizure management: An international vignette-based survey.推荐
PMID 43032991 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 14
作者：Punia V, Galovic M, Brigo F, Trinka E et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Acute symptomatic seizures (ASyS) account for nearly half of new onset seizures, but evidence guiding management remains limited. We aimed to characterize international variation in ASyS management across common etiologies. METHODS: We conducted a cross-sectional, vignette-based survey through the International League Against Epilepsy national chapters (February to May 2025). Five etiology-based vignettes assessed clinical and electrographic ASyS scenario after ischemic stroke, traumatic brain injury (TBI), meningitis, hyponatremia, and alcohol withdrawal. Respondents who managed neonatal seizures completed a neonatal stroke vignette. We assessed antiseizure medication (ASM) initiation, duration after clinical ASyS, and continuous electroencephalography (cEEG) use. Interrespondent variability was quantified by normalized Shannon entropy (H'). Multivariable generalized estimating equations accounted for repeated responses. RESULTS: Among 963 respondents (92 countries), 62% recommended ASM initiation in clinical and 61% in electrographic ASyS scenarios. Overall ASM initiation variability was high (H' = .96), highest for alcohol withdrawal (H' = 1.0), followed by stroke (H' > .9), and consistently higher for electrographic than clinical ASyS. Etiology was the strongest driver of ASM initiation. Compared with stroke, ASM initiation odds were higher for TBI (odds ratio [OR] = 2.5, 95% confidence interval [CI] = 2.2-2.8) and meningitis (OR = 1.9, 95% CI = 1.7-2.2), and lower for hyponatremia (OR = .2, 95% CI = .2-.2) and alcohol withdrawal (OR = .5, 95% CI = .5-.6). Compared with Europe, initiation odds were higher in North America (OR = 2.7, 95% CI = 2.0-3.6), Asia-Oceania (OR = 2.4, 95% CI = 2.0-3.0), and Latin America (OR = 2.0, 95% CI = 1.5-2.7), but not Africa. These differences persisted after accounting for country income. Compared with stroke, the mean ASM duration was 3.2 months shorter for hyponatremia and .6 months longer for TBI. Alcohol withdrawal durations were polarized (41% discontinued at discharge; 20% continued >12 months). cEEG use was markedly higher in North America than Europe (OR = 11.3, 95% CI = 7.8-16.5). Among 291 neonatal respondents, 75% continued ASM beyond discharge. SIGNIFICANCE: ASyS management varies markedly, reaching near-maximal heterogeneity in several common scenarios. Although etiology drives decisions, geography and provider characteristics also influence care, underscoring an urgent need for randomized trials and international consensus guidance to harmonize care.
Chemical Composition and Anxiolytic Potential of Nectandra grandiflora Essential Oil in Adult Zebrafish (Danio rerio): Behavioral, Molecular Docking, and ADMET Approaches.推荐
PMID 43032971 · Chemistry & biodiversity · 2026 Nov
作者：Rodrigues LKM, da Silva LE, Ceneviva DCDS, Amaral WD et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Nectandra grandiflora essential oil was investigated for its chemical composition and neuropharmacological effects in adult zebrafish (Danio rerio), complemented by molecular docking and ADMET predictions. The oil was administered intraperitoneally at 4, 20, and 40 mg/kg (n = 6/group). No evident acute toxicity was observed after 96 h (LD50 >40 mg/kg). In the Light/Dark Test, only 4 mg/kg increased the time spent in the light compartment versus control (p < 0.0001), indicating an anxiolytic-like effect. Flumazenil significantly reduced this response, supporting GABA receptor-mediated neuromodulation. Conversely, none of the tested doses protected against PTZ-induced seizures, differing significantly from diazepam (p < 0.0001). Molecular docking identified SEL as the compound with the most favorable predicted binding affinity to the GABAA receptor, while ADMET analysis suggested potentially favorable pharmacokinetic properties; however, both findings require experimental validation to confirm their biological and pharmacological relevance. Overall, the oil showed anxiolytic-like, but not anticonvulsant, activity under the evaluated conditions.

Ketogenic diet therapy in adolescents and adults with epilepsy: A scoping review of cognitive outcomes.推荐
PMID 43032929 · Epilepsia open · 2026 11 14
作者：Ibrahim T, Åkerstedt AM, Rudberg AS
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：OBJECTIVE: The ketogenic diet treatment (KDT) is an established non-pharmacological treatment for drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE). However, rigorous research for seizure efficacy based on randomized controlled studies in adolescents and adults remains limited, and cognitive outcomes are rarely assessed using standardized measures. METHODS: This scoping review has mapped evidence on cognitive outcomes of KDT in adolescents and adults with epilepsy. Following PRISMA-ScR guidelines, PubMed and Embase were searched for studies published between 1986 and 2026. Eligibility for this review required inclusion of adolescents or adults with epilepsy receiving KDT and reporting cognitive outcomes. RESULTS: Of 951 records, 19 studies were included. Overall, KDT was associated with favorable seizure and cognitive outcomes, although reports of cognitive outcomes were generally subjective. SIGNIFICANCE: Rigorous research designs were scarce, and evidence remains limited; future studies should use stronger designs, larger samples, standardized cognitive assessments, and longer follow-up periods. PLAIN LANGUAGE SUMMARY: Research shows that people with epilepsy often experience difficulties with the brain's ability to process information. While dietary treatments have been shown to be effective for seizure control, less is known about their effects on cognition. We reviewed published research examining cognitive outcomes in adolescents and adults with epilepsy following dietary treatment. Most studies suggested that these diets had a positive effect on aspects of cognitive functioning. However, the available evidence remains limited. More studies with better designs using well-established cognitive assessments are needed to better understand these effects.
Scalp high-frequency oscillations differentiate neonates with hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy with and without electroencephalographically confirmed seizures.推荐
PMID 43032925 · Epilepsia · 2026 11 14
作者：Karatza P, Cserpan D, Gennari AG, Grubenmann A et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜1区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q1
摘要：OBJECTIVE: Hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) is a leading cause of neonatal seizures, but seizure detection is challenging when continuous electroencephalography (EEG) is unavailable and neonates undergo only a single routine EEG. Biomarkers derived from routine EEG may aid identification of neonates with seizures. We investigated whether scalp high-frequency oscillation (HFO) rates derived from the first eligible routine EEG (1) differentiate neonates with HIE with and without seizures; and (2) contribute to seizure classification alongside established clinical, neurophysiological, and imaging biomarkers. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed routine scalp EEG recordings from 33 term neonates with HIE (17 with seizures, 16 without), obtained at a mean postnatal age of 4 ± 3 days. The mean analyzed EEG duration was 38 ± 14 min in neonates with seizures and 36 ± 20 min in those without. HFO rates in sleep segments were quantified using a validated automated detector. RESULTS: Scalp HFO rates were significantly higher in neonates with seizures than in those without (.12 ± .11 vs. .03 ± .02 HFOs/min/channel, adjusted p = .02). An HFO rate threshold of .07 HFOs/min/channel optimally discriminated neonates who had seizures from those who did not (area under the curve [AUC] = .81). HFO rates did not differ significantly according to HIE severity, therapeutic hypothermia, antiseizure medication response, EEG background activity, or magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) abnormalities. Multivariable models incorporating HFO rate, EEG background activity, and MRI findings showed discrimination (AUC = .92, 95% confidence interval [CI] = .69-1.00 for logistic regression; AUC = 1.00, 95% CI = .75-1.00 for random forest), with HFO rate as the strongest contributor to seizure classification. SIGNIFICANCE: These findings support scalp HFO rate as a potential electrophysiological biomarker of seizure occurrence in neonatal HIE, complementary to EEG background assessment and MRI findings. Prospective studies are needed to determine whether HFOs can predict subsequent seizures.

Strengths and weaknesses in epilepsy training across residency programs in Italy: A multicenter survey by the Italian chapter of the Young Epilepsy Section (YES-I).推荐
PMID 43032900 · Epileptic disorders : international epilepsy journal with videotape · 2026 11 14
作者：Cappelletto P, Cognolato E, Lozza F, Trevisan M et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜4区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q3
摘要：OBJECTIVE: The objective of the study was to assess epilepsy-related services across Italian neurology and child neuropsychiatry residency programs, evaluate residents and early-career specialists' perceived strengths/weaknesses in epilepsy training, and identify unmet needs and expectations for improving residency programs and Young Epilepsy Section (YES)-Italy educational initiatives. METHODS: A nationwide cross-sectional survey was conducted between January and April 2026 by a focus group of the YES-Italy Education Task Force. A structured questionnaire was distributed to neurology and child neuropsychiatry residents and specialists within 2 years of completing residency, assessing demographics, epilepsy-related services, self-perceived competencies, training strengths and limitations, and suggestions for improving residency curricula with YES-Italy educational activities. Descriptive statistics were used; group comparisons (neurology vs. child neuropsychiatry; residents vs. early-career specialists) employed the Mann-Whitney U test. RESULTS: The survey was completed by 150 participants. Epilepsy training was widely available (85.3%) but variable and often brief (<6 months). EEG training was mostly embedded within epilepsy rotation (32.7%), organized as a distinct brief rotation (<3 months; 33.3%), or not available (19.3%). Core epilepsy services were widely present (inpatient wards 82.7%; neurophysiology laboratories 93.3%), while other services were less common (such as epilepsy surgery). Marked variability in Status Epilepticus management emerged across centers. Participants perceived adequate competence for core clinical-diagnostic domains, but lower competence for EEG interpretation and complementary disciplines. Perceived competence was higher in chronic than in acute care. Child neuropsychiatrists reported greater competence in neuropsychology and genetics, neurologists in neuroimaging and acute management. Clinical rotations and multidisciplinary case discussions were major strengths, whereas lectures and direct teaching required improvement. Suggestions for the YES-I group highlighted improving EEG training, structured educational initiatives, access to courses, webinars, and scientific materials. SIGNIFICANCE: This survey highlights substantial heterogeneity and weaknesses in Italian epilepsy training, particularly regarding EEG interpretation, complementary diagnostics, and acute management, underscoring the need for national standardization, multidisciplinary educational pathways, and strengthened educational initiatives.

Promotion and economic considerations of domestic Vagus nerve stimulation within the Chinese three-tier epilepsy center system.推荐
PMID 43032689 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 21
作者：Ou Z, Le Z, Mi J, Yan R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：BACKGROUND: Epilepsy affects approximately 10 million people in China, with about 30% developing drug-resistant epilepsy (DRE). Vagus Nerve Stimulation (VNS) is an established neuromodulation therapy for DRE. Domestic VNS devices completed clinical trials in 2014 and entered clinical use in 2016. This study synthesizes clinical outcomes, promotion pathways, and economic considerations of domestic VNS devices within the Chinese three-tier epilepsy center network established by the China Association Against Epilepsy (CAAE). METHODS: This narrative synthesis integrates data from previously published multicenter prospective real-world studies, CAAE accreditation records, market research, and health economics literature. Clinical outcome data were derived from studies encompassing 124 adult and 87 pediatric patients with DRE across 86 epilepsy centers. A cost comparison between domestic and imported VNS devices was performed based on market data and implantation volumes from 2022-2025. RESULTS: In adults, responder rates (≥50% seizure reduction) increased progressively from 35.4% at 1 month to 76.6% at 12 months post-implantation, with seizure-free rates reaching 10.9%. In pediatric patients, according to the available follow-up data, ≥50% responder rates (combining seizure freedom, 75% reduction, and 50% reduction) were 43.4% at visit 2, 40.6% at visit 3, 56.6% at visit 4, and 55.8% at visit 5; seizure freedom rates ranged from 1.1 to 9.3% across visits. Significant improvements were observed in sleep quality (PSQI: 5.43 → 4.44), depression (NDDI-E: 6.49 → 4.83), anxiety (GAD-7: 7.15 → 4.95), and quality of life (QOLIE-31: 54.40 → 61.33). Adverse events were mild and transient, primarily voice changes (20.3%) and cough (8.6%). The three-tier network comprises 526 centers (45 Tier-3, 259 Tier-2, 222 Tier-1) with standardized protocols and telemedicine platforms. CONCLUSION: Domestic VNS demonstrates encouraging efficacy, favorable safety, and improvements in comorbidities in both adult and pediatric DRE populations within the CAAE three-tier network. The network provides a structured framework for standardized application. However, the evidence is limited by the uncontrolled design of the primary studies, short follow-up, and the absence of a formal cost-effectiveness analysis. Prospective comparative clinical and economic validation is warranted.
Endothelial glycocalyx remodeling and galectin signaling in aging associated epilepsy.推荐
PMID 43032655 · Frontiers in neurology · 2026 10 19
作者：Legradi A, Pareo C, Mir MY
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：综述
中科院分区：2025版｜医学｜3区
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜CLINICAL NEUROLOGY：Q2；NEUROSCIENCES：Q2
摘要：Physiological aging can alter the brain endothelial glycocalyx and blood-brain barrier (BBB), while experimental seizures independently engage galectin-3 (Gal-3) and inflammasome-associated inflammatory signaling. This narrative review critically examines whether these observations justify a shared mechanism in aging-associated epilepsy. Primary studies support endothelial mucin-type O-glycosylation as a determinant of BBB integrity and Gal-3 as a modulator of inflammation in selected seizure models. They do not establish that endothelial glycan remodeling changes Gal-3 binding or initiates epileptogenesis. We distinguish extracellular galectin-glycan recognition from intracellular inflammasome signaling and address the anatomical separation between parenchymal microglia and the luminal glycocalyx. Glycan elongation, ligand density, terminal modifications, and steric accessibility could alter binding in different directions; uniform exposure of galectin ligands cannot be assumed. Galectin-1 also has context-dependent protective and detrimental effects. We propose compartment-resolved experiments combining glycan measurements, cell-specific perturbations, BBB assessment, and longitudinal electroencephalography to test the convergence hypothesis.
Development of electrochemical aptamer-based sensors for therapeutic monitoring of lamotrigine.浏览
PMID 43032535 · Chemical science · 2026 11 09
作者：Bryant A, Wang L, Byrd C, Xiao Y
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：The anti-epileptic drug lamotrigine has a range of concentrations within which it is effective and unlikely to cause toxicity. However, inter- and intra-individual variation in pharmacokinetics makes it difficult to appropriately dose this drug, necessitating frequent and careful monitoring. Gold-standard quantitative methods such as immunoassays typically require sample preparation, analysis by trained personnel in centralized laboratories and have long turnaround times, rendering them inadequate for timely, convenient, and frequent dose adjustments. To address this issue, we develop an electrochemical aptamer-based (EAB) sensor capable of detecting physiologically relevant concentrations of lamotrigine in unprocessed whole blood. We first discover a new DNA aptamer that binds specifically to lamotrigine with a dissociation constant (K D) of ∼0.8 µM. We then adapted this aptamer in an EAB sensor, demonstrating rapid detection of clinically relevant levels of lamotrigine in whole blood. Importantly, our sensor shows excellent specificity against the major metabolite of lamotrigine, other anti-epileptics, and commonly used pharmaceuticals. With the capability to directly and rapidly quantify lamotrigine in unprocessed blood samples, this EAB sensor represents the simplest, fastest, and most convenient approach to lamotrigine detection to date. With further validation and optimization, the sensor described here could facilitate therapeutic monitoring of lamotrigine at the point-of-care.
Diagnostic Odyssey in a Patient with Incontinentia Pigmenti Elucidated by Optical Genome Mapping: A Case Report.浏览
PMID 43032273 · Case reports in dermatology · 2026 07 28
作者：Nascimento-Vidoti CG, Gomide LV, Salathiel ASM, Secamilli EN et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：病例报告
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：INTRODUCTION: Incontinentia pigmenti is a rare neurocutaneous disorder caused by variants in the IKBKG gene, with X-linked dominant inheritance and a high mortality rate in hemizygous males. This disorder has a characteristic natural history comprising 4 stages (bullous, verrucous, hyperpigmented, and hypopigmented skin lesions); additionally, the phenotypic spectrum is highly variable, and defects in hair, nails, teeth, and other systemic involvements can occur. Molecular confirmation can be performed to detect a common deletion of exons 4-10 in approximately 65% of cases, or sequence variants in less than 10% of cases. However, currently over 20% of cases may still yield a negative molecular result. CASE PRESENTATION: A female patient was referred due to neurodevelopmental delay, epilepsy, microcephaly, retinal detachment, and cutaneous changes, including diffuse vesiculobullous lesions in the neonatal period evolving in the first year to stable hyperpigmented macules and patches with a blaschkoid distribution. Molecular investigation using different methods, including targeted IKBKG sequencing and short-read whole-genome sequencing, initially ruled out the diagnosis of incontinentia pigmenti and revealed a heterozygous pathogenic variant in the COL7A1 gene, suggesting a diagnosis of transient bullous dermolysis of the newborn. However, persistent molecular investigation using optical genome mapping and long-read whole-genome sequencing confirmed that she had incontinentia pigmenti due to a partial deletion of the IKBKG gene. CONCLUSION: The case presents the challenges of a diagnostic odyssey and proposes that individuals with incontinentia pigmenti reported as negative for the intragenic deletion should be reanalyzed using long-read technologies.
Case Report: Late postpartum eclampsia-associated reversible posterior encephalopathy syndrome with anti-DPPX encephalitis.推荐
PMID 43031854 · Frontiers in immunology · 2026 10 30
作者：Xing X, He Y, Ma L, Zhang L
链接：PubMed · DOI

中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Posterior reversible encephalopathy syndrome (PRES) is a clinical-radiological syndrome characterized by headaches, seizures, visual disturbances, and focal neurological deficits, accompanied by specific cranial imaging findings. Common triggers of PRES include hypertension/eclampsia, renal disease, autoimmune disorders, and drug abuse. PRES associated with autoimmune encephalitis is a rare phenomenon that presents diagnostic challenges in clinical settings, often leading to missed diagnosis and delayed treatment. We report a case involving a patient with late-onset eclampsia, occurring 26 days after cesarean section, who exhibited dual neurological syndromes of reversible posterior encephalopathy syndrome and anti-DPPX encephalitis. MRI resonance imaging revealed vasogenic cerebral edema and extensive enhancing lesions.
The Sv DC Field: Large-Scale and Hierarchical Functions Regulator.推荐
PMID 43031843 · Research (Washington, D.C.) · 2026 11 13
作者：Yin K, Yu T, Xiao M, Li R et al.
链接：PubMed · DOI
命中检索：不限期刊主题候选（待筛选）
发表形式：期刊论文（研究设计待核对）
中科院分区：2025版｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
JCR分区：2026发布版（2025数据）｜待核验（期刊资料未覆盖）
摘要：Large-scale dynamic reorganization of brain functional networks has been extensively revealed by functional magnetic resonance imaging, yet its direct electrophysiological basis remains unclear. In our prior research on epilepsy, we identified a distinctive electrophysiological phenomenon referred to as the slow-varying direct current (Sv DC, 0.01 to 0.5 Hz) field. This field demonstrates both the stability characteristic of a physical direct current and the slow-varying property of bioelectrical signals, enabling it to reflect the dynamic alterations in relative bioelectric field fluctuations. To further characterize its behavior across different functional states, we recorded intracranial electroencephalography from epilepsy patients implanted with depth electrodes during visual, motor, and language tasks. The Sv DC field exhibited large-scale convergence and suppression, accompanied by information transfer to other frequency bands, during these tasks. In visual functional areas, Sv DC field displayed rhythmic oscillations tightly coupled with gamma activity and increased power, whereas in nonvisual areas, Sv DC field power decreased significantly. During seizures, Sv DC field showed characteristic spatiotemporal spreading dynamics, a phenomenon consistently replicated in macaque monkeys, rats, and mice. These electrophysiological patterns suggest that the Sv DC field exhibits both large-scale cortical functional networks and region-specific modulations, potentially offering a novel understanding on dynamic organization of brain functional networks.
